\section{Exact endpoint and proof spine}
Let $E$ contain $(t,s)$ only when one fixed, uniform, deterministic, total,
correct ordinary-SAT decider handles every encoded $N$-bit input in FLMV's indexed
read-only-input random-access model in time $N^{t+o(1)}$ and writable/index space
$N^{s+o(1)}$.  The relevant forbidden box is strict:
\[
                  t<\frac{241}{200},\qquad
                  s<\frac{11642479}{23232400}.             \tag{1}
\]
The retired universal endpoint $t+s<2\cos(\pi/7)$ is not used.  Under $P=NP$
there is some unknown finite polynomial diagonal $(K,K)\in E$; neither the
specific seed $d=901/500$ with subpolynomial space nor two resource bounds from
different algorithms follows.

\section{Current exact barriers and hostile controls}
\paragraph{Global backdoors.}
For proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ three-CNF, $3m=4n$ and every name meets four
distinct clauses, so any global clause-hitting set has size at least $m/4=n/3$.
For $k$ source-disjoint copies of the verified fifteen-variable UNSAT source $W_1$,
the minimum is exactly $5k$ and the dense physical encoding has
$N=\Theta(k\log k)$; a connected clause-monotone family has the same asymptotic
incidence obstruction.  This falsifies an all-UNSAT $O(\log N)$ \emph{global}
header, not adaptive local refutation or SAT runtime.  A single UNSAT component
can be refuted without touching all copies.

\paragraph{Resolution and algebraic coverage.}
LSPV-1 ARC-SOURCE-RESOLUTION-BARRIER186 proves an explicit linear-record
simulation of every accepted BRANCH/EMPTY/PATH certificate by general Resolution:
at most $m+B+L+2E$ lines and width $\max\{3,h+2\}$.  Hence connected odd-charge
cubic-expander Tseitin sources require $2^{\Omega(N/\log N)}$ records for this
grammar.  This source is signed exact-$(4,4)$, not exact-$(2,2)$.

LSPV-3 SOURCE-PAIRED-AFFINE132 gives linear characteristic-two certificates for
those same Tseitin sources, so the Resolution barrier does not transfer to an
affine-aware grammar.  PC-BRANCH-COLOUR134 supplies the stronger, different
counterfamily: properize standard graph-colouring by replacing each binary clause
$a\vee b$ with $(a\vee b\vee z)\wedge(a\vee b\vee\neg z)$ for a fresh $z$.
The common restriction $z=0$ preserves the PCR degree lower bound.  Combined with
restriction-gated leaf transport and the shallow-leaf/deep-tree dichotomy, the
explicit unshared fixed-$\mathbb F_2$ hybrid grammar has certificate floor
$2^{\Omega(N/\log N)}$.  Cutting planes, unrestricted semantic DAGs, mixed fields
and guest-$A$ leaves are not covered.  VERTEX-CUT-CRITICALITY134 separately shows
that clause essentiality, biconnectivity and high treewidth coexist with linear
affine/PC proofs, so those static measures are not universal hardness resources.

\paragraph{New residual-interface firewall.}
Define $\Sigma(G)$ for a 2CNF residual to retain its unordered clause-pair support
multiset and every variable's positive/negative occurrence counts.  Then
\begin{align*}
A={}&(\neg x\vee\neg y)(\neg x\vee y)(x\vee\neg z)(x\vee z)\in\UNSAT,\\
B={}&(\neg x\vee\neg y)(x\vee y)(\neg x\vee\neg z)(x\vee z)\in\SAT
\end{align*}
have the same $\Sigma$, while $B$ has exactly two models.  The eight-clause proper
ternary parent
\[
P=\bigwedge_{C\in A}(s\vee C)\wedge
  \bigwedge_{D\in B}(\neg s\vee D)                         \tag{2}
\]
has residual $A$ at $s=0$, residual $B$ at $s=1$, and exactly two models.
Thus a shared DAG cannot merge these children using $\Sigma$.  LSPV-3's SCC leaves
remain sound because they authenticate full signed implication endpoints and source
offsets, information deliberately omitted by $\Sigma$.  The bounded exhaustive audit
checked 1,070 assertion groups with zero failures; see
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-26_0848_LSPV-5_INTERFACE_SIGN_COLLISION165.tex}.

\section{Certificate polarity, circuit projection and resource firewalls}
For a fixed verifier $V(F,\pi)$ under a computable length-only cap, the correct
co-nondeterministic SAT machine guesses $\pi$ and rejects iff verification succeeds.
Soundness gives all accepting branches on SAT; completeness gives some rejecting
branch on UNSAT.  Malformed, over-cap and uniformly timed-out guesses must accept.
This quantifier orientation supplies neither the cap nor deterministic discovery.

For a witness verifier $V_N(F,w)$, a full SAT decision circuit must compute
\[
                       D_N(F)=\bigvee_w V_N(F,w).             \tag{3}
\]
A uniform polynomial constructor for $D_N$ gives $P=NP$; polynomial-size nonuniform
$D_N$ is NP$\subseteq$P/poly$.  Therefore LSPV-2's exact
CIRCUIT-RECORD-OUTPUT-SELECT169 accessor and $O(N\log^3N)$ verifier-gate envelope
do not instantiate van Melkebeek's full decision-circuit premise.  The corrected
inverse consumer uses the next comparator stride; final scan, inverse-route outputs,
aliases/constants and complete source Rank/Select remain open.

LSPV-4 PINNED-CIRCUIT-OUTPUT-FLOOR131 shows that explicitly serializing the displayed
proper circuit CNF leaves a linear physical output floor, so two levels do not become
power-smaller ordinary SAT children.  REPLAY-TAPE134 correctly separates a one-way
nondeterministically chosen stream from a free two-way proof tape.  The general
deterministic multipass equality inequality $(2p-1)S\ge k$ was already established in
PASSMEM84; it is not new here and remains a restricted sequential-access theorem.

\section{Highest-value next theorem}
Specify one source-authenticated certificate DAG whose node interface is
\emph{proved} sufficient for exact SAT and UNSAT gluing under all permitted merges,
including sign correlation, original-name identity and reconstruction.  Either prove
a polynomial length-only cap on every UNSAT ordinary input or immediately falsify the
cap with a proof-system simulation/lower bound.  Then give a uniformly clocked
coNTIME verifier with correctly oriented malformed/timeout branches and separately
derive one fixed deterministic all-input successor from arbitrary unknown $K$, charging
parsing, source/proof queries, inverse access, child generation, failed strategies,
replay, caches, ancestors, buffers and peak writable/index space.  No existing theorem
closes these arrows.


\section{Three-model resource firewall}
\paragraph{MODEL-TRANSFER-CONTROLS166 (26 October 2026, 15:48 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT:} for a fixed deterministic machine with one sequential
read-only input and fixed $k$ sequential work tapes, the conservative
single-read/write-tape rescan construction satisfies
\[
 T_1=O\!\left(T_k(N+S_k+\log(T_k+2))\right),\qquad
 P_1=O(N+S_k+\log(T_k+2)).
\]
These are upper constructions only.  M1 footprint includes the initial input,
so $P_1\ge N$ even when Mk uses zero work cells; it is not the FLMV writable
space coordinate.  The scan already includes probes, head motion and traffic.
Hennie--Stearns is a fast \emph{two}-tape simulation and is not substituted
for this one-tape construction.

A work-content-only crossing snapshot is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}: equal
work contents can coexist with distinct input-head positions and opposite next
outputs.  At one fixed boundary, a sound record must include finite control,
direction, all visited work-cell values and every work-head position.  With
that complete record the abstract exact22 equality-family splice/counting
schema is \textbf{PROVED/EXACT conditional on the published locality
construction}; its native sequential-input consequence is
\[
 T_k(S_k+\log(T_k+N)+1)=\Omega(N^2/\log N).
\]
The exact generator scripts named in governance were not present at the cited
repository path, so their properness/locality audit remains independently
pending.

\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED model transfer:} the sequential exact22
$t+s\ge2$ stripe is not a RAT/RAM bound.  Williams's indexed model pays
$O(\log T)$ to write an address, while a sequential input head may travel
$\Omega(N)$ for the same INDEX request.  The exact22 equality promise is
decidable in near-linear indexed bit time and logarithmic work space; this
easy promise neither decides full SAT nor refutes a full-SAT worst-case lower
bound.  RAM--RAT polylogarithmic correspondence additionally requires
$\Theta(\log N)$ words, polynomial addresses and bit-compilable operations.
Unlimited unit-cost words and semantic primitives are outside the transfer.

The deterministic reproducer passed 105 assertion groups with zero failures.
Readback-verified evidence is
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-26_1548_LSPV-5_MODEL_TRANSFER_CONTROLS166.tex}.
The living load-bearing arrows remain an all-input affordable UNSAT
certificate and one fully charged same-machine successor from arbitrary
unknown $K$ in the native FLMV model.  ARC-EDGE-90 and $P\ne NP$ remain
\OPEN.

\section{Crossing-average quantifier repair and newest trace audit}
\paragraph{CROSSING-AVERAGE-QUANTIFIERS167 (26 October 2026, 16:48 PDT).}
For the published exact22 left--gap--right family, fix one deterministic
machine with one sequential read-only input head and fixed $k$ sequential
work tapes. A complete record at a gap crossing contains finite control,
direction, all visited work contents and every work-head position, using
\[
 B=O_k(S+\log(T+N)+1)
\]
bits. At each fixed gap cut, the $2^m$ accepting computations on
$E_m(a,a)$ have pairwise distinct nonempty complete crossing sequences:
equality would splice an accepting run on the false hybrid $E_m(a,b)$.
A shortest-code counting lemma gives the exact average bound
\[
 2^{-m}\sum_a q_g(a)\ge \frac{m}{4B}.
\]
Summing this inequality over the $\Theta(N)$ gap cuts and interchanging
the two finite sums yields one \emph{common} $a_*$ with total crossing
count $\Omega(Nm/B)$. Since $m=\Theta(N/\log N)$,
\[
 T(N)\bigl(S(N)+\log(T(N)+N)+1\bigr)
       =\Omega(N^2/\log N).
\]
This is \textbf{PROVED/EXACT from the declared locality hypotheses} and
\textbf{CONDITIONAL} as a concrete exact22 theorem until the named generator
scripts are independently available/reconstructed. The shortcut
``each cut has some long input, therefore one input is long at every cut''
is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}: a $32\times32$ cost matrix has sum of
cutwise maxima $1024$ but largest single-input total $63$.
The deterministic audit checked 1,738 assertion groups with zero failures.

The product is native to one sequential input head. It does not transfer
to RAT/RAM, multiple input heads, malformed full SAT, or FLMV without a
new charged simulation. LSPV-3 SOURCE-LOCAL-RAM-TAPE-CERT136 supplies a
constant local authenticated refutation for one exact22 mismatch, confirming
that this easy family's tape cost is separated-source acquisition rather
than proof size. LSPV-1 ARC-INDEXED-TRACE-TRADEOFF188 is
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted} for the pair language $(F,\tau)$:
a given full deterministic rejecting trace is verifiable by indexed
last-write scans in $\widetilde O(N+R^2)$ time and logarithmic workspace.
It gives no uniform favorable cap on $R$, no free rereadable proof tape to
one-way nondeterminism, and no arbitrary-$K$ ARC successor.

\subsection*{LSPV-5 synthesis: COMMUNICATION-POLARITY-FIREWALL168 (26 October 2026, 17:48 PDT)}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted countercontrol; unrestricted arrows OPEN.}
The independently reconstructed 15-variable exact-(2,2) two-switch module has
model counts $(772,272,272,0)$.  On $d$ disjoint modules SAT is DISJ and
UNSAT is $\bigvee_i(a_i\wedge b_i)$.  The same family has deterministic
communication complexity at least $d$, GF(2) matrix rank $2^d-1$, and exact
XOR/AND/NOT multiplicative complexity $2d-1$, yet admits a uniform
one-sequential-input-head co-nondeterministic SAT verifier in
$N\polylog N$ time and $O(\log N)$ work: guess one coordinate, accept
invalid/malformed guesses and timeouts, and reject iff both switches are one.
Thus SAT has all branches accept and UNSAT has some rejecting branch.

This \textbf{FALSIFIES/RETIRES} any arrow from deterministic
communication, rank, or promised circuit hardness to an all-UNSAT coNTIME
certificate lower bound.  Since $d=\Theta(N/\log N)$, the exact promised
gate lower bound $2d-1$ is only $\Omega(N/\log N)$ and cannot be promoted
to the superlinear full-decision circuit threshold.  The family is
disconnected and promised; it supplies neither connected-source hardness nor
an unrestricted ordinary-SAT lower bound.

The machine-model firewall is unchanged.  The conservative
$M_k\to M_1$ inequalities are construction upper bounds, not lower bounds;
a direct one-tape algorithm may bypass a wasteful source program.  A RAT can
index switch positions without sequential head travel, so the sequential
$t+s\ge2$ stripe does not transfer.  All future-readable tapes and heads
belong in a splice record, and crossing records must be padded to one common
worst-case $T(N),S(N)$ alphabet before averaging.  Movement/probes already
occur inside transitions and are not double charged.

The deterministic reproducer checked 349,561 assertion groups, 87,380 promise
pairs, 844,684 coNTIME branches, and 174,760 malformed accepting branches,
with zero failures and no randomness.  Full report and raw evidence:
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-26_1748_LSPV-5_COMMUNICATION_POLARITY_FIREWALL168.tex},
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-26_1748_COMMUNICATION_POLARITY_FIREWALL168_repro.py},
and
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-26_1748_COMMUNICATION_POLARITY_FIREWALL168_raw.json}.
The decisive all-UNSAT polynomial cap, arbitrary-$K$ fully charged
same-machine successor, ARC-EDGE-90 and $P\ne NP$ remain \OPEN.

\subsection*{LSPV-5 synthesis: HEADMOVE-REVERSAL-JUMP169 (26 October 2026, 18:48 PDT)}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT RESTRICTED; unrestricted SAT and ARC arrows OPEN.}
For the ordered exact-(2,2) DISJ promise and one deterministic two-way
sequential read-only input head with fixed sequential work tapes, complete
crossing transcripts and double averaging select one common SAT run satisfying
\[
H(N)(S(N)+1)=\Omega(N^2/\log N),\qquad
(R(N)+1)(S(N)+1)=\Omega(N/\log N).
\]
The reversal conclusion uses a heavily crossed cut on that same run; it does
not sum per-cut maxima or mix witnesses.  Crossing labels include control,
direction, all visited work contents, every work-head position and delimiters.

A charged $B$-bit buffer saturates $h+s=2$ and $r+s=1$ on this easy
promise.  In the separately declared radius-$J=N^{j+o(1)}$ jump-head model,
simulating every binary-offset jump on an ordinary head gives
$t+s+j\ge2$; the promise optimum is
$t=\max\{1,2-s-j\}$.  This is not a RAM inequality.  A two-input-head
control parks one head at each controlled shore and decides the same promise
with $O(N)$ total movement, zero reversals and $O(\log N)$ work, explicitly
\textbf{FALSIFYING/RETIRING} any transfer of the one-head products to two
input heads, RAT or RAM.

The finite reconstruction checked 125,923 assertion groups, 21,844 physical
formulas, 8,190 pivotal-query subsets, 54 buffer/jump itineraries and six
two-head controls, with zero failures.  At $m=128$, the two-head normalized
movement and reversal products were 0.507 and 0.461 and continue downward.
Report and evidence:
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-26_1848_LSPV-5_HEADMOVE_REVERSAL_JUMP169.tex},
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-26_1848_HEADMOVE_REVERSAL_JUMP169_repro.py},
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-26_1848_HEADMOVE_REVERSAL_JUMP169_raw.json}.

The independently read LSPV-3 RAM-INDEXED-LOCAL-ACQUISITION137 result adds
a $2m$ indexed bit-probe floor on every equality input and an $m-1$
unique-mismatch locator lower bound, with a matching $N^{1+o(1)}$ indexed
upper control.  This is \textbf{PROVED/EXACT RESTRICTED} and confirms,
rather than erases, the model firewall: indexed acquisition is exponent one,
not sequential physical transport.  Neither result supplies all-ordinary-
UNSAT coverage, two power-short hereditary levels, an arbitrary-$K$
same-machine successor, ARC-EDGE-90, or $P\ne NP$; all remain \OPEN.

\subsection*{LSPV-5 synthesis: SIGN-SENSITIVITY-WORD-FIREWALL171 (26 October 2026, 20:48 PDT)}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT RESTRICTED; arbitrary word/RAM and unrestricted ARC arrows OPEN.}
For a fixed read-only input layout, every correct deterministic NO run,
sound accepting UNSAT-certificate path, or rejecting branch under the
co-nondeterministic SAT convention must probe a cell hitting every
YES-producing perturbation.  Thus $p$ individually sensitive bits give only
$\lceil p/W\rceil$ probes in an arbitrary $W$-bit cell layout.  If the
sensitive positions have spacing $\Delta$, one cell contains at most
$\lfloor(W-1)/\Delta\rfloor+1$ of them.  For $q$ position-disjoint
promise-preserving pairs, the same denominator gives a
$\lceil q/(\lfloor(W-1)/\Delta\rfloor+1)\rceil$ pair-hitting bound.

This independently upholds LSPV-1 ARC-CONNECTED-SIGN-AUTH191 in its exact
scope.  The odd-charge prism Tseitin source has $2h$ vertices, $3h$ edge
variables, $8h$ proper distinct clauses, $24h$ signed positions and exact
$(4,4)$ occurrence quotas.  Every one-sign mutant is SAT as ordinary CNF;
the $12h$ disjoint opposite-endpoint two-sign mutants preserve proper
signed exact-$(4,4)$ syntax and are SAT.  Under canonical interleaved
sign-plus-name records, $N=\Theta(h\log h)$ and
$\Delta=\Theta(\log N)$, so contiguous $W=\Theta(\log N)$ words preserve
$\Omega(N/\log N)$ authentication for both scopes.

The layout qualification is load-bearing.  Sign-major packing weakens the
generic guarantee to $\Omega(N/\log^2N)$ word probes, and a unit-cost
$W\ge N$ word collapses it to one.  Hence raw bit sensitivity is
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED as a word-width- and layout-invariant RAM lower
bound}.  Reformatting the ordinary source must itself be charged.  Cell
probes, one-head movement, FLMV indexed bit time and writable space remain
different resources; no endpoint is imported between them.

The finite independent control checked $h=3,4$, all vertex and vertex-pair
deletions, every mutation, quotas, clause uniqueness and aligned cell
occupancy: 1,783 assertions, zero failures.  LSPV-2's canonical/arbitrary
Rank/Select split and LSPV-3's streamed SCC leaves remain restricted
access/authentication results, not full decision circuits or all-UNSAT
coverage.  LSPV-4's physical output floor is retained.  Exact-$(2,2)$,
uniform affordable all-ordinary-UNSAT certificates, a charged two-level
ordinary-SAT successor from arbitrary unknown $K$, ARC-EDGE-90 and
$P\ne NP$ remain \OPEN.

\subsection*{LSPV-5 synthesis: PERTURBATION-TRANSVERSAL172 (26 October 2026, 21:48 PDT)}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT RESTRICTED; unrestricted SAT and ARC arrows OPEN.}
For a fixed baseline NO source and legal YES-producing perturbation family
$\mathcal E$, quotient source coordinates by the actual read-only input-cell
layout $\mathcal P$.  Every correct deterministic NO run, sound accepting
UNSAT-certificate path, or rejecting co-nondeterministic SAT branch probes a
cell transversal of the quotient perturbation hypergraph.  Thus the exact
source-authentication invariant is $\tau_{\mathcal P}(\mathcal E)$, not the
raw number of legal neighbors.

This gives the independently reviewed general form of Role 4's
ARC-SENSITIVITY-LOCALITY144 two-level surface:
\[
 \tau_{\mathcal P}(\mathcal E)\le |Q|
 \le p_1+q_1p_2+q_1q_2L_2.
\]
Individual sensitive bits and pairwise disjoint quota-preserving mutations
specialize to the published formulas.  Overlap is load-bearing: a star has
$n-1$ two-bit label-changing neighbors but transversal one; all pairs give
$\binom n2$ neighbors but transversal $n-1$.  Raw neighbor count is therefore
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED} as a general probe lower bound.

LSPV-3 INDEXED-LOCATOR-GAP140 is independently upheld in its canonical
fixed-record, bounded-word scope.  Its $m$ four-sign groups are disjoint and
an $O(\log N)$ contiguous word meets only $O(1)$ groups, so deterministic
acquisition has a worst-case singleton-defect UNSAT input requiring
$\Omega(m)=\Omega(N/\log N)$ word probes.  A guessed coNTIME branch can name
the bad coordinate cheaply; the connected bridge variant is not signed
exact-$(2,2)$; and two PIN descendants retain $\Theta(N)$ length.  Hence this
is not a superlinear time/space or ARC contraction theorem.

ARC-JUMP-REVERSAL-CERT192 remains restricted to one jumping sequential input
head, with corrected record factor $S+\log(N+TJ+2)+1$ and only conditional
ordinary-head time transport.  CIRCUIT-SCAN-REVERSE-SELECT173 remains a
table-free source-access result, not witness quantifier elimination, full
decision-circuit evaluation or child compression.

The deterministic audit exhaustively checked all 32,767 nonempty
perturbation-hypergraph families on four coordinates, every aligned cell
width, overlap controls, 64 tight two-level OR maps, and Role 3's record
spacing through $m=32$: 781,937 assertions, zero failures.  Semantic and
algebraic oracles can bypass the declared source-cell interface; packing
changes the quotient and must be charged.  Uniform affordable
all-ordinary-UNSAT coverage, a global compiler forced to instantiate a large
transversal after representation changes, arbitrary-$K$ same-machine ARC,
ARC-EDGE-90 and $P\ne NP$ remain \OPEN.

\subsection*{LSPV-1: ARC--PACKED--SOURCE--REFUTATION193, authenticated exponential upper bound (26 October 2026, 22:00 PDT)}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT RESTRICTED, independent peer review pending.}
On any proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ three-CNF with $n$ active original
variables, $m=4n/3$ clauses, and immutable source byte offsets, a greedy
set of pairwise variable-disjoint clauses has
\[
 h\ge\left\lceil\frac{m}{10}\right\rceil
 \ge\left\lceil\frac{2n}{15}\right\rceil .
\]
Branching on the three original variables of each selected source clause
closes one of the eight assignments by an authenticated original-clause
$\mathsf{EMPTY}(p)$ proof leaf, and continues the remaining seven.
Complete binary branching on all remaining $n-3h$ original variables
then closes every assignment of an UNSAT source by its actual falsified
original source clause; for a SAT source, a genuine satisfying model
survives and prevents a false proof. The exact number of leaves is
\[
 L(h,n)=\frac{7^h-1}{6}+7^h2^{n-3h}
 \le\frac76\,2^{\alpha n},\qquad
 \alpha=1+\frac{2}{15}\log_2(7/8)
 =0.9743139896076806\ldots .
\]
A source-authenticated $\mathsf{BRANCH}/\mathsf{EMPTY}$ tree with $2L-1$
nodes and $O(nL\log(N+2))$ emitted proof bits is therefore constructible
without parent-SAT queries. This is a modest quantitative strengthening
of LSPV-3's existing exhaustive $2^n$ certificate, \emph{not}
polynomial all-ordinary-UNSAT coverage or a superpolynomial lower bound
on \emph{optimal} certificates. Ordinary restricted descendants are
\emph{not} reclassified as exact-$(2,2)$, and no time/space contraction
from an unknown $P=NP$ exponent $K$ follows.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} one source-aligned zero-dependency reproducer
passed 529{,}210 deterministic assertions with zero failures on four
signed exact-$(2,2)$ SAT/UNSAT controls: $G$ (8 leaves),
$W_1$ (22{,}009), $G\dot\cup G$ (57), and
$W_1\dot\cup G$ (154{,}064), including 64 genuine two-level
restriction checks per eligible source. Explicit source-offset replay
found 330{,}130 proof bits on $W_1$ and 2{,}619{,}082 on
$W_1\dot\cup G$. These are finite controls, not the proof of the
asymptotic statement.

\textbf{MODEL FIREWALL:} The $M_1$ shared read/write tape counts the
initial input in footprint and any retained printed proof; the
fixed-$k$ sequential-input model charges head travel, all visited work,
and a one-way output stream; FLMV's indexed-input RAT charges every
index/writable bit. A simple implementation has
$\operatorname{poly}(N)L$ time and polynomial peak work, not a favorable
FLMV exponent. The conservative decision-only simulation
$t_1\le t_k+\max(1,s_k)$ is an upper construction, not a tape
lower bound transported to RAM. The earlier PIN-BUDGET171
full-cube retention lemma and ARC186 resolution-simulation barrier
are preserved; no prior scientific result is retired.

\textbf{DAG:} source-authenticated exponential upper construction
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT RESTRICTED}; complete finite both-label replay
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}; polynomial affordable all-ordinary-UNSAT
certificate cap \textbf{OPEN}; uniform arbitrary-$K$ same-machine ARC
successor \textbf{OPEN}; ARC-EDGE-90 and $P\ne NP$ \textbf{OPEN}.

\paragraph{ARC-JUMP-REVERSAL-CERT192 (26 October 2026, 20:56 PDT): signed-jump direction versus locally authenticated contradictions.}

For the real proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ DISJ source $F_m(a,b)$ with
$N=\Theta(m\log m)$, take one deterministic machine with \emph{one}
read-only input head (allowed target-only jumps of radius $J$) and fixed
$k$ sequential work tapes, with simultaneous visited work space $S$.
Every all-source-correct decider has \emph{some YES run} with
\[
 (R+1)\bigl(S+\log(N+TJ+2)+1\bigr)=\Omega(N/\log N),
\]
where $R$ counts signed input-head direction reversals \emph{including
jumps}. The full-work-configuration deterministic fooling-set transcript
across a fixed Alice/Bob cut transfers $\Omega(m)$ bits; all cross-cut
movements, however discontinuous, alternate direction. For polynomial
ceilings this gives the native augmented \emph{reversal}-space exponent
$r+s\ge1$ independent of $j$; this is \textbf{NOT} a random-access SAT
time bound. The separate charged $M_J\to M_k$ jump simulator yields
$t+s+j\ge2$ only \textbf{CONDITIONALLY} on the already governed native
HEADMOVE tape product for this declared family. On a NO source with one
$H(1,1)=W_1$ component a \emph{source-authenticated 205-line Resolution
proof} with maximum width eight exists, emphasizing guessed NO proof
versus total deterministic YES checking and certificate discovery.
The deterministic reproducer passed $36{,}536$ assertions, including

\paragraph{ARC-CONNECTED-SIGN-AUTH191 (26 October 2026, 20:05 PDT): every-path literal authentication on connected odd Tseitin sources.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT RESTRICTED, independent referee review pending.}
For the explicit odd-charged cubic prism Tseitin proper signed 3CNF $F_h$,
$h\ge3$, with $2h$ parity vertices, $3h$ Boolean edge names, $8h$ clauses,
and $N_h=H_h+24h(w_h+1)=\Theta(h\log h)$ binary input bits,
In the unbounded family $H_h=O(\log h)$ is a self-delimiting
header for widths/counts; the finite reproducer uses an 88-bit fixed
header only on its bounded tested cases.  $w_h=\lceil\log_2(3h+1)\rceil$, every one of its $24h$ literal sign
bits is \emph{individually SAT-sensitive}.  Flipping one original
source sign changes UNSAT to SAT while preserving physical length,
source variable IDs, and proper ternary clause structure.  Hence, on
the SAME UNSAT source, EVERY accepting path of ANY
all-ordinary-input sound nondeterministic UNSAT verifier (equivalently,
every rejecting coNTIME SAT branch), and every rejecting computation of
a deterministic total ordinary-SAT decider, reads ALL $24h$ designated
sign positions: $Q\ge24h=\Omega(N_h/\log N_h)$.
This is a necessary source-bit-authentication cost, not a lower bound
on proof-system size or a superlinear SAT runtime exponent.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT RESTRICTED promised countercontrol:} each
original edge's eight signed occurrences split into four
opposite-sign endpoint pairs.  These $12h$ disjoint pairs over the
whole formula cover all $24h$ source sign bits.  Flipping BOTH bits of
any pair produces a SAT \emph{proper signed exact-$(4,4)$} formula,
using solvability of the parity equations after removing two adjacent
vertices.  Thus any path of an UNSAT verifier sound throughout the
signed exact-$(4,4)$ promise queries at least one bit of every pair:
$Q\ge12h$.  It is \emph{not} an exact-$(2,2)$ or clause-monotone
transport.  Earlier LSPV-3 exact \emph{clause deletion} criticality
and short characteristic-two PC proofs are adopted, not claimed new;
ARC182 provided a distinct ordinary source-probe family.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} $320{,}730$ assertions, zero failures,
$624$ one-sign SAT mutants, $624$ individually balanced double flips,
$312$ perfect-matching double flips, $438$ independent DPLL controls,
and $30$ serialized two-level SAT/UNSAT descendant controls.
The same path is charged in three distinct machines:
$M_1$ one shared tape including input footprint, $M_k$ one
sequential input head with visited-work-cell storage, and
RAT indexed-read-only input.  HEADMOVE crossing/reversal claims
and FLMV random-access inequalities are kept in their native models.
The conservative $t_1\le t_k+\max(1,s_k)$ is a simulation UPPER
bound, not a SAT lower bound.  No shorter hereditary SAT children
or arbitrary-$K$ same-machine ARC successor are obtained.
\textbf{All-UNSAT affordable coverage, ARC-EDGE-90 and $P\ne NP$
remain OPEN.}

\paragraph{ARC-OUTPUT-LEDGER190 (26 October 2026): physical output-footprint convention firewall.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in specified input/output machine conventions; independent referee review pending.}
For any nonempty valid proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ 3CNF $F$ of $N$ physical bits,
the source-authenticated streamer $\Phi_r(F)$ emits $r$ disjoint variable-renamed
copies, preserving SAT/UNSAT, signed quotas and clause monotonicity when present.
At $r=N$ its output is $L=\Theta(N^2)$ physical bits, although one sequential
read-only-input multitape transducer with an \emph{exempt never-reread output port}
uses $T_k=N^{2+o(1)}$, $S_k=N^{o(1)}$ writable cells. A target
\emph{single shared read/write tape} that must physically retain the output
has $P_1\ge L$, so its general transducer footprint is not
$O(N+S_k+\log T_k)$. The correctly scoped conservative simulation is
\[
T_1=O(T_k(N+S_k+\log(T_k+N+2))),\quad
P_1=O(N+S_k+L+\log(T_k+N+2)).
\]
An append-only physical output prefix plus a compact shifting live simulation
zone proves both upper bounds. For one-bit SAT deciders $L=O(1)$, the original
directive's $t_1\le t_k+\max(1,s_k)$ and $p_1\le\max(1,s_k)$
remain valid. Two real $r_1,r_2$ levels preserve grammar and both labels,
but $L_1=\Theta(N^2), L_2=\Theta(N^4)$ for $r_1=N,r_2=L_1$,
so no ARC contraction is obtained. The full audit had 199 assertions
with zero failures; twelve physical output streams were emitted and hashed.
A conservative indexed-input-to-sequential simulation maps the native one-tape
$t_1\ge2$ to $t+\max(1,s)\ge2$ and the native one-head
$t_k+s_k\ge2$ to $t+\max(1,s)+s\ge2$; both are tautological over
$t\ge1,s\ge0$. The HEADMOVE $h+s\ge2$, $r+s\ge1$, and augmented
$t+s+j\ge2$ surfaces remain one-sequential-input-head model-native,
not RAT/FLMV bounds. This is a \textbf{model-transfer correction}, not
all-UNSAT certificate coverage or an arbitrary-$K$ successor.
\textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 and $P\ne NP$ remain OPEN.}

\paragraph{ARC-VIRTUAL-ADDRESS-ITINERARY189 (26 October 2026, 19:04 PDT): necessary indexed source-address structure, not RAM time.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT RESTRICTED; independent review pending.}
On the project proper signed exact-$(2,2)$, $15m$-variable/$20m$-clause DISJ subfamily
with $N=\Theta(m\log m)$ physical source bits and a $\Theta(N)$ fixed
middle gap, fix ONE deterministic total ordinary-SAT decider $A$ in the
FLMV indexed read-only-input random-access Turing machine model.
For its chronologically read source addresses $i_0=0,i_1,\ldots,i_q$,
write $D=\sum_j|i_j-i_{j-1}|$ and let $V$ be the number of sign
reversals of the nonzero address differences. If $B_A$ bounds the
complete mutable configuration bit length (including a pending input
target), then on ONE SAT input of the form $F_m(a,\bar a)$ both
\[
  (D+1)(B_A+1)=\Omega(N^2/\log N),\qquad
  (V+1)(B_A+1)=\Omega(N/\log N).
\]
The proof uses the $2^m$ DISJ fooling set, complete configuration messages
at \emph{virtual} crossings of each central bit cut, and double
averaging to select one common SAT input. FLMV Section~2.1 gives
$B_A=O(S_A+\log N)$ for the stated polynomial-time indexed-tape model.
For a polynomial-address log-word RAM with $W$ mutable words,
use $B_A=O((W+1)\log N)$ instead; do not silently equate words with bits.
\textbf{FALSIFIED AS MODEL TRANSFER:} $D$ and $V$ do NOT count
indexed source-read bit-time. A genuine on-promise comparator uses
$2m$ source probes and $O(m\operatorname{polylog}N)$ bit-time at
$O(\log N)$ workspace while its virtual itinerary can have
$D=N^{2-o(1)}$, $V=N^{1-o(1)}$. Thus this contributes a
\emph{source-address trace axis}, NOT a new RAM $t+s$ lower bound
or an ARC/FLMV contradiction.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 21,844 physical source instances,
994 SAT itineraries, 4,970 central-cut transcript uniqueness checks
and 140,125 assertions with zero failures. GitHub verified report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-26_1904_LSPV-1_ARC_VIRTUAL_ADDRESS_ITINERARY189.tex},
source \path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-26_1904_ARC189_virtual_itinerary_audit.py},

\paragraph{ARC-INDEXED-TRACE-TRADEOFF188 (26 October 2026, 16:27 PDT): indexed read-only authenticated proof and one-way coNTIME firewall.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent mathematical review pending:}
for one fixed deterministic total exact ordinary-SAT decider $A$ on
a standard fixed-$k$ sequential-input machine, a full
$R$-step rejecting trace $\tau$ on source $F$ of $N$ bits has
$P=\Theta(R\log(N+R+2))$ source-authenticated proof bits. On the
\emph{indexed read-only compound input} $F\#\tau$ of length
$X=\Theta(N+R\log(N+R+2))$, one uniform deterministic random-access
verifier authenticates the original input, finite transitions, all
work-cell last writes and the final rejecting halt in
\[
T_{\rm RAT}=O((N+R^2)\log^3(N+R+2)),\qquad
S_{\rm RAT}=O(\log(N+R+2)).
\]
It makes $O(R^2)$ fully charged indexed prior-proof queries rather than
buffering the complete work tape. This is a \emph{pair-language
witness verifier}, not a full SAT decision circuit or an ordinary-SAT
FLMV-achievable pair. \textbf{FALSIFIED AS A MODEL TRANSFER:}
free two-way indexed reading of a proof already supplied in immutable
input cannot be assumed for a one-way nondeterministic certificate
without copying and charging all $P$ bits or proving a different
one-way verifier. The literal guess-copy-index implementation uses
$\Omega(P)$ writes and peak storage; this is not a universal lower
bound on other nondeterministic machines. For the same pair-language
implementation, separate M1/Mk/RAT conservative native upper ledgers are
\[
\begin{array}{c|cc}
 &T&S\ {\rm (M1:\ footprint)}\\
{\rm RAT\ indexed}&\widetilde O(N+R^2)&O(\log(N+R))\\
{\rm Mk\ sequential\ index\ emulation}&\widetilde O(X(N+R^2))&O(\log(N+R))\\
{\rm M1\ shared\ tape}&\widetilde O(X^2(N+R^2))&O(X).
\end{array}
\]
The M1 row uses $T_1=O(T_k(X+S_k+\log(T_k+2)))$, giving
$t_1\le t_k+\max(1,s_k)$ only against the same compound input length.
The previous ARC187 faster Mk sorter has
$(T,S)=(\widetilde O(X),\widetilde O(X))$ and is a DIFFERENT
verifier; no cross-algorithm time/space coordinate mixing is permitted.
For a faithful $R=N^{K+o(1)}$ accepting UNSAT trace under hypothetical
$P=NP$, the indexed pair-verifier's upper source-time exponent is
$2K$, not the strict van-Melkebeek $c<1+1/K$ route for $K\ge2$.
Deliberate actual delay steps can inflate faithful records, but say
nothing about other compressed proof systems.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} independently coded read-only indexed last-write
auditor, 32 canonical five-bit toy short-CNF sources (9 SAT, 23 UNSAT),
128 actual two-original-variable-level descendants, 256 corrupted
traces rejected, 641 assertion groups, zero failures. The toy does
not satisfy the proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ grammar; no in-class ARC
shrinkage or measured FLMV machine bound is claimed.

\paragraph{ARC-CANONICAL-SELECTOR-AUTH184 (26 October 2026, 03:13 PDT): universal succinct conflict selectors are not authenticated UNSAT certificates.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, independent referee review pending:} for every well-formed proper signed three-CNF $F=\bigwedge_{j=1}^m C_j$, a fixed uniform source-only constructor outputs a bounded-fan-in circuit $J_F(y)$ of $O(m\log(m+2))$ gates that returns the FIRST clause falsified by assignment $y$, or sentinel $0$ if all clauses hold. Define $\operatorname{Conf}_F(y,j)=[1\le j\le m\ \wedge\ C_j(y)=0]$. The fully quantified identity for \emph{all} sources, assignments and labels is
\[
\boxed{\ \operatorname{Conf}_F(y,J_F(y))=\neg F(y)\ },
\qquad
\boxed{\ \forall y\,\operatorname{Conf}_F(y,J_F(y))\ \Longleftrightarrow\ \UNSAT(F)\ }.
\]
The original source may belong to the larger proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ class or the strictly smaller clause-monotone exact-$(2,2)$ class without affecting the construction; $J_F$ is a CIRCUIT on assignment bits, not itself a target-class CNF child or a full input-to-decision circuit $C_{A,N}$. \textbf{KNOWN THEOREM/COROLLARY:} canonical-pair totality testing remains coNP-complete even on D\"ocker's proper clause-monotone exact-$(2,2)$ source grammar, since the uniform polynomial-size pair $(F,J_F)$ is valid iff $F$ is UNSAT. This sharpens Role-5 SKOLEM-VALIDATION159: witness \emph{generation} is inexpensive even for SAT sources, but authenticating its universal statement is \emph{exactly the original parent SAT query}; $\exists y\,\neg\operatorname{Conf}_F(y,J_F(y))\iff\SAT(F)$. Authentication through the supplied uniform solver $A$ retains a charged parent-scale call with only its original $N^{d+o(1)}$ upper bound, not a favorable coNTIME exponent. Every finite-depth restriction tree has such uninformative syntax circuits on \emph{every} node, including SAT roots, until genuine child proof or global totality validation is paid.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} real AND/OR/NOT gate-list constructor, 58 source formulas (56 SAT, two UNSAT), 68,136 fully enumerated source assignments, eight actual two-variable restriction descendants, 338,274 independent explicit assertions, zero failures. W20 is genuine signed exact-$(2,2)$ UNSAT, Q19 has 244 models, Y8 is genuine clause-monotone exact-$(2,2)$ SAT with 20 models. The eight two-level residuals remain ordinary short CNF, NOT exact-$(2,2)$, so recursive target eligibility is not claimed. Physical FLMV bit traffic and peak storage are not measured.

\textbf{OPEN proof-DAG arrows:} a uniform all-input, sound-and-complete and \emph{locally authenticated} adaptive UNSAT certificate mechanism with fully charged favorable time; the unrelated deterministic simultaneous small-space seed for unknown achievable exponent $K$ under $P=NP$, or a fully charged ARC machine successor. Van Melkebeek Theorem 1.3 requires both a conondeterministic SAT algorithm and a small-space deterministic SAT algorithm; Theorem 6.3 concerns full SAT decision circuits and a small-space $\Sigma_2$SAT algorithm, not $J_F$. The stricter extra $d=901/500,s=o(1)$ hypothesis still does NOT follow from $P=NP$. All five existing roles retain schedules and original ownership regions. ARC-EDGE-90 and $P\ne NP$ remain \textbf{OPEN}.

\paragraph{ARC-UNSAT-ACCEPTING-PATH182 (26 October 2026, 01:10 PDT): accepting-path source-probe floor and full two-algorithm OR frontier.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT on ordinary proper signed three-CNF; peer review pending:}
Reusing the exact fixed-incidence ARC181 parity family $F_{p,b}$ ($n=4p+1$, $m=8p$, physical $N=\Theta(p\log p)$, $\SAT(F_{p,b})=\bigoplus_i b_i$), the all-zero instance is UNSAT and changing any one bit yields SAT by changing only four source sign-bit fields in one of $p$ disjoint source-address sets $D_i$. \emph{Every accepting branch of any sound nondeterministic UNSAT verifier}, including any guessed certificate and arbitrary adaptive read strategy, must read from each $D_i$ on the UNSAT all-zero source: replaying a branch omitting $D_i$ on the SAT single-flip neighbor gives identical observations and an unsound acceptance. Consequently its initial-input accepting-branch time is at least $p=\Omega(N/\log N)$, but \emph{not} superlinear; the parity promise has a near-linear/logspace checker and a linear NAND decision circuit. Neither exact signed-$(2,2)$ nor clause-monotone $(2,2)$ transport is asserted without an indexed, fully charged and independently checked compiler.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT primary-text algebra:} Van Melkebeek's ECCC TR07-099, Theorem 1.3 (2007), uses the disjunction $(c-1)d<1$ \emph{OR} $cd(d-1)-2d+1<0$ for a conondeterministic $N^c$ SAT algorithm paired with a separately allowed deterministic \emph{simultaneous} $N^d$-time/$N^\epsilon$-space SAT algorithm. The correct combined frontier is
\[
c<H(d)=\max\!\left\{1+\frac1d,\frac{2d-1}{d(d-1)}\right\},
\qquad C(d)-(1+1/d)=\frac{2-d}{d-1}.
\]
The curved branch dominates for $1<d\le2$; the first dominates for $d\ge2$ and $H(d)>1$ for every finite $d>1$. At the \emph{extra} $d=901/500=1.802$ seed, $C(d)=651000/361301\approx1.801821749732$, without implying any such seed from $P=NP$.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 166 small proper signed 3CNF labels (97 SAT/69 UNSAT), 35 single source-bit-sensitive flips, 501 blind-group witness tests, seven exact threshold cases, 810 assertions, zero failures. Complete report/code/data/bibliography and shared five-role handoff:

\paragraph{ARC-PARITY-TRANSCRIPT-FIREWALL181 (26 October 2026, 00:05 PDT): a necessary correction to checkpoint-transcript quantifiers.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT; independent peer review pending:} There is a uniform fixed-incidence and fixed-length family of \emph{ordinary proper signed three-CNFs} $F_{p,b}$, $b\in\{0,1\}^p$, containing $4p+1$ variables and $8p$ clauses, with $\SAT(F_{p,b})=\bigoplus_i b_i$ and physical length $N=\Theta(p\log p)$. Each input bit changes exactly four source-polarity positions in a disjoint fixed group. Every total deterministic exact ordinary-SAT decider, from an input-independent initial state before any source-dependent read, must probe at least one bit from \emph{each} group on every family member. Its full source-address/answer transcripts therefore have $2^p$ distinct values, although its final decision labels number only two. Thus the newest executive statement ``SAT's one-bit output implies $R\le2$'' is valid only for the \emph{final-output} interpretation of $R$, \emph{not} the full probe-transcript interpretation. The general $2^{S+qw}$ counting inequality is retained, with an explicit checkpoint and source-charge convention. A second, post-preprocessing checkpoint may retain the parity in one bit and require zero further probes; no arbitrary-checkpoint $S+q\ge p$ is claimed.

Transcript multiplicity alone therefore gives neither an exponential SAT decision circuit nor an unrestricted SAT time-space lower bound; the bit-probe lower bound is only $\Omega(N/\log N)$ on an easy promise. Transfer to the proper \emph{signed} exact-$(2,2)$ grammar using the peer $R_{15}$ locally readable compiler is \textbf{CONDITIONAL on an independent compiler audit}; no clause-monotone transfer is asserted. The all-UNSAT affordable certificate and arbitrary-$K$ small-space seed remain \textbf{OPEN}. ARC-EDGE-90 and $P$ versus $NP$ remain \textbf{OPEN}.

\paragraph{ARC-KSEED-DEPTH-COVER180 (25 October 2026, 23:28 PDT): a sharp arbitrary-polynomial-seed depth and separator-budget theorem, without an all-input certificate construction.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT symbolic ledger, independently reviewable but peer review pending.}
For a \emph{hypothetical} uniformly clocked, sound, all-UNSAT-complete adaptive certificate family, assume each length-$n$ node actually generates at most $n^{1/2+\kappa+o(1)}$ ordinary SAT children of length at most $n^{1/2+o(1)}$, every emitted child bit has polylogarithmic amortized generator work, all local verification/provenance/original-name-compatible joins cost $n^{1+o(1)}$ per node, every terminal child runs the \emph{same fixed} exact ordinary-SAT guest $A$ in time $m^{K+o(1)}$, and every other failed route, source read, index, output, certificate bit and ancestor cost is included. At fixed depth $L$ allowed to depend on fixed $K$ but not source $N$, the certified level-$L$ leaf population, maximum leaf-size exponent, total terminal volume and charged eager/guest-time exponent are
\[
P_L=(1+2\kappa)(1-2^{-L}),\quad
\lambda_L=2^{-L},\quad
b_L=1+2\kappa(1-2^{-L}),\quad
c_L=1+2\kappa+(K-1-2\kappa)2^{-L}.
\]
For $K\ge2$ the source-verified van Melkebeek Theorem~1.3 has the strict co-nondeterministic window $c<H(K)=1+1/K$, provided a \emph{separate} deterministic SAT decider has simultaneous time $N^K$ and suitably \emph{small} writable/index space $N^e$ in the matched random-access model. \textbf{PROVED/EXACT arithmetic:} for this \emph{declared upper-bound ledger} some finite depth enters the strict window if and only if $\kappa<1/(2K)$; the least depth satisfies
\[
2^L>\frac{K-1-2\kappa}{1/K-2\kappa},
\qquad\text{in particular }2^L>K(K-1)\text{ for }\kappa=0.
\]
Examples: $(K,\kappa,L)=(10,0,7),(10,1/50,8),(100,0,14)$ enter; $\kappa=1/20$ at $K=10$ and $\kappa=1/100$ at $K=100$ cannot be certified by this bound at any depth. A fixed positive $\kappa$ does \emph{not} suffice for all unknown polynomial $K$. With per-emitted-bit exponent $g$, root output imposes the additional strict upper-budget requirement $\kappa+g<1/K$; per-terminal multiplicative access overhead $u$ requires $2\kappa+u<1/K$ in the limiting ledger. These are \emph{conditional quantitative design constraints, not lower bounds for other SAT algorithms}.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} an independent standard-library exact rational reproducer passed 2,276 assertions, including 80 SAT and 80 UNSAT ordinary proper-3CNF roots, two genuine levels of full original-name-compatible relation joins per root, and adversarial all-local-SAT opposite-parent-label controls. This is a semantic test, \emph{not} a two-level hereditary M22 child implementation. Earlier ARC177/GATE179 exact-class tests are preserved without double counting.

\textbf{OPEN:} all-UNSAT affordable certificate coverage and construction; the needed algorithm-dependent $\kappa(K)$ acquisition without uncharged work; small-space deterministic seed for arbitrary $K$ (P=NP yields merely some $(K,K)$); local indexed ordinary-SAT/exact-(2,2) transport, full circuit production, same-machine FLMV time and peak space, ARC-EDGE-90 and $P\ne NP$. The extra $(1.802,o(1))$ seed is \textbf{not} a consequence of P=NP. Primary source was checked directly: van Melkebeek 2007 survey Theorem~1.3, p.~2, and Bathie--Williams 2024 NAND uniform depth in its own model. No new full SAT decision-circuit compression is claimed.

\paragraph{CERTIFICATE-TWOALGO-GATE179 (25 October 2026, 23:00 PDT): co-nondeterministic certificate theorem gate, strict parameter arithmetic, and an exact-grammar label-join counterexample.}
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM, primary source rechecked:} van Melkebeek's 2007 SAT lower-bound survey Theorem~1.3 says: if $(c-1)d<1$ \emph{or} $cd(d-1)-2d+1<0$, then for some $e>0$ ordinary SAT cannot simultaneously possess a co-nondeterministic random-access time-$N^c$ machine and a deterministic random-access time-$N^d$, space-$N^e$ machine. The two machines may differ; each machine's own bounds and input model must hold. The union of the two strict inequalities gives a \textbf{PROVED/EXACT algebraic} threshold
\[
 c<H(d)=\max\left\{1+\frac1d,\frac{2d-1}{d(d-1)}\right\}
 =\begin{cases}\frac{2d-1}{d(d-1)}&1<d\le2,\\[2pt]1+\frac1d&d\ge2.\end{cases}
\]
At the \emph{extra, unjustified by $P=NP$} seed $d=901/500$, $H(d)=651000/361301=1.801821749732\ldots$. \textbf{PROVED/EXACT conditional interface:} a uniformly clocked sound and all-UNSAT-complete certificate verifier $V(F,\pi)$ yields coNTIME($N^c$) by rejecting a computation branch only upon a valid UNSAT proof, accepting all malformed and timeout guesses. For real adaptive ordinary child lengths $m_i$, $\sum_i m_i^d\le M^{d-1}V_{\rm leaf}$ with $M=\max_i m_i$, $V_{\rm leaf}=\sum_i m_i$; including proof reading $N^v$, separate local checking $N^r$ and per-call multiplicative access overhead $N^u$, the conditional upper certificate is
\[
 c\le\max\{v,r,u+b+\lambda(d-1)\}\quad
 [M\le N^{\lambda+o(1)},\ V_{\rm leaf}\le N^{b+o(1)}].
\]
For $\lambda=1/2,u=0$, crossing the cited strict frontier requires $b<1.400821749732\ldots$ and $v,r<1.801821749732\ldots$, together with actual all-UNSAT coverage. The near-boundary deterministic hypothesis $S_A=N^{o(1)}$ does \emph{not} follow from $P=NP$ (which only supplies some unknown finite jointly achievable $(K,K)$). Therefore even a contradiction under that stronger seed would \emph{not} prove $P\ne NP$.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT clause-monotone exact-$(2,2)$ label-only-join falsifier, with independently checkable finite dependencies:} the original proper 198-variable/264-clause UNSAT D\"ocker source $U$ and a positive-incidence-swapped SAT mate $V$ both have exactly two positive and two negative occurrences per variable and proper, unique, exclusively monochromatic clauses. Partition each into four source blocks of 1, 88, 88 and 87 clauses. All four original blocks of \emph{both} parents are SAT despite $\SAT(U)=0$ and $\SAT(V)=1$. The earlier ARC177 exact occurrence-restoring compiler creates eight genuine target-class SAT descendants, with explicit witnesses (compiled clause counts $92,560,668,1016$ per parent). Consequently a global SAT join depending only on the vector of child SAT labels fails on this actual NP-complete grammar. This does \emph{not} falsify richer original-name-compatible boundary joins and does \emph{not} prove any unrestricted lower bound. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 77 explicit passing assertions, checked $U$ core/refutation and $V$ witness, 8 real grammar-valid children, 4,672 total serialized compiled clauses, four accepted full small UNSAT trees, 35 malformed trees rejected. No FLMV machine-bit trace is claimed.

\textbf{OPEN:} a complete universally affordable original-name-compatible UNSAT certificate system with all terminal size, total volume, discovery, source and bit accesses, selected guest calls and verifier costs charged; an arbitrary-unknown-exponent same-machine successor; a legitimate near-boundary deterministic seed; and $P$ versus $NP$. The earlier source-only fast normalizer and retired sum-cosine shortcuts remain invalid. Theorem~6.3 for \emph{full} SAT decision circuits and the Bathie--Williams uniform NAND-depth result are not imported into per-instance gate traces. No five-agent schedule or peer-owned region changed.

\paragraph{ARC-DP-CURVE-FRONTIER178 (25 October 2026, 10:53 PDT): strict curved FLMV firewall for source-only scoped normalizers.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT derivative within the source-only uniform streaming interface, independent peer audit pending:}
If a fixed uniform source-only all-ordinary-SAT-input normalizer produces a valid named OPEN/CLAUSE/CLOSE stream of size $M\le N^{\mu+o(1)}$ and live width $w\le\kappa\log_2N+o(\log N)$, taking charged time $N^{p+o(1)}$ and peak writable/index space $N^{\sigma+o(1)}$, one-frontier streaming bitset DP gives a \emph{single} total exact ordinary SAT decider in the same indexed-input FLMV model with joint exponents
\[
 (t,s)\le\bigl(\max\{p,\mu+\kappa\},\max\{\sigma,\kappa\}\bigr).
\]
Thus such a source-only generator is impossible whenever $c=\max\{p,\mu+\kappa\}>1$ and $\max\{\sigma,\kappa\}<\beta(c)=((c+2)/c^2-c)/2$ (strict asymptotic slack understood). In the explicit near-linear slice $\mu=1$, $p\le1+\kappa$, $\sigma\le\kappa$, the forbidden interval is $0\le\kappa<\kappa_*$ where $\kappa_*=0.309774739896\ldots$ is the unique positive root of $3\kappa^3+7\kappa^2+4\kappa-2=0$. Under the strict rectangular endpoint the sufficient source-only no-go is $\kappa<41/200$, $p<241/200$, $\sigma<11642479/23232400$; this includes ARC171's \emph{conditional} $\kappa<41/800$ hereditary width ambition. The direct frontier DP was ALREADY known to dominate the same-stream guest architecture (ARC-DP-DOMINANCE167; INTERFACE-PROJECTION8 treated $\kappa=1/20$); new here is the exact curved coefficient boundary and the ARC171 alignment audit. ARC177's $w_{\mathrm{child}}\le3w_{\mathrm{parent}}+210$ on a square-root child grants only coefficient $\kappa'\le6\kappa+o(1)$, not hereditary $\kappa$.

\paragraph{ARC-EPOCH-TAPES173 (25 October 2026, 08:30 PDT): resetless sequential SAT guest tapes and the FLMV far-index firewall.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent review pending:}
For one fixed uniform deterministic total exact ordinary-SAT guest
$A$ with simultaneous $(t,s)$, a correctly typed total parent policy
using $Q\le N^{a+o(1)}$ \emph{sequential} genuine ordinary child calls
of length $L\le N^{\lambda+o(1)}$ can simulate fresh guest work and
index tapes without a separate $Q S_A(L)$ tape-wiping cost.
Encode each guest logical tape cell at a contiguous physical position
using an $O(\log N)$-bit epoch tag and symbol; increment the epoch per
call and treat old tags as blank. Guest indexed accesses, every tag
read/write (unchanged writes included), original-source reads, and
virtual-input generation are paid. Given fully flattened per-guest-bit
generation exponent $g$, local work exponent $r$, generator-space
exponent $\eta$, and retained parent/cache/ancestor exponent $\kappa$,
the \emph{same compiled machine} has the conditional envelope
\[
t'\le\max\{r,a+\lambda t+g\},\qquad
s'\le\max\{\kappa,\eta,\lambda s,0\}.
\]
This is a \emph{conditional implementation theorem}, not an actual
all-input favorable ARC machine transformation. Any full original-parent
$A$ call contributes its separately charged original-scale time and
space; it cannot be hidden in the short-child terms.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted FLMV model countercontrol:}
one indexed work-tape read at physical position
$\lceil N^{3/4}\rceil$ costs $\Omega(N^{3/4})$
in the FLMV space gauge even if only one work-tape value bit is
nonblank. Thus the peak number of nonblank or abstract live bits is
\emph{not} by itself a valid FLMV work/index space certificate.
The epoch compiler explicitly lays out work and index tape cells
densely, so its physical position bound is
$O((S_A(L)+1)\log N)$, including the epoch tags.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL, source-level only:}
ten ordinary signed-ternary roots (five SAT and five UNSAT),
20 first children, 40 genuine second-level grandchildren, and
ten deliberately paid original-parent guest baseline calls
(total 70 guest calls); 33,928 actual bit-probe iterations and
174,587 assertions, zero failures.
Each child is fully serialized and reparsed, with DPLL checked
against independent exhaustive truth tables. Hostile controls
include malformed inputs and the incompatible shared-name join.
Symbolic epoch-tag counters are not physical FLMV-TM transition
measurements, and the baseline full-parent calls make this
demonstrator intentionally noncontracting.

\textbf{OPEN:} the machine-producing ARC-EDGE-90,
a fully charged $A$-dependent exact all-input ordinary-SAT or

\paragraph{ARC-PIN-BUDGET171 (25 October 2026, 07:00 PDT): restricted two-level full-Shannon-cube retention and image-inflation transport firewall.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent peer audit pending:}
For an ordinary dense bounded-occurrence ternary-CNF family with $m$ clauses,
$N=\Theta(m\log m)$ bits, and variable occurrence bound $D$, a
\emph{complete} two-real-level Shannon cube fixing $k$ distinct variables
retains at least $m-Dk$ untouched clauses in \emph{every} leaf.
If its total leaf budget is polynomial $B=2^k\le N^{a+o(1)}$,
then $k=O(\log N)$ and all fully serialized ordinary descendants,
even after dense renumbering, remain $\Theta(N)$ bits
($\lambda=1$). A fixed $\lambda<1$ by this \emph{literal-restriction-only full-cube}
architecture requires $k=\Omega(m)$ and exponential fanout.
This is \emph{not} a general SAT lower bound: adaptive pruned trees,
semantic normalizers, easy bypasses and padded promise gadgets
are outside the theorem; ARC-ADAPTIVE-COMB170 already exhibits
restricted short descendants with adaptive pruning.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted transport:} for a declared reduction
image of length $N(L)=L^{p+o(1)}$, any pipeline that really emits
all $N(L)$ output bits incurs $\Omega(L^{p-o(1)})$ counted work;
$p\ge241/200$ precludes strict source-scale FLMV time entry
\emph{for that full-emission architecture}. This explains why the
quartic comb-image promise alone cannot transport a strict
ordinary-source bound. Arbitrary lazy local-bit access remains
subject to separate fully charged source/index/cache audits and
is not excluded by this lemma.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} deterministic six SAT and six UNSAT
ordinary-CNF roots, 84 first-child and 1,008 second-child
serialized/reparsed DPLL evaluations, 2,236 assertions,
zero failures; q=8 to 256 minimum grandchild/root
byte ratio rises from $0.747368$ to $0.977403$.

\paragraph{ARC-POLICY-SLOPE156 (24 October 2026, 22:58 PDT): paid state-dependent selector channel and parent-sized black-box query barrier.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted query-ledger algebra and mandatory black-box padding lemma, mathematical peer review pending; COMPUTATIONAL 57 exact-rational recurrence checks.}
All five active staggered roles have been reallocated to this \emph{single} ARC/FLMV simultaneous time--space contraction spine; previously valid UWI, M22, wrappers, selectors and proof-system results remain supporting evidence only. The current target is the upward-closed set $\mathcal E$ of \emph{jointly} achieved exponents for one uniform total ordinary-SAT exact decider on all inputs in the FLMV indexed read-only-input random-access model. Under $P=NP$ some $(K,K)\in\mathcal E$ exists. The strict matched FLMV forbidden box is
\[
t<\frac{241}{200},\qquad s<\frac{11642479}{23232400}.
\]
The old universal cosine endpoint remains \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}. The finite-hit lemma from FINAL-LINK-ARC is a \emph{conditional} existential proof template, not an all-input machine-producing edge.

For a \emph{genuine} total all-input uniform wrapper of fixed supplied $A$ with joint exponents $(t,s)$, suppose a paid adaptive rule/selector channel makes at most $N^{b+o(1)}$ sequential solver calls on ordinary virtual queries of length $N^{\rho+o(1)}$, every per-step input probe costing at most $N^{g+o(1)}$ bit operations. With the proposed but still \textbf{OPEN} recursive base channel $(a,\lambda)=(3/5,1/2)$, nonchild time exponent $11/10$, and fully paid parent/ancestor/generator space exponent $1/2$, the \emph{conditional} same-machine envelope is
\[
t'\le\max\{11/10,\,3/5+t/2,\,b+g+\rho t\},\qquad
s'\le\max\{1/2,\,s/2,\,\rho s\}.
\]
For $\rho<1$, the numerical time fixed point is
\[
T_*=\max\{6/5,(b+g)/(1-\rho)\}.
\]
A strict endpoint via \emph{this envelope} requires $b+g<(241/200)(1-\rho)$. For $\rho=1/2$ this is $b+g<241/400$, leaving only $1/400$ exponent of slack beyond $3/5$. Eager materialization of a policy input of length $N^\rho$ places the entire child buffer in counted workspace; $\rho=3/5$ or $3/4$ therefore misses the space target even when the \emph{lazy numerical} fixed point remains $6/5$. Actual bit-probe decoding, live ancestry, failure and replay costs remain charged.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted padding no-go:} assuming $P=NP$, artificially delay a fixed correct polynomial-time SAT decider by $\Theta(M^K)$ steps to obtain a uniform total exact polynomial-time decider $A_K$. Any wrapper that must \emph{execute} the supplied $A_K$ on a $\Theta(N)$-bit SAT query for infinitely many parent lengths has worst-case time $\Omega(N^K)$, and so cannot promise a strict speedup for \emph{every} supplied solver by compulsory full-length lookahead. \paragraph{M22-PORT-CROSSING154 (24 October 2026, 21:55 PDT): necessary endpoint-witness crossings in strict pure-sign port gadgets.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT within a declared static existential port-gadget model, independent mathematical review pending; COMPUTATIONAL LP controls.}
For any pure-sign Boolean OR-CNF gadget $G(p,y)$ with $r\ge2$ port names and

r\le \min\{d_+,d_-\}|D|,\qquad
|D|\ge\left\lceil\frac{r}{\min\{d_+,d_-\}}\right\rceil.
\]

extension witnesses}, \textbf{not} a temporal computational bit flip,
live-memory lower bound or SAT decision-time obstruction.

\paragraph{M22-NAE-PSD-CLIQUE150: exact local-marginal PSD obstruction (24 October 2026, 20:04 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the fixed uniform six-row clause-law model,
independent review pending; COMPUTATIONAL full six-name controls.
ARC-EDGE-90 and DIRECT-POST OPEN.}
The earlier M22-NAE-PAIR149 theorem gives pairwise overlap-compatible
clause-local distributions $\mu_S$ uniform over the six nonconstant
three-bit rows, including partially complemented source supports.
The new theorem proves these \emph{same} local laws do not, in general,
admit a \emph{global joint distribution or PSD degree-two moment matrix}.
For spins $Z_i=2X_i-1$ the law enforces
$\mathbb E Z_i=0$ and $\mathbb E Z_iZ_j=-1/3$ for every cooccurrence
edge. On any $r$-clique of the variable cooccurrence graph the moment
matrix is $\frac43I_r-\frac13J_r$, with eigenvalue $(4-r)/3$.
For $r\ge5$ it violates positive semidefiniteness, equivalently
\[
\mathbb E\left[\left(\sum_{i\in Q}Z_i\right)^2\right]
=\frac{r(4-r)}3<0.
\]
This is a strict \emph{restricted model} obstruction, not a general
SoS feasibility result, an UNSAT certificate, or an all-algorithm
decision-time lower bound. In fact the canonical valid $\mathcal C_\star$
SAT fixture $Y$ (twenty models) contains a five-clique
$\{1,2,3,5,6\}$, with proposed quadratic value $-5/3$.
A second pair-free in-target SAT source $Z$ with positive supports
$123,124,356,456$ and negative supports $125,126,345,346$
has a six-clique, twenty-two satisfying assignments and witness
$(0,0,1,1,0,0)$, with proposed moment $-4$.
An additional elementary theorem proves \emph{all} valid six-name
$\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}$ instances SAT: there are eight clauses
with average random violated count one, but the all-zero assignment
violates four positive clauses, ruling out UNSAT.
Independent complete six-name audit enumerated $75^2=5625$
signed regular sources, $5550$ in $\mathcal C_\star$,
all SAT; clique histogram $\omega=3:915$, $\omega=5:3420$,
$\omega=6:1290$, zero failures. Among nonpaired sources,
$4710$ trigger the fixed-law PSD obstruction.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED} is any inference that mutual clause-marginal
consistency automatically supplies this global PSD law or that its
failure requires expensive unrestricted SAT decision.
Neither the fixed-law inconsistency nor its cheap five-clique detection
is a complete-state noncircular post-acquisition potential.

\paragraph{M22-NAE-PAIR149: full-partner-compatible local relaxation and sharp correlation firewall (24 October 2026, 18:58 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted local-marginal theorems, independent review
pending; COMPUTATIONAL finite audit, no all-decider lower bound.}
For every proper clausewise-monotone three-literal OR source, including
signed exact-$(2,2)$ sources with \emph{partial or full} complementary-clause
pairs, assign to each clause the uniform law on its six nonconstant
three-bit assignments. Every row satisfies either pure-sign clause, and
the named marginal laws agree on \emph{every} intersection of clause
supports, including identical supports across opposite signs. In
contrast, prior PARITY-LOCAL142's four-row pairwise-independent parity
distributions were exact only for \emph{pair-free} sources.
For any unbiased distribution on the six NAE rows of one complemented
support,
\[
 \sum_{i<j}\operatorname{Corr}(X_i,X_j)=-1;
 \qquad \max_{i<j}|\operatorname{Corr}(X_i,X_j)|\ge1/3.
\]
The common six-row law attains correlation $-1/3$ on each pair,
so unbiased two-wise independence is impossible in this extended scope.
It remains valid only in the prior pair-free scope. This is a
\emph{local CSP-marginal consistency} theorem, not global SAT feasibility,
a high-level Sherali--Adams or PSD construction, or a forced amount
of computation. A post-acquisition potential initialized solely from
local-LP infeasibility is zero for all canonical target inputs and cannot
give the needed worst-case lower bound. Exhaustive six-name control:

complement pairs, $157500$ clause-pair marginal comparisons
(including $4500$ complete-support overlaps) with zero failures.

$(k,m,q)=(198,264,132)$ and $34716$ audited clause-support overlaps;

\paragraph{Current LSPV-1 archive and spine reconciliation, M22-CLONE-SHARPNESS148 (24 October 2026, 17:58 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent review pending; COMPUTATIONAL finite controls only.}
Role~5's M22-CLONE-EQUALITY-FLOOR147 proves that a proper ternary
clone-only CNF defining exactly $\{0^r,1^r\}$ needs at least $2r$
clauses. The new explicit sharp construction, for $r\ge3$, is
\[
 G_r=\bigwedge_{i=0}^{r-1}
  [(\neg z_i\vee z_{i+1}\vee z_{i+2})\wedge
   (\neg z_i\vee z_{i+1}\vee\neg z_{i+2})],
\]
with indices modulo $r$. The paired clauses reduce to
$(\neg z_i\vee z_{i+1})$, so $G_r$ is the directed equality
implication cycle, has exactly $2r$ unique proper clauses,
models precisely $0^r,1^r$, and gives each clone $(3^+,3^-)$
occurrences. The peer $2r$ floor is therefore \emph{sharp in
mixed-sign proper CNF}. Separately, \emph{no} clone-only
clausewise-monotone CNF can admit both constants and enforce
their equality: any positive clause fails all-zero, any
negative clause fails all-one, and the empty conjunction is
not equality for $r\ge2$. Thus a standalone monotone equality
normalizer needs auxiliaries or explicit contextual semantics.
The sharp gadget is \textbf{OUTSIDE} canonical $L_\star$ and does
\emph{not} fill LOCAL-COOK-TAGGED-SIGNED22-1.
Exhaustive verification for $r=3,\ldots,12$: 8,184 total
assignments, 4,290 mixed proper ternary boundary clauses,
1,430 pure-sign control clauses, zero discrepancies; $r=20,50,100$
only structural checks. This is \textbf{not} measured FLMV
machine work, nor two legal SAT-preserving recursive levels.
Prior peer-compatible results: Role~2 PIN-ERASURE147 excludes
ordinary type-hereditary connected-UNSAT pinning; Role~3
MAXDEFECT-FACTOR101 exhibits high-frontier easy target SAT;
Role~4 DP-TWOLEVEL103 projects exact SAT and UNSAT answers
through two ordinary mixed-width CNF stages, but not target
children and not compulsory enumeration; Role~5's proper
clone-only floor is now quantitatively tight. The Role~1
LAZY-ACCESS-BRIDGE147 simulation bounds remain conditional.
Canonical target $\mathcal C_\star$ excludes \emph{only fully}
complement-paired clausewise-monotone exact-$(2,2)$ sources,
allows partial pairs and easy inputs, and has
$N=\Theta(k\log(k+2))$ in dense indexed encoding.
\textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 remains OPEN}: all-input uniform joint
resource pairs of one FLMV-matched ordinary SAT machine must
reach the strict box $t<241/200$, $s<11642479/23232400$
through a proved finite charged route; the illustrative

\paragraph{M22-PARITY-LOCAL142 (24 October 2026): exact two-local pseudomarginal ceiling with matched target SAT/UNSAT controls.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in a restricted source model, independent review pending; COMPUTATIONAL finite original-source checks; ARC-EDGE-90 and DIRECT-POST OPEN.}
For every proper three-variable OR clause $C$ on distinct unsigned source
names, let $z_C$ be its unique falsifying three-bit row.  The uniform
distribution $\mu_C$ on the four rows of XOR parity
$1-\bigoplus_i(z_C)_i$ is supported on assignments satisfying $C$.
Every one- or two-name projection is perfectly uniform.  Therefore,
if distinct clauses have distinct \emph{unsigned} triple supports,
all pairwise satisfying clause marginals agree on every shared name.
This applies to the \emph{pair-free subfamily} of the canonical target
$\mathcal C_\star$, not to every partially paired target input.
Even a globally UNSAT formula admits these pairwise-compatible local
satisfying marginals.  They do not imply one globally consistent model.

The original D\"ocker 198-variable 264-clause pair-free monotone UNSAT
source $U$ and a valid same-size 198-variable monotone SAT mutant $V$
(two negative source identifiers swapped, a 198-bit witness independently
replayed) both have $q=132$ and exactly the same unsigned clause-pair
intersection histogram $(33579,1086,51,0)$ over $34716$ pairs.  Both
have zero local-marginal conflicts.  The globally strengthened system
of opposite-parity XOR equations is \emph{inconsistent for both},
with GF(2) coefficient rank 198.  Thus directly imposing the parity
clauses is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED as an exact SAT normalizer}: it
rejects the SAT source $V$.  The UNSAT label for $U$ relies on the
published D\"ocker theorem and Role 2's prior certificate replay,
not a fresh full UNSAT proof in this run.  The six-variable target SAT
anchor $Y$ has 20 models, while the fully paired six-variable SAT
control lies outside $\mathcal C_\star$ and has 18 models.

For every full $\mathcal C_{\rm M22}$ source there are two exact original
source-name-labeled loopless cubic multigraphs $G_+,G_-$ whose edge labels
are the $k$ variables: the TRUE edge set must cover all positive-clause
vertices and its complement must cover all negative-clause vertices.
This is source-invertible, \emph{not} a shrinking ordinary SAT child.
The fractional inequalities have the uniform strictly feasible point
$z_x=1/2$ even on the UNSAT control, ruling out fractional feasibility
or two-local consistency alone as a decision-hardness obstruction.

\paragraph{M22-PIN-DIVISIBILITY141 (24 October 2026): survivor-retaining normalizers cannot create power-smaller explicit ordinary children.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted syntax and source-injection theorem, independent review pending; COMPUTATIONAL finite controls, no unrestricted SAT lower bound.}
Preserve the canonical clausewise-monotone ordinary \(3\)-CNF
\(\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}\) with unique proper clauses and exactly
two occurrences per variable per polarity;
\(\mathcal C_\star=\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}\setminus\mathcal P_{\rm pair}\)
excludes \emph{only fully} complementary-clause-paired sources, not
partially paired inputs. Invalid encodings are rejected.
With \(k\) active original variables, \(m=4k/3\),
\(N=\Theta(k\log(k+2))\), any nonempty \(\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}\)
has \(k\in3\mathbb Z_{\ge2}\).
After pinning \(d\) distinct original names, an explicit ordinary
\(\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}\) child \(G\) that retains \emph{every}
unpinned original name injectively as a distinct active name must satisfy
\[
 k(G)\ge\max\{6,3\lceil(k-d)/3\rceil\}
       =\max\{6,k-3\lfloor d/3\rfloor\},\qquad
 m(G)=4k(G)/3.
\]
For \(d=1,2\), the child has at least the parent's \(k\) names and
\(m\) clauses; for \(d=o(k)\), its explicit dense indexed bit length is
\(\Omega(N)\), not \(N^{\lambda+o(1)}\) with fixed \(\lambda<1\).
Two \emph{actual sequential} one-pin, survivor-retaining
normalizations likewise obey \(k_2\ge k_1\ge k_0\).
This is \emph{only} a grammar/injection floor; it neither enforces
conditional SAT/UNSAT branch semantics nor forbids semantic elimination,
alternative representations, or charged virtual access.
Role~5's earlier one-alias repair additionally loses pin semantics,
returning an alpha-renamed full parent. A raw Shannon pin exits the M22
grammar. Role~2's \(q=2\) four-color NAE witness and Role~3's
perfect-matching family remain positive solver controls.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 48 integer floor pairs, verified six-variable
\(Y\) with 20 models and connected \(Z_h\) controls, 12 single-pin
and 120 ordered two-pin raw descendants; 358 assertion-accounted
checks, zero discrepancies. No FLMV bit-work/peak-space, flip or
cumulative-bit-time measurement.
The exhaustive budget
\(\Psi=2^d2^{k-d}=2^k\), \(R_{\rm search}=0\), is optional and
does not force arbitrary decider work.
\textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 OPEN} for an all-input uniform jointly achieved
ordinary-SAT favorable route into
\(t<241/200,\ s<11642479/23232400\), with the historical
universal cosine landing \textbf{RETIRED}.
\textbf{DIRECT-POST OPEN} for an independent superpolynomial
complete-state potential with bounded decrease for every adaptive step.
\textbf{P versus NP remains OPEN}.

\paragraph{M22-CONNECTED-CYCLIC-LIFT139 (25 October 2026): connected high-defect easy-island firewall.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT on an explicitly indexed restricted formula family; independent review pending. COMPUTATIONAL finite controls; no unrestricted lower bound.}
Preserve canonical \(\mathcal C_{\rm M22}\), the proper unique clausewise-monotone
2-positive/2-negative ordinary 3-CNF language, and
\(\mathcal C_\star=\mathcal C_{\rm M22}\setminus\mathcal P_{\rm pair}\):
only \emph{fully} complement-paired instances are excluded.
For each \(h\ge1\) form \(Z_h\) from \(h\) copies of fixed six-variable
fixture \(Y\) (positive \(123,145,246,356\), negative \(124,136,235,456\))
by moving the first positive \(a_i b_i c_i\) clause's \(a_i\) occurrence
to \(a_{i+1}\) cyclically. The other seven clauses per cell stay unchanged.
A full source-quota, uniqueness, no-complement and incidence proof gives
a \emph{connected}, completely unpaired \(\mathcal C_\star\) source with
\[
k=6h,\qquad m=8h,\qquad
q=|P\setminus Q|=4h=\tfrac23k,\qquad
N=\Theta(k\log(k+2)).
\]
The explicit witness \(a_i=b_i=c_i=1,\ d_i=e_i=f_i=0\) satisfies it;
canonical source recognition and witness checking are
polynomial bit work with logarithmic index space.
A paid scan-first recognize-then-fallback \emph{uniform exact}
\(L_\star\) algorithm runs in polynomial work on every \(Z_h\).
Thus a terminal-zero \(\Gamma(N)=N^{O(1)}\)-bounded-decrease
complete-state potential \(\Phi\) cannot be initialized at
\(\Phi(C_{\rm scan}(F))\ge2^{\Omega(q(F))}\) on \emph{all}
connected \(F\in\mathcal C_\star\): the telescoping inequality would
contradict this execution. This falsifies only the pointwise defect
and connectivity potential, \emph{not} a worst-case
\(\forall A\,\forall p\,\exists F\) lower bound.
The original fixed D\"ocker UNSAT source \(U\) disjoint union \(Z_h\)
gives a type-correct opposite-label (disconnected) control, relying
on the published UNSAT theorem rather than a fresh brute-force check.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} exact model counts \(20,356,6680\)
for \(h=1,2,3\); validated \(h=1,2,3,4,8,16\) and paired
six-variable control (18 models); no discrepancies.
Bit flips, cumulative bit-time and physical FLMV-machine
time-space were not measured; all source/access/lookup/fallback
charges remain required. Role 2's one-polarity factor \(3^q\) upper
bound, Role 4's deletion closure and Role 5's alpha-renaming collapse
are compatible. Prior \(Y^{\dot\cup h}\) easy sources were disconnected;
the new delta is connectedness. Both
\textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 and DIRECT-POST remain OPEN};
\textbf{P versus NP remains OPEN}.

\paragraph{M22-TOTAL-ANCHOR138 (24 October 2026): exact target-scoped, invalid-input-reflecting bit-local anchor.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT for one declared dense indexed clause-list grammar; independent review pending.}
The canonical $\mathcal C_{\rm M22}$ consists of distinct proper clausewise-monotone
3-CNFs with exactly two positive and two negative occurrences of each active
source variable. Let $\bar C$ flip signs \emph{inside OR clauses} and let
$\mathcal C_\star=\mathcal C_{\rm M22}\setminus
\{F:\forall C\in F,\bar C\in F\}$.
Only fully complementary-clause-paired formulas are excluded; partial pairs,
disconnected and easy formulas remain valid. The target language accepts
satisfiable members and rejects malformed encodings. For valid sources,
$3m=4k$, $m_+=m_-=2k/3$ and $N=\Theta(k\log(k+2))$.
D\"ocker's $\mathcal C_{\rm M22}$ NP-completeness and fully paired SAT
result establish target NP-completeness but no time lower bound.
The previously validated six-variable fixture $Y$, with positive supports
$123,145,246,356$ and negative supports $124,136,235,456$, has
20 models and no complementary partner.
\texttt{M22-TOTAL-ANCHOR138} strengthens Role~4's previously
\emph{valid-source} no-test anchor with a \emph{total} reduction for a
declared dense encoding: a paid header-only framing gate checks
$k,m,w,N$, copies all original signed literal fields, maps any
old out-of-range variable ID to illegal target ID zero, and appends
a fresh, renamed copy of $Y$.
This preserves and reflects every original grammar violation
without a quadratic full-input validity scan:
\[
\operatorname{Valid}_{\rm M22}(x)\iff
\operatorname{Valid}_{\rm M22}(A_{\rm total}(x)),\qquad
\#\operatorname{SAT}(A_{\rm total}(x))=20\#\operatorname{SAT}(x)
\quad\text{on valid }x.
\]
All target bit accesses require $\operatorname{polylog}N$ paid
elementary bit work and $O(\log N)$ extra live writable/index bits.
Thus a single uniform exact $\mathcal C_\star$ decider with joint
$(t,s)$ exponent upper bounds transports to $\mathcal C_{\rm M22}$
with the \emph{same} upper exponents for $t\ge1,s\ge0$,
in this declared machine/encoding, not to ordinary unrestricted SAT.
The output is \emph{longer}, and illegal pinned children are not
repaired by extra $Y$ copies. This supplies no ARC favorable successor.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 5625 exhaustively enumerated six-variable balanced
sources, 510 compact malformed mutations, 1402 expanded malformed mutations,
all grammar-reflection assertions passed; the expanded accessor counted
1,680,622 returned bits and 2,063,880 abstract source-bit reads.
The separate standard-library scripts and machine-readable results are
not physical FLMV time/space or flip measurements.
The easy family $Y^{\dot\cup p}$ has unmatched-support defect
$q=4p=\Theta(k)$ yet admits paid polynomial template recognition;
$q$ alone does not force a per-instance every-algorithm
post-acquisition lower bound. A stronger worst-case quantified
potential remains \textbf{OPEN}. PHASE-SANDWICH136's paid
clean scan-first repair remains applicable. Both complementary
proof routes are explicitly retained: \textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 OPEN}
for an all-input ordinary-SAT joint resource transition, and
\textbf{DIRECT-POST OPEN} for a noncircular large
bounded-decrease complete-state potential. The strict FLMV
box is $t<241/200$, $s<11642479/23232400$;
the historical cosine landing is \textbf{RETIRED}.
\textbf{P versus NP remains OPEN}.
