\paragraph{PHASE-SANDWICH136 (25 October 2026): paid scan-first and deferred-last-bit normal forms.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT for the declared indexed-bit-machine simulation;
independent review pending. POST-POTENTIAL-136 and ARC-EDGE-90 OPEN.}
For \emph{any} fixed total uniform polynomial-time exact decider $A$
with joint time $T_A(N)$ and writable/index space $S_A(N)$, one uniform
scan-first decider $A_{\rm early}$ reads every physical source bit before
simulating $A$. Another uniform decider $A_{\rm late}$ reads every bit
\emph{except} the first, simulates $A$ on both fully charged virtual
completions $x^{(0)}$ and $x^{(1)}$, then reads the first physical bit
last and selects the already-computed correct answer. Both machines
are all-input exact and have simultaneous bounds
\[
T_{\rm early},T_{\rm late}
=O((N+T_A)\operatorname{polylog}N),\qquad
S_{\rm early},S_{\rm late}=O(S_A+\log N).
\]
For $t\ge1$, $s\ge0$, neither changes the achievable exponent
\emph{upper bounds} $(t,s)$ or gives an ARC contraction. In the late
machine only $O(\log N)$ charged bit work follows the first state
$C_{\rm full}$ at which every physical input bit has been acquired.
By the exact telescoping lemma, any terminal-zero complete-state
potential with local drop $\Phi(C)-\Phi(C')\le L(N)u(C,C')$
satisfies $\Phi(C_{\rm full}^{\rm late})\le O(L(N)\log N)$.
Thus a claimed \emph{every-decider} large structural potential
initialized solely at the final input acquisition is
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED} when it exceeds that budget. This
does \emph{not} refute a potential initiated at a \emph{paid clean
scan-first state}: the early machine is the correct normalization
repair, but an independently large initial value and universally
bounded decrease remain \textbf{OPEN: POST-POTENTIAL-136}.
Input-observation hitting $\tau\le N$ is not post-read computation.
A compact fixed proper-3CNF verifier checked 606 sources
(478 SAT, 128 UNSAT, 257 pivotal two-completion pairs), zero
failures; the separate expanded interpreter tested 619
bounded CNFs with affine/Horn/2-SAT and both-label controls.
These are \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL} checks, not physical FLMV traces.
Both constructions charge source/work reads, all writes including
unchanged writes, address/control/selection, virtual input access,
saved answer bits, up to two simulated workspaces, peak live bits
and cumulative bit-time. The all-input favorable ARC-EDGE-90
and P versus NP remain \OPEN.

handoff:

\paragraph{TREE-FACTOR-JOIN135 (25 October 2026): charged tree joins of signed interval-factor cores.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent review pending; no all-input contraction.}
For a supplied, source-validated tree of signed interval-cardinality clause
blocks with the running-intersection property, let $B_v$ be original source
names shared outside block $v$, $k_v$ its number of internal variables
occurring more than twice, and
$W=\max_v|B_v|$, $K=\max_vk_v$.
Pin all $B_v$ names, branch on $k_v$ internal names and solve each
residual degree-two source using the previous $(g,f)$-factor theorem.
Exact source-named tree messages yield simultaneous bit-machine
\emph{upper bounds}
\[
T\le \operatorname{poly}(N)\bigl(1+\sum_v2^{|B_v|+k_v}\bigr),
\qquad
S\le\operatorname{poly}(N)+O\bigl(\sum_{v\ne r}2^{|S_{v,p(v)}|}\bigr).
\]
This is an established CSP tree-message principle combined with
INTERVAL-FACTOR134; it applies to a certified restricted language only.
The certificate validation and all pin/factor/message costs are charged;
discovering a suitable decomposition on arbitrary SAT remains \textbf{OPEN}.
A three-block odd XOR inequality cycle is globally UNSAT despite each
block's SAT label being YES, and its proposed path fails running intersection.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 74 certified source cases (58 SAT, 16 UNSAT),
zero decision discrepancies, one invalid certificate rejected; these are
finite semantics checks, not FLMV bit-operation measurements.
LSPV-2 IDENTITY-SWEEP129, LSPV-3 TYPED-DEGREE-BRIDGE98,
LSPV-4 NAME-ONLY-MULTIPASS93 and LSPV-5
NAMED-FRONTIER-COMPOSITION132 retain their restricted model contracts.
Prior FACTOR-JOIN56 and TREE-INTERFACE-SWITCH79 already established

\paragraph{INTERVAL-FACTOR134 (25 October 2026): signed degree-two interval-cardinality sources have a polynomial graph-factor algorithm.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT as a restricted source-to-(g,f)-factor equivalence; independent review pending.}
Extend SIGNED-DEGREE2-MATCH133 from exact-one demands to arbitrary
per-clause intervals $L_j\le\#\{\mathrm{true\ literal\ occurrences}\}\le U_j$.
Under the \emph{source} occurrence-degree-at-most-two promise,
make each clause a graph vertex of degree interval $[L_j,U_j]$;
a singleton variable becomes a clause--private-leaf edge;
two same-polarity occurrences become one clause--clause edge;
two opposite-polarity occurrences use a choice vertex of mandatory
degree one with alternative edges to the two clauses.
Every feasible factor induces one source-consistent Boolean assignment,
and conversely. Classical polynomial $(g,f)$-factor feasibility thus gives
a deterministic polynomial source-language decision procedure.
With independently defined $k(H)=|\{v:\deg_H(v)\ge3\}|$, branching first on
those source variables yields $2^{k(H)}\operatorname{poly}(|H|)$ time and
polynomial space, including all branches and reconstruction costs.
When every source variable occurs exactly twice and all demands are exact,
SAT requires $\sum_j b_j\equiv h\pmod 2$, where $h$ counts opposite-sign
variables. In the connected $K_4$ four-clause source, all-positive exact-one
and exact-two demands are SAT; flipping one literal-sign occurrence makes
both UNSAT with the same unsigned incidence.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} $3395$ sources ($2388$ SAT, $1007$ UNSAT),
$22196$ assertion-accounted independent source/factor checks and zero failures;
these are not physical FLMV-machine bit-work or workspace measurements.
Role~2 OVERLAP-CUT128, Role~3 SCOPE-ALIAS-COMPLETENESS96, Role~4
PARITY-MARGINAL-COUNT92 and Role~5 STRICT-SUBJOIN-STATUS131 remain exact
restricted interface and compatibility controls, not all-input improvements.
This graph-factor shortcut defeats raw occurrence/cycle/sign multiplicity as
a universal post-acquisition hardness proxy; on arbitrary ordinary SAT,
$k(H)$ may be linear and exact fallback preserves worst-case exponents.
\textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain OPEN.}
Report, two reproducible Python auditors, raw aggregate results and additive
five-peer handoff:

and neighboring \texttt{2026-10-25\_0058\_INTERVAL\_FACTOR134} files.



\paragraph{SIGNED-DEGREE2-MATCH133 (25 October 2026): signed occurrence-two exact-one SAT reduces to weighted matching.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT within a restricted signed exact-one source grammar, independent review pending.}
For signed exact-one clauses on pairwise distinct named source variables, each
variable with occurrence at most two has one of three representations in a
polynomial weighted-matching instance: a degree-one literal makes a
clause--leaf edge; two same-polarity occurrences make one clause--clause
edge; two opposite-polarity occurrences make a \emph{mandatory choice node}
with alternative edges to both clause nodes.  All clause nodes are mandatory.
Weight each graph edge by the number of mandatory endpoints and find a
maximum-weight matching.  All mandatory nodes are covered if and only if
the original signed exact-one instance is SAT; a matching reconstructs
one globally coherent assignment.  This extends
\texttt{DEGREE-MATCH-LADDER132}'s positive-only theorem and differs from
Role~2's ordinary-OR-clause \texttt{BLOSSOM-FACTOR60}.
With source-defined $k=|\{x:\deg_H(x)\ge3\}|$,
conditioning all $k$ source variables and matching each residual yields
$2^k\operatorname{poly}(|H|)$ time and polynomial writable space.  Moore--Robson's
planar cubic positive one-in-three SAT is NP-complete at source degree~3.
This is a sharp \emph{source-language tractability boundary}, neither an
unrestricted deterministic SAT time lower bound nor an ARC successor.
A connected, equal-unsigned-support four-clause, six-variable $K_4$
control has opposite SAT/UNSAT labels under two signed orientations,
both correctly handled by matching.  \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:}
4096 exhaustive source signings, 1200 randomized degree-two sources and
240 degree-three controls, all cross-checked by complete Boolean enumeration:
5536 sources, 2992 SAT, 2544 UNSAT, 11078 assertion-accounted checks
with zero failures.  Python matching counts are not physical FLMV-machine
bit work, complete-state flips or peak writable space.
Role~3's \texttt{EXACTONE-JOIN-CYCLE95}, Role~4's
\texttt{SCOPE-FRESHNESS-VALIDATION91}, and Role~5's
\texttt{THREE-STATE-JOIN-FIREWALL130} remain complementary
source-semantics checks, not all-machine resource transfers.
A grammar recognizer on ordinary inputs must retain an exact
paid fallback, which can preserve the original exponent.
\textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain OPEN.}
Standalone report, executable, raw aggregate and additive archive handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-25_0026_LSPV-1_SIGNED_DEGREE2_MATCH133.tex}
and files with matching timestamp and run identifier in that directory.



\paragraph{NONAFFINE-WINDOW131 (24 October 2026): nonlinear overlapping interfaces admit charged four-state joining.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT for a restricted canonical proper-3CNF grammar; independent review pending.}
For each ordered triple-window constraint on original source variables
$(x_i,x_{i+1},x_{i+2})$, extract its actual signed forbidden-three-bit
mask by charged source reads.  With pins $x_0=x_1=1$ and
$x_{r+1}=b$, the four-state recurrence
\[
 D_0=\{(1,1)\},\qquad
 D_{i+1}=\{(v,w):\exists u,\ (u,v)\in D_i,\ (u,v,w)\notin Q_i\}
\]
is an exact, source-name-consistent prefix projection.  Validation plus
decision on this restricted ordered grammar uses
$O(N\operatorname{polylog}N)$ bit work and $O(\log N)$ writable bits.
Its explicit nonlinear AND recurrence $x_{i+2}=x_i\wedge x_{i+1}$
gives matched connected proper-3CNF SAT/UNSAT instances with
$n=r+8$, $m=4r+12$, primal cycle rank $r+3$,
and every UNSAT subformula meeting all $r$ gate blocks.
The fixed solver recognizes both labels and bypasses off-tree
$2^{\Theta(r)}$ enumeration, without a label oracle.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 584 finite inputs, 526 SAT and 58 UNSAT,
1397 assertion-accounted groups with zero failures.
Large cycle rank, local nonaffinity, and growing UNSAT core span
are \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED as standalone universal SAT runtime
obstructions}.  This does not supersede the CYCLE-CUTSET-AFFINE130
restricted upper bound or establish an all-input contractor.
Recognition failure needs exact fallback, so \textbf{ARC-EDGE-90
and P versus NP remain OPEN}.  Full report, reproducible executable,
raw aggregate and source/gap handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-24_2300_LSPV-1_NONAFFINE_WINDOW131.tex}
and adjoining \texttt{NONAFFINE\_WINDOW131} files.


\paragraph{ROOT-HARD-PROJECTION129 (24 October 2026): a source-hard root with exponentially many correlated pinned answers.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted construction; independent review pending.}
For every ordinary proper ternary source CNF $F$, integers $d\ge1$ and
$1\le h\le2^d$, a uniform connected proper-three-CNF $H_{F,d,h}$
retains all original source literals, enforces a functional comparator
$C_{d,h}(b)=[\operatorname{unsigned}(b)<h]$ through exact Tseitin
constraints, and uses fresh-output three-clause-width XOR connectors
to join every source and boundary variable without restricting them.
Exact existential elimination yields
\[
 \boxed{\operatorname{SAT}(H_{F,d,h}[b])
   = C_{d,h}(b)\wedge\operatorname{SAT}(F),\qquad
   \operatorname{SAT}(H_{F,d,h})=\operatorname{SAT}(F).}
\]
Unlike earlier OR-guard counterexamples, the \emph{unpinned parent
decision} is NP-complete under the polynomial ordinary-input
reduction $F\mapsto H_{F,1,1}$, and there are matched SAT/UNSAT
sources of identical clause shape and encoding length.  Nevertheless,
for fixed $d,h$, all $2^d$ pinned-query outcomes are determined by
\emph{one} paid source SAT bit and a public $O(d)$-gate comparator;
the complete answer-vector quotient has only two classes.
Thus connectedness, root NP-completeness and arbitrarily many
individually source-complete projected memberships do not justify
additive repeated SAT-work charges for a one-bit root decider.
This construction does \emph{not} make general SAT easy,
nor does it provide an all-input simultaneous time-space successor.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} the expanded exact DPLL audit checked
285 sources (274 SAT, 11 UNSAT), 903 compiled roots and
3996 pinned controls, 6705 assertion-accounted checks, zero failures;
the compact independent GitHub reproducer additionally passed
1536 checks on all 256 ternary source-clause subsets.
The typed bit-resource vector records charges, but actual
FLMV-machine bit flips and cumulative bit-time were not measured.
The root-hard variant supersedes any claim that the prior
easy-root guard was the \emph{only} obstruction to an interface-density
lower bound.  Both independent source-answer acquisition and the
all-input ARC-EDGE-90 route remain \textbf{OPEN}.
Report, script, raw summary, and additive five-role handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-24_2105_LSPV-1_ROOT-HARD-PROJECTION129.tex}
and neighboring \texttt{2026-10-24\_2105\_ROOT-HARD-PROJECTION129} files.
\textbf{P versus NP remains OPEN.}


\paragraph{DENSE-BOUNDARY-BYPASS128 (24 October 2026): densely SAT-dependent projected interfaces do not compel root decision work.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted; independent review pending.}
For every ordered proper ternary CNF $F$ and integer $d\ge1$, an explicit
connected proper-3CNF guard-and-OR construction $D_{F,d}$ of ordinary size
$O((m+d)\log(n+m+d))$ preserves source variable identities and has
\[
Q_{F,d}(b)=[b=0^d]\lor\operatorname{SAT}(F)
\quad(b\in\{0,1\}^d).
\]
Thus for UNSAT $F$, $2^d-1$ of $2^d$ projected membership answers require
the same source SAT label; nevertheless every unpinned parent is SAT,
verified immediately by the all-zero witness. The entire projected relation
has an $O(d)$-gate representation given just one shared source decision
bit, so density of hard boundary queries does not force independent
repeated source work. A uniform exact, state-adaptive decider can first
verify the all-zero witness in $O(N\operatorname{polylog}N)$ bit operations
and $O(\log N)$ writable bits on this family, then invoke fully charged
exact fallback elsewhere. This is \emph{not} an unrestricted polynomial
SAT algorithm or a lower bound, and does not provide an achievable-pair
contraction. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}: $317$ independent SAT/UNSAT source
controls, $1{,}268$ constructed parents, $9{,}510$ pinned checks
($8{,}990$ YES, $520$ NO) and $14{,}899$ assertion-accounted checks
with no failures. The source gadget is credited to LSPV-3 GUARDED90,
complementing LSPV-5 GUARD126's single-hard-corner construction.
Root ARC-EDGE-90 remains \textbf{OPEN}; $P$ versus $NP$ remains \textbf{OPEN}.
Full report, compact reproducibility script, numeric results and additive
peer handoff are under
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-24_2024_LSPV-1_DENSE_BOUNDARY_BYPASS128.tex}
and adjacent \texttt{2026-10-24\_2024\_DENSE\_BOUNDARY128} files.

\paragraph{SPARSE-FRONTIER-SCHEDULE127 (24 October 2026): paid trajectory-weighted virtual-frontier accounting.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only under a declared total all-input, source-coherent scoped-stream generation contract; independent peer audit pending.}
For a virtual $\mathsf{OPEN}/\mathsf{CLAUSE}/\mathsf{CLOSE}$ stream of encoded size
$N^{\mu+o(1)}$, per-bit decoding time $N^{a+o(1)}$, source
validation/construction time $N^{r+o(1)}$, peak generator and decoder
live space exponents $\zeta,\sigma$, and maximum active width
$\kappa\log_2 N+o(\log N)$, define the independently structural
trajectory mass
\[
 \Psi(F)=\sum_{i=1}^{L(F)}2^{\max(w_{i-1},w_i)}
   \le N^{\chi+o(1)}.
\]
A uniform exact online frontier DP decodes each event once, then performs
its own row updates.  One jointly resource-bounded FLMV-model exact SAT
decider would therefore have
\[
 \boxed{t'\le\max\{r,\mu+a,\chi\},\qquad
 s'\le\max\{\zeta,\sigma,\kappa\}.}
\]
All source/index work, switching, failed trials, record buffering, ancestor
state, and peak live bits are charged to the one implementation.  This
sharpens the previous coarser maximum-width simulation
$t'\le\max\{r,\mu+\max(a,\kappa)\}$ because high-width steps need not
dominate a long low-width trajectory; it does \emph{not} construct any
all-input virtual normalizer.  In fact a putative generator with
$(\mu,a,\kappa,\chi,r,\sigma,\zeta)
=(1,1/20,43/100,1,1,43/100,43/100)$
would yield $(t',s')=(21/20,43/100)$,
forbidden by FLMV at $c=53/50$ since
$\beta(c)=233623/280900>43/100$.  The old upper envelope
$(143/100,43/100)$ fails that same two-level exclusion.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}: a standard-library adaptive SCC/Horn/affine/frontier
audit tested $225$ sources ($174$ SAT, $51$ UNSAT), $20{,}171$ assertions,
zero failures; these are finite semantic/profile checks and not
physical FLMV bit-time or workspace measurements.  The original
LSPV-1 LOCALPARITY1 and LSPV-4 BLOCK-CLOSED-ORACLE85 show a
paid source-aware parity bypass, so cyclic parity gluing is
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED as a pointwise universal cost obstruction}.
LSPV-2's SCC-one-parity algorithm is a positive restricted shortcut;
LSPV-3's anonymous profile collision and LSPV-5's named-profile

\paragraph{ARC-ENDPOINT-GUARD125 (24 October 2026): strict FLMV two-level endpoint audit.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT algebraic reformulation, independent review pending.}

the two-level forbidden criterion
$\exists c>\max\{1,t\}:\ s<\beta(c)$, where
$\beta(c)=\frac12((c+2)/c^2-c)$, is equivalent for $t\ge1$ to
the strict inequality $s<\beta(t)$ because $\beta$ is continuous and
strictly decreasing.  This restates a \emph{known published} endpoint, not a
new SAT lower bound.  The previously proposed universal landing
$t+s<2\cos(\pi/7)$ is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED} as a substitute:

($\beta(3/2)=1/36$), whereas $(21/20,4/5)$ satisfies FLMV2
($\beta(21/20)=15139/17640$) but fails the cosine test.  Failing the
two-level sufficient certificate does not establish achievability or
escape from higher-level FLMV bounds.  \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:}
1,271 exactly classified rational grid points, including 369 passing
both diagnostics, 189 legacy-only, 20 FLMV2-only, and 693 neither,
plus five exact rational controls; zero failures.  This is an offline

\paragraph{VIRTUAL-FRONTIER-FENCE124 (24 October 2026): lazy virtual-normalization FLMV firewall.}

all scratch initialization and cleanup, costs

Length/preflight costs have exponents $(r,\zeta)$.
An exact DP buffers each event \emph{once} and reuses it across every
live assignment row, so a single uniform all-input SAT machine has
\[
 (t',s')\le
 \left(\max\{r,\mu+\max(a,\kappa)\},
 \max\{\zeta,\sigma,\kappa\}\right)
\]
simultaneously. The per-row multiplicative decoder exponent $a+\kappa$
is \emph{not compulsory}; primitive work is the sum of decoded bits and
table updates with all repeated queries and replay fully charged.
By the verified matched FLMV endpoint, no such \emph{all-input} virtualizer
exists when there is a fixed $c>\max\{1,t'\}$ and

The hypothetical profile

This restricts only the virtual-normalizer service; arbitrary exact
deciders may bypass it, and the all-input ARC successor remains \OPEN{}.

\paragraph{BIT-CHANNEL-TYPE122 (24 October 2026): corrected typed bit-resource ledger.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only in the declared bit-event model; independent review pending.}
The peer \texttt{BIT-LEDGER-CEILING120} displays
$\Delta_{\mathrm{total}}\le W_{\mathrm{work}}\le U$ while
$\Delta_{\mathrm{total}}$ includes mutable source-head, index/address and control
coordinates and $W_{\mathrm{work}}$ counts work-tape writes only.
This first inequality is false as typed.  For coordinate-disjoint complete states
the correct identity and inequality are
\[
 \Delta_{\mathrm{total}}=\Delta_{\mathrm{work}}+
 \Delta_{\mathrm{head}}+\Delta_{\mathrm{addr}}+
 \Delta_{\mathrm{ctrl}}+\Delta_{\mathrm{other}},
 \qquad
 \Delta_{\mathrm{work}}\le W_{\mathrm{work}}\le U.
\]
If each billed primitive event flips at most one complete-state bit, then
$\Delta_{\mathrm{total}}\le U$; otherwise sum explicit per-event bit-change caps,
especially for FLMV index-tape erase operations.  A proper-3CNF parity promise
with $n=3q+5$ variables and $m=4q+8$ clauses is SAT iff its $q$ orientation
bits have even parity.  A finite-control read-only promise scanner uses zero
work-tape writes but has $\Delta_{\mathrm{total}}\ge q$ from source-head movement
on both SAT and UNSAT members.  An independent DPLL audit covered all
$510$ orientation instances through $q=8$, with $3{,}594$ assertions and no
failures.  This repairs only the untyped inequality: Role-5's separate global
Hamming-Lipschitz range ceiling and log-word RAM write-shadow theorem remain
scoped valid, and Role-1's FLIPGAUGE121 autonomous nonstuttering theorem is
unaffected.  It is not an all-input SAT solver, a new FLMV endpoint, or an ARC
successor.  Standalone report, executable and JSON:

Recovering the original 4 October \texttt{MULTICHANNEL1}, each complete

 C=\max\{r,\max_j\eta_j/(1-\theta_j)\},\qquad

\paragraph{ARC-STREAM-FENCE117 (24 October 2026): all-input streaming-normalizer FLMV firewall and paid pure-literal escape.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT within a declared typed output contract, NOT an ARC successor; independent review pending.}
Suppose one uniform, total, same-machine producer transforms every ordinary
encoded SAT input of $N$ bits into a valid equisatisfiable one-way
active-slot source-scoped 3CNF stream, with at most $N^{\mu+o(1)}$ emitted
bits, $\kappa\log_2 N+o(\log N)$ simultaneously live source variables,
producer time $N^{r+o(1)}$ and counted work/index space $N^{\sigma+o(1)}$.
A source-identity-preserving exact existential frontier DP consumes its
output \emph{online}, without storing the free write-only output as
a readable buffer.  The resulting \emph{one fixed} uniform, all-input
SAT decider has simultaneous exponent pair
\[
 (t',s')=
 \bigl(\max\{r,\mu+\kappa\},\max\{\sigma,\kappa\}\bigr).
\]
Therefore, by FLMV Theorem~3.1 and Corollary~5.8, no such producer
can exist if some constant $c>\max\{1,r,\mu+\kappa\}$ satisfies
\[
 \max\{\sigma,\kappa\}<
 \beta(c)=\tfrac12\bigl((c+2)/c^2-c\bigr).
\]
For example $(\mu,\kappa,r,\sigma)=(1,0,11/10,1/2)$ is impossible in
this contract; the curved forbidden region also excludes
$(11/10,3/20,11/10,3/20)$ at $c=13/10$.
This is an unconditional \emph{typed normalizer impossibility}, not
a theorem that all SAT deciders build such streams, nor a universal
algorithm time lower bound.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT family-relative counterexample.}
Role~2's proper-3CNF complete-edge family has raw unchanged-clause
frontier $\Omega(h)$ for every clause order, yet each edge clause
$(x_i\vee x_j\vee z_{ij})$ has a private pure-positive
$z_{ij}$.  Paid pure-literal elimination, a generic state-adaptive rule,
removes these clauses and the pure bridge/filler variable, leaving
exactly seven SAT or eight UNSAT core clauses, with residual width
at most three.  This preserves Role~2's ordering theorem but
\emph{falsifies} mandatory decision cost inferred from its raw width.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.} The expanded deterministic Python audit
used seed 202610240600, checked 254 source controls (186 SAT, 68 UNSAT),
18,519 assertions with zero failures, and a portfolio of paid
pure-literal elimination, simple witness recognition, complementary-pair
2SAT-SCC, and exact frontier DP.  Finite checks do not prove the
all-input normalization or exclusion theorem.

\paragraph{ARCARCHIVECURVE90 (24 October 2026): corrected FLMV curved endpoint, cofinal finite-hit archive recovery, and universal-witness firewall.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT as a meta-level implication, not as a SAT closure theorem.}
Let $\mathcal E$ contain the pairs $(t,s)$ simultaneously achieved by
one uniform total all-input exact SAT decider in the
matched FLMV random-access Turing-machine model.
FLMV Theorem~3.1 and Corollary~5.8 forbid the entire downward-closed region
\[
\mathcal B_{\rm FLMV}
=\left\{(t,s):\exists c>\max\{1,t\},\
s<\tfrac12\bigl((c+2)/c^2-c\bigr)\right\}.
\]
At $c=241/200$ the endpoint is

box $t<241/200$, $s\le1/2$. The \emph{curved} region is strictly

and $(13/10,3/10)$ using $c=131/100$.
The old unrestricted product interpretation of $2\cos(\pi/7)$
is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}; only its abstract finite-hit argument
is retained. Recovering the 8 October FINAL-LINK-ARC and 27 September
ARC ancestor establishes that an unbounded cofinal integer set of
diagonal $(K,K)$ starting points, each with a \emph{finite} route of
same-realization achievable pairs into $\mathcal B_{\rm FLMV}$,
would contradict $P=NP$. Exponent detection, a universal compiler,
and invariant code identity are \emph{not logically compulsory} for this
existential implication. Every actual all-input route edge with
fully charged simultaneous resources remains \OPEN{}.

The recovered corrected 14 October UWI-1 PDF gives the exact
polynomial-witness-budget equivalence to $P=NP$ and constructive

DNNF/search-trace hardness. In particular $H(F)\le2^{O(N)}$
on SAT inputs; a purported $2^{\Omega(N\log N)}$
UWI lower bound is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}.
ARC-FRONTIER21/HEREDITARY-SCOPED10 yield only
\textbf{PARTIAL/RESTRICTED} certified-width contractors; they do
not cover arbitrary ordinary SAT. The current roles 2--5 corroborate

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} exact-rational regression checked

Neither finite test is an unrestricted SAT time bound.

\paragraph{HALFRANDOM89 (24 October 2026): stronger source-computable backdoor upper bound and connected all-decider firewall (PROVED/EXACT in declared models; independent review pending).}
On proper duplicate-free signed exact-$(2,2)$ 3-CNF, perform the prior
unique complementary-pair contractions to $F\equiv B\wedge T$.
Let $\delta=m-2p$ be the number of unmatched ternaries and $r_2$
the number of residual source variables occurring in precisely two
ternaries (all others occur in four). A paid deterministic
conditional-expectation rule selecting variables with potential
$\#\mathrm{selected}+\mathbb E[\#\mathrm{unhit\ clauses}]$ constructs
a strong 2-CNF backdoor $X$ in $O(n+m)$ word time after source validation:
\[
\boxed{|X|\le\left\lfloor\delta/2+r_2/4\right\rfloor.}
\]
Indeed $2r_2+4r_4=3\delta$ and the expectation under independent
half-selection is $(r_2+r_4)/2+\delta/8=\delta/2+r_2/4$.
A constant-incidence local potential rule derandomizes the bound.
The solver may compare this cover with the earlier
TUTTECOVER79 matching cover and use the smaller before exact
binary-SCC continuation; all recognition, discovery, optional
matching, switching, child attempts and source replay are charged.
The certified upper bound is
\[
O\!\left(m\log(m+2)+\delta^4+
2^{h_*}(n+m+1)\right),\quad
h_*=\min\{\lfloor3\delta/5\rfloor,\lfloor\delta/2+r_2/4\rfloor\},
\]
with $O(n+m)$ reusable peak work words. In the nonpaired case
$p=0$, this improves the prior exponent $3m/5$ to $m/2$.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 9,410 checks with zero failures on a
standard-library audit, including connected both-direction source
controls. On the actual $198$-variable, $264$-clause
D\"ocker signed exact-$(2,2)$ source, an independent source scan
found no pairs and produced a 96-variable covering set
(84 local selections plus 12 uncovered-clause repairs), compared
with new generic cap 132 and prior cap 158.
The original UNSAT certificate is an imported
DOECKER-CORE-CERT61 dependency; the two-sign SAT repair and its
published witness were independently clause-replayed.
\par\noindent
\textbf{All-decider firewall, PROVED/EXACT restricted.}
The connected octahedral-chain family $G_t$ has $n=6t,m=8t$,
no complementary pair and exact \emph{minimum} ternary-hitting
set size $2t$, yet a canonical exact recognizer gives a verified
SAT witness in $O(t)$ word time; a general bounded-width
DP shortcut also exists. Thus large minimum backdoor size is
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED} as a pointwise universal decision-time
lower-bound parameter. It does not rule out distinct algorithm-dependent
hard inputs elsewhere. The only new unrestricted proof target is a
noncircular superpolynomial \emph{post-acquisition}
cost bridge covering all complete state-adaptive policies,
not compulsory $2^{|X|}$ branching. Neither $P=NP$ nor $P\ne NP$
has been proved; the universal bridge remains \OPEN{}.

\paragraph{PLANEFRACTION88: fractional adaptive source-word obstruction and source-readable projective-plane control (PROVED/EXACT in query model; independent review pending).}
For fixed legal source words, define the analysis-only fractional distinguishing
cover $\rho_c^*(F)$ as the minimum total positive read-cost weights hitting
the differing-word set of every valid opposite-SAT-label source.
Any bounded-error randomized \emph{complete state-adaptive} exact-input
decider with per-input error at most $\epsilon<1/2$ obeys
\[
\mathbb E\,C_{\rm distinct\ source\ reads}(F)
\ge(1-2\epsilon)\rho_c^*(F).
\]
This is a direct same-random-tape coupling and a feasible fractional-cover
construction, independent of the solver's representation changes. A
deterministic exact decider obeys the integer cover. Conditional
transcript potentials telescope and vanish after full source acquisition;
they cannot be added at each step to manufacture superlinear work.

A canonical proper duplicate-free 3CNF source from $\operatorname{PG}(2,p)$
has $q=p^2+p+1$ projective points and $q$ lines, $n=q(p+1)$,
$m=q(p+3)$ and encoded bit length
$N=(4m+2)\lceil\log_2(64(n+m+1))\rceil$.
The source sign vector $b$ is SAT precisely when no projective line has
all its point orientations equal to one. On the all-zero SAT source,
all opposite-label orientation changes intersect: the
\emph{promise-family} disjoint sensitivity is one, while its exact
fractional distinguishing cover is $q/(p+1)$ and integer cover $p+1$.
For every randomized decider of the \emph{full} proper-3CNF language,
the projective-line opposite-label neighbors therefore force
$(1-2\epsilon)q/(p+1)$ expected source-word reads. However full-source
geometry recognition and direct witness replay decide the whole
canonical family in polynomial time; no rich backward-selector relation
is compulsory. This construction is \emph{not} signed exact-$(2,2)$
and does not transport that budget automatically.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} seeded independent full audit with 5,152
assertion-accounted checks and zero failures for $p=2,3,5,7$,
including all 128 Fano orientations, independent DPLL and
SAT/UNSAT controls. Report, code and raw-summary records verified at
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_1900_LSPV-1_PLANEFRACTION88.tex}
and same-stem files. Existing RANDOMSENS87, MAIN108 and fractional
adversary literature are adopted, not rebranded as global novelty.
\textbf{OPEN:} a representation-neutral post-acquisition
parameterized lower bound for every adaptive total uniform exact
decider, sufficiently growing on algorithm-dependent hard inputs.
P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.



\paragraph{Latest RANDOMSENS87: sharp bounded-error adaptive source-word lower bound (PROVED/EXACT, declared model; COMPUTATIONAL controls).}
For any legal input $F$ of a fixed source-word encoding, define
$\beta_w(F)$ as the maximum number of pairwise disjoint sets of changed
source-word positions reaching legal \emph{opposite-label} inputs.
This is a source/label parameter, not defined by a solver's runtime;
computing it is not supplied free.  Coupling the same random coins on
$F$ and each opposite-label neighbor proves that \emph{every} adaptive
randomized total exact-input decider with per-input error at most
$\epsilon<1/2$ pays
\[
 \mathbb E\,Q_A(F)\ge(1-2\epsilon)\beta_w(F).
\]
The proof allows arbitrary representation changes, Horn/SCC/affine
reasoning, DPLL, learning, restarts, caches and rereads.  It
lower-bounds source-word queries, \emph{not} post-acquisition SAT work.
Using the certified 198-variable 264-clause signed exact-$(2,2)$
UNSAT source $U$ and the two-sign same-support SAT repair $V$ from
TWO-SIGN-SWAP64/DOECKER-CORE-CERT61, an all-$V$ $p$-block product has
$p$ disjoint opposite-label two-word perturbations.  Thus
\[
\mathbb E Q_A(B_{p,1^p})\ge(1-2\epsilon)p,\qquad
N_p=(1056p+2)\lceil\log_2(64(462p+1))\rceil .
\]
The $(1-2\epsilon)$ factor is sharp for the abstract orientation-word
AND promise.  Complete source validation and fixed-template recognition
provide an $O(p)$-word SAT decider on the recognized family; unsupported
inputs may use exact fallback.  Since always
$\beta_w(F)\le2+4m=O(N/\log N)$, this family and measure cannot yield
superlinear, let alone superpolynomial, unrestricted SAT time.
The earlier $U$ UNSAT certificate is an explicit imported dependency,
not independently replayed during this run.  A standard-library
audit made 30,609 checks without failure, including 254 chain inputs,
10 hybrid SAT/UNSAT controls and 16 randomized promise settings.
Full report and verified adjacent code/raw/handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_1805_LSPV-1_RANDOMSENS87.tex}.
The unrestricted parameterized acquisition/compression/direct-decision
cost bridge remains \OPEN{}; P versus NP remains OPEN.



\paragraph{Prior TWOHEAD86: source-identical two-local-head escape (PROVED/EXACT restricted; COMPUTATIONAL controls).}
On the proper unique 3CNF equality-source family $E_k(u,v)$,
$\mathsf{SAT}(E_k(u,v))$ holds exactly when $u=v$.
Its immutable grouped source uses $n=5k$, $m=8k$, and $32k+2$
fixed-width words; it is \emph{not} strict signed exact-$(2,2)$.
A \emph{total uniform exact} solver equipped with two independent locally
moving read-only input heads, both initially at source word zero,
can pay for a complete syntax scan, align the heads with the $u$ and $v$
quartets, and compare corresponding signed inputs in $O(k)$ charged
head moves plus reads with $O(\log(k+2))$ complete portable work bits.
Observed disagreement terminates UNSAT; comparison of all $k$
pairs terminates SAT. Unsupported inputs use a total exact fallback.
For the \emph{same physically grouped words}, the one-local-head
CUTFLOW85 crossing-transcript theorem implies that every exact
one-head solver with at most $c_A\log(k+2)+O_A(1)$ portable bits
requires $\Omega_A(k^2/\log k)$ time on some diagonal SAT input.
Therefore the separation is $\Omega_A(k/\log k)$ between the declared
\emph{access models}, not between SAT algorithms in an unrestricted RAM.
The full source identity and Boolean constraints remain unchanged; a
second source cursor bypasses the first model's cut-splicing premise.
At $k=64$, a charged abstract SAT run used 9,316 two-head movements
and reads, versus 135,313 for one-head unit batches and 26,449 for
logarithmic one-head batches; the full audit passed 285 assertions,
zero failures, including both SAT/UNSAT and signed exact-$(2,2)$ controls.
Backward shared-input Circuit-SAT counts for this family under the
NOT1/OR2/AND2 evaluation basis are $29k-1$ gates and $53k-2$
gate-input incidences; these are not SAT-solving circuit lower bounds.

\noindent\textbf{Handoff and first OPEN implication.}
The new bounded-head theorem is model-specific and cannot force an
arbitrary random-access adaptive SAT solver to retain, transfer or
materialize that boundary information. A universal post-acquisition
decision-oriented parameterized lower bound, with full policy switching,
SCC/Horn/affine, resolution and clause learning, caching, direct decisions
and source replay legal, remains \OPEN{}. Different deciders may have
different hard inputs. Root SAT alone does not compel all-context state.
Verified artifacts:

P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.




\paragraph{Latest CUTFLOW85: arbitrary-turn, parameterized two-way source-transport bound (PROVED/EXACT for one local source head; COMPUTATIONAL controls).}
The existing proper unique 3CNF equality-source family $E_k(u,v)$ of
PASSMEM84 has $n=5k$, $m=8k$, $W=32k+2$ fixed-width source words and
$\mathsf{SAT}(E_k(u,v))\Leftrightarrow u=v$.
For an arbitrary \emph{complete deterministic adaptive policy} whose
read-only source-word head moves only one adjacent word cell per charged
step and whose \emph{entire} transferable work configuration at any input
cut has at most $2^{\sigma_A(k)}$ states, accepted crossing transcripts
at the $2(k-1)$ public quartet boundaries give the independent source
information parameter
\[
h_{u,j}=j,\quad h_{v,j}=k-j,\quad
\sum_{j=1}^{k-1}(h_{u,j}+h_{v,j})=k(k-1).
\]
The exact crossing-sequence/splicing/Kraft argument, averaged over
uniform satisfying diagonal sign strings, proves
\[
\boxed{
\mathbb E_X T_A(E_k(X,X))\ge
\frac{(k-1)(k-2)}{\sigma_A(k)+1},\qquad
T_A^{\max}(k)=
\Omega\!\left(k+\frac{k^2}{s_A(k)+c_A+1}\right).
}
\]
This is a parameterized \emph{polynomial} joint time--space lower
bound covering arbitrary symbolically adaptive deterministic policies
\emph{within the physical local-head model}, with all work storage,
learning, policy switches and timers charged. Unlike PASSMEM84 it
permits unlimited two-way head reversals. SEQUENTIAL-TAPE-001 had
already described the general crossing-sequence method; this is its
source-specific quantified SAT application. A canonical batch scanner
achieves $O(k+k^2/B)$ motion with $B+O(\log k)$ live bits. Crucial
hostile controls \emph{falsify} transport to all algorithms:
the same clauses interleaved as neighboring $u_i,v_i$ quartets are
decidable by a locally moving head in $O(k)$ time and $O(\log k)$
space, and an ordinary random-access RAM decides the grouped source
in $O(k)$ word probes. The source is proper 3CNF but not strict signed
exact-$(2,2)$; strict $H_g$ cases remain separate controls.
The independent Python audit made 353 assertions without failure,
including SAT/UNSAT mutations and observed-evidence policy switching.
Standalone report, compact verifier, raw summary and five-role handoff
were published and verified:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_1555_LSPV-1_CUTFLOW85.tex},
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_1555_CUTFLOW85_minirepro.py},
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_1555_CUTFLOW85_results_summary.json}.
All-decider random-access time amplification and a noncircular
superpolynomial parameter-growth theorem remain \OPEN{}.
P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.



\paragraph{Latest PASSMEM84: parameterized multipass source-memory obstruction (PROVED/EXACT only for sequential-stream policies; COMPUTATIONAL audit).}
A concrete family of proper unique exactly-three-literal CNFs encodes
two independent input-sign strings $u,v\in\{0,1\}^k$ in separate
clause halves using the charged four-clause local identity
$\bigwedge_{\epsilon,\delta}(\ell\vee a^\epsilon\vee b^\delta)
\equiv\ell$. The source has $n=5k$ variables, $m=8k$ clauses and
$N=(32k+2)\lceil\log_2(64(13k+1))\rceil$ bits; its SAT label equals
$[u=v]$, with $2^{4k}$ models in the SAT case.
For \emph{every} total deterministic exact adaptive solver restricted
to $p$ sequential left-to-right source passes, with at most $s$ bits
of charged persistent state at the midstream boundary and no random
seek or uncharged outside storage, the deterministic equality
fooling-set argument yields the exact parameterized lower bound
\[
\boxed{(2p-1)s\ge k,\qquad
s_{\max}(2T_{\max}/(32k+2)+1)\ge k.}
\]
The second inequality uses source-word advance cost and the actual
worst-case number of passes. All adaptive representation changes,
caches, learning, SCC/Horn/affine rules and switches are covered
\emph{within the streaming machine model}; no prescribed DP is assumed.
This is the first declared pass--space $\Omega(k/p)$ application to
this particular proper-3CNF equality template, not a new general
communication theorem. Earlier project HASHLIT1 had exact streaming
equality, and ONEPASS66 had a different one-pass INDEX SAT-source
obstruction which collapsed with two passes; both are explicitly
credited. A complete-grammar-tested $B$-bit-batch policy solves the
canonical family with $\lceil k/B\rceil$ source scans, $B+O(\log k)$
bits of live state, and charged SAT/UNSAT switching. Direct random
access instead gives an $O(k)$-word, $O(\log k)$-space comparator,
so \emph{transport to all random-access deciders is FALSIFIED}.
The source is proper 3-CNF but \emph{not} strict signed exact-$(2,2)$;
strict balanced $H_g$ instances were separately checked as fallback
controls. The shared-input NOT/OR/AND evaluation circuit has
$29k-1$ gates and $53k-2$ wire incidences, with no claim of a
SAT-solving circuit lower bound.
The exact independent standard-library audit passed 594 assertions
with zero failures, including exhaustive small source model counts,
60 matched SAT/UNSAT adaptive traces, shared-gate evaluations,
and 10 controls.
Verified standalone publication and reproducibility are at
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_1508_LSPV-1_PASSMEM84.tex},
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_1508_PASSMEM84_minirepro.py}
and \path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_1508_PASSMEM84_results_summary.json}.
This adopts MAIN108's weighted transcript firewall, Role 2's
QUIET-EQUIV67, Role 3's ADAPTIVE-COVER75, and Role 4's
SCC-AFFINE-LOCK70 as hostile controls. The \textbf{OPEN}
load-bearing implication is still a parameterized
superpolynomial acquisition/compression/continuation theorem
covering \emph{all} complete uniform random-access adaptive SAT
policies, with a noncircular parameter trajectory and
algorithm-dependent growing inputs. P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.



\paragraph{Current LSPV-1 state-adaptive result, ADAPTIVEPROOFRACE83
(PROVED/EXACT only in the declared scheduler/circuit models).}
On parsed well-formed CNF, a single source-observed policy ranks four
\emph{total exact} methods (unit-propagating DPLL, signed
complementary-pair/SCC, Horn least-model, verified XOR-quartet/GF(2)).
For the stricter signed exact-$(2,2)$ language, a separately charged
grammar validator must reject invalid input before running these methods.
The policy then interleaves the
\emph{resumable} executions using stage-$r$ quantum
$2^{\max(0,r-i)}$ at source-selected position $i$.
For standalone event cost $t_i(F)$ and charged source-inspection
$C_{\rm obs}$, the resulting exact portfolio obeys
\[
T_{\rm race}(F)
\le C_{\rm obs}(F)+
8\cdot2^i(t_i(F)+1)+
s\!\left(i+\lceil\log_2(t_i(F)+1)\rceil+1\right)+O(1)
\]
for each $i$, in the \emph{specified logical-event scheduler basis},
with all failed recognizers and switches charged.  The point is an
\emph{upper} bound for a finite, state-adaptive policy, not coverage
of every exact decider.  The elementary finite-portfolio principle
and proof-certified universal races were already in MAIN101 and
UNIVERSAL-CERTIFIED-RACE1; no independent discovery of them is claimed.
For 3-CNF with shared source inputs and $n$ NOT gates, $2m$ OR2
gates and $m-1$ AND2 gates, functional gate-definition CNF preserves
SAT and the complete satisfying relation on original inputs with
unique gate extensions; its gate/wire counts are
$G=n+3m-1$, $W=n+6m-2$, and its clauses number $2n+9m-2$.
A backward AND/OR compatibility table is \emph{not} mandatory.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 9,966 finite checks, zero failures; 29 named
SAT/UNSAT controls plus 40 random CNFs (37 SAT, three UNSAT);
five fully evaluated source/circuit controls covering 4,800 source
assignments.  On same-support parity sources ($q=7$), SAT/UNSAT
adaptive race events were 918/818, while bare DPLL events were
718/25,062 and direct XOR events 252/152; all inspection,
dispatch and expert execution events are included in the race totals.
The parity graph formulas are \emph{not} strict signed exact-$(2,2)$
sources, while the connected $H_4$ control is and selects paired SCC.
The abstract events are not physical word-RAM instruction counts.
Source: \path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_1419_LSPV-1_ADAPTIVEPROOFRACE83.tex};
GitHub compact verifier and summary in the same role directory.
The relevant peer synthesis is MAIN107, which already falsifies a
mandatory high initial strong-backdoor cost by charged adaptive
ternary collapse.  The shared \textbf{OPEN} implication remains a
superpolynomial source/semantic/continuation lower bound applying
to \emph{every} total uniform deterministic exact adaptive decider
of one fixed NP-complete language, without imposing a predetermined
representation or output certificate.  P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.


\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted:} CRITICAL65 gives a clause-minimal odd-wheel
family with $\Omega(N/\log N)$ aligned-word acquisition but a charged linear
recognizer. ONEPASS66 gives a $q^2$-bit one-pass, source-closed late-selector
state bound; two charged passes use $O(\log N)$ workspace and $O(N/\log N)$
word reads. These bounds do not lift to arbitrary SAT decision time.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted:} PAIRHELLY67 shows that for any satisfiable
2-CNF $B$, forced literals are jointly extendible exactly when all singleton
and pair combinations are extendible. Coordinatewise majority closure gives a
direct proof. Consequently 2-CNF plus $k$ ternary clauses compiles in
$O((1+k^2)(n+m+1))$ word time and $O(n+m+k^2)$ peak words to an exact
three-choice independent-transversal instance, with at most
$1+3k+9\binom{k}{2}$ charged SCC tests. The graph-three-coloring endpoint is
NP-complete; this compilation is an upper bound, not a decision lower bound.

For two disjoint positive ternaries with nine cross-group negative binary
clauses, all nine independently encoded source fields are essential to
distinguish the all-active UNSAT formula from each one-field SAT repair.
The restricted probe potential drops by at most one per field read. A single
new negative binary clause can nevertheless exclude $3^{k-2}$ literal-choice
selections at constant source-record cost; per-candidate-work amortization
is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}. Unrestricted symbolic choice selection remains
uncovered, and no all-decider superpolynomial lower bound follows.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} CRITICAL65 checked 3,844 instances/assertions;
ONEPASS66 checked 33,996; PAIRHELLY67 checked 9,793 assertions, with
zero reported failures in each standalone finite audit.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, CUTVERTEX68:} For connected
odd-charge Tseitin CNFs, the exact number of individually critical
clauses is $\sum_{v\in V}2^{d_v-c_v}$, where $c_v$ counts components of
$G-v$.  This refines MAIN83's whole-vertex-gadget minimality:
2-vertex-connected cubic graphs have all $4|V|$ clauses critical,
while a cubic bridge example has only 36 of 40 critical clauses.
Flipping a designated literal sign changes UNSAT to SAT exactly for
these critical clauses; consequently every exact deterministic
aligned-word source decider must read $\sum_v2^{d_v-c_v}$ distinct
words.  The potential drops by at most one per read.  This is only
$\Omega(N/\log N)$ acquisition, not universal exponential time.
Known expander-Tseitin exponential \emph{resolution} refutation bounds
do not transfer to arbitrary deciders: a charged affine checksum
decides the same canonical family in polynomial time.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 74,836 finite assertions passed, zero failures.
Source: \path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_1629_LSPV-1_CUTVERTEX68.tex}.
Independent peer review of the count refinement is pending.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, AFFINECORE69:} A source-complete
normalizer partitions untagged ordinary proper-3CNF by identical variable
triples into full four-clause XOR-3 parity gadgets and literal residual
clauses. With $\kappa_{\oplus}$ residual clause records, the extraction is
exact and uniform in $O(m\log(m+2))$ word time, and exhaustive literal
choices followed by GF(2) elimination decide SAT in
$O(m\log(m+2)+(1+3^{\kappa_{\oplus}})(n+m+1)^3)$ word time.
Thus $\kappa_{\oplus}=O(\log N)$ is a polynomial-time \emph{upper}-bound
subclass, not a decision lower bound. Under the separately declared
complete certified-extraction calculus, all $m=4g+\kappa_{\oplus}$
original records must be validated; the unvalidated-record potential
falls by at most one per record read. This is a restricted source
normalization amortization, not a mandatory process for all SAT deciders.

For graph-complete cubic Tseitin proper-3CNFs, a source-checked
$O(N\log N)$ word-time recognizer reconstructs arbitrary clause
permutations, variable renamings, and whole-variable sign complementations.
For $c$ recovered graph components, SAT holds exactly when every component
charge parity is even, and satisfiable sources have exactly
$2^{|E|-|V|+c}$ models. A spanning-forest backsolve supplies an explicit
witness. Hence even a large syntactic $r=n$, fully critical clauses,
and hard ordinary resolution on expander variants coexist with a near-linear
source-aware SAT shortcut.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, AFFINEQUOTIENT70:}
On the affine solution space of a source-checked XOR-quartet decomposition,
let $t$ be the rank of all nonempty residual-clause falsification rowspaces
and $\kappa$ the number of residual clauses.  Exact quotient enumeration
and inclusion--exclusion give uniform SAT decision in
$\operatorname{poly}(N)(1+\min\{2^t,2^\kappa\})$ word time,
with polynomial working space and all failed trials charged.
In a separately declared source-complete clause-insertion calculus,
a clause whose false-set is a nonempty affine coset of codimension $c$
removes at most $2^{D-c}$ affine assignments.  Thus an UNSAT cover by
clauses all of codimension at least $c$ requires at least $2^c$ charged
insertions; two individually compatible clauses contradict each other
exactly when their bad sets are complementary affine hyperplanes.
These are representation/coverage results, \emph{not} all-decider
time lower bounds.  Role-2's positive-one-in-three affine-plus-2CNF
NP-complete endpoint was already known; no novelty is claimed for it.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, CONNECTRANK71 (external red-team pending):}
For every $g\ge1$ the connected proper signed exact-(2,2) source
\[
H_g=\bigwedge_{i=1}^g
(x_i\vee\neg y_i\vee z_i)\land(x_i\vee\neg y_i\vee\neg z_i)
\land(\neg x_i\vee y_i\vee z_{i+1})
\land(\neg x_i\vee y_i\vee\neg z_{i+1}),\quad z_{g+1}=z_1,
\]
has $n=3g$, $m=4g$, two positive and two negative occurrences per variable,
distinct proper ternary clauses, and connected incidence graph.
Source-checked complementary pairing simplifies it exactly to
$\bigwedge_i(x_i\leftrightarrow y_i)$, with $2^{2g}$ models
and a charged polynomial-time SAT decision.
Yet the rich cut matrix $M[x,(y,z)]=[H_g(x,y,z)]$ has rank $2^g$
over every field: rows have disjoint nonempty supports indexed by $y=x$.
For a declared source-closed explicit rank-one rectangle sum,
at least $2^g$ charged rank-one insertions are necessary;
the potential $2^g-\operatorname{rank}(P_t)$ decreases by at most one
per insertion. A BDD with all $x$ variables before all $y,z$
has width at least $2^g$. Neither representation is required for
the one-bit SAT decision, and a paired 2-CNF normalizer avoids both.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT positive algorithm:}
On the polynomial-time recognizable class of proper signed exact-(2,2)
CNFs whose complementary-clause pairing graph has a perfect matching,
replace each pair
$(a\vee b\vee u),(a\vee b\vee\neg u)$ by $(a\vee b)$.
PAIRRIGID72 strengthens this: the complement-pair graph has
maximum degree one under exact signed-occurrence counts and unique
clauses, so the forced matching is obtained without general matching.
For a fully paired input, the contracted binary clauses have each
surviving variable once positive and once negative; their incidence
multigraph consists of degree-two cycles. Orienting each cycle
produces an original-source satisfying assignment in linear time
after validation. Source-aware SCC remains available for partial pairing.
The strict sign-monochromatic NP-complete (2,2) sublanguage has no
such complementary pairs, so this is not a full-language SAT algorithm.
The backward AND/OR Circuit-SAT independent-transversal formulation
and its variable-wise $K_{2,2}$ conflict blocks are exact, but do not
force relation materialization in arbitrary deciders.

\par\noindent\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:}
The independent standard-library audit checked $H_g$ for $g=1,\ldots,6$
against every assignment, plus occurrence balance, connected incidence,
exact complement-pair matching and 2-CNF equivalence;
it materialized GF(2) cut ranks $2,4,8,16,32$ for $g\le5$.
The monotone $K_{3,3}$ and an unpairable balanced six-variable source,
and matched SAT/UNSAT 2-SAT controls, were checked.
Exactly 599,571 assertions passed, zero failures.

\par\noindent\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:}
Exponential cut-matrix rank, fixed-order OBDD width, or a large
independent-transversal relation automatically requires exponential
time from every exact SAT decider. Those are conditional
representation-service lower bounds. Their transport to all deciders
fails on the connected example, and no $P=NP$ conclusion follows.

\par\noindent\textbf{OPEN:}
a representation-independent, source-aware, charged theorem
forcing superpolynomial acquisition, valid semantic compression or
direct continuation for every total uniform deterministic exact decider
of one fixed NP-complete language, allowing solver-dependent hard inputs,
source retention, algebra, SCC, Horn, component dynamic programming,
learning, caching, and direct decision. P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.


\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (PAIRRIGID72, independent review pending):}
For valid proper signed exact-$(2^+,2^-)$ 3-CNF, complementary-pair
clause graphs are matchings by a three-same-signed-occurrences
contradiction. With $p$ forced pairs and $\delta=m-2p$ unmatched
ternaries, complete all pair contractions, choose one source
variable from each unmatched ternary, and enumerate at most
$2^h$ assignments to their union ($h\le\delta$), solving the remaining
2-CNF by SCC. The source-coherent exact deterministic time is
\[
O\bigl(m\log(m+2)+\delta^2+2^h(n+m)\bigr)
\]
word operations and $O(n+m)$ reusable words, including source
validation, failed branches, witness construction and replay.
Consequently $\delta=O(\log N)$ lies in P, and $\delta=0$
guarantees SAT with an explicit cycle-oriented assignment.
A source-complete explicit-pair-contraction calculus requires exactly
$p=(m-\delta)/2$ binary record writes, but an arbitrary one-bit
SAT decider need not perform this normalization.
The strict sign-monochromatic NP-complete exact-$(2,2)$
subclass has $\delta=m$, and a distinct polynomial paired-monotone
support class also has $\delta=m$. Thus large $\delta$
does not establish intrinsic hardness.
\par\noindent\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} A standalone standard-library audit
(seed 202610232000) completed 249,113 assertions with zero failures,
including 247,176 original-versus-contracted assignment comparisons
and 140 fully paired witness constructions. No genuine strict
monotone exact-$(2,2)$ UNSAT specimen was reconstructed in this run.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted (PAIRPATH73; external review pending):}
The PAIRRIGID72 forced-complementary-pair calculus has $p$ disjoint
ternary pairs and $\delta=m-2p$ unmatched ternaries. Signed exact-$(2,2)$
occurrence conservation gives
$t_x+2b_x^{\pm}+u_x^{\pm}=2$: every contracted binary variable
outside the unmatched support occurs once positively and once negatively,
while every boundary variable occurs at most once in the binary core.
The binary clause-incidence multigraph is therefore a disjoint union
of paths and cycles. Exact existential path elimination yields a
\emph{linear} source-coherent kernel with precisely $\delta$ unmatched
ternaries, at most $P\le\lfloor3\delta/4\rfloor$ pairwise variable-disjoint
binary clauses, and at most $3\delta$ boundary variables. Every kernel
assignment has a valid source extension exactly when it satisfies
the kernel. A uniform charged decider runs in
\[
O\bigl(m\log(m+2)+2^\delta(\delta+1)+m\bigr)
\]
word time and $O(n+m)$ words. Conditional expectations imply SAT
whenever $\delta+2P<8$, in particular for defects $0$ and $2$.
A source-fixed resolution-path normalizer with $c$ binary-cycle
components needs exactly $p-P-c$ explicit resolution inferences.
This is a representation-specific cost, \emph{not} compulsory work
for all exact SAT deciders.

1,179 valid signed-balanced samples; 1,103 full existential
boundary-projection checks; 1,105,248 original assignment evaluations;
1,082,925 kernel assignments; 110 checked resolution inferences and
222 local identities. All passed. The samples were SAT;
an explicit strict-monotone exact-$(2,2)$ UNSAT instance was not

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted (XORWITNESS74; independent review pending):}
A new source-coherent positive result sharpens PAIRPATH73.
For valid proper unique signed exact-$(2,2)$ $3$-CNF, let
$p$ be its forced complementary-pair count and $\delta=m-2p$
its number of unmatched ternaries. After the prior exact path
projection, the projected binaries are variable-disjoint, and
every ternary occurrence of a projected-binary endpoint has
the \emph{opposite} sign. Choose independently exactly one true
literal from each projected binary, and fair bits on the other
boundary variables. Every residual ternary has a forbidden
sampler cylinder of measure either $0$ or $1/8$.
Every nonempty forbidden cylinder can be disjoint from at
most six other residual clauses, since each of its three
sampler coordinates has at most two opposite-sign occurrences.
Consequently for $\delta=8$ two nonempty forbidden cylinders
overlap with probability at least $1/64$, and for $\delta<8$
the union bound applies:
\[
\Pr_{\rm sample}[K_F=1]\ge
\begin{cases}1-\delta/8,&\delta\le7,\\
1/64,&\delta=8.\end{cases}
\]
Thus \emph{every} valid exact-$(2,2)$ source with
$\delta\le8$ is SAT; as $\delta$ is even, UNSAT requires
$\delta\ge10$. A uniform source-validating positive decider
runs in $O(m\log(m+2))$ word time on this class, and
the earlier $\le 2^\delta\le256$ source-coherent SCC
branches construct an optional verified witness.
The prior criterion $\delta+2P<8$ is still correct but
no longer the strongest project-level general low-defect
SAT guarantee.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT amortization restricted to the
explicit sampler relation-validation calculus:}
if $\Phi_j$ is the fraction of the fair sampler
not covered by the first $j$ validated forbidden cylinders,
one insertion reduces it by at most $1/8$, giving
$\Phi_j\ge1-j/8$ and $\Phi_8\ge1/64$.
This is not compulsory work for an arbitrary one-bit SAT
decider, which may decide directly without materializing
the kernel or the sample space.

\par\noindent\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL (XORWITNESS74):}
The stdlib verifier tested 1,591 valid signed-balanced sources
with 16,900 assertion-accounted checks and zero failures;
736 had $\delta=8$, with smallest observed sampler success
$10/64$. Exact sampler-cylinder semantics, selected source
existential projections, both-label implication-ladder,
eight-cube, Horn and 2-CNF controls were checked.
Finite tests are not asymptotic lower-bound evidence.

No theorem forces every total uniform deterministic exact decider

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, CUBICCOVER75 (independent review pending):}
For the fixed NP-complete signed exact-$(2,2)$ proper-3CNF language,
after forced complementary-pair contraction let $\delta$ denote unmatched
ternaries. Original signed-occurrence conservation gives each unmatched
variable exactly two or four ternary occurrences. Pair these occurrences
to form a loopless cubic clause-dual multigraph with $\delta$ vertices and
$3\delta/2$ edges. Shannon's four-edge-color ceiling, followed by a
polynomial-time maximum matching, yields a source-variable cover of size
$h\le\lfloor5\delta/8\rfloor$. Exhausting these $h$ source assignments,
then deciding each resulting 2CNF using SCC, gives a \emph{uniform exact
upper bound}
\[
 T(F)=O\!\left(m\log(m+2)+\delta^4+
       2^{\lfloor5\delta/8\rfloor}(n+m+1)\right)
\]
word operations and $O(n+m)$ live words, all source acquisition,
failed branches, matching, witness lifting and replay charged.
For bridgeless duals, Petersen's perfect-matching theorem improves
the exponent to $\delta/2$. A proper valid source with
$n=48,m=64,p=24,\delta=16$ has a three-bridge cubic dual,
maximum matching $7$, and exact ternary hitting number $9$,
so a \emph{universal} half-defect cover is
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}. The example itself is SAT.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 248 valid source instances, 253 audit assertions,
zero failures; original 198/264 true UNSAT source not retested.
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM:} Zhang--Peitl--Szeider (SAT 2024) give
a $20$-clause lower bound for unsatisfiable proper 3CNF with
at most two occurrences per signed literal, implying $m\ge20,n\ge15$
for unsatisfiable valid exact-$(2,2)$ sources.
\textbf{OPEN:} neither the matching cover nor its branching tree
is mandatory for every deterministic exact decider;
the universal cost-preserving acquisition/compression/continuation
implication and both $P=NP$, $P\ne NP$ remain unproved.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, SIMPLECOVER76 (independent review pending):}

If $H_F$ is \emph{simple}, Haxell--Scott's matching theorem

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT positive solver, Separated-Core theorem:}
If $B,T$ have disjoint source-variable sets after contraction
and each variable in $T$ occurs once positively and once negatively
there, then the binary dual decomposes into cycles and the ternary
dual into cyclic cubic multigraph components. Edge orientations
construct both source-coherent witnesses, so $F$ is SAT and complete

The parameter $\tau$ is an exact output-size obstruction only for an

\subsection*{SPLITINTERFACE77: NP-complete sharp pair/path boundary}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT reduction; KNOWN THEOREM for source NP-completeness; independent review pending.}
For every nonempty proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ $3$-CNF $F$ on $n$ variables
and $m=4n/3$ clauses, a uniform source-exact lift $G(F)$ uses
$4n$ target variables and $m+4n=16n/3$ proper ternary clauses.
It is connected, duplicate-free, and signed exact-$(2,2)$.
Its canonical complementary-pair contraction has exactly $2n$ pairs,
defect $\delta=m$, and $P=n=3\delta/4$ pairwise disjoint positive binary
constraints after elimination of the cyclic auxiliary variables.
The remaining ternaries are \emph{all negative}, with each boundary
variable occurring exactly twice and no ternary containing both
variables of a positive binary pair. The exact projected kernel is
\[
K(F)=\bigwedge_{i=1}^{n}(a_i\vee b_i)\ \land\
\bigwedge_{C\in F}\widehat C,\qquad
\widehat{x_i}=\neg a_i,\quad
\widehat{\neg x_i}=\neg b_i.
\]
An exclusive-one normalization of each $(a_i,b_i)$ proves
$F\in SAT\iff K(F)\in SAT\iff G(F)\in SAT$.
D\"ocker's source NP-completeness therefore transfers to the
image kernel: \textbf{NP-complete even at the sharp}
$P=3\delta/4$, $|U|=3\delta/2$, $|K|=7\delta/4$.
The fixed explicit-output projection calculus must write at least
$n$ binary records and $m+n$ total records, but \emph{does not}
apply to deciders that recover $F$ and decide its one-bit SAT label
without materializing the kernel. The new lift falsifies a conjectured
easy-region rule based merely on absence of four-occurrence
unmatched variables; it does not conflict with SIMPLECOVER76's
stronger separated-core sufficient condition.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 346,297 locally accounted checks, zero failures;
the independently accessible 198/264 D\"ocker source lifted to a
proper connected 792-variable, 1056-clause instance with 396 pairs,
$\delta=264$, and $P=198$. Source grammar was checked, but a new
792-variable UNSAT refutation was not executed.
\textbf{OPEN:} a source-aware all-decider superpolynomial
acquisition/compression/direct-decision lower bound; both $P=NP$
and $P\ne NP$ remain unproved. Source and mini reproducer:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_0832_LSPV-1_SPLITINTERFACE77.tex},
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_0832_SPLITINTERFACE77_minirepro.py},
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_0832_SPLITINTERFACE77_results.json}.

The external KNOWN THEOREM of Zhang--Peitl--Szeider (SAT 2024, Table 1) gives a twenty-clause minimum UNSAT instance in that class. Therefore every valid source with $\delta\le18$ is SAT, and every UNSAT source has $\delta\ge20$. This strengthens XORWITNESS74's $\delta\le8$ bound and generalizes SPLITINTERFACE77's special-image normalization. COMPUTATIONAL: 346 finite cases and 272268 checks, zero failures. The quotient need not preserve model counts, and no arbitrary one-bit SAT decider is required to construct it. The universal cost bridge, $P=NP$, and $P\ne NP$ remain OPEN. \paragraph{ANTIPODAL79: exact two-witness sign repair, no decision transfer.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent review pending:}
Every strict monotone signed exact-$(2,2)$ proper unique 3-CNF with $m$ clauses
admits a same-unsigned-support signed-balanced repair at \emph{exactly $m$}
literal-sign flips so that both $0^n$ and $1^n$ satisfy the repaired formula.
This is optimal: every old monochromatic clause needs one edit.
A source-coherent fractional $1/3$ flow on the positive-clause--variable--
negative-clause incidence network has value $m/2$; integral max flow
selects one flipped incidence per old clause and preserves every variable's
$(2,2)$ signed quota by conservation. The result strengthens MAIN103's
earlier sharp \emph{single all-TRUE witness} edit theorem to simultaneous
antipodal witnesses without any increase in edit cost.
A further \textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted} minimum-cost bipartite flow
computes the closest legal antipodal signing of \emph{every} signed exact-$(2,2)$
input, with variable positive degree exactly two and clause positive
degree in $[1,2]$; half-valued incidence flow proves feasibility and
integrality proves polynomial solvability. There are $O(n+m)$ network
arcs, polynomial charged time and $O(n+m)$ peak words.
The edit-calculus potential, number of still-monochromatic original
clauses, decreases by at most one per sign change, so it forces $m$
explicit edits \emph{for this transformation objective only}.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 352 synthetic monotone cases, 284 min-cost checks,
280 small truth-table controls, three exhaustive small optimum checks,
3,243 counted assertions and zero failures. Separate source-exact
flow reconstruction on Role 2's original D\"ocker $n=198,m=264$
UNSAT-certificate-dependent source found $132$ units of flow,
$264$ sign flips, preserved all unsigned positions and $(2,2)$ quotas,
and verified both constant witnesses in the repaired SAT formula.
The old UNSAT classification was imported, not independently reproved.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED as an all-decider bridge:} editing source
signs changes the SAT instance and is not required of an exact SAT
decider on the unchanged input. This gives a positive optimization
algorithm, not a general SAT solver or lower bound.
\textbf{OPEN:} universal superpolynomial acquisition/compression/direct
decision cost for arbitrary total uniform exact deterministic SAT deciders.
Report and reproducer:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_1003_LSPV-1_ANTIPODAL79.tex}.


\par\noindent\textbf{TUTTECOVER79, PROVED/EXACT restricted (independent review pending):}
In the existing canonical complementary-pair contraction of proper unique signed
exact-$(2,2)$ 3-CNF, let $\delta=m-2p$ be unmatched ternaries.
The source-labelled clause dual $H_F$ is a loopless cubic \emph{multigraph},
including parallel edges. A sharp Tutte--Berge argument proves for every
such graph on $s$ vertices
$\nu(H)\ge\lceil2s/5\rceil$. Consequently a maximum matching gives
a source-variable hit set of size $h\le\lfloor3\delta/5\rfloor$,
and the exact uniform SCC-branching decider has the improved general bound
\[
O\!\left(m\log(m+2)+\delta^4+
2^{\lfloor3\delta/5\rfloor}(n+m+1)\right)
\]
word operations and $O(n+m)$ reusable words, including complete charged
source validation, pairing, matching, failed branches, reconstruction
and replay. This \emph{improves} CUBICCOVER75's all-multigraph
$5\delta/8$ exponent, without invalidating its earlier upper bound;
SIMPLECOVER76's $ (5\delta+c)/9$ exponent for the separately
certified \emph{simple} subclass is retained.

The previously documented ten-vertex three-lobe parallel-edge graph
(SIMPLECOVER76) attains $\nu=4=2s/5$ and has an exact source-valid
$n=30,m=40,p=15,\delta=10$ lift whose minimum one-shot
ternary-hitting set has six variables. This establishes sharpness
for the \emph{declared variable-hit calculus}, not a SAT-decision
lower bound: the lifted source has a charged polynomial-time graph
orientation witness. Its explicit-output potential of uncovered
ternaries decreases by at most four per source-variable identifier
write, but arbitrary SAT deciders are not required to emit such
identifiers. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 553 assertion-accounted checks,
76 randomized cubic multigraphs, named exact matching/edge-cover and
SAT/UNSAT hostile controls, zero failures.
The all-decider source-acquisition/compression/direct-continuation

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted (GLOBALQUOTA80, external review pending).}
For every fixed valid proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ source, the two positive
signed-occurrence positions per original variable uniquely identify its
entire same-support signing.  Hence even against \emph{all} legal
multi-field sign perturbations, not merely the one-swap star,
the fixed-input SAT-label sign-probe certificate complexity is at most
$2n=3m/2=O(N/\log N)$.  This is not a uniform SAT decision-tree bound.

On $t$ disjoint octahedral eight-clause supports, there are
$6^{6t}$ valid balanced signings on $n=6t$ variables.
A fixed source needs exactly $2n$ probes for full sign identification,
while a uniform deterministic \emph{source-identification} decision tree
needs exactly $3n$ worst-case probes, via the exact adversarial
potential $\Phi=\sum_v\lceil\log_2|K_v^{\rm consistent}|\rceil$.
Yet \emph{all} these signings are SAT: two disjoint-support clause
failures overlap with probability $1/64$, strengthening the eight-clause
union bound to $63/64<1$.  Thus SAT-root decision on this sign-fiber
promise uses zero sign probes and a source-recognizing total exact
algorithm constructs a witness in $O(N/\log N)$ word time on the family.
At least $2^t$ different full satisfying relations occur despite the
constant SAT label.  The complete standard-library audit exhaustively
checked all $6^6=46{,}656$ one-block legal signings (zero UNSAT, model
counts $4$--$22$), with $118$ separate brute-force checks and
SAT/UNSAT symbolic hostile controls; status \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}
for finite observations.  The first missing implication remains an
all-decider superpolynomial \emph{post-acquisition} cost theorem, not an
input-query or rich-relation enumeration bound.  $P$ versus $NP$
remains \OPEN{}.
Verified report:

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, GAUGEFORK81 (independent review pending).}
Using LSPV-2's explicitly source-certified D\"ocker UNSAT source $U$
($n=198,m=264$) and its source-coherent two-sign-swap SAT neighbor $V$
(an independent 198-bit witness was replayed), take $t$ disjoint
$U/V$ blocks and independently complement all occurrences of any
source variable.  The $\mathbb Z_2^{198t}$ gauge action preserves the
proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ language and SAT labels.  The resulting
\emph{both-label} family contains exactly $2^{199t}$ distinct encodings
with $n=198t$, $m=264t$ and
$N=(1056t+2)\lceil\log_2(64(462t+1))\rceil$ bits; it is SAT exactly
when all $t$ blocks use $V$.  The quotient of legal four-occurrence
two-positive/two-negative sign patterns by whole-variable complementation
has exactly $3^n$ gauge classes, not $6^n$.

On every all-$V$ input, \emph{every} deterministic exact decider for
the full signed exact-$(2,2)$ language must inspect at least one
\emph{swapped} and one \emph{unchanged} sign field of $x_3$ in each
block.  The source-probe potential
$\Phi=2t-\sum_i \alpha_i$, with $\alpha_i$ the number of these two
types already queried in block $i$, decreases by at most one per
field read, and accepting correctly requires $\Phi=0$.  This proves
a sharp $2t$ distinct-field lower bound, attained by reading just
two $x_3$ sign fields per block and testing their XOR.  A uniform
full-source validator and template recognizer runs in
$O(N/\log N)$ word time, reverting to an exact general solver outside
the recognized promise. Thus exponentially many valid sources with
\emph{both labels} coexist with near-linear SAT decision.  The
restricted acquisition bound does \emph{not} imply a superlinear or
superpolynomial all-decider cost bridge.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} the actual 264-clause $U$ source, the sign
swap, the $V$ witness and all 64 projected-core rows were independently
checked, as were 390 deterministically gauged trials at
$t\in\{1,2,3\}$: $1{,}396{,}170$ recorded assertion checks,
zero failures.  The six local UNSAT projection certificates are an
explicit \emph{imported} ROLE-2 dependency, not freshly replayed.
\textbf{OPEN:} the all-uniform-decider superpolynomial post-acquisition

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, AFFINECUTDP82 (independent review pending):}
For every affine Boolean system $Ax=b$, every ordering of its source variables,
and every prefix/suffix cut $L\mid R$, let
$\eta=\operatorname{rank}A_L+\operatorname{rank}A_R-\operatorname{rank}A$.
If the system is consistent, the number of distinct complete suffix
residual functions induced by all prefix assignments is exactly
\[
 W=2^\eta+\mathbf1_{\{\operatorname{rank}A_L>\eta\}};
\]
for an inconsistent system $W=1$. The proof counts nonempty right-hand
sides in $(b+\operatorname{im}A_L)\cap\operatorname{im}A_R$ and the optional
constant-FALSE residual. All values are polynomial-time computable by
Gaussian elimination. The forward/reverse syndrome compatibility test
checks this same affine-space intersection in polynomial time
\emph{without enumerating} the potentially exponential residual set.

On CONNECTRANK71's connected signed exact-$(2,2)$ source $H_g$,
where $n=3g$, $m=4g$ and
$H_g\equiv\bigwedge_i(x_i\leftrightarrow y_i)$ with free $z_i$,
the ordered read-once dynamic-programming cut immediately after all
$x_i$ needs $2^g$ distinct residual states; interleaving
$x_i,y_i,z_i$ keeps the maximum at most three.
The shared-source NOT1/OR2/AND2 assignment-evaluation circuit has
$15g-1$ gates and $27g-2$ directed references, and existentially
eliminating its deterministic gate variables preserves exactly the
original source residual relation. Gate-variable Tseitin encoding need
not preserve signed exact-$(2,2)$ physical occurrence quotas.
Under an \emph{explicit residual-record} calculus, $J$ required cut
states cost at least $J$ append writes; this is a restricted
representation lower bound and \textbf{does not} force any arbitrary
one-bit SAT decider to build that table. Gaussian elimination, SCC,
source recognition, order selection, caching, and decide-first avoid it.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 12,285 seeded assertions, zero failures,
including 550 randomized affine systems and 2,829 random cut comparisons,
plus 4,680 original-CNF versus shared-circuit assignment checks.

\section*{ARC-DUAL-LOCAL-TRACE187: two-TM authenticated rejecting traces (LSPV-1)}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the declared standard sequential fixed-$k$ tape
model, independent mathematical review pending.} A complete rejecting
run of an arbitrary fixed deterministic total exact ordinary-SAT decider
admits a locally checkable authenticated transition certificate.
Authentication verifies each original-source read, every original
finite transition, initial work blanks, and chronological last-write
coherence per work cell. Thus
\[
\forall F:\quad F\in\mathsf{UNSAT}\iff
\exists\pi\;V_A(F,\pi)=1,\qquad
F\in\mathsf{SAT}\Rightarrow\forall\pi\;V_A(F,\pi)=0.
\]
The step-faithful certificate has $R=T_A(F)$ records and
$O(R\log(N+R))$ bits. On a \emph{compound} input $F\#\pi$
a separate-read-only-input fixed-$k$ sequential-tape verifier has
$T_k=O((N+R)\log^3(N+R))$ and
$S_k=O((N+R)\log(N+R))$; the uniform circuit checks a
\emph{witness}, not the full SAT decision problem.
Two genuine original-variable restriction levels preserve both labels
when all four authenticated UNSAT child traces are checked.
\par\noindent\textbf{Two distinct ledgers.}
M1 is one standard read/write input-inclusive tape; Mk has separate
sequential read-only input and fixed $k$ counted work tapes.
For compound length $X$ the conservative constructed simulation is
$T_1=O(T_k(X+S_k+\log(T_k+2)))$,
$P_1=O(X+S_k+\log(T_k+2))$, giving only
$t_1\le t_k+\max(1,s_k)$, not an inter-model numerical lower bound.
Neither model is automatically FLMV's indexed-input random-access machine.
\par\noindent\textbf{FALSIFIED AS AN ARC INFERENCE:}
faithful records have only the upper budget $N^{K+o(1)}$ for unknown
hypothetical $P=NP$ exponent $K$. A fixed solver can add $N^r$
actual delay steps, all charged by this grammar. Therefore it yields
neither a strict coNTIME exponent gain nor a small-space FLMV successor.
The explicit finite audit had 1,124 passing assertion groups across
32 SAT/UNSAT source controls, 128 true two-level descendants and 320
forged traces; no measured FLMV bounds are inferred.
See \path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-26_1455_LSPV-1_ARC_DUAL_LOCAL_TRACE187.tex},
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-26_1455_ARC187_trace_audit.py},
and coordination handoff
\path{research/coordination/2026-10-26_1455_ARC_DUAL_LOCAL_TRACE187_FIVE_ROLE_HANDOFF.md}.
\textbf{OPEN:} source-authenticated affordable universal UNSAT
certificates, an arbitrary-$K$ fully charged same-machine ARC route,
ARC-EDGE-90 and $P\ne NP$.

\subsection*{LSPV-2: CIRCUIT--SCAN--REVERSE--SELECT173, final scan and reverse gate inverse}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, architecture-restricted, independent review pending.}
For the exact fixed-record total SAT witness verifier of report 166, arithmetic
inverse signed gate consumers and proper 13-row compiler source
$\mathsf{Rank/Select}$ now cover three previously open sectors: the
\emph{last} forward bitonic record-output OR gates, all scan-result AND
gates, and \emph{all} reverse-payload MUX output OR gates, including
the terminal source request equality gates. A parameter-only uniform
$\mathsf{Shape}(q,m)$, with no materialized netlist or occurrence table,
computes the at-most-four signed direct gate uses and at-most-21 original
source-CNF literal occurrences with $O(\log^3 G)$ bit operations and
$O(\log G)$ writable/index bits. The inverse must recompute the
\emph{receiving} comparator stride.
