\subsection*{LSPV-2: CIRCUIT--SOURCE--SELECT--BARRIER172, signed exact-$(2,2)$ indexed-incidence model firewall}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted; independent review pending.}
For each $k\ge2$, form $k$ physically permuted source blocks from the original
$15$-variable/$20$-clause proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ family, separately
using either an all-SAT $H(0,0)$ component or an all-UNSAT W1 component.
Even an oracle giving one entire unknown physical block in one query
requires $k-1$ distinct component probes in the worst case to return the
first physical occurrence of original variable $x_1$, since two uninspected
blocks can be exchanged without changing any already read source bit.
For standard fixed-width clause encoding
$N_k=16+2w+60k(w+1)$, $w=(20k).\mathrm{bit\_length}$, arbitrary-input
direct $\mathsf{OccSelect}(x_1,0)$ therefore requires
$\Omega(k)=\Omega(N_k/\log N_k)$ physical source-bit probes
\emph{without preprocessing or supplied incidence metadata}.
This is an inverse-access restriction, not a SAT decision-time lower bound.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT canonical exception:}
The distinct HEADMOVE-1 three-zone fixed layout has algebraic
four-occurrence signed $\mathsf{Rank/Select}$ for every source variable:
a fixed 20-row ROM, at most two authentic input polarity reads,
$O(\log^3N)$ charged bit operations and $O(\log N)$ live work/index bits,
including source addresses and output-bit emission.
Full template/malformed-input validation still reads
$\Omega(N)$ bits on an accepted canonical encoding.
Neither source-access theorem is the complete bitonic
gate compiler inverse or a total ordinary-SAT parser.

\textbf{Exact promise-circuit ledger:}
canonical disjointness SAT has $2k$ typed Boolean gates
($k$ AND, $k-1$ OR, one NOT), $2k$ primary source-anchor wires,
$4k$ distinct signal nodes, $4k-1$ gate-input edges and one output bit.
Fixed-width explicit operand descriptors use $\Theta(k\log k)=\Theta(N_k)$
written bits. This is a \emph{promise} decision circuit, not
the full all-input $C_{A,N}$ or an ARC-shrinking child.

\textbf{HEADMOVE jump firewall:}
a radius-$J$ target-only jump may skip the complete gap without
visiting interior cut cells, so native reversal/crossing statements
cannot simply transfer. Conditional on the published one-sequential-head
native crossing theorem, fully simulating a radius-$J$ instruction
in $O(J+\operatorname{polylog}N)$ sequential time
and $O(\log N)$ additional visited work cells yields
$t+s+j\ge2$ only in the separately declared $M_J$ machine.
The M1 input-inclusive footprint and the Mk sequential input/work
space ledgers remain distinct from indexed FLMV/RAM.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:}
$131{,}072$ actual W1 assignment evaluations, 306 genuine signed
SAT/UNSAT permuted formulas, 340 canonical both-label source controls,
$18{,}780$ per-variable inverse checks, 340 typed promise-circuit checks,
90 bounded-jump itineraries, and at least $134{,}924$ assertion groups
with zero failures. The previous complete four-sweep M1/Mk equality
verifier was rerun with byte-identical raw evidence across 340 controls
and $9{,}320$ head/work-state agreements.

\textbf{OPEN:}
final scan and reverse MUX, constants, aliases, physical gate compiler
Rank/Select, arbitrary ordinary-SAT source normalizing and genuinely

\subsection*{LSPV-2: CIRCUIT-PARITY-TRANSPORT164, signed parity promise transfer}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted (peer review pending):}
For the fixed-incidence proper-three-CNF parity promise $F_{p,b}$ of
ARC181, the arithmetic source incidence has a uniform indexed
$\operatorname{Lit/Count/Rank/Select}$ interface.  The exact historical
$Q_{15}/J$ signed exact-$(2,2)$ regularizer $R_{15}$ transports this
promise through every \emph{fixed} number $h$ of actual levels:
\[
 \mathrm{SAT}(R_{15}^{h}(F_{p,b}))
 \iff \bigoplus_{i=1}^{p}b_i=1,
 \qquad |D_i^{(h)}|=4\cdot 2^{h},
 \qquad D_i^{(h)}\cap D_j^{(h)}=\varnothing\ (i\ne j).
\]
The $D_i^{(h)}$ are precisely the changed physical \emph{sign-bit}
addresses; all variable IDs and headers are input-independent.  The
fully lazy fixed-depth accessor pays $\log^{O_h(1)}p$ bit time per read
and $O_h(\log p)$ writable/index bits under the explicit source
interface.  Input length is $\Theta_h(p\log p)$, not a fixed-power
contraction.  The result applies to \emph{signed} exact-$(2,2)$,
not the stricter clause-monotone class.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} Q15 exhaustive $2^{15}$ replay, two real
regularization levels, complete eager-versus-lazy polarity support
audits and both-label parity controls for $p=2,3$, together with
ordinary SAT, UNSAT, Horn and duplicate-clause hostile controls:
$3{,}260{,}422$ checks, zero failures.  At $h=2$ each $b_i$ changes
exactly $16$ target sign-bit positions and no header/name bits.
An input-independent starting state must inspect at least one site in
each $D_i^{(h)}$, giving $p$ probes and $2^p$ transcripts, but only
$\Omega(N_h/\log N_h)$ probes on an easy parity promise; a linear NAND
circuit computes its promised function.

\textbf{OPEN:} total arbitrary encoded SAT fixed-verifier base,
mixed-sign clause-monotone adapter, full-length-$N$ decision circuit

The October 26 source \emph{SAT resource extension} proposes a
fixed-sorting-verifier bit-local SAT-to-clause-monotone-exact-$(2,2)$
reduction and a separate hypothetical seed with
$d=901/500=1.802$, $S=N^{o(1)}$ and two-algorithm boundary
$C(d)=651000/361301=1.801821749732\ldots$.
$P=NP$ does not supply this seed; no companion with $c<C(d)$
or uniform full $N$-input SAT decision circuit compression was built.

\textbf{FILLED/RESTRICTED, independent review pending.}
The historical \(15\)-variable \(19\)-clause forced-false
three-port \(Q_{15}\) from PCRETRACT1 can replace the new PDF's
unobserved \(348\)-variable \(463\)-clause \(Q_{348}\) in
its \emph{signed}, source-preserving occurrence-cycle regularizer.
Using the actual seven-clause transparent gadget \(J\), for
an ordinary proper ternary source with \(m\) clauses and \(n\)
declared original variables the verified port conditions give
\[
 (v_{15},m_{15})=(33m+48n,\ 44m+64n),\quad
 3m_{15}=4v_{15},\quad
 \mathrm{SAT}(R_{15}(F))\iff\mathrm{SAT}(F).
\]
All active output variables have exactly two occurrences of
each polarity; clauses use three distinct names.  Two actual
ordinary-CNF recursive levels were constructed and both labels
audited: SAT $(3,2)\mapsto(210,280)\mapsto(19320,25760)$,
and UNSAT $(3,8)\mapsto(408,544)\mapsto(37536,50048)$,
where pairs denote (variables, clauses).
Exhaustive \(Q_{15}\) checking found \(244\) models, all with
ports \(1,7,13\) false. SAT witnesses extend at two levels;
UNSAT preservation uses exact port projection, not an
unperformed exponential enumeration.
A \(131\)-clause \emph{monotone adapter}, if its unobserved
mixed-\(M_j\) blocks are separately validated, changes
the symbolic coefficients to
\(v_M=4323m+6288n\), \(m_M=5764m+8384n\).
Constant-factor savings are not an exponent contraction.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT with fixed-incidence restriction.}
If every source clause position has an input-independent
variable identity and source-controlled literal signs,
every output bit of a fixed-layout $R_{15}$ is a constant
or a copy/complement of one source sign bit. A finite
two-level four-source test checked \(77{,}280\) second-level
sign positions, only eight input-dependent. An
\emph{executed} input-dependent sorting permutation
is not local-literal, but the proposed construction encodes
a fixed sorting network as SAT verifier gates, so that
objection does not refute the PDF's method.
The claimed full end-to-end polylogarithmic
output-bit \emph{index} generator remains OPEN without
the inverse occurrence-offset construction and a
complete malformed-input protocol.

\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED inference.}
A gate trace on one input is only its specialized constant,
not the full \(C_{A,N}\) deciding \emph{every} length-\(N\)
SAT input. Instance-specific \(q=1.29\) block/interface
relations do not qualify as full circuits. A builder
that first writes \(N^{d-o(1)}\) gate records cannot
later attain construction time \(N^{C(d)-\epsilon}\) by
compressing the materialized network, since the writing
cost was already incurred. A direct all-input
compiler that avoids those writes remains OPEN.
The PDF's original \(Q_{348}\) DIMACS/RUP bundle
and all mixed-\(M_j\) tests were not recovered, and
must not be marked independently verified.
Indexed-input random-access machine-to-circuit
compilation needs model-matched transport;
Pippenger--Fischer's one-dimensional tape result
is not a free general RAM simulation.

\subsection*{LSPV-2: ARC-MEMBERSHIP-JOIN162, exact module signatures versus paid acquisition}
\textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 OPEN. P versus NP OPEN.}
For any actual source-named ordinary-CNF decomposition into modules
\(D_i(Y,U_i)\) with one shared original boundary \(Y\) and disjoint
private \(U_i\), write \(P_i=\{u:\exists U_i\,D_i(u,U_i)\}\) and
\(\sigma(u)=(\mathbf{1}_{u\in P_i})_i\).
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the declared finite semantic model,
independent peer review pending:} a quotient \(h\) commutes with
\emph{all common boundary filters} modulewise,
\(h(P_i\cap S)=h(P_i)\cap h(S)\), precisely when every
\(h\)-fiber has a constant \(q\)-bit membership signature.
Thus \(\sigma\) is the coarsest such quotient, with at most
\(2^q\) realized labels despite possibly large boundary width.
A separate new \(q\)-way sufficient condition is pairwise nested
supports within every label, extending (but not replacing)
Role~3's original two-module ARC-CNF-CONTINUATION121 criterion.
These are SOURCE-DEPENDENT semantic partitions, not cheaply
generated machine configurations.

A concrete \emph{all-input} \(A\)-assisted wrapper checks a supplied
decomposition, enumerates boundary words and executes the fixed
correct ordinary-SAT guest \(A\) on each actually serialized
module-plus-proper-3CNF-pin child. Source identity and both
parent labels are exact. Unsupported/malformed inputs execute
\(A\) on the FULL parent; this destroys a claimed favorable edge.
With \(q=N^a\), \(2^{|Y|}=N^b\), true child length \(N^\lambda\)
and fully charged work/generator/ancestor exponents \(r,g,\eta,\kappa\),
only the \textbf{CONDITIONAL} eager ledger
\[
t'\le\max\{r,a+b+\lambda+g,a+b+\lambda t\},\qquad
s'\le\max\{\lambda,\lambda s,\eta,\kappa\}
\]
holds. A fully materialized \(2^{|Y|}\times q\) table adds
peak space exponent \(a+b\). For a dense, noncompressing,
verbatim source-module partition, the special full-table EAGER
policy must write \(\Omega(2^{|Y|}N)\) child bits and satisfy
\(a+\lambda\ge1\); this is \emph{not} an arbitrary-decider
lower bound or a prohibition on lazy/semantic generators.

\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED as a heredity inference:}
one fixed root signature need not survive module-specific
ordinary-CNF descendants. An explicit proper-3CNF two-module
SAT, SAT, UNSAT chain keeps original \(x\) and fresh pin
auxiliaries but its STALE constant signature falsely reports SAT
at level two. Recomputed signatures repair correctness
with additional paid guest work. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:}
750 random support families with exhaustive boundary filters;
39 genuine proper-3CNF both-label source controls, 78
first children, 156 DPLL-solved second-level children,

\subsection*{LSPV-2: ARC-LP-INDEPENDENT-FRONTIER161, exact LP pin firewall}
\textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 OPEN; P versus NP OPEN.}
For ANY proper signed ordinary 3CNF source \(F\) and a
primal-independent set \(I\) of original source variables, EVERY
partial Boolean assignment \(\alpha\) on \(I\) extends to the basic
real clause LP by setting all other \(x_j=1/2\).
Every clause has at least two unpinned variables, so even a
false pinned literal leaves clause LP sum \(1/2+1/2=1\).
Hence no exactly validated Farkas UNSAT certificate or
source-valid LP nogood can use only pins from \(I\).
A root dual cut's nonzero pin support must contain a
co-clausal original-variable pair. At deeper levels the
ancestral pins count toward this support.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT scoped source consequence.}
For \(F\in C_{\mathrm{M22}}\), every name occurs four
times and primal maximum degree is at most eight,
so a greedy independent boundary has at least
\(\lceil k/9\rceil\) variables. On the retained connected
198-name, 264-clause canonical UNSAT source, the greedy
set contains 42 names and all \(2^{42}\) Boolean
conditionings retain an LP-feasible rational point,
with 96 entire source clauses untouched after all 42 pins.
The UNSAT label is \emph{imported} from the published source,
not independently rerun in this report.

\textbf{ALL-INPUT assumption-dependent but noncontracting wrapper.}
Given one fixed total ordinary SAT decider \(A\), eagerly
branching on two independent names, constructing actual
ordinary first/second-level CNF children, calling \(A\)
on up to four real final descendants and OR-joining their
answers is exact on all ordinary encodings (unsupported
inputs delegate to \(A\)). Under fully charged sequential
bit parsing/serialization/guest costs, this construction
gives only \(t'\le\max\{1,t\}\),
\(s'\le\max\{1,s\}\) for \(A\in E\).
On canonical M22, pinning \(d=o(k)\) names leaves at least
\(m-4d\) untouched ternaries and raw \(L=\Omega(N)\);
there is no demonstrated fixed \(\lambda<1\) power shrink.
Numerical LP agreement and enumerative residual budgets
are not universal machine lower bounds.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 31 sources, 16 SAT/15 UNSAT,
108 second-stage real ordinary-CNF encodings,
104 actual small exact-DPLL leaf decisions checked
against exhaustive truth tables, zero failures;
four additional large-U198 second-stage children were
structurally generated without rerunning their UNSAT proof.
Physical FLMV indexed work, flips, bit-time and peak
workspace were not measured by Python.
The strict FLMV endpoint remains
\(t<241/200,\ s<11642479/23232400\).
The old universal cosine endpoint remains RETIRED.
The remaining all-input favorable \(A\)-dependent
successor, or a cofinal finite typed route to the
FLMV hard-image box, is \textbf{OPEN}.

Role-owned report:
\url{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/blob/main/research/agents/lspv-2/2026-10-25_0312_LSPV-2_ARC_LP_INDEPENDENT_FRONTIER161.tex}.
Shared handoff:
\url{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/blob/main/research/coordination/2026-10-25_0312_ARC_LP_INDEPENDENT_FRONTIER161_FIVE_ROLE_HANDOFF.md}.
Next: a solver-assisted, non-independent boundary that produces
genuinely power-short source-consistent ordinary SAT children,
with two real typed levels and a joint charged FLMV ledger.

\subsection*{LSPV-2: ARC-SCOPE-TOLL160, hard-image cheap-normalizer resource toll}
\textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain OPEN.}
Against the one fixed FLMV-hard Cook-image promise of Role~1's
\texttt{ARC-SINGLE-HARD-IMAGE161}, consider a uniform exact streaming
source-to-scoped-CNF normalizer with charged time $N^{r+o(1)}$,
writable/index workspace $N^{\eta+o(1)}$, explicitly emitted
event/name bits $N^{\mu+o(1)}$, and live source-name width
$w\le\delta\log_2N+o(\log N)$ on \emph{every} hard-image input.
One-pass exact frontier DP takes $N^{\mu+\delta+o(1)}$ work and
$N^{\delta+o(1)}$ writable bits; FLMV's locally indexed SAT
reduction supplies the virtual input bits at polylogarithmic
overhead. Thus the \emph{same uniform machine} for the FLMV-hard
language would have
\[
(t',s')=
(\max\{r,\mu+\delta\},\ \max\{\eta,\delta\})
\]
at exponent scale. Therefore no \emph{unconditional} normalized-image
constructor can satisfy both strict inequalities
\[
\max\{r,\mu+\delta\}<\frac{241}{200},\qquad
\max\{\eta,\delta\}<
\frac{11642479}{23232400}.
\]
For near-linear streams $\mu=1$, any $\delta<41/200$ makes the
continuation itself endpoint-cheap, so normalization must pay
the excluded time or space toll. This is a \textbf{PROVED/EXACT
consequence of KNOWN FLMV} within the declared streaming model,
independent mathematical review pending, not an unrestricted
time lower bound against all SAT algorithms.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 24 independent roots, 12 SAT/12 UNSAT;
2,133 actually serialized, parsed and scoped-validated ordinary
children; 1,992 actual exact solver leaves; 190,254 scoped DP
table updates and 986,912 emitted child bits; zero mismatches.
Two real recursive levels preserve source-name pin compatibility
on these controls, but sometimes a child remains nearly parent
size or grows. Python counters are \emph{not} full FLMV
bit-machine work, peak writable/index bits or bit-time.

The positive all-hard-image \emph{$A$-dependent} semantic
normalizer that would meet the forbidden resource profile
is still \textbf{OPEN}. It cannot be replaced by a free
source-only reduction, by a complete parent-scale $A$ call
or by scoped-width promises satisfied only by easy sources.
The old universal cosine endpoint remains \textbf{RETIRED}.
Substantive report:

\subsection*{LSPV-2: RELAX-DUAL-CUT159, prior relaxation recovery and exact ARC nogoods}
\textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 OPEN; P versus NP OPEN.}
Re-reviewed September PROGRESS2-0129/0130 heterogeneous fuzzy/LP/local-consistency
failures and ES-STRUCTURE-0019 witness--relaxation squeeze.
M22-NAE-PAIR149's overlap-consistent local NAE pseudodistributions and
REPAIR-GAP64's zero-valued fractional selector optimum persist on UNSAT
sources. Numerical consensus or feasible LPs therefore cannot decide
SAT or supply a mandatory machine-time resource.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, classical rational-Farkas corollary in a restricted
source-pinned interface:} Given original clause/box rows and fixed boundary
pins $\alpha$, a nonnegative rational Farkas infeasibility certificate
using only the pin rows for $J\subseteq\operatorname{dom}(\alpha)$ proves
the exact parent-source consequence
\[
  \bigvee_{i\in J}(x_i\ne \alpha_i).
\]
One verified clause excludes $2^{|B|-|J|}$ boundary assignments,
with source-coherent reuse and no unverified numerical claim.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} two ordinary restriction levels, 105 test groups
(96 random, evenly split SAT/UNSAT), 541 LP calls, 187 rationally checked
source-valid cuts, 94 executed exact fallback calls; zero failures.
The LP solver proposes candidates, while the exact fraction checker
determines whether pruning is sound.

\textbf{FALSIFIED as an ARC successor:} basic clause LP accepts the
all-half vector with margin $1/2$ in every proper 3CNF clause, including
all $C_\star$ inputs; LP feasibility need not signal SAT. A raw restriction
of a canonical $C_{\rm M22}$ formula on $d=O(\log N)$ adaptively selected
source variables leaves at least $m-4d$ untouched source ternaries, and
thus $\Omega(N)$ child bits in dense indexed encoding. No fixed
$\lambda<1$ child-length exponent, no all-input learned-cut coverage
bound and no FLMV-compatible simultaneous peak workspace follow.
A fully charged assumption-dependent exact ordinary-SAT successor and
its finite cofinal FLMV landing remain \textbf{OPEN}.
Relevant standalone report:
\url{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/blob/main/research/agents/lspv-2/2026-10-25_1753_LSPV-2_RELAX_DUAL_CUT159.tex}.

% BEGIN LSPV-2 NOTE::ARC-PADDED-GUEST-GATE157::2026-10-25_0112
\subsection*{LSPV-2: ARC-PADDED-GUEST-GATE157, charged polynomial-guest query slope}
\textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 OPEN; P versus NP OPEN.}
For the single-machine ordinary-SAT achievable region \(E\) in the
FLMV indexed read-only-input random-access model, the strict
published endpoint remains
\(t<241/200,\ s<11642479/23232400\).
The numerical claimed all-decider successor
\((t,s)\mapsto(\max\{11/10,3/5+t/2\},\max\{1/2,s/2\})\)
is \textbf{CONDITIONAL/OPEN}, not established by arithmetic.
The universal cosine endpoint remains \textbf{RETIRED}.

\textbf{New PROVED/EXACT scoped theorem, independent mathematical review
pending.} Assume \(P=NP\) supplies one fixed total uniform correct
ordinary-SAT machine \(B\) of time/space exponent \(K_0\).
For any fixed integer \(D>K_0\), define \(\operatorname{Pad}_D(B)\)
to delay for \(\Omega(N^D)\) counted steps \emph{before} evaluating
the source and then execute \(B\). It is another polynomial SAT decider,
with achievable joint diagonal \((D,D)\). Any compiled machine which
\emph{actually executes and awaits} this guest on an ordinary query
of length \(N^{\theta+o(1)}\) spends at least
\(N^{\theta D+o(1)}\) work. For the proposed time bound
\(q(D)=\max\{11/10,3/5+D/2\}\), necessarily
\(\theta D\le q(D)\); \(N^{b+o(1)}\) sequential
executed queries each of that length require
\(b+\theta D\le q(D)\).
Hence no \emph{executed} padded guest query of length
\(N^{1/2+\varepsilon+o(1)}\), or full-size parent query,
can be hidden under this successor for arbitrarily large
fixed \(D\). These are \emph{real guest-execution costs}, not
unrestricted SAT lower bounds: a source-aware compiler may strip
the delay, use another algorithm, cache or restructure calls.

\textbf{Positive FILLED/RESTRICTED all-input program-syntax construction.}
For explicit finite source grammar \(\operatorname{Pad}_{D_2}
(\operatorname{Pad}_{D_1}(B))\), deleting each marked
semantically empty delay prefix is a uniform finite source transformation
and preserves SAT and UNSAT on every ordinary input. Two actual
\emph{compiler} iterations yield first \(\operatorname{Pad}_{D_1}(B)\),
then \(B\), with both numerical exponent improvements for
\(D_1\ge2K_0,D_2\ge2D_1\). It does not cover arbitrary solver
programs, produce fractional ordinary SAT children, or constitute
two recursive SAT solver levels. It stops at a possibly high-exponent
unpadding target and therefore \emph{does not close ARC-EDGE-90}.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL finite controls:} six published mini cases
with 24 real ordinary DIMACS two-stage pin/serialize/parse/solve leaf calls;
ten extended local both-label cases with 40 leaf calls, exact
two-level OR gluing and twice-peeled guest labels, zero failed assertions.
Maximum measured second-level child length ratio is \(1\).
Padding counts are symbolically charged, \emph{not} physical FLMV
instruction timing, complete-state flips, peak live work/index bits,
bit-time or representation/hardware size.
These finite observations assert no asymptotic power shrink.
All children are ordinary SAT and are not asserted to preserve
the narrower clausewise-monotone exact-\((2^+,2^-)\) \(C_\star\) grammar.

\textbf{OPEN receiving lemma:} a genuinely polynomial-promise-sensitive,
all-input correct arbitrary-SAT transformation applicable to \emph{every}
fixed polynomial correct solver, using semantically exact ordinary
short children with paid discovery, source/read/write/index traffic,
selection, switching, failed branches, caching, ancestor storage,
reconstruction and two \emph{real} recursive solver levels, deriving
a favorable same-machine edge. The original \texttt{FINAL-LINK-ARC}
finite-hit logic is retained with FLMV's corrected box, not its retired
cosine criterion. Previous source-only/extensional oracle elimination,
boundary-join promised contractors, and selector results stay scoped.
Published report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-2/2026-10-25_0112_LSPV-2_ARC_PADDED_GUEST_GATE157.tex}.
Reproducer and raw results are adjacent; shared five-role handoff:
\path{research/coordination/2026-10-25_0112_ARC_PADDED_GUEST_GATE157_FIVE_ROLE_HANDOFF.md}.

\subsection*{LSPV-2: ARC-FROZEN-CODE-SELECTOR156, static solver-text firewall}
\textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 OPEN; P versus NP OPEN.}
A scoped corollary of earlier MODELBRIDGE-0011 and Role~4
ARC-EXT-SELECTOR-NOGO109 establishes that merely inspecting the
\emph{fixed finite program text} of one actually existing known exact
ordinary-SAT solver before compilation does not evade downward SAT-bit
oracle elimination.  Freeze the resulting all-input correct controller
and suppose online calls expose \emph{only membership bits} for complete
ordinary children of length at most $N^{\lambda+o(1)}$,
$\lambda<1$, with $N^{a+o(1)}$ queries, local bit work
$N^{r+o(1)}$, and peak live writable/index storage
$N^{h+o(1)}$ including all selection, source traffic, buffers and
ancestor costs.  Replacing each short call by recursive execution of
the \emph{same} constructed decider gives one unconditional total
uniform exact ordinary-SAT machine with
\[
t_B\le\max\{1,r,a/(1-\lambda)\},\qquad
s_B\le\max\{h,\lambda\}.
\]
Thus the illustrative all-input static-code-only envelope
$(r,a,\lambda,h)=(11/10,3/5,1/2,1/2)$ would land at
$(6/5,1/2)$ inside the strict FLMV box
$t<241/200,\ s<11642479/23232400$ and cannot exist.
This is a \textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted quantifier clarification,
independent review pending}, not a new SAT lower bound or favorable edge.
An intensional transformation that exists only under a
\emph{polynomial-time solver promise}, has charged runtime/internal-state
observations or another actual hypothesis-dependent mechanism, remains
outside the lemma and \textbf{OPEN}.  Do not use retired
$t+s<2\cos(\pi/7)$ as a universal endpoint.

A \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL} Python audit on 23 SAT/UNSAT ordinary sources
performed 157 real guest SAT query executions per comparison and 88
genuinely executed two-level ordinary-CNF leaf calls, with zero
answer/transcript mismatches for a fast and delay-padded guest.
A static code tag changed 22 of 23 selection transcripts, demonstrating
algorithm-identity dependence without supplying a new SAT answer channel.
Instrumentation reported 136533 serialized node-write bits, 79000

\paragraph{ARC-CHAIN-INTERFACE155 (24 October 2026, 23:41 PDT): connected minimally-UNSAT proper-3CNF strict-subset SAT-label obstruction and charged two-level boundary repair.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT RESTRICTED, independent mathematical review pending; COMPUTATIONAL 20 SAT/UNSAT roots; CONDITIONAL promised-family same-machine ledger.}
For every $n\ge3$, convert the Horn implication chain
$H_n^0=(x_1)\wedge\bigwedge_{i<n}(\neg x_i\vee x_{i+1})\wedge(\neg x_n)$
to proper ordinary ternary clauses, using two fresh three-literal clauses per binary original clause and four fresh clauses per unit. The result $U_n$ is a connected minimally UNSAT ordinary proper-3CNF with $2n+6$ unique ternary clauses; $Y_n$ instead ends in $(x_n)$ and is SAT of equal length. Every \emph{strict proper source-clause subset} of either is SAT. Hence adaptive, \emph{source-blind} controllers that see only SAT-status bits of such strict clause subsets cannot distinguish these two opposite-label parents. This is not an ordinary-SAT machine lower bound; both parent chains are Horn-recognizable and the gadgets are not certified monotone exact-$(2,2)$.
With original source names preserved and auxiliaries fresh, the complete ordinary child relation
$R_{a,b}(u,v)=\operatorname{SAT}(\operatorname{proper3}[(x_a=u)\wedge
\bigwedge_{i=a}^{b-1}(\neg x_i\vee x_{i+1})\wedge(x_b=v)])$
obeys the exact shared-boundary join
$R_{a,c}(u,w)=\bigvee_{v\in\{0,1\}}R_{a,b}(u,v)\wedge R_{b,c}(v,w)$.
Fully materialized parseable root$\to$group$\to$leaf ordinary children with a real generic total DPLL on every leaf give two genuine semantic call layers and both root labels. A declared restricted nested implementation has
$L_1=N^{1/2+o(1)}$, $L_2=N^{1/4+o(1)}$,
$B=N^{3/4+o(1)}$ leaf calls, and conditional
$t'_{\rm promise}\le\max\{1,3/4+t/4\}$,
$s'_{\rm promise}\le\max\{1/2,s/4\}$;
full source discovery, all ordinary header/body writes and child buffers are charged in the corresponding construction. A total recognizer with original-parent $A$ fallback instead retains
$t'_{\rm all}\le\max\{t,1,3/4+t/4\}$ and
$s'_{\rm all}\le\max\{s,1/2,s/4\}$, failing a favorable all-input successor for a mandatory DELAY-padded fallback. \paragraph{ARC-ORAND-FUSION154 (24 October 2026, 23:12 PDT): two-level exact ordinary-SAT guarded OR--AND, fused child-buffer accounting and all-input fallback firewall.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT RESTRICTED Boolean semantics and leaf-answer decision-tree theorem, independent mathematical review pending; COMPUTATIONAL both-label controls; CONDITIONAL promised-input FLMV resource ledger.}
The latest user direction assigns all five existing roles exclusively to the ARC/FLMV all-ordinary-SAT \emph{same-machine} resource-contraction gap, superseding older UWI-only governance text without changing schedules, task counts or peer-owned regions. For pairwise disjoint original-name ordinary proper-3CNF leaves $F_{ij}$ with exact guard selectors and private auxiliaries, the ordinary root $H_b$ has the exact two-level identity
\[
\operatorname{SAT}(H_b)\iff
\bigvee_{i=1}^{b}\bigwedge_{j=1}^{b}\operatorname{SAT}(F_{ij}).
\]
One rigid canonical tagged promise yields $N=\Theta(b^3\log b)$ parent bits, fully ordinary group children $L_1=\Theta(b^2\log b)$ and ordinary leaf children $L_2=\Theta(b\log b)$. A real root-to-group-to-leaf solver executes at most $b^2=N^{2/3+o(1)}$ ordinary leaf SAT calls, with exact original variable names and no sibling witness collisions. An answer-bit-only deterministic controller has $b^2$ worst-case independent leaf queries on \emph{both} SAT and UNSAT profiles; this is \emph{not} an arbitrary SAT machine lower bound. Source-fused leaf serialization can avoid a writable intermediate group buffer on this syntactically certified promise. For an all-input base solver with joint $(t,s)$ the \emph{conditional promise} simultaneous ledger is
\[
t'_{\rm promise}\le\max\{1,2/3+t/3\},\qquad
s'_{\rm promise}\le\max\{1/3,s/3\};
\]
all source scans/replays, recognized tags, actual emitted header/body bits, failed group attempts, guest input probes, arithmetic/index control, caches, live ancestors and peak writable/index storage are charged. Eager group buffering instead costs $N^{2/3+o(1)}$ live bits. For ordinary sources outside the rigid promise, calling the supplied $A$ on the full original input restores correctness but gives only $t'_{\rm all}\le\max\{t,1,2/3+t/3\}$ and $s'_{\rm all}\le\max\{s,1/3,s/3\}$. Padded correct $A_K$ supplies an explicit $\Omega(N^K)$ countercontrol against this \emph{particular} fallback wrapper, not against other existential routes. Role~3 MULTIBIT-ORACLE93 had already established guarded OR and a nested-OR promise; this report's incremental scope is alternating OR--AND, an actual group-to-leaf executor and fused input storage, not original invention of guarded source selectors.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} seed-free independent DPLL on six real proper ordinary CNF roots, all groups and leaves, $b=2,3,4$ both labels, $b^2$ actual leaf calls for each worst-case case, complete $2\times2$ sixteen-profile finite audit and opposite-label outside-promise controls; total root DPLL nodes $677$, no discrepancies. Source bits by $b$: $(N,L_1,L_2)=(4620,1066,546),(11970,1876,644),(24736,2910,750)$. Python materializes children and does not measure physical FLMV flips, bit-time or workspace. \textbf{OPEN:} ARC-EDGE-90 still requires a fully charged uniform all-input assumption-dependent favorable achievable-pair route from cofinal diagonals into the correct strict FLMV box $t<241/200$, $s<11642479/23232400$. \paragraph{M22-FIXED-DEPTH-STREAM153 (24 October 2026, 22:12 PDT): any \emph{fixed} number of source-replayed exact DP batches admits logarithmic-space virtual output ports.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in a restricted projector model, independent review pending; COMPUTATIONAL finite controls.}
Extending the already established two-round Role~4 M22-DP-TWOLEVEL103 and Role~2 M22-LOWSPACE-DP-BATCH152, a fixed integer $h$ of independent-pivot Davis--Putnam rounds admits a deterministic uniform source-backed clause-membership and bit-output accessor with $O_h(\log N)$ peak writable/index bits, polynomial $N^{O_h(1)}$ bit work and $O_h(N)$ fully ordinary output bits.  Preserve original names and exact pointwise semantics
\[
 F_h(z)\equiv\exists_{I_0\cup\cdots\cup I_{h-1}}F(x,z).
\]
With maximum total variable occurrence $d_j$, clause width $w_j$ and primal degree $D_j$, safe recurrences are $d_{j+1}\le d_j^2$, $w_{j+1}\le2(w_j-1)$, $D_j\le d_j(w_j-1)$, with first-round M22 sharpening $(d_1,w_1,D_1)\le(8,4,24)$.  The short-seed local-minimum selector pays every unsuccessful polynomial-hash seed, source probe and address operation.  Therefore $k_2\le64k/75$ and $k_3\le(64/75)(320/321)k$.  A separately declared single-residual exhaustive upper continuation has $\Psi=2^{k_h}$, $R_{\rm search}=k-k_h$, and time $2^{k_h}N^{O_h(1)}$; neither continuation nor its enumeration budget is mandatory for arbitrary algorithms.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} Three genuine preprocessing rounds on published eligible U198, with UNSAT label imported, yielded $(k,m,N)$ equal to $(198,264,7936)$, $(156,264,9448)$, $(132,330,14830)$ and $(119,615,39400)$ in one declared ordinary encoding.  Eligible SAT Y6, connected cyclic F5 SAT, and paired H6 SAT \emph{outside} $\mathcal C_\star$ passed exhaustive finite existential-projection checks; Horn, affine and contradictory 2SAT outside-target controls also passed.  These tests do not measure physical FLMV state flips, bit-time or actual writable-space peaks, and use materialized greedy test pivots rather than the theorem's seed selector.

\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED as an ARC inference:} bounded-round $\Theta(k)$ variable removal and $O_h(\log N)$ projector space do not guarantee $N^\lambda$, $\lambda<1$, ordinary child size; the U198 encoded output actually expands.  The descendants leave the $\mathcal C_\star$ grammar, and these are not two recursive SAT solver levels. \textbf{OPEN:} ARC-EDGE-90 still requires a fully charged all-ordinary-input same-machine cofinal favorable route into the strict FLMV box $t<241/200$, $s<11642479/23232400$; DIRECT-POST still requires a noncircular complete-state post-acquisition potential. The retired universal cosine product endpoint remains invalid. P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.

with maximum child widths $4,6$ and ordinary dense-indexed outputs both $O(N)$ bits. \paragraph{M22-DP-LOCALITY151 (25 October 2026, 12:20 KST): fixed-depth resolution is exact but cannot power-shrink M22 ordinary clauses.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the declared bare-resolution model, independent peer review pending.}
For any valid $F\in\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}$, $k$ original active variables,
$m=4k/3$, and any fixed constant $d$ distinct original pivot variables,
$G_d=\operatorname{DP}_{x_d}\cdots\operatorname{DP}_{x_1}(F)$ obeys the
pointwise identity
\[
 G_d(z)=\exists x_1\cdots\exists x_d F(x_1,\ldots,x_d,z).
\]
Every original clause avoiding the pivots survives verbatim; hence
$m(G_d)\ge m-4d$.  If $a_0=0$ and $a_{j+1}=a_j+(a_j+2)^2$, then
$m(G_d)\le m+a_d$; the maximum generated clause width is at most
$2^d+2$.  For standard dense-indexed ordinary-CNF encoding and fixed
$d$, the explicit child length is
$N_d=N(1+O_d(1/\log k))=N^{1+o(1)}$.  Thus bare
constant-depth Davis--Putnam source pivots do not produce
$N^{\lambda+o(1)}$-bit ordinary children for any fixed
$\lambda<1$.  The residual is not asserted to belong to
$\mathcal C_\star$; no eligible recursive $\mathcal C_\star$ solver call follows.
A source-backed constant-size patch admits paid virtual-bit access,
but no FLMV resource contraction is claimed.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 3,984 finite pointwise projection comparisons,
nine both-label/control sources, zero mismatches; the published
198-variable UNSAT label is imported rather than solved anew.

\paragraph{ARC-STREAM-NORMALIZER150 (25 October 2026, 11:27 KST): exact source-order scoped-stream costs and FLMV normalization firewall.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent mathematical review pending; KNOWN FLMV endpoint; COMPUTATIONAL finite controls.}
For every properly serialized ordinary proper 3CNF $F$, the exact source-order normalizer emits one named \texttt{OPEN} immediately before each variable's first containing clause, every original \texttt{CLAUSE}, and a \texttt{CLOSE} immediately after its last containing clause. This yields $m+2k$ events, $\Theta(N)$ ordinary indexed encoded bits, and $\operatorname{SAT}(E(F))=\operatorname{SAT}(F)$, with an eager $O(N\operatorname{polylog}N)$-work, $O(k\log(m+2))$-space directory implementation or a replay $O(N^2\operatorname{polylog}N)$-work, $O(\log N)$-space \emph{streaming} implementation. The Python checker materializes output and is not a physical low-space RTM measurement.
For \emph{every} exact $(2^+,2^-)$ clausewise-monotone M22 source whose positive clauses precede all negatives, each original variable spans the positive/negative boundary, forcing \emph{this declared compiler's} live width
\[
\boxed{w=k=\Theta(N/\log N),}
\]
for both SAT and UNSAT. Reordering or alternative representations may avoid that width; no universal decider lower bound is inferred.
More generally, suppose one total, exact, uniform ordinary-SAT source-to-valid-stream transducer in the indexed read-only-input FLMV RTM has simultaneous work $N^{a+o(1)}$, writable/index bits $N^{s+o(1)}$, output length $N^{b+o(1)}$ and width $\gamma\log_2N+o(\log N)$. Immediate frontier DP composes into \emph{one fixed ordinary-SAT decider} with pair
\[
(t,s')\le\bigl(\max\{a,b+\gamma\},\max\{s,\gamma\}\bigr).
\]
The published FLMV transfer forbids \emph{both} inequalities $\max\{a,b+\gamma\}<241/200$ and $\max\{s,\gamma\}<11642479/23232400$ at once. This is a \textbf{derived conditional no-go}, not a favorable all-input successor nor a new SAT lower bound. A $\mathcal C_\star$-only result requires paid fine-grained SAT transport before the ordinary-SAT endpoint applies.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 9 deterministic SAT/UNSAT controls, including matched connected eligible SAT $Z_{33}$ and published eligible UNSAT $U$ with $k=198,m=264,N=11910$ source bits, $M=31050$ stream bits and $w=198$ each; 9 eager/replay stream equalities; two true \emph{restriction/properization} levels on both labels, not ARC recursion. Source code measures logical field reads, not temporal physical flips, peak FLMV bits or bit-time; original $U$ label imported from D\"ocker. All pin children tested leave $\mathcal C_\star$. Valid target grammar remains clausewise monotone, unique proper triples, exactly two occurrences per sign and only fully paired instances excluded.

\paragraph{M22-TWO-PIN-LIFT148 (25 October 2026, 10:12 KST): mathematical two-variable robustness for the six-name M22 source class.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent mathematical review pending; COMPUTATIONAL complete 5,625-source audit.}
For \emph{every} valid clausewise-monotone, unique, proper $(2^+,2^-)$ 3-CNF source $F\in\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}$ on exactly six active original names, every assignment of either prescribed Boolean value to any two distinct names extends to a satisfying full source assignment. This symbolically strengthens the \emph{prior} computational pairwise-full projection of Role~3 MINIMAL-NAMED-JOIN102, while retaining Role~3 ANTIPODAL-SIX103's earlier $12+h$ balanced-witness theorem and full unary feasibility priority. For opposite prescribed values, at least two Hamming-weight-three extensions exist. For equal pinned values, a balanced extension exists except when $P$ and $Q$ both have two supports through the pinned pair and their third-vertex tip pairs are disjoint; in that exceptional case an explicit source-coherent weight-two (pins 00) or weight-four (pins 11) extension exists. The sharp connected, valid $\mathcal C_\star$ counterexample
$P=\{123,124,356,456\}$ and $Q=\{125,126,345,346\}$ has extension counts $(1,10,10,1)$ for pins $(00,01,10,11)$, while the equal-pin cases have \emph{no} balanced extension. Thus unrestricted \emph{balanced-only} two-pin continuation is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}, not SAT itself. A third prescribed variable can destroy feasibility by falsifying a positive source clause.
Full finite $75^2=5625$ source census (5,550 in $\mathcal C_\star$, 75 fully paired outside), all 15 source-variable pairs and all four values gave 337,500 positive extension tests, 180 balanced exceptions matching the theorem, 1,350,046 abstract assertion-accounted checks, zero failures. Two ordinary pin-and-simplify levels on connected SAT controls and the published connected in-target UNSAT source $U$ continue to leave the canonical grammar, with ordinary child lengths $\Theta(N)$, not power shrink. The UNSAT label for $U$ is imported from D\"ocker's published construction, not independently exhaustively decided. These are logical and serialized-size controls, \emph{not} physical FLMV bit transitions, peak working space, complete-state flips or cumulative bit-time.
On a recognized disjoint union of $r$ six-name components containing a nonpaired component, the direct source-aware component solver is polynomial even though an artificial all-four-way two-pin independent exhaustive list has $\Psi=4^r2^{4r}=2^{6r}$ and $R_{\rm search}=0$. Such enumeration is not necessary decision work. Retain $k$ original active variables, $m=4k/3$, $N=\Theta(k\log(k+2))$ actual bits, $T_A=C_A+T_{\mathrm{finish},A}$ and jointly charged peak $S_A$ in one fixed uniform adaptive machine. \textbf{OPEN:} source-consistent, fully charged all-ordinary-input successor ARC-EDGE-90 and cofinal finite-hit route into strict FLMV $t<241/200$, $s<11642479/23232400$; complete-state post-acquisition structural $\Phi$ with independently large initial value and universally bounded decrease; P versus NP. \paragraph{M22-PIN-ERASURE147 (25 October 2026, 09:16 KST): ordinary-pin target-type rigidity with a sharp component-erasure budget.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only for the declared source-preserving pin-and-simplify grammar; independent mathematical review pending. COMPUTATIONAL finite controls.} For every valid clausewise-monotone exact-$(2,2)$ source $F\in\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}$ and arbitrary partial assignment $\rho$, if the ordinarily simplified residual $F\!\restriction\rho$ still lies in $\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}$, it is an unchanged union of \emph{complete original clause--variable incidence components}. Every component met by a pinned variable must disappear with every original clause satisfied already by the pins. Because each pinned source variable can satisfy at most two of its four original clauses, erasing a component with $k_i$ source variables and $m_i=4k_i/3$ clauses requires at least $m_i/2=2k_i/3$ pinned variables. This threshold is \emph{sharp} for the six-variable connected in-target SAT fixture $Y$, which is erased by four pins. In particular, for connected $F\in\mathcal C_\star$, every nonempty pin batch of fewer than $2k/3$ leaves $\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}$; if the connected source is UNSAT, \emph{every} nonempty ordinary pin batch leaves this grammar, since terminal TRUE would imply parent SAT. The whole-original-clause component-closure antecedent was \emph{already proved} by Role~4 COMPONENT-CLOSURE97; the specialized pin-corollary and numerical threshold are the new restricted claim. A charged type-restoring compiler or different algorithm representation is not excluded.
Exhaustive ordinary restrictions on $Y$ and fully paired $H$ each covered $3^6=729$ partial pins, with no source/child semantic discrepancies; $Y$ retains exactly one $\mathcal C_\star$ status (unrestricted root), has $500$ nonterminal out-of-grammar, $184$ FALSE-clause and $44$ TRUE residuals. Original D\"ocker $U$ (198 variables, 264 clauses, connected in-target UNSAT according to its published construction) passed $396$ one-pin and $264$ selected two-pin grammar checks; two genuine ordinary pin stages yielded $12{,}048\to11{,}986\to11{,}920$ serialized source-namespace bits, with neither child in $\mathcal C_\star$. The disconnected $Y\dot\cup H$ positive control returns to valid $\mathcal C_\star$ only \emph{after} an invalid intermediate recursive child. All cited bit counts are representation traffic proxies, not same-machine FLMV time/space measurements. At an adaptive checkpoint preserve $T_A=C_A+T_{\mathrm{finish},A}$ and peak $S_A$; for exhaustive residuals $R_{\mathrm{search}}=k-\log_2\sum_j2^{r_j}$ is \emph{not} mandatory decision work.

\paragraph{M22-DEGREE3-TRANSFER146 (24 October 2026, 16:15 PDT): recovered uniform bounded-occurrence SAT upper bound and typed degree-three bridge.}
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM, restricted upper bound:} Jain, Neoh and Stephan (arXiv:2411.07389, 2024), Theorem~8.3 and Corollary~8.5, give an exact \(O^*(1.1199^{(d-2)n})\) algorithm for CNF-SAT with maximum \(d\) variable occurrences, improving the Peng--Xiao IJCAI 2023 \(O^*(1.1238^{(d-2)n})\) bound. Every canonical M22-NONPAIRED1 input \(F\in\mathcal C_\star\), with \(k\) active source variables, \(m=4k/3\) unique wholly signed proper ternary clauses, \(N=\Theta(k\log(k+2))\) \emph{actual encoded bits}, has \(d=4\), so a known exact \emph{available completion upper bound} is
\[
\boxed{O^*(1.1199^{2k})=O^*(1.25417601^k).}
\]
Against predecessor ONE-SIDED-FACTOR133's \(3^q\operatorname{poly}(N)\) upper budget, this is better when \(q/k>2\log_3(1.1199)=0.20614987976\ldots\), notably for maximal defect \(q=2k/3\). The strategy may adaptively compare both charged upper budgets; neither enumerator is necessary work. \textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted translation:} replace two positive occurrences of each \(x_i\) by fresh \(v_i\) and add \((\neg v_i\vee x_i)\); the new formula is equisatisfiable, with \(2k\) variables, \(7k/3\) clauses and every variable occurring exactly three times. Its mixed-sign binary clauses are \emph{outside} \(\mathcal C_\star\), but eligible for a generic degree-three CNF solver. Ordinary child serialization remains \(\Theta(N)\), not \(N^\alpha\) for fixed \(\alpha<1\). \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL finite controls:} canonical \(Y\) has 20 SAT models; 12-variable transformed \(G(Y)\) has 44 models all projecting correctly. Illustrative source/compiled bit lengths are \(129\to233\); two actual restrictions yield ordinary degree-at-most-three children of 169,174,174,187 bits. These are not physical FLMV machine measurements. The older PIN-DIVISIBILITY141 and restriction no-shrink observations remain prior results. An original in-target connected UNSAT two-level control and a full charged FLMV trace remain uncompleted. \textbf{OPEN:} favorable same-machine all-ordinary-input ARC-EDGE-90 successor and cofinal finite route to strict FLMV \(t<241/200,\ s<11642479/23232400\); independent post-acquisition complete-state lower-bound potential; P versus NP. \paragraph{M22-Q3-PALETTE-FIREWALL144 (24 October 2026, 15:12 PDT): first-three-defect four-color coarsening barrier.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only for the declared six-variable source and its explicit unbounded disconnected \(\mathcal C_\star\) family; independent review pending. COMPUTATIONAL six-variable census.}
Canonical \(P=\{123,124,356,456\}\) and \(Q=\{123,145,256,346\}\) have \(k=6,m=8,q=3\), every source signed degree \((2,2)\), and one paired support. The lex-first proper positive-primal four-coloring \(c=(0,1,2,2,0,1)\) fails all three binary pair partitions: the negatives \(256,145,346\) are respectively monochromatic under \(01|23\), \(02|13\), \(03|12\). \par\noindent\textbf{IDENTITY-SWEEP129 (25 October 2026, LSPV-2): exact source-name union sweep and low-width approximation bridge.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only on validated source-scoped streams; COMPUTATIONAL finite controls; peer review pending.}
For a valid original-source-named OPEN/CLAUSE/CLOSE stream with cut live sets $S_i$ and $W=\max_i|S_i|$, the number of consistent boundary pin pairs on a cut edge $(i,j)$ is $Q(i,j)=2^{|S_i\cup S_j|}$, as established in OVERLAP-CUT128. A new backward event sweep computes each exact $|S_i\cup S_j|$ without materializing any intermediate named live set: reversing CLOSE$(v)$ adds one to the union count and records $v$ as pending, and reversing OPEN$(v)$ subtracts one exactly when $v$ was pending. CLAUSE leaves the count unchanged. Using a $b$-window cyclic shortest-path DP and an $O(b)$-entry pending-name dictionary gives \emph{exact} minimization of $\widehat C(P)=\sum_{(i,j)\in P}2^{|S_i\cup S_j|}$ in $O(Lb\log(b+1))$ deterministic name comparisons and $O(Lb)$ bounded-bit arithmetic, with $O(b)$ value-only numerical/name entries \emph{after separately charged validation}. Dense name stamps give $O(Lb)$ word operations at the cost of $O(V+b)$ working words. Direct predecessor traceback uses additional $O(L)$ entries. Here $L$ is event count, $b$ the maximum block length, and $V$ the number of named variables.

\textbf{New exact global approximation bridge:} If $P_{\times}$ minimizes the older product proxy $C_{\times}(P)=\sum2^{|S_i|+|S_j|}$, and $P_\cup$ minimizes $\widehat C$, then
\[
\widehat C(P_\times)\le C_\times(P_\times)\le C_\times(P_\cup)\le2^W\widehat C(P_\cup).
\]
Thus in the promised $W=o(\log N)$ regime, the faster width-bucket planner of CUT126 and streamed traceback of REPLAY127 lose at most an $N^{o(1)}$ multiplicative factor in the \emph{eager compatible-pair enumeration count}, preserving that particular exponent without paying the exact named-sweep planning cost $L^{3/2+o(1)}$ at $b\asymp\sqrt L$. This does not bound \emph{unavoidable} SAT calls, provide an ordinary all-input normalizer, or give a favorable jointly achieved SAT exponent pair.

A complete standard-library extended experiment, seed 202610250112, checked 560 matched SAT/UNSAT budget cases, 900 random scoped traces, 20 persistent-width controls, 55 brute compatible-pair counts, 80 brute SAT/UNSAT labels and 1460 approximation assertions; 233060 candidate edges examined, zero mismatches. \par\noindent\textbf{CYCLE-AFFINE-SWITCH123: chord-source joins versus Gaussian bypass
(PROVED/EXACT restricted; COMPUTATIONAL tests; ARC-EDGE-90 OPEN).}
For a \emph{validated} multigraph of $B$ local two-CNF-plus-one-parity blocks, each shared Boolean variable belonging to exactly two blocks and representing one labelled edge, let $q=|E|-B+c$ for $c$ connected components. A charged spanning forest and exhaustive assignments to the $q$ off-tree source variables reduce the remaining joins to TREE-AFFINE-JOIN122. Exact word upper bounds are $O(2^qL^2)$ time and $O(L+q)$ working words; indexed bit-machine simulation has ordinary logarithmic overhead. This is classical cycle-cutset conditioning in a source-matched specialization, not a novel FPT dichotomy.

The connected cubic prism $P_r$ with $2r$ vertices and $3r$ edges gives ordinary proper three-CNF parity formulas $F_r^{+}$ and $F_r^{-}$ of $n=3r$ variables, $m=8r$ clauses and identical unsigned supports. The two parity-charge choices yield SAT and UNSAT, respectively. Their edge-labelled cycle rank is $q=r+1$ and affine row rank $2r-1$; deleting any complete vertex parity gadget from the UNSAT source restores SAT, hence any UNSAT clause core has at least $2r$ clauses. Yet a charged canonical recognizer plus parity scan decides both labels in $O(N\operatorname{polylog}N)$ elementary bit work and $O(\log N)$ writable bits; arbitrary validated XOR3 conjunctions are decidable by polynomial Gaussian elimination. The edge variables have $4$ positive and $4$ negative occurrences, \emph{not} the strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ quotas. Thus neither cycle rank nor core span forces arbitrary-machine SAT continuation work. A declared exhaustive chord policy checked $2^{13}=8192$ assignments and 1,572,864 local table rows at $r=12$ UNSAT, while Gaussian used 23 row XORs; these are different uncalibrated logical costs, not comparable FLMV bit metrics. The extended audit completed 335 assertion groups with zero failures across 14 prism sources, 160 small general CNF controls and five binary/Horn controls. Its finite state-adaptive policy chose SCC, Horn, affine or exact fallback without a label oracle; switching costs were counted logically. Complete-state flips, physical bit time and peak live work-bit metrics were \emph{not} measured. Independent mathematical review pending. Verified report:

\par\noindent\textbf{TREE-AFFINE-JOIN122: growing global parity rank on a source-validated tree (PROVED/EXACT restricted; COMPUTATIONAL finite controls; ARC-EDGE-90 OPEN).}
A certified singleton-interface tree of 2CNF-plus-one-parity blocks admits exact bottom-up joining through unary child feasibility messages and at most two local SCC-orbit parity calls per block, in conservative $O(L^2)$ word time and $O(L)$ working words including source, messages, caches and reconstruction. This combines previously known acyclic-join and rank-one parity tools; it does not supply a general SAT algorithm.
A connected canonical ordinary proper-3CNF both-label chain with $b$ parity blocks has $n=7b+5$, $m=12b+8$, and independent decoded affine rank $b$. Its SAT label is the XOR of the $b$ source parity bits compared with one endpoint pin. Each UNSAT subformula must contain a clause from every parity gadget, hence at least $b$ clauses; nevertheless a fully source-validating canonical recognizer decides both labels in $O(N\operatorname{polylog}N)$ elementary bit work and $O(\log N)$ writable bits. This is a restricted algorithmic upper bound, not a contradiction or a lower bound on arbitrary deciders.
The extended standard-library experiment verified 20 canonical growing SAT/UNSAT cases, 108 randomized nonaffine tree controls, 39 parity-gadget deletion controls, 10 independent small global brute checks, 60 unsupported exact fallback controls and other gadget/grammar checks with zero assertions. Its Python abstract counters are not physical FLMV bit-machine resource measurements. Verified standalone:

\par\noindent\textbf{SELECTOR-TRIM121: exact nonempty witness selector and
compiler-size correction (PROVED/EXACT within declared representation;
ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain OPEN).}
For each original proper ternary clause, introduce three selectors and
require their OR, not parity-one or exactly-one.  One implication per
occurrence to the original source literal gives the exact existential
source relation; alternatively conflict clauses forbid selecting
complementary occurrences of any common original variable, permitting
source-consistent reconstruction.  No intra-clause triangle constraints
are necessary.  For strict signed exact-$(2,2)$, $4n=3m$ and
$3m$ complement edges form disjoint $K_{2,2}$ components.  The mixed
formula has $m$ positive ternary selectors and $3m$ negative binary
conflicts on $3m$ selectors.  Binary-to-proper-ternary replacement using
a fresh private auxiliary per edge yields exactly $6m$ target variables
and $7m$ proper ternary clauses.  The older parity-plus-triangle compiler
correctly used $9m$ variables and $16m$ clauses but was not size-optimal.
For the canonical $198/264$ pair, the new ordinary output has $1584$
variables, $1848$ clauses, $133092$ fixed-record bits, against
$321062$ bits for the old output, a $58.54632\%$ reduction, but still
larger than the $15870$-bit original source.  Therefore the inherited
ordinary-child scale is $\lambda=1$, not $1/2$, and there is no
all-input favorable ARC edge.  The named-source implication 2CNF
has a directed acyclic implication graph of path depth at most two;
the source-free negative-conflict 2CNF has depth one.  Both are
individually SAT, while their globally consistent conjunction with
positive selector OR blocks remains NP-complete on the strict input
language.  This does not prove unconditional decision-time hardness.
The fully charged finite source/hybrid audit tested $556$ formulas
($514$ SAT, $42$ UNSAT), with $170$ matching certificate routes,
$383$ exact fallback routes and zero label discrepancies.  Peak
machine bits, actual flips and cumulative bit-time were not measured.

\par\noindent\textbf{SCC-ODD-ORBIT120: exact rank-one parity switch over 2-CNF
(PROVED/EXACT restricted algorithm, COMPUTATIONAL independent controls;
ARC-EDGE-90 OPEN).}
Let $B$ be a source-valid explicit 2-CNF on $n$ variables and $m$
clauses, and let $\bigoplus_{i\in S}x_i=d$ be one global parity row.
Compute implication SCCs and pair each component with its literal
complement. Each pair $P$ has analytical parity weight
$W_P=|\{i\in S:P(i)=P\}|\bmod2$. Any two models of $B$ are connected
by a path of satisfying assignments that flips one paired SCC group
at a time in reverse condensation topological order. Given one $B$
model, a source-paid opposite-unit 2-SAT test for each odd-weight
group decides whether target parity is reachable; on a flexible
group, the same path constructs a verified witness. This exact,
uniform \emph{restricted mixed-language} algorithm uses
$O(n(n+m+1))$ word time and $O(n+m+1)$ peak working words in one
realization. Its bit-cost implementation charges parsing, SCC calls,
failed unit tests, switches and source replay; value flips and
cumulative live bit-time are separate unmeasured coordinates.
The \emph{classification} of every fixed affine-rank rung is already
known from Brakensiek--Gopi--Guruswami's 2-SAT-MOD theorem, recorded
by LSPV-5 FIXED-SYNDROME-FIREWALL113. This run supplies the explicit
SCC-orbit constructive proof, not a novel fixed-rank dichotomy.
The verified compact audit independently classifies $2100$ small
both-label cases and $16$ growing controls with zero mismatches;
a separately instrumented extended audit covers $3520$ small cases.
SELECTOR-SPLIT119 requires $m$ \emph{independent} parity rows, so
repeating this rank-one dispatcher does not furnish an all-input
ordinary-SAT contractor or solve the source-consistent join.

\par\noindent\textbf{SELECTOR-SPLIT119: source-consistent affine/2-CNF compilation firewall
(PROVED/EXACT for the declared compiler; joint all-input ARC resource improvement OPEN).}
For every proper 3-CNF $F$ with $m$ clauses and $n$ source
variables, create a selector $y_{ij}$ for each of the $3m$
literal occurrences. The affine part
$\bigwedge_i(y_{i1}\oplus y_{i2}\oplus y_{i3}=1)$
has $m$ independent equations; the bijunctive part uses
three at-most-one clauses per triple plus one source implication
$y_{ij}\Rightarrow\ell_{ij}$ per occurrence, for exactly $6m$
binary clauses on $3m+n$ Boolean variables. The exact source-coherent
equivalence is
\[
  F\in\mathrm{SAT}\iff A_F\wedge B_F\in\mathrm{SAT}.
\]
Both $A_F$ and $B_F$ are \emph{individually satisfiable for every
input $F$}. To obtain actual \emph{ordinary SAT} children,
compile each XOR3 parity row into four proper ternary clauses
and each binary $(a\vee b)$ into the two proper ternaries
$(a\vee b\vee z)\wedge(a\vee b\vee\neg z)$ with private $z$.
The full ordinary 3-CNF has $16m$ clauses and $9m+n$ variables,
and partitions into $10m$ independently satisfiable
$O(\log(n+m))$-bit named child groups (or $16m$ singleton clauses).
Therefore child SAT labels alone do not decide the parent. This
is a firewall on label-only splitting, \emph{not} on arbitrary
source-aware algorithms or existential ARC closure.
For signed exact-$(2,2)$, the original-source variables can be
eliminated from the bijunctive part, leaving a $K_{2,2}$ per
source variable, $4n=3m$ opposite-sign edges, $3m$ clause
triangle edges, and $6m$ binary clauses on $3m$ selectors,
each of degree four. This is equivalent to the known
independent-transversal formulation, not a new NP-completeness theorem.
Under the declared bit-record encoding, the canonical $198/264$
benchmark has input $N=15{,}870$ bits but materialized children
totaling $103{,}558$ output bits in the \emph{mixed-language} form.
The fully ordinary proper-3CNF source contains $4{,}224$ clauses,
$2{,}574$ variables and $321{,}062$ written bits. Mandatory one-pass input reads
plus writes cost at least $119{,}428$ bit operations before control;
eager sequential storage needs at least $85{,}572$ writable child bits.
A fully lazy alternative must charge child bit generation on demand.
Actual state flips and bit-time were not measured.
Executed tests: 162 small original-source/selector comparisons,
136 SAT and 26 UNSAT, 5350 checks with zero mismatches;
the named hybrid uses a matching certificate on 47 inputs
and an exact exponential fallback on 115. The $198/264$ SAT
neighbor's witness was replayed; UNSAT status for its paired source
was \emph{imported} from DOECKER-CORE-CERT61 and not newly proved.
The user-facing complete execution archive and compact GitHub
reproducer differ in coverage and are labeled accordingly.
\textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 remains OPEN; P versus NP remains OPEN.}

\par\noindent\textbf{FALLBACK-PADDING118: charged black-box parent-fallback firewall
(PROVED/EXACT within declared conditional compiler model; ARC-EDGE-90 OPEN).}
Let $\mathcal C$ be polynomial-time recognizable with infinitely many
growing ordinary SAT sources outside it.  Consider only a black-box
hybrid $H[A]$ which uses certified polynomial specialists on
$\mathcal C$ and otherwise calls the supplied guest $A$ on the
\emph{unchanged full $N$-bit parent}, awaiting its answer.
Under the counterfactual $P=NP$, choose any fixed uniform exact
polynomial SAT decider $D$ with time exponent $d$ and space exponent
$s$. For each sufficiently large fixed integer $K>d$, form the
total uniform exact guest $A_K$ by running at least $N^K$ charged
counter steps in $O(K\log N)$ mutable bits and then executing $D$.
The single machine $A_K$ has simultaneous exponents
$(K,\max\{s,0\})$ with $o(1)$ slack, yet
\[
 \max_{|F|=N}T_{H[A_K]}(F)\ge N^K
\]
along an unbounded sequence of lengths outside $\mathcal C$.
Thus this \emph{specified} black-box recognizer with an unmodified
parent-scale fallback cannot uniformly guarantee $K-\varepsilon$
time-exponent contraction over all supplied guests.
The result does \emph{not} refute existential closure of the achievable
region $E$, because under $P=NP$ the alternative faster machine $D$
exists; it also does not exclude code-aware or other adaptive
all-input algorithms, fractional source-consistent children or
independent machine-existence theorems.

The executed finite adaptive controls acquire source parameters, try
2-SAT SCC, Horn, dual-Horn and grouped-XOR Gaussian elimination,
and charge failed recognition and full-parent exact DPLL fallback.
Of 54 cases (45 SAT, nine UNSAT), all independent truth-table
labels agree: policy routes 2/5/5/2/40 respectively, with zero
failures. Relative to a specified padded baseline, the hybrid
avoids 1,323,244 of 6,349,564 billed dummy bit-events on these
inputs. These Python events are not a physical FLMV
time/space simulation, and the growing connected UNSAT controls
retain a fixed contradictory core. Separate accumulator value-flips
are not necessary work: unchanged writes remain charged.
The prior CHILD-SCALE-PREFIX117 hereditary-width denominator
counterexample also remains valid:
$w=o(\log N)$ and $L\le N^{\lambda+o(1)}$ with
$w_{\rm child}\le w+O(1)$ do not imply
$w_{\rm child}=o(\log L)$ without a \emph{lower} descendant
length bound or explicit child-promise validation.
The complete HEREDITARY-SCOPED10 generator is still unrecovered.
The retired cosine product endpoint is not used.
\textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain OPEN.}

\par\noindent\textbf{TORUS-EXACT22-FRONTIER116, strict signed source-frontier firewall
(PROVED/EXACT original-clause model; general ARC closure OPEN).}
A new family improves the earlier unbounded-occurrence complete-graph
frontier control to \emph{proper, connected, signed exact-$(2,2)$}
3-CNFs. For each multiple of three $h\ge6$ there are opposite-label
sources with identical unsigned supports, two sign changes,
\[
 n=3h^2+198,\quad m=4h^2+264,\quad
 N=(4m+2)\lceil\log_2(64(n+m+1))\rceil.
\]
Every source has exactly two positive and two negative occurrences.
For \emph{any} ordering retaining the original toroidal grid clauses
and shared source identifiers, at the balanced grid-edge cut
\[
 w\ge\lceil h/2\rceil
 =\Omega(\sqrt{N/\log N}).
\]
Proof: fewer than $h/2$ mixed vertices leave more than $h/2$
intact rows and columns; all their $2h(h-b)>h^2$ edges have the
same color, contradicting the balanced bipartition of $2h^2$ edges.
Both labels remain easy under canonical source recognition:
$O(N\,\operatorname{polylog}N)$ time and $O(\log N)$
work bits on the \emph{recognized} family, with exact exponential
fallback on unsupported inputs. Neither Hall clause matching
($m>n$) nor complementary-pivot contraction can initially terminate.
The original fixed 254-clause UNSAT core persists, so this is
\emph{not} hardness amplification and does not forbid source-aware
rewrites or another all-input contraction.
Six sizes through $h=192$ ($N=14{,}181{,}168$ bits, width bound $96$)
and 164 independently brute-forced small controls
(126 SAT, 38 UNSAT) passed; the source-local 537-node UNSAT
certificate and 252-clause six-module coverage were independently
rechecked. P versus NP remains OPEN.

\par\noindent\textbf{FLMV-CURVE-FRONTIER115, contraction source-tranche (PROVED/EXACT only in declared models; all-input SAT closure OPEN).}
Re-examining the original FLMV Theorem~3.1 and Corollaries~3.2/5.8
repairs MULTICHANNEL1's old endpoint: the purported universal
$t+s<2\cos(\pi/7)$ SAT landing is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}.
The correct forbidden curve is
\[
\beta(c)=\tfrac12\left((c+2)/c^2-c\right),
\qquad \beta(241/200)=\frac{11642479}{23232400}>\tfrac12.
\]
For a genuinely all-input, fully charged, self-contained recursive
SAT contractor with finite channels
$(\eta_j,\theta_j)$, $\theta_j<1$, local work exponent $r$,
and local/child peak exponent $\rho$, the exact
conditional closure bound is
\[
C=\max\{r,\max_j\eta_j/(1-\theta_j)\},\qquad
\rho=\max\{\sigma,\max_j\theta_j\}.
\]
If $C>1$ and $\rho<\beta(C)$, the asserted contractor contradicts
the known FLMV endpoint; therefore such a universal contractor
cannot satisfy all those premises.
This is a strict \emph{firewall}, not a construction of the
missing all-input machine or of existential closure of the jointly
achievable region $E$.

An independent connected proper 3CNF both-label family, consisting
of every edge clause $(x_i\vee x_j\vee z_{ij})$ on $K_h$ and one
constant-size contradictory/satisfiable control gadget, has
$N=\Theta(h^2\log h)$ and, in \emph{every} unchanged-clause scoped
ordering, live frontier
\[
w\ge\min\{u:\tbinom u2\ge\lfloor\tbinom h2/2\rfloor\}
=\Omega\!\left(\sqrt{N/\log N}\right).
\]
At $h=256$ the certified lower bound is $182$, whereas
$\log_2N\simeq21.518$. Yet a uniform charged source recognizer
directly decides both labels in polynomial time; its general exact
fallback is exponential. This refutes clause \emph{reordering}
as universal sublog-width normalization and raw width as a time
lower bound, without excluding symbolic preprocessing or any other
all-input ARC successor. Extended audits passed 974 frontier
checks and 83 exact-rational/finite-route checks, no failures.
The unproved link remains cofinal finite charged entry into a
model-matched forbidden box, with one uniform SAT machine and
simultaneous resources at each node. Independent review pending.

\par\noindent\textbf{Earlier PROOF-SUPPORT73: paid single-pass UNSAT proof provenance, and a strict exact-$(2,2)$ prime-interface firewall (PROVED/EXACT restricted, COMPUTATIONAL scaling; independent review pending).}
For a source-certified local component $H_j(Y_j,U_j)$, a checked
source-labelled DPLL tree under assumptions $\alpha$ can annotate unit
implications and source conflicts by the assumptions and branch choices
on which they depend. Eliminating each split token only after both child
refutations yields a sufficient original assumption subset
$\beta\subseteq\alpha$, and the sound learned no-good
$D_\beta=\bigvee_{(v=b)\in\beta}(v\ne b)$.
One dependency pass plus one independent replay suffices, polynomial in
the \emph{explicit certificate size}, not necessarily in original input
size. Original proof acquisition, source reads, annotation, switching,
failed attempts, full witness replay and peak live space remain charged.
This is the known proof-core principle in a newly measured adaptive
policy, not a general SAT shortcut.

On a connected proper (but \emph{not} signed exact-$(2,2)$)
both-label family of guarded contradictory XOR chains with $r=6$ and
$k$ irrelevant boundary sources, the specified local UNSAT DPLL proof
has 127 nodes. At $k=4,16,32,64$, repeated proof-replay minimization
uses $75{,}860$, $385{,}772$, $995{,}212$, $2{,}886{,}860$
source-clause scans, including a rejected deletion attempt; the
provenance pass and one checker use respectively
$37{,}608$, $48{,}120$, $62{,}136$, $90{,}168$ scans and learn the
same unit. This only compares named proof-processing policies: a direct
affine recognizer may avoid the XOR refutation entirely.

On the original strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ $198/264$ benchmark,
each of the six learned local no-goods is prime over its actual source
interface: all 15 single-assumption deletions have replayed SAT local
witnesses, and provenance extracts no smaller support. A uniform
128-node direct probe solves the SAT neighbor at node 104; the original
UNSAT input needs the prior exact boundary continuation.
The tested adaptive policy uses 18 local calls/725 local nodes, worse
than LAZY-BOUNDARY71's 16/688, so no dominance claim is made.
Three independently run experiment modes pass 1,432 recorded assertion
groups with zero failures, including 640 independent small source
restrictions, 12 renaming/polarity controls, and a damaged-certificate
rejection. All-decider representation/decision coverage and any
superpolynomial charged potential remain \OPEN{}; $P=NP$ and
$P\ne NP$ both remain unresolved.

\par\noindent\textbf{Prior CORE-REPLAY72: checked proof-core weakening and state-adaptive exact master. PROVED/EXACT restricted; COMPUTATIONAL controls; P versus NP OPEN.}
For a source-verified decomposition
\[
H=G(Y)\land\bigwedge_j H_j(Y_j,U_j)\subseteq F
\]
with disjoint private sources, a checked DPLL refutation of
$H_j$ under assumptions $\alpha$ can be replayed under
$\beta\subseteq\alpha$. If the independent checker accepts the
\emph{same} proof under $\beta$, the stronger no-good
$D_\beta=\bigvee_{(v=b)\in\beta}(v\ne b)$
is a source-entailment. At most $|Y_j|$ attempted assumption deletions
are made, with all failed proof replays and source access charged.
The exact master solves $G\wedge\bigwedge_j D_{\beta_j}$, rather than
enumerating all boundary rows. An UNSAT master implies parent UNSAT
through source-checked subformula inclusion; SAT requires reconstruction
and full original-source clause replay. A uniform total policy uses a
bounded initial direct DPLL probe and an exact original-source fallback.
Proof size and boundary support are acquired state parameters, not
labels or runtime-defined invariants. A 16-node observed proof cutoff
skips costly attempts.

On a new \emph{connected, proper three-literal} opposite-label family
(not signed exact-$(2,2)$) with $n=2k+5,m=2k+8$, the earlier
full-row policy uses exactly $2^k$ local UNSAT calls whereas proof
replay learns one unit clause from one local call, with two exact
master calls and $O(k^2)$ charged word work; the supplied dense bit-mask solver stores $O(k^2)$ mask bits plus $O(k)$ source records, while a sparse-clause realization may use $O(k)$ working words.
Checked old-call values were $2,8,32,128,512$ at
$k=1,3,5,7,9$, compared with $1$ new local call each; the
new policy also ran with one call at $k=16,32$.
All SAT controls used one new call. A randomized source-coherent
audit covered 140 six-variable instances (51 SAT, 89 UNSAT);
125 of 253 attempted assumption deletions succeeded, with
independently replayed source proofs.

On the connected strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ benchmark U198/264,
all six local refutations exceeded the cutoff; no assumptions
could be deleted by the tested rule. It used 18 local calls and
725 local nodes, \emph{worse} than LAZY-BOUNDARY71's 16 calls and
688 nodes. The matching SAT neighbor V is instead solved by the
uniform initial 128-node probe at node 104. This is
\emph{not} a universal improvement or an unrestricted time lower
bound. General proof memory, solver time, separator acquisition,
and the all-decider representation-versus-computation bridge remain
\OPEN{}. Both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$ remain OPEN.

\par\noindent\textbf{Prior LAZY-BOUNDARY71: charged state-adaptive source-interface no-good learning, PROVED/EXACT restricted, independently checked finite evidence, external review pending.}
For a source-verified clause subformula
\[
H=G(Y)\land\bigwedge_{j=1}^{J}H_j(Y_j,U_j)\subseteq F,
\qquad U_i\cap U_j=\varnothing\ \ (i\ne j),\quad Y_j\subseteq Y,
\]
an exact local UNSAT decision under $\alpha|_{Y_j}$ proves the no-good
$D_{j,\alpha}=\bigvee_{y\in Y_j}(y\ne\alpha(y))$.
It may be learned without materializing every positive interface row.
All learned clauses are source-entailments; an UNSAT master proves the original
parent UNSAT even if the verified subformula omitted cross-clauses.
A positive abstract witness must be reconstructed and replayed against
\emph{all} of $F$ before SAT is accepted.
With source-closed positive-result caching and permanent negative blocking,
at most $\sum_j2^{|Y_j|}$ distinct local solver calls occur in this
declared algorithm, although the master may still perform $2^{|Y|}$
candidate tests per refinement and local solves can be expensive.
This is a constructive source-coherent call bound, not an arbitrary-decider
time lower bound.

On the previously source-certified connected strict signed exact-$(2,2)$
$198$-variable/$264$-clause UNSAT $U$ and its two-sign SAT neighbor $V$,
both initial Hall and tautological-pivot shortcuts fail. The previous
eager six-module projection used $36$ local calls/$1038$ nodes/$86856$
clause scans on $U$. The new lazy positive-cache policy used
$16$ calls/$688$ nodes/$62878$ scans; no cache required
$21$ calls/$764$ nodes/$67708$ scans. Six checked negative
no-good clauses refute the six-variable master, with $537$
independently checked UNSAT proof nodes and \emph{no}
enumeration of the $30$ positive boundary rows.
A total exact policy first probes the entire original input for $128$
nodes: $V$ produces a replayed SAT witness at node $104$,
whereas $U$ exhausts the probe, switches to the lazy interface,
and closes in $128+688+3=819$ charged search nodes, plus source
parsing, candidate tests, cache operation costs, proof output
and checking. A support-first order makes only $15$ calls
but \emph{more} clause scans ($63190$ versus $62878$).
Thus even within a finite hybrid menu there is no scalar best
choice without declaring the resource. Generic complete-separator
tests gave $58$ SAT and $82$ UNSAT over $140$ independently brute-forced
small formulas; $145$ negative local proof trees were checked.
All $335$ recorded assertions passed. The full benchmark has ten
cross-module clauses omitted only from its UNSAT-preserving subformula;
the abstract SAT branch is forbidden from accepting without full replay.

\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM:} counterexample-guided abstraction refinement,

\par\noindent\textbf{Latest HALL-SWITCH70: source-coherent Hall witness after paid pair contraction (PROVED/EXACT in a restricted certificate model; external review pending).}
In strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ proper unique 3CNF, $4n=3m$, so the original clauses cannot be injectively matched to $n$ sources. After certified complementary-pair contraction, $p$ pairs replace $2p$ ternaries by $p$ binaries and leave $m'=m-p$ reduced clauses pointwise equivalent to the original source. A bipartite matching saturating all $m'$ reduced clause nodes with \emph{distinct original source variables} yields an exact SAT witness by satisfying the corresponding matched literal in each clause; all original clauses are then replayed. Sorting, pair recognition, actual failed Hopcroft--Karp searches, switching and witness verification take
\[
O(m\log(m+2)+(n+m)\sqrt{n+m})
\]
addressed-word time and $O(n+m)$ peak words for the certificate step. Hall failure is not an UNSAT determination. The theorem applies to a recognized matching certificate, not to every possible SAT decider.

The new connected shifted-permutation strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ family has $n=6q,m=8q,p=2q,\delta=4q$. For fixed-point-free socket permutations, all $2q$ eligible residual pivots fail the earlier CANCEL-JOIN69 \emph{tautological} test, yet a $6q$-clause saturating Hall matching yields an immediate SAT witness with no selector enumeration. On unshifted modules, the adaptive policy instead cancels the $4q$ ternaries and switches to SCC. The q=256 shifted audit checked $1{,}536$ variables, $2{,}048$ original clauses, $1{,}024$ unmatched ternaries, 0 taut pivots, 1,536 source-distinct clause matches, 27,670 actual matching edge scans and original-source witness replay. An additional true structural counterexample combines either member of the earlier $198$-variable opposite-label pair with 66 disjoint PAIRPATH73 gadgets: it has $m'=n=594$ but 264 core clauses see only 198 source variables, so it fails Hall on both labels. The UNSAT label of the D\"ocker core is \emph{imported} from DOECKER-CORE-CERT61 rather than re-proved in this run. Overall finite evidence: 720 assertion-accounted checks, 0 failures, including separate small SAT/UNSAT fallback controls; independent peer review pending.

\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} a required exponential continuation merely because $\delta$ is large, the initial tautological-pivot test fails, or a complete backward selector service has exponentially many candidates. \textbf{OPEN:} the proposed all-decider charged parameterized potential, structural-growth and representation/decision bridging theorems.
