\par\noindent\textbf{CANCEL-JOIN69: Charged state-adaptive source-coherent pivot cancellation in strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ proper 3CNF (PROVED/EXACT restricted; independent peer review pending).}
After unique complementary-pivot contraction $F\equiv B\land T$,
original signed-source quotas impose
\[
t_x+2b_x^++u_x^+=2,\qquad t_x+2b_x^-+u_x^-=2.
\]
If source $x$ pivots exactly once, then $b_x^\pm=0$ and $u_x^\pm=1$,
so the two remaining ternaries $(x\vee A),(\neg x\vee D)$ admit the
\emph{known} Davis--Putnam existential projection
$\exists x[(x\vee A)(\neg x\vee D)]\equiv(A\vee D)$.
When the resolvent contains a complementary pair, it is tautological:
both source clauses can be deleted and $x$ reconstructed from an explicit
mate, with full original-source witness replay. This is a new \emph{charged
application} in the current strict signed-source calculus, not a newly
invented general resolution rule. Unlike SCC-ISOLATION68's idle binary-SCC
prepass, it acts directly on unmatched ternaries.

A cyclic connected strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ family $C_q$ has
$n=6q,m=8q$, exactly $2q$ complementary pairs, initial unmatched
defect $\delta=4q$ and minimum \emph{postpair} strong 2CNF deletion
backdoor $h=q$. Exactly $2q$ local tautological pivot eliminations remove
all $4q$ unmatched clauses, leaving $2q$ binary clauses; choosing
all $b_i=c_i=1$ and inverse-tracing $x_i=y_i=\neg z_i$ provides a
checkable satisfying original-source witness. Parsing, forced pairing,
failed/successful mate checks, live incidence updates, mode switching,
SCC and complete replay take $O(m\log(m+2)+n)$ addressed-word time and
$O(n+m)$ peak live words on this recognized family. A \emph{restricted}
nonpruning fixed-backdoor enumerator has $2^q$ leaves, but this gives
no arbitrary-algorithm lower bound. A $q=2$ two-negative-socket hostile
swap remains connected, unique-clause, and balanced, while only two of
four pivot eliminations remain tautological, requiring bounded non-taut
resolution plus exact DPLL. The source quota formula must be recomputed
from actual live incidences after the first projection.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 13,287 assertion-accounted checks and zero
failures, with q values through $512$ ($n=3072,m=4096$,
$N=311334$ encoded bits, $\delta=2048$ eliminated). Original/projection
exhaustive checks were restricted to $n\le12$. The separately certified
D\"ocker opposite-label $198$-variable/$264$-clause pair is not simplified
by this prepass: $p=0,\delta=264$ on both labels. Numerical HiGHS reports
infeasible/feasible respectively, but no new independently checkable UNSAT
proof was produced. Disjoint additions of the easy cyclic family preserve
the hard residual while eliminating the easy component. Generic
unsupported inputs retain total exact DPLL fallback with no polynomial
claim. \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} a mandatory every-input exponential
runtime lower bound derived only from initial unmatched defect,
strong backdoor size, selected occurrence count or optional rich service.

The three universal obligations remain \OPEN{}: a noncircular
decision-only progress bound against \emph{every} uniform deterministic exact
SAT decider, its independently justified growing parameter on suitable
algorithm-dependent inputs, and fully charged all-method coverage.

\par\noindent\textbf{SCC-ISOLATION68: PROVED/EXACT in certified strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ pivot-contraction calculus; independent review pending.}
Complementary-pivot pairs contract source-exactly to binary clauses $B$,
leaving unmatched ternaries $T$. Original occurrence conservation is
\[
 t_x+2b_x^++u_x^+=2,\qquad t_x+2b_x^-+u_x^-=2.
\]
Every source literal belonging to a nontrivial implication SCC of $B$
must occur with both signs in $B$, hence its source variable has
$u_x^+=u_x^-=0$ and is absent from $T$. Therefore a paid SCC-equivalence
prepass over \emph{only these} binary pivot contractions can never shorten
the remaining ternaries. This is a source-grammar-specific
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED transfer} of the QUIET-EQUIV67 shortcut;
QUIET-EQUIV67 remains a valid polynomial mixed-width result under its
different hypotheses. No SCC operation after a partial source assignment
is ruled out. The binary graph has degree at most two; PAIRPATH73's
existing exact existential path/cycle projection yields a matching of
binary clauses over \emph{distinct ternary boundary source pairs},
which cannot be discarded merely because $B$ forces no individual literal.
An additional source-count corollary rules out renamable Horn and
renamable dual-Horn forms \emph{before} source transformation because
each sign has $2n>m=4n/3$ occurrences.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 265 assertion groups, zero failures. Strict
paired-cycle, affine-quartet and six-variable connected projected-path
controls were checked; the paid adaptive policy selected path projection,
source-coherent branch/SCC, affine reconstruction or an exact fallback
on equal-size 240-variable, 320-clause SAT/UNSAT controls containing the
authenticated 198-variable D\"ocker opposite-label pair. The independent
HiGHS experiment returned SAT and UNSAT respectively, but did not produce
a new formal UNSAT refutation; previous DOECKER-CORE-CERT61 remains the
certificate anchor. Skipping the idle pre-SCC pass saves only a redundant
linear implication scan, not a new worst-case exponent. The complete
all-decider rich-service/expenditure implication and the conditional
$T_A(F)\ge2^{a r_A(F)}/N^b$ target remain \OPEN{}.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted (QUIET-EQUIV67, peer review pending).}
A paid SCC-equivalence quotient can collapse genuine ternary clauses
\emph{without any initial unit or binary-backbone forced source literal}.
For $q\ge2$, the connected mixed-width opposite-label family
$F_q^\pm$ has $n=3q$, $m=7q-2$, $q$ variable-disjoint ternaries,
and raw syntactic strong 2-CNF backdoor $h=q$.
The binary backbone equates all $3q$ source variables yet has precisely
two satisfying assignments and no forced literal.
All-positive ternaries give SAT; changing only the last ternary to
all-negative gives UNSAT with identical unsigned supports and encoded length.
One charged SCC pass on the binary backbone merges every positive source
literal into one class and every negative source literal into another;
each ternary becomes a unit. A second SCC decides both labels with
$25q-8$ implication arcs total, $O(N/w)=O(q)$ addressed-word work,
$O(q)$ live logical words, and zero search branches, including
recognition and original-source SAT witness replay.
By contrast a \emph{specifically defined} complete strong-backdoor
enumerator uses exactly $2^q$ child calls on either label.
This comparison constrains that policy alone, not arbitrary DPLL or
other uniform exact SAT algorithms.
The mixed-width family is \emph{not} proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ 3CNF.
The strict 198-variable, 264-clause opposite-label D\"ocker source
pair and saturated affine-quartet controls are not solved by this
binary-SCC recognizer; an exact fallback is retained. The original
UNSAT label relies on the earlier DOECKER-CORE-CERT61 rather than
a newly regenerated proof.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 32 assertion-accounted case groups, zero failures;
scalable matched SAT/UNSAT inputs through $q=512$ and strict-source
non-transfer controls. This falsifies an initial raw backdoor size
as a universal exponential runtime potential, but it does \emph{not}

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted (ADAPTIVE-BIDIR65, independent review pending).}
Forward and reverse SAT reasoning can change source-coherent representations

components, complete parity quartets, graph-incidence parity equations,

\[
n=3q+6,\qquad m=8q+7,\qquad k=8q+1,\qquad
N=(32q+30)\lceil\log_2(64(n+m+1))\rceil,

the paired formulas differ in exactly one parity charge and have identical

Each parity source occurs in two equation supports, permitting a linear-time
spanning-forest assignment/contradiction check. The whole adaptive policy
uses $O(N)$ addressed-word time, $O(N)$ live logical words and zero branches
on this recognized family, including recognition and source replay.
The instances are \emph{not} strict signed exact-$(2,2)$: each parity-edge
source has four positive and four negative CNF occurrences.
The SAT trajectory switches binary backbone $\to$ components $\to$
graph parity $\to$ Horn $\to$ SCC; UNSAT ends at graph parity.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 90 both-label policy runs, 180 independently

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted (REPAIR-GAP64; independent review pending).}
For proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ 3CNF, an explicit integer polarity-selector
model has optimum $\mu(F)$, the minimal number of original source variables
selected in both signs by a capacity selector.  Its natural linear relaxation
always has the \emph{same zero optimum} on SAT and UNSAT sources: assign each
occurrence selector $1/3$, each sign-usage indicator $1/2$ and every
mixed-source penalty $0$.  Thus the relaxed model is label-blind.
Furthermore $\mu(F\sqcup G)=\mu(F)+\mu(G)$ for source-disjoint unions; by
D\"ocker's strict-monotone NP-completeness, for \emph{every fixed} $B\ge0$
testing $\mu(F)\le B$ remains NP-complete (reduce $H$ to $B+1$ renamed copies).
These are exact statements about an optional integer program and its LP,
not about work necessary for every SAT machine.

The certified opposite-label 198/264 pair $U,V$ satisfies
$\mu(U)=1$, $\mu(V)=0$, relying on the separately checked
DOECKER-CORE-CERT61 UNSAT certificate and the supplied source-replayed
two-sign-swap SAT witness.  The growing pairs $U^{\sqcup p},V^{\sqcup p}$
have $\mu=p$ versus $0$ and identical fractional objective $0$,
yet a paid canonical-template recognizer decides the entire
recognized block-major family in $O(n+m)$ addressed word operations.
The template family is disconnected; the recognizer is not a general SAT
solver and unsupported inputs need an exact fallback.  The local
stdlib audit checked fifteen matched cases, 36 exhaustive small valid cases
and 9,170 literal-selection trials, general 3CNF UNSAT and 2-SAT/SCC controls,
with zero failed assertions.  The source UNSAT certificate was imported rather
than rerun in this audit.
\textbf{OPEN:} a uniformly constructible all-input polynomial SAT algorithm,
or an all-total-uniform-exact-decider superpolynomial lower bound robust to
source scans, alternative representations, and direct decision.  Neither
$P=NP$ nor $P\ne NP$ is proved.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted (POLARITY-REPAIR63, independent review pending).}
For signed proper exact-$(2,2)$ source 3CNF with $4n=3m$, a clause-to-two-source-clones bipartite Hall matching always saturates all $m$ original clauses; it deliberately ignores selected polarity and therefore succeeds on source-certified UNSAT inputs as well. Let $\mu(F)$ be the minimum number of source variables selected with both polarities in a capacity-two one-occurrence-per-clause selector. If $d_{\rm SAT}(F)$ is minimum swaps of a positive and negative occurrence of the \emph{same} source variable needed to make an \emph{edited} source SAT, then
\[
\boxed{\mu(F)=d_{\rm SAT}(F)\le \lfloor m/2\rfloor=2n/3.}
\]
The proof maps a mixed selected variable to one legal sign swap with an unused positive occurrence, and pulls a satisfying selector backward from any edited SAT formula. Thus $\mu=0$ iff original SAT, so computing the optimum is not supplied by the polynomial Hall matching. The constructive \emph{SAT-edit} algorithm charges $O(N/w+n+m)$ reads, $O(m\sqrt{n+m})$ matching, $O(n+m)$ source edits/replay, $O(N)$ output bits and $O(n+m)$ live words. This edit intentionally changes the SAT label and does not solve unchanged SAT.
On the real D\"ocker source $U$ ($n=198,m=264,N=15870$) and Role 4 two-sign SAT neighbor $V$, the prior certified UNSAT proof of $U$ plus direct $V$ witness replay yields $\mu(U)=1$, $\mu(V)=0$. A deterministic unrestricted Hall matching on $U$ is nonoptimal, with 42 mixed original source variables and a 42-swap SAT edit. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} complete original source/sign checks and 300 small positive brute-force controls, zero failed assertions; the earlier UNSAT proof is imported, not rerun. \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} an unsigned Hall-capacity match, large semantic sign-repair distance, or an expensive chosen representation establishes necessary superpolynomial SAT continuation. \textbf{OPEN:} all-decider lower-bound or general uniform polynomial algorithm, and both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$. Verified standalone:

\section{LSPV-2 (backward algorithms and charged normalization)}

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted (DUALPAIR61; independent review pending).}
For a proper unique signed exact-$(2,2)$ original-source 3CNF, canonical opposite-monotone support pairing has the globally optimal defect
$t_{\min}=m-2\sum_S\min(p_S,q_S)$ within the explicit
positive/negative-support extraction calculus. Exact $(2,2)$ occurrence
counts guarantee every variable belongs to at most two extracted supports;
signed source counts and the output certificate are constructed and checked
in $O(m\log(m+2))$ word time and $O(n+m)$ words. The defect is even
because $m=4n/3$ is divisible by four, \emph{not} because the input
necessarily has equally many monotone positive and negative clauses.
Two valid source-only SAT families have equal $n=3q$, $m=4q$, equal encoded
length $N$, and opposite parameter extremes:
$(t_{\min},\delta)=(0,4q)$ for prism paired-NAE supports and
$(4q,0)$ for two pivot implication cycles. Both have linear-time
direct witnesses. This \textbf{FALSIFIES/RETIRES} any unconditional
time-cost inference from either optional defect parameter alone.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 1,375 checks, zero failures; 60 random strict
signed-source controls, 863 small two-pin extension checks and two
non-promise both-label controls. A universal two-pin extension theorem
is only \textbf{CANDIDATE/OPEN}; 198/264 genuine strict signed-regular
UNSAT source and SAT mutant remain historical DOCKER-QUOTIENT59 anchors
and were not rerun. \textbf{OPEN:} universal all-decider acquisition,
representation or continuation cost, and both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted (BLOSSOM-FACTOR60; independent review pending):}
A source-exact \emph{nonbipartite} interval-factor continuation now
completes the implementational gap left by WITNESS-DEFECT58.
For every free original source edge $e=uv$, a weighted-matching gadget
has two required endpoint stubs and $U_v$ slots at each support vertex,
of which $L_v$ are required.  An auxiliary edge has weight equal to
the number of its required endpoints.  A feasible source-coherent factor
exists iff an integer-weight blossom maximum matching attains the
total weight $|R|$, with both implications proved by matching stub
pairs versus matching both stubs into endpoint slots.
Canonical greedy pairing of positive/negative source triple supports,
using each source variable in at most two selected supports, leaves
$t$ true source defects and gives an exact algorithm for every
proper CNF of width at most three in
\[
O\bigl(N/w+m\log(m+2)+3^t(n+m)^3\bigr)
\]
word operations.  This is a charged \emph{upper} bound with original
source reconstruction, not a lower bound.  \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:}
2,238 assertion groups, zero failures; same-size SAT/UNSAT K4 and
prism controls; a genuinely global matching-shortfall $2<3$
despite nonempty local degree intervals; 140 general proper
CNF controls; nonbipartite supports through 147 original variables.
The six-defect augmentations are not globally strict signed-$(2,2)$.
\textbf{OPEN:} all-input polynomial small-defect normalization,
D\"ocker's 198-variable strict-monotone UNSAT source audit,
independent peer review, a universal mandatory-cost bridge,
$P=NP$, and $P\ne NP$.  The paired-support defect $t$ is distinct
from BOUNDARY-EXCEPTIONS59's pivot-defect $\delta$.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted (BOUNDARY-EXCEPTIONS59, independent review pending):}
For proper signed exact-$(2^+,2^-)$ 3CNF, PAIRPATH73 contracts $p$
forced complementary clause pairs and projects all internal binary paths
and cycles. Set $\delta=m-2p$, and let $U$ be the source-variable
boundary of the $\delta$ unmatched genuine ternaries. The projected
$P\le3\delta/4$ binary clauses have disjoint variable supports, each
endpoint appearing twice with opposite polarity in the unmatched ternaries.
Exact signed occurrence conservation sharpens the PAIRPATH bound to
\[
|U|=3\delta/2-a\le3\delta/2,
\]
where $a$ counts boundary variables occurring four times in the ternaries.
Every satisfiable projected kernel has a satisfying assignment with
exactly one TRUE \emph{signed literal} per projected binary: changing
a second TRUE endpoint to FALSE only turns its negative-polarity
ternary appearances TRUE. This preserves the one-bit SAT label,
not all original source assignments.
Choosing one independent fair bit for each such binary and each other
boundary variable makes every ternary bad event either impossible
or probability $1/8$. A bad event conflicts with at most six others,
because each of its three failure requirements has at most two
opposite source occurrences. Therefore
\[
\Pr[\mathrm{no\ ternary\ fails}]
\ge\begin{cases}1-\delta/8,&\delta<8,\\1/64,&\delta=8.\end{cases}
\]
In particular every source-valid $\delta\le8$ instance is SAT,
and any UNSAT source has even defect $\delta\ge10$. This strengthens
PAIRPATH73's former $\delta+2P<8$ sufficient-SAT criterion.
A source-checkable optimized normalizer uses
$O(m\log(m+2))$ word work and $O(n+m)$ reusable words;
optional witness recovery enumerates at most
$2^{|U|-P}\le4096$ boundary samples at $\delta\le8$,
uses SCC to lift the source assignment, and replays all original clauses.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 360 random balanced sources,
144 valid abstract kernels, 840 exact boundary projection checks,
87 resolution attempts (82 tautology discharges), 3,712 evaluator gate
checks, and 5,928 source-replay literal reads; zero final assertion failures.
The five scalable Role-4 SAT/UNSAT controls do not themselves satisfy
the signed exact-$(2,2)$ promise. The D\"ocker 198-variable/264-clause
strict-monotone UNSAT source is a published existence theorem,
not computationally reverified here; the journal calls it Proposition 2,
correcting some stale project citations of Proposition 1.
\textbf{OPEN:} a source-aware mandatory-cost bridge against every exact
uniform deterministic SAT decider, and both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted (WITNESS-DEFECT58; independent review pending):}
For a source-certified paired positive/negative triple core with each original
variable in at most two paired supports and $t$ unpaired signed proper-3CNF
clauses, one TRUE witness occurrence per defective clause followed by a
deterministic interval $(g,f)$-factor algorithm gives
$3^t\operatorname{poly}(N)$ exact source-coherent decision and witness
reconstruction. This replaces the earlier $2^{3t}$ complete-boundary
enumeration for this verified representation class, not for arbitrary SAT.
Pair discovery, rejected witness choices, factor work, original-source replay
and peak space are charged. The fully executed standard-library solver uses
bipartite lower-bound circulation; it explicitly declines nonbipartite inputs,
whose polynomial theoretical algorithm uses existing deterministic
matching-based factor results.
COMPUTATIONAL: 162 checked assertions, zero failures; 14 same-size
SAT/UNSAT prism tests with six signed nonmonotone defect clauses;
10 nonlocal bipartite matched-core tests; a certified failed circulation
with locally feasible but globally incompatible shared source assignments;
eight independent Role-4 shared-switch controls.
The paired cores, but \emph{not} their six-defect augmented formulas,
are exact signed $(2^+,2^-)$ monotone 3CNF. The published 198-variable
exact-regular UNSAT original remains untested.
\textbf{OPEN:} independent review of newly implemented nonbipartite factor in BLOSSOM-FACTOR60; arbitrary
source-readable full exact-$(2,2)$ SAT algorithm; universal
mandatory-representation/equally-expensive-shortcut implication;
both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$. Verified report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-2/2026-10-23_2012_LSPV-2_WITNESS_DEFECT58.tex}.


\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted (REVERSE-SELECTOR57, independent peer review pending):}
For a proper source-coherent 3-CNF, backward evaluation of the AND-of-OR
circuit yields one \emph{selected witness occurrence} per clause,
not an exactly-one constraint on original literal truth.  A consistent
selection exists iff the source CNF is SAT. For exact signed
$(2^+,2^-)$ occurrence 3-CNF, $4n=3m$; the complement-conflict graph
is the disjoint union of $n$ copies of $K_{2,2}$, with $4n$ vertices
and $4n$ edges. Adding $m$ clause triangles gives exactly $8n$
edges and degree four, and an independent set of size $m$ iff SAT.
The fan-in-two \emph{evaluation} circuit has $3m-1$ gates and
$6m-2$ directed gate-input wires; its functional Tseitin trace has
$9m-2$ clauses, but none of these counts is a SAT-solving lower bound.

A further constructive restricted result: if positive and negative
triple supports are paired exactly, their cubic source-sharing graph
can be satisfied by taking an arbitrary \emph{maximal} matching $M$,
then matching each unmatched vertex to a distinct matched neighbor.
Hall holds since $3|Z|\le2|N(Z)|$ for unmatched sets.
Color source edges in the two matchings TRUE and all other source
edges FALSE. Every paired triple receives one or two TRUE variables.
This yields $O(n^2)$ charged word time and $O(n+m)$ space, with original
source assignment reconstruction and clause replay.
For $t$ unpaired signed clauses, branch on at most $3t$ actual
source variables and solve the remaining NAE degree intervals via
integral circulation when the remaining source-support graph is
bipartite. This gives a checked
$2^{3t}\operatorname{poly}(N)$ upper bound with full source coherence;
the executable does not yet contain the nonbipartite general-factor
algorithm. Older paired satisfiability and independent-transversal
equivalence were already known; the constructive matching/defect
pipeline is the run's new restricted algorithmic contribution.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 353 assertion groups, zero failures; source,
circuit and functional Tseitin exhaustion through $q=5$,
scalable paired tests through $q=40$, nonmaximal greedy matching and
mismatched-support/flow controls. The UNSAT proper-3CNF hostile
controls are \emph{not} themselves exact signed $(2^+,2^-)$; the
published exact-regular UNSAT instance is not yet independently audited.
\textbf{OPEN:} a general misaligned exact-regular source solver or
any compulsory rich-representation/equally-expensive-shortcut theorem
for every deterministic SAT decider. Neither $P=NP$ nor $P\ne NP$
is established. Verified source:
\path{research/agents/lspv-2/2026-10-23_1912_LSPV-2_REVERSE_SELECTOR57.tex}.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, AFFINE-INTERFACE54:}
For any width-three CNF, sound signed-clause recognition extracts $g$
canonical four-clause XOR3 blocks. Let $S$ be the \emph{actual shared
source variables} with the remaining 2CNF plus $t$ exceptional ternaries,

variables exactly onto $S$. Enumerating $S$ and a $t$-variable strong
2CNF backdoor gives a certified
$2^{h+t}\operatorname{poly}(N)$-time decision procedure, with
source-row and SCC-path UNSAT certificates and full source witness replay.
This is a uniform restricted upper bound, not a SAT lower bound.
A matched connected family has $n=6+2g$, $k=12+4g$ ternaries, $h=1$,
the same encoded length, and $2^{g+3}$ versus zero original models:
large ternary counts do not force exponential continuation.
Conversely, XOR3-parity-one plus three binary at-most-one clauses
expresses positive one-in-three, a known NP-complete language;
the unrestricted affine/bijunctive interface can have $h=n$.

certificates with zero reported assertions failing.

$O(k(u+m+k)+2^h(u+m+k))$-word decision upper bound,

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, FACTOR-JOIN56:}
For a supplied and source-verified separator $Y$ of $b$ original
variables, deleting $Y$ splits a width-three CNF into independent

$O(n+m+2^bm_0+2^b\sum_i2^{k_i}(n_i+b+m_i+1))$-word decision

A four-clause source block
$(a\vee b\vee c)\wedge(\neg a\vee y)\wedge
(\neg b\vee y)\wedge(\neg c\vee y)$
projects exactly to $(y)$ with three independently checked
resolution steps and a source-witness lifting.

The matched connected source formulas $F_{p,q}^{\pm}$ have

clauses, no unit or pure literals, and a minimum strong
syntactic 2-CNF backdoor of size $k$.  Nevertheless,

refutes $F^-$ using only one block per side.

\paragraph{Adaptive-Certificate-Parity-Firewall124 (25 October 2026): source-exact UNSAT grammar and strict signed parity saturation.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent peer review pending;
COMPUTATIONAL 401 finite assertion groups with zero failures;
CONDITIONAL near-boundary leaf budget; all-UNSAT bounded coverage OPEN.}
A typed source-ID adaptive UNSAT proof tree has two exact BRANCH descendants,
literal-falsified original source clause leaves, replayable
resolution/PC/CP derivations, audited characteristic-two affine
terminals, identical-original-name boundary joins, and optional
\emph{executed and charged} calls to one fixed total exact ordinary-SAT
decider $A$ on actual ordinary residual inputs. It is semantically
sound on every SAT input and \emph{unboundedly} complete on every
UNSAT input by exhaustive branching, but has no proved all-UNSAT
time/leaf-volume advantage. Full branch depth $h$ plus leaf
polynomial-calculus degree $k$ yields an \emph{upper} global degree
bound $h+k$ through guard lifting, \emph{not} a small proof length.
A standard direct four-clause XOR triple consumes all two-positive
and two-negative signed source occurrences of each variable.
Distinct direct complete XOR bundles therefore cannot share original
variables inside strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ proper 3CNF, and a
direct-bundle-only source is SAT. Ordinary connected odd-charge cubic
Tseitin instead uses four positive and four negative occurrences
per edge variable and has an explicit degree-three
$\mathbb{F}_2$ refutation from fully audited vertex clause IDs.
Neither result excludes occurrence-managed strict reductions or
imports PC proof hardness into arbitrary deterministic SAT time.
For actual same-$A$ leaf encoded sizes $m_i$, with $M=\max m_i$,
$V=\sum m_i$ and $d\ge1$, the exact charged guest bound is
$\sum_i m_i^d\le M^{d-1}V$.
Only \emph{conditionally} on universal successful proofs with
$M\le N^\lambda$, $V\le N^b$, processing exponent $r$ and
per-guest-step parent-scale access exponent $u$ would the
nondeterministic UNSAT verifier have time exponent
$\max\{r,u+b+\lambda(d-1)\}$.
At $d=1.802,\lambda=1/2,u=0$, $b<1.4008217497$
and $r<1.8018217497$ are \emph{conditional} budgets
for the proposed boundary, whose precise source/model implication
still requires independent review. Full certificate soundness,
invalid-branch clocks, memory and source bit charges remain mandatory.
Original strict signed-$(2,2)$ D\"ocker 198-variable 264-clause
UNSAT source was structurally checked; its UNSAT label is
primary-literature inherited rather than brute-force reverified.
The independent strict signed prism SAT control and all 16
K4 charge controls were brute-forced.
All-UNSAT bounded certificate coverage, ARC-EDGE-90,
and P versus NP remain \textbf{OPEN}.

\paragraph{ARC-FIXEDPATH-CAPSULE123 (25 October 2026): sharp precommitted two-level SAT-status synopsis and all-future quantifier firewall.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent proof review pending; COMPUTATIONAL 50,989 exact checks with zero failures; ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP OPEN.}
For a \emph{precommitted} compatible cumulative continuation path
$F_i=F_0\wedge C_1\wedge\cdots\wedge C_i$ of $d$ true
ordinary encoded descendants with exact original variable identities,
the SAT/UNSAT trace is necessarily $(1^k0^{d+1-k})$ for
$k\in\{0,\ldots,d+1\}$. An exact \emph{path-only} source-erased
status synopsis needs at most $\lceil\log_2(d+2)\rceil$ bits,
and this is sharp if strictly nested feasible boundary contexts
have witnesses in every successive difference and at the last level.
Ordinary proper-3CNF singleton/empty supports attain every trace.
At two actual proper-3CNF PIN levels, all $256$ source supports
on three original names and both real serialized/reparsed ordinary
children were independently SAT/UNSAT checked: trace counts
$1$ UNSAT/UNSAT/UNSAT, $15$ SAT/UNSAT/UNSAT,
$48$ SAT/SAT/UNSAT, $192$ SAT/SAT/SAT.
Horn, lifted 2SAT, affine, disconnected, seeded both-label and
\emph{wrong source-name alias} hostile controls passed:
$50{,}989$ assertions, zero failures. The actual ordinary children
\emph{grow}; they are $\Theta(N)$, not $N^\lambda$, $\lambda<1$.

\textbf{Critical quantifier distinction:} the two-bit status
transcript answers \emph{only} one predetermined three-query path.
ARC-BOUNDARY-CAPACITY122's $\Omega(N/(\log N)^2)$ lower
bound applies instead to \emph{every possible future compatible
continuation} after permanently erasing the source. The present
result \emph{does not refute} that earlier theorem, provide any
source-compatible child generator from two bits, or settle adaptive
future context selection. Its exact uniform acquisition requires
three calls to the \emph{same} fixed all-input SAT decider $A$
on original-scale SAT encodings. Eager full-child materialization
costs joint $(\max\{1,t\},\max\{1,s\})$ upper exponents;
a charged source-indexed virtual accessor can avoid an $N$-bit
buffer but still incurs $(\max\{1,t\},s)$ exponent-scale
time/space, with paid guest probes and $O(\log N)$ index
state. No favorable joint time/space contraction is established.
No free SAT answer, parent-scale fallback disguised as contraction,
cross-machine resource borrowing, or retired universal cosine
endpoint is revived. FLMV's strict sufficient target remains
$t<241/200$, $s<11642479/23232400$, with one fixed
uniform deterministic all-input exact ordinary-SAT machine per pair.
A source-aware online branching, \emph{hereditary}, two-level
all-input power-short child transformer remains \textbf{OPEN}.

\paragraph{ARC-BOUNDARY-CAPACITY122 (25 October 2026): source-erasing boundary-capsule capacity, restricted.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent proof review pending; COMPUTATIONAL 41,875 checks and zero failures; ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain OPEN.}
For $b$ original boundary names, each balanced support
$S\subseteq\{0,1\}^b$ is the exact existential projection of
a proper signed-3CNF of $N=O(b^2 2^b)$ encoded bits. A complete
source-\emph{erasing}, all-continuations boundary capsule built
with any fixed total SAT decider $A$ must distinguish
$\binom{2^b}{2^{b-1}}$ supports, and some capsule needs at least
$2^b-\log_2(2^b+1)-1=\Omega(N/(\log N)^2)$ bits. Thus
universally $O(N^\lambda)$, $\lambda<1$, standalone complete
capsules under this precise source-erasure contract are
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}. This is \emph{not} a general
SAT decision-time/space lower bound: indexed parent rereads,
context-selected adaptive interfaces, virtual child-bit regeneration
and direct decisions can evade the contract with charged costs.
An exact two-level ordinary-DIMACS both-label witness
$S=\{0000\}$ versus $T=\{0001\}$ has root/child
$(\mathrm{SAT},\mathrm{SAT})$ for both and grandchild
$(\mathrm{SAT},\mathrm{UNSAT})$; physical child sizes grow,
so this does not establish ARC contraction.

\paragraph{ARC-CNF-CONTINUATION121 (25 October 2026, LSPV-3): exact arbitrary common-CNF-continuation gate and pin-safe second-level failure.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent mathematical review pending; COMPUTATIONAL hostile controls; ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP OPEN.}
Two fixed ordinary CNF modules $D(Y,U),R(Y,V)$ with the same ordered original boundary-name vector $Y$, disjoint private names and projected feasible-word supports $P,Q\subseteq\{0,1\}^k$ admit one fixed semantic quotient $h$. The quotient join
$[h(P\cap S)\cap h(Q\cap S)\ne\varnothing]$ exactly matches
$[P\cap Q\cap S\ne\varnothing]$ for \emph{both SAT and UNSAT after every common ordinary proper-3CNF continuation} whose projected source-boundary support is $S$ if and only if
\[
 \boxed{\ \forall u\in P\setminus Q\ \forall v\in Q\setminus P,
             \quad h(u)\ne h(v).\ }
\]
Necessity is certified by an actual source-compatible proper-3CNF selector for $S=\{u,v\}$: one fresh Boolean selector plus $2k$ binary implications expanded to $4k$ proper ternary clauses with $2k$ fresh auxiliaries; sufficiency follows because every colliding survivor must already be a genuine common feasible word. This \emph{fixed two-module/common-filter} quantifier is distinct from earlier FIBER-HEREDITY117's fixed-prefix/all-opposing-suffix saturation, and strictly strengthens PIN-HEREDITY120's shared-coordinate-pin criterion. A source-independent quotient safe for every module pair must be injective, but a paid source- or $A$-dependent quotient is not ruled out.

A genuinely encoded two-real-level proper-3CNF counterexample uses the same original $x,y,z$ names at all levels,
$D=\neg x\land(\neg y\lor\neg z)$, $R=\neg y\land(\neg x\lor\neg z)$, projected supports $P=\{0,2,4\}$ and $Q=\{0,1,4\}$, and a fixed $h$ colliding only the exclusive words $u=2,v=1$. The quotient passes \emph{all 27 common-coordinate pin patterns}, but not the non-pin continuation $(x\lor y)$: root $F_0=D\land R$ (9 variables, 12 clauses, 133 ASCII bytes) has exact SAT, first actual descendant $F_1=F_0\land\operatorname{PIN}(\neg z)$ (11,16,182) remains SAT, and the second $F_2=F_1\land\operatorname{BINARY}(x\lor y)$ (12,18,201) becomes UNSAT. Reused quotient labels are falsely SAT,SAT,SAT. All source identities, fresh auxiliaries, source parser and complete ordinary headers/bodies are explicit. The children \emph{grow} instead of power-shrinking, so this is a semantic-inheritance falsifier \emph{not} an ARC transition.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} deterministic seed 20261025, 6,461 assertion groups and zero failures: complete $k\le2$ support/quotient/filter enumeration (4,160 cases and 65,792 filter comparisons), 360 deterministic seeded $k=3$ cases (92,160 filter comparisons), 64 ordinary two-word-selector CNF encodings, all 27 relevant pins, exact SAT/UNSAT two-level parsing, Horn/affine/disconnected/UNSAT and wrong-source-alias controls. Temporal flips, physical circuit hardware, bit-time and actual FLMV simultaneous peak space were \emph{not} measured.

The certificate is extensional, not cheaply acquired by fiat: recognizing a cross-exclusive collision for the constant one-label quotient is SAT-hard already with one shared boundary name using $D=F(U)\land\operatorname{PIN}(\neg y)$ and $R=\operatorname{PIN}(y)$. This is \emph{not} a universal SAT-decider runtime lower bound. For ONE fixed all-input uniform exact SAT decider $A$ achieving joint $(t,s)$ in the indexed read-only-input FLMV model, $N^b$ guest calls on $N^\rho$ genuinely ordinary child strings budget the paid worst-case term $N^{b+\rho t}$ plus full changed/unchanged writes, source rereads, reconstruction, controller/index state and jointly live ancestors. A parent-scale $\rho=1$ SAT lookahead does not certify favorable exponent contraction. Eager conditional ledger remains
$t'\le\max\{r,a+\lambda+g,a+\lambda t\}$,
$s'\le\max\{\lambda,\lambda s,\eta,\kappa\}$; literal fully virtual repeated-probe ledger
$t'\le\max\{r,a+\lambda t+g\}$,
$s'\le\max\{\lambda s,\eta,\kappa\}$.
No actual all-input solver-dependent $A$-assisted, source-compatible, two-level hereditary short-child machine is established. The published strict target remains
$t<241/200$, $s<11642479/23232400$
where $\beta(c)=((c+2)/c^2-c)/2$; the illustrative ARC-EDGE-90 recurrence and $P$ versus $NP$ remain \textbf{OPEN}, the universal cosine endpoint \textbf{RETIRED}, and no time/space coordinates are borrowed from different solvers.

\paragraph{ARC-PIN-HEREDITY120 (25 October 2026): exact source-consistent shared-pin criterion and two genuine ordinary-3CNF levels.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only for the stated extensional two-module semantic interface, independent mathematical peer review pending; COMPUTATIONAL hostile controls; ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain OPEN.}
Fix a pair of ordinary CNF modules with precisely matching original boundary names
$Y=(y_1,\ldots,y_k)$, disjoint private names and projected feasible-name
supports $P,R\subseteq\{0,1\}^k$. For one fixed interface quotient
$h:\{0,1\}^k\to H$ let $E(u,v)=\{i:u_i=v_i\}$ and
$C(u,v)=\{w:w_i=u_i\ \forall i\in E(u,v)\}$. The quotient support-join
is exact for \emph{both} SAT/UNSAT labels after \emph{every consistent
partial assignment applied to both modules} if and only if
\[
  \boxed{\quad
  \forall u\in P\ \forall v\in R,\quad
  h(u)=h(v)\ \Longrightarrow\
  P\cap R\cap C(u,v)\ne\varnothing.\quad}
\]
Necessity pins precisely the coordinates on which $u$ and $v$ agree;
sufficiency observes that any two same-label witnesses surviving a
shared pin agree on all pinned coordinates, so the displayed joint
witness survives as well. This is an exact \emph{semantic} test, not a
polynomial algorithm to acquire $P,R$ or their quotient. It specializes
the previous \texttt{ARC-CONTEXT-CONFLICT119} conflict graph to the
closed family of all shared-pin contexts, and does \emph{not} cover
new suffixes, replacement modules or changed source-name adapters
without a fresh certificate.

An explicit proper ordinary signed-3CNF control has boundary supports
$P=\{000,011,111\}$ and $R=\{001,011,111\}$ on the same three
original names and uses $h(x,y,z)=(x,y)$. Its root is SAT and a
genuine first child with $x=0$ pinned on \emph{both} modules remains
SAT; the actual second-level child with $y=0$ also pinned on
both modules is UNSAT while the reused quotient reports false SAT.
Each original support is encoded by five distinct proper ternary
clauses; each pin uses four proper ternary clauses with fresh distinct
private auxiliaries on each side. The true ordinary parent sizes
are $163\to340\to484$ indexed bits, with separately serialized,
reparsed and independently decided modules of $70\to148\to208$
bits. Renaming the original shared name $z$ on one side
falsely turns the level-two conjunction SAT, showing the source-name
identification gate. A growing padded variant keeps
$\Theta(N)$-length children, not $N^{\lambda}$ with fixed
$\lambda<1$.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} the independent deterministic self-contained
standard-library V8 reproducer compared all $256$ two-bit support
pairs, $15$ set partitions and all nine consistent shared pins
($3{,}840$ pair/quotient cases, including $662$ unsafe), plus
$1{,}024$ seeded three-bit cases with $27$ pins each.
The explicit ordinary-CNF level-two gluing passed along with
Horn, 2SAT-lift, affine parity and disconnected controls and
source-alias falsification: \textbf{5,396 assertion groups, zero
failures}. These logical counters do not measure physical
FLMV machine bit work, flips, circuit hardware, bit-time or
\emph{simultaneous peak} writable/index workspace.

\textbf{OPEN load-bearing arrow.} Computing these extensional
supports via $A$ on original-length pinned parents can incur
$N^{b+t+o(1)}$ for $N^b$ invocations, not a favorable
$N^{b+\lambda t}$ for $\lambda<1$. No new all-input uniform,
fixed-$A$-dependent contractor produces \emph{eligible}
power-short ordinary children with hereditary exact both-label
joins and paid source reads, unchanged writes, policy switching,
failed routes, reconstruction, child headers/bit probes and
peak simultaneously live ancestor/index/workspace. The conditional
eager and lazy ledgers, strict FLMV box
$t<241/200,\ s<11642479/23232400$, retired cosine endpoint
and single-machine requirement remain unchanged.

\paragraph{ARC-CONTEXT-CONFLICT119 (25 October 2026): exact contextual semantic-join graph, two-level inheritance firewall and SAT-hard implicit palette.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only for declared source-compatible restricted context families; independent mathematical review pending; COMPUTATIONAL finite ordinary-3CNF controls.}
Given a fixed named boundary $B=\{0,1\}^{Y}$ and a declared family of real
ordinary proper-3CNF prefix/suffix pairs with exactly compatible common
source names and disjoint private variables, form the \emph{context-conflict
graph} with vertices $b\in B$: connect two words if they lie on opposite
supports of some \emph{actually UNSAT} declared pair.
A fixed reused boundary quotient $h$ preserves \emph{both} SAT and UNSAT for
\emph{every} declared pair if and only if $h$ properly colors the graph.
Hence its exact minimum number of extensional labels is $\chi(G)$.
This is a proof about semantic supports, not an efficient procedure to
compute them. For all singleton ordinary suffixes, $\chi(G)$ equals the
number of different descendant feasibility signatures, exactly recovering
the already-verified LSPV-3 \texttt{ARC-HEREDITARY-KERNEL118} result rather
than replacing it.

A \emph{real} ordinary proper-3CNF root, child and grandchild give boundary
supports $15,3,1$ on two original source names. With two declared actual
suffixes of supports $1,8$, the graph is two-colorable and the fixed
quotient keeping one boundary bit is correct through \emph{both} child
levels; allowing every singleton suffix instead requires three colors.
An ordinary newly allowed suffix of support $2$ produces a genuine
grandchild UNSAT-to-false-SAT error under the stale quotient.
Exact ordinary self-delimiting encoded prefix sizes are
$34\to103\to163$ bits; joined parents $163\to223\to340$ bits.
The specified source-name-preserving pin construction \emph{does not}
power-shrink inputs.

A one-shared-boundary-bit construction
$P=\mathrm{PIN}(\neg y)$,
$Q_0=\mathrm{PIN}(\neg y)$ and
$Q_F=\mathrm{PIN}(y)\wedge F$ has minimum context palette
$\chi(G)=2$ if $F$ is SAT and $\chi(G)=1$ otherwise.
This proves SAT-hardness of \emph{implicit optimal semantic palette
selection} while $P\wedge Q_F$ itself is immediately UNSAT from visible
contradictory pins, so \emph{no arbitrary SAT decider lower bound follows}.
The construction has two real ordinary descendant levels with a
four-by-four bipartite conflict graph exactly when $F$ is SAT.
A charged generic way to learn the palettes calls the \emph{same fixed}
$A$ on parent-scale pinned SAT inputs and therefore does not provide
the favorable exponent $\lambda<1$ needed for an ARC successor.

The independent deterministic peer executable passed \textbf{17,708}
assertion groups, including \textbf{3,840} exhaustive
one-pair quotient checks, \textbf{7,680} seeded two-by-two checks,
both SAT/UNSAT labels for real serialized ordinary descendants, Horn,
affine equality/contradiction, disconnected and private-name collision
controls; zero failures. These counts are not FLMV elementary
machine work, temporal flips, circuit hardware, bit-time or peak live
index/writable space.

\textbf{Receiving arrow remains OPEN.}
No all-input total uniform $A$-dependent contractor has been constructed
that cheaply discovers source-compatible contexts, emits genuine
$N^\lambda$ ordinary children for $\lambda<1$, pays all source bit and
writable/index work and peak simultaneous ancestor state, and inherits
correctness through two real levels. Thus ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP
remain \textbf{OPEN}, and the cosine-product endpoint stays retired.

\paragraph{ARC-SCOPED-DEPTH-BUDGET112 (25 October 2026, LSPV-3, independent fixed-depth audit).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only for a validated restricted scoped source model, mathematical peer review pending; COMPUTATIONAL finite controls; ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP OPEN.}
The exact original-name pinned-child gluing and the bound $w_{\rm child}\le w+2$ are \emph{not new}: they were established in SCOPED-BLOCK-REBUILDER92, and consistent overlapping boundary assignments were tightened in OVERLAP-CUT128. The current additional charged \emph{fixed-depth} result considers a validated $N$-bit canonical scoped stream with original width $w\le\delta\log_2 N$, balanced square-root clause blocks, and genuine ordinary proper-3CNF children. After any fixed $d$ levels, every leaf has $N^{2^{-d}+o(1)}$ encoded bits, and the total leaf count is at most $N^{1-2^{-d}+2d\delta+o(1)}$. One serial implementation using a single fixed all-input ordinary SAT leaf solver of pair $(t,s)$ has \emph{restricted promised-source} resource bounds
\[
t_d\le\max\{1+2d\delta,\ 1-2^{-d}+2d\delta+2^{-d}t\},
\qquad s_d\le\max\{1/2,2^{-d}s\},
\]
with full child serialization, indexed source reads, boundary metadata, ancestor buffers and selection work charged. True scope width grows at most $w+2d$ but the \emph{relative} width fraction can grow like $2^d\delta$: the fixed relative-width heredity needed for unlimited ARC iteration does not follow. For $d=2$ and $\delta<1/80$, the relative $(\log N_i)/20$ promise holds asymptotically through both actual child levels, \emph{not} on all ordinary SAT sources. The complete code tested 19 SAT/UNSAT inputs (12/7), 152 independent pin semantics, 174,449 grandchild leaf calls and six larger size-only paired controls; zero discrepancies. A 100-clause instance has a larger grandchild than first child due to pin headers, which prevents false finite-size extrapolation.
The source-only low-width all-input normalizer or A-assisted all-input semantic contractor remains \OPEN{}. The old cosine-product endpoint remains retired. Earlier SCOPED92 and latest ARC-STREAM-NORMALIZER150, ARC-EXT-SELECTOR-NOGO109, FANOUT110 and BRANCH-SELECTOR-TRILEMMA159 remain valid, model-scoped historical controls.

\paragraph{ARC-FACE-KERNEL112 (25 October 2026, LSPV-3): exact uniqueness of parity among complete Boolean face-count collisions, without free semantic work.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only for a restricted Boolean boundary-interface theorem, independent mathematical review pending; COMPUTATIONAL finite two-level proper-3CNF audit. ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain OPEN.}
For Boolean feasibility relations $f,g:\{0,1\}^b\to\{0,1\}$, equality of EVERY codimension-one exact count marginal
$H_i(z)=f(z0_i)+f(z1_i)$ implies
$f(x)-g(x)=c(-1)^{\sum_j x_j}$ by alternating propagation along Boolean-cube edges. Since $f,g$ are Boolean, either $f=g$ or they are exactly the complementary even/odd parity indicators. Thus $2^{2^b}$ Boolean relations yield exactly $2^{2^b}-1$ full face-count profiles; a full profile plus one fully pinned feasibility bit uniquely determines the relation. \emph{This is information, not computational affordability}: the profile contains $b2^{b-1}$ entries, a one-bit SAT solver does not return exact counts, and a pinned parent-scale query may cost $N^{t+o(1)}$ time and $N^{s+o(1)}$ peak space. Model counts over private variables are weighted, where non-parity support collisions exist; SAT-status-only projections are weaker. The affine parity family is cheaply solved by source-aware Gaussian elimination, so there is no unrestricted lower bound.
Independent Python exhaustive checks of all Boolean relations for $b=1,2,3,4$ found $3,15,255,65535$ profiles with exactly one parity pair collision each (9,841 checks, zero failures); a separate Node.js reproducer passed 5,410 checks. Ten opposite-label \emph{ordinary proper-3CNF} parents (source-shared parity modules and nonaffine OR3/pin controls) were fully encoded, source-name reverse mapped and decided through TWO true ordinary 3CNF pin descendants. Correct labels persisted; sizes \emph{increased} (e.g.\ parity $b=6$: $2522\to2730\to2938$ bits); hence no $\lambda<1$ and no ARC simultaneous successor. All-input, fully charged, same-machine source-aware semantic joining of genuinely power-short children remains \textbf{OPEN}, as does transport to strict FLMV $t<241/200$, $s<11642479/23232400$. The old universal cosine endpoint stays retired. Original logged HEREDITARY-SCOPED10 standalone executable remains unrecovered. All five roles remain ARC-only and scheduled tasks are unchanged.

\paragraph{ARC-PARITY-INTERFACE111 (25 October 2026): exact strict-marginal semantic-join firewall, auxiliary budget, and two-level ordinary-source-name fence.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, pending independent mathematical review; KNOWN THEOREM parity-CNF encoding bound; COMPUTATIONAL finite both-label audit. ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain OPEN.}
Two source-named proper ordinary 3CNF parity modules with $b$ shared original variables and disjoint private XOR/pin auxiliaries form equal-parity YES and opposite-parity NO parents. Each has $3b+2$ variables, $8b$ distinct proper ternary clauses, dense indexed $N=\Theta(b\log(b+2))$, and exactly the same proper-coordinate marginal feasibility and exact counts even though the parent labels differ: $\#YES=2^{b+3}$ and $\#NO=0$. Thus even unlimited adaptive proper-boundary marginal-status/count queries are insufficient for a source-BLIND combiner, but an explicit source-aware affine procedure decides this control efficiently; no arbitrary-decider lower bound follows. A CNF for the full $b$-bit parity interface without auxiliaries has $2^{b-1}$ width-$b$ clauses (known), while fresh auxiliaries permit $4b$ proper ternary clauses per module. Emdin--Kulikov--Mihajlin--Slezkin's known encoding theorem gives $m=\Omega((u+1)2^{b/(u+1)}/b)$ for $u$ auxiliaries. Explicit full-source-name ordinary children containing all $b$ names need $\Omega(b\log b)=\Omega(N)$ bits on this matched family, so first-level and second-level children do NOT have a fixed $N^\lambda$, $\lambda<1$; virtual compression needs independently paid decoding/semantic proof. The local Python audit passed 10,690 listed checks and a separate Node.js peer repro passed 8,772; both included SAT/UNSAT, exact signed source-name decoding, actual two-level proper 3CNF descendants and size checks. At $b=128$, parent 36,898 bits, first children 16,927 each, grandchildren 6,829 and 7,828 per module. This FALSIFIES only the specified marginal-only and full-named constant-depth architecture, not arbitrary A-assisted all-input contractions. No favorable simultaneous all-input FLMV pair is produced, and the strict forbidden box $t<241/200$, $s<11642479/23232400$ remains the sole endpoint. The retired cosine-sum endpoint remains retired. \paragraph{ARC-NAMED-CYCLE-INTERFACE110 (24 October 2026): all-proper-subjoin SAT-status firewall and charged two-level ordinary children.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent mathematical review pending; COMPUTATIONAL finite audit.}
The latest user allocation makes the \emph{single all-input same-machine ARC/FLMV contraction} the sole active five-role target; previous M22/UWI/direct-potential results remain evidence, not separate current spines. Construct ordinary proper signed 3CNFs from an \(r\)-edge original-name parity cycle: every edge enforces equality, except the matched NO mate has one disequality edge. Each edge is encoded by four distinct proper ternary clauses and two private auxiliaries. The YES root has exactly \(2^{2r+1}\) models, the NO root none, yet \emph{every proper union of whole edge modules is SAT in both cases}. Therefore, even an adaptive combiner receiving every proper-union SAT bit, without direct source-sign inspection or contextual pins, cannot decide the root. The source-consistent two-name relations \(R_i(a,b)=[a\oplus b=\delta_i]\), in contrast, give a correct parity join in polynomial bit work. These sources are outside \(\mathcal C_\star\) but belong to the ordinary-SAT all-input receiving domain. This sharpens earlier EXACTONE-JOIN-CYCLE95 and STRICT-SUBJOIN-STATUS131 rather than claiming a new universal hardness statement.

\noindent\textbf{Two actual serialized/parsed ordinary child levels.}
The explicit unary-header signed-index encoding has \(N=3h+1+3m(h+1)\), \(h=\lceil\log_2(\max(n,m)+1)\rceil\). For \(r=4\), full input \(304\) bits gives two \(133\)-bit children, each giving two \(58\)-bit grandchildren; for \(r=128\), \(16927\to7708\to3481\) bits. Original-name mappings, complete child headers/bodies, SAT and UNSAT labels, and descendant eligibility were checked across both levels. \textbf{51,119 assertion-accounted executable checks, zero exceptions.} Because every fixed-depth balanced child is \(\Theta(N)\) in the growing family, this source-retaining architecture has no \(N^\lambda\), \(\lambda<1\), child. Eager buffered children cost \(\Omega(N)\) writable bits; virtual ports may save peak buffer bits but must pay all accesses/replay and still feed \(A\) a logical \(\Theta(N)\)-length query. A source-aware parity scan decides this easy family, so neither projected-boundary counts nor local status collisions yield an arbitrary-\(A\) lower bound.

\noindent\textbf{Receiving implication still OPEN.}
No verified finite cofinal route of \emph{joint} \(A\)-derived achievable SAT pairs reaches the strict FLMV box
\(t<241/200,\ s<11642479/23232400\).
The historic cosine-product universal endpoint is retired. General-source compatibility, ordinary \(N^\lambda\) child eligibility, on-demand access, dynamic policy costs, SAT/UNSAT reconstruction, buffers, live ancestors and two-level heredity remain unproved on \emph{all ordinary inputs}. P versus NP remains \textbf{OPEN}.

\paragraph{M22-WEIGHTED-LEAF-FACTOR110 (24 October 2026): exact original-name minimum-cost paired-boundary service.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT RESTRICTED using known weighted degree-constrained subgraph algorithms; mathematical independent review pending.}
For source-valid canonical clausewise-monotone signed exact-$(2^+,2^-)$ $F\in\mathcal C_{\star}$, put $H=P\cap Q$ for complementary support pairs, $P'=P\setminus Q$, $Q'=Q\setminus P$, $D=\bigcup P'=\bigcup Q'$, and $Z=V\setminus D$. Its source-named paired NAE boundary is
\[
 B_H=\left\{a\in\{0,1\}^{D}:\exists z\in\{0,1\}^{Z}\;\bigwedge_{h\in H}\operatorname{NAE}_3((a,z)|_h)\right\}.
\]
For \emph{arbitrary binary-encoded integer unary costs} $c_d(0),c_d(1)$ and any specified original-name hard-pin set $J\subseteq D$ with prescribed values, there is a deterministic \emph{polynomial charged bit-work and writable-space} algorithm which minimizes $\sum_{d\in D}c_d(a_d)$ over feasible pinned $a\in B_H$ and reconstructs a coherent internal original-name assignment. Each $H$ support becomes a vertex; each original internal name in two paired supports becomes a \emph{single} graph edge between its two supports, each one-exposed boundary name becomes a private leaf edge, and unattached boundary names remain unconstrained. Selecting edges for TRUE imposes support degree interval $1\le \deg_{\rm selected}\le2$ (exact paired NAE), leaf pin bounds and leaf weight $c_d(1)-c_d(0)$; a constant $\sum_dc_d(0)$ adjusts the objective. Existing polynomial weighted $(g,f)$-factor/min-cost degree-constrained subgraph theorems supply the optimizer, including distinct parallel edges and signed binary weights. This extends the already-known paired-core \emph{membership} service from DELTA-INTERFACE105, not the full M22 SAT decider. It does not assume an even delta-matroid algorithm or erase unmatched clauses.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL finite source-consistent controls.}
An independently executed standard-library finite enumerator checked $1{,}348$ weighted objective/compatible-witness and two ordinary restriction assertions with zero discrepancies: canonical eligible $Y$ has $k6,m8,H0,|B_H|64,20$ full models; the connected eligible partially paired $k6,H2,D5$ source has $|B_H|=28/32$, $22$ full models and excludes named words $00110,00111,11000,11001$; a connected $k9,H3,D6$ source has full $64$-word boundary and $86$ models; connected $k15,H7,D6$ has full $64$-word boundary and $1694$ models. Falsely copying the hidden $z$ in paired supports of the partial-six instance changes $28/32$ to $32/32$: same-source identity and gluing are essential. Two ordinary restriction levels use declared encoded sizes $504\to352\to\{259,251,251,318\}$ bits, each leaf independently SAT, but \emph{all} children leave $\mathcal C_\star$ and finite shortening proves no hereditary power shrink. The published D\"ocker $U$ UNSAT input and prior source-valid SAT mutant $V$ are both eligible, have $H=\varnothing$ and are unaffected by this optimizer. $U$'s UNSAT label is imported from the publication, not independently solved anew. Finite Python edge enumeration is \emph{not} the polynomial Gabow implementation nor measured FLMV bit work, bit flips, bit-time or peak space.

\textbf{OPEN receiving arrows and complete work charges.}
ARC-EDGE-90 still needs one uniform total exact ordinary-SAT machine jointly witnessing each time/space pair on every ordinary input, and a finite fully charged cofinal-diagonal route to strict FLMV $t<241/200$, $s<11642479/23232400$. The old cosine sum bound is RETIRED; source-promise restriction, type-exiting children and an H-empty fallback supply no favorable all-input edge. DIRECT-POST still needs an independently large, terminal-zero complete-state potential after acquisition whose decrease is bounded for \emph{every} actual charged adaptive transition. Source-readable weighted boundary repair refutes compulsory-work inferences from boundary cardinality, Hamming geometry or delta structure alone. For fixed uniform adaptive $A$ at a declared checkpoint, $T_A(F)=C_A(F,R)+T_{\rm finish,A}(F,R)$ and $S_A$ is actual jointly live writable/index peak. Charge parsing, source reads/rereads, changed and unchanged writes, address/control/arithmetic, weight bit lengths, failed choices, factor augmentations, strategy selection/switching, caches and retained ancestors, actual child bits, agreement and reconstruction. Keep total primitive work, traffic, complete-state flips, physical gates/wires/switches, peak bits, cumulative actual live-bit-time and representation size distinct. \paragraph{M22-EDGE-DEFECT-PUSH109 (24 October 2026): zero-or-one-exposure source-supported paired-core erasure.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT RESTRICTED; independent mathematical review pending.}
For canonical properly validated $\mathcal C_\star$ signed exact-$(2^+,2^-)$ source $F$, write $P,Q$ for original positive/negative unsigned support sets, $H=P\cap Q$, $P'=P\setminus Q$, $Q'=Q\setminus P$, $D=\bigcup P'=\bigcup Q'$, $Z=V\setminus D$. If each paired support $h\in H$ has \emph{zero or one} exposed original name, $|h\cap D|\le1$, then \emph{every} original-name boundary assignment $a:D\to\{0,1\}$ extends by a deterministic polynomial-bit-work algorithm to satisfy \emph{both signs} of all paired clauses. Therefore
\[
\boxed{\operatorname{proj}_{D}\operatorname{SAT}(H_{\rm paired})=\{0,1\}^{D},
\qquad
\operatorname{SAT}(F)\iff\operatorname{SAT}(P'\wedge Q').}
\]
The proof represents every original internal $z\in Z$ as a distinctly indexed edge joining its two paired-support clauses. This yields a loopless multigraph whose support vertices have degree two (one exposed bit) or three (no exposed bit). Degree-two vertices forbid two incident edge values both equal to the exposed bit, and degree-three vertices forbid monochromaticity. A constructive \emph{defect-push edge recoloring} resolves one defective vertex, routes any induced defect along a pivotal edge and terminates on a healed or first-revisited vertex. Each walk takes at most $|H|+1$ logical edge toggles and strictly decreases the number of defects; at most $|H|$ walks suffice. All source reads, recognition, parameter discovery, addressing, changed/unchanged writes, repairs, selection/switches, reconstruction and cache use remain charged ordinary \emph{bit work}. This strictly strengthens prior SINGLE-EXPOSURE-ERASURE108, which required \emph{exactly one} exposed name per paired support and used a Hall matching. The former equality $|Z|=|H|$ cannot be presumed under the new guard.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL finite controls.}
A connected within-target original-name $k=15,m=20,q=3$ formula with seven paired supports (four zero-exposure, three one-exposure) has all $64$ paired-boundary assignments feasible, $26$ satisfying unmatched residual words and $1694$ complete satisfying assignments by exhaustive $2^{15}$ scan. Independently checked defect recoloring succeeded for all $21{,}195$ labelled connected simple degree-two/three graph/boundary cases through six vertices and $2997$ multigraph/initial-bit cases, with no discrepancies. The declared ordinary encoding test gives $2664\to680\to540\to400$ bits over erasure and two restrictions, but all restriction descendants leave $\mathcal C_\star$; this is neither asymptotic power shrink nor a typed recursive adapter. Python counted \emph{logical edge toggles}, not physical time, complete-state flips, peak live writable bits or bit-time. The original in-target published D\"ocker $U$ (UNSAT from literature) and its independently certificate-replayed in-target SAT mutant $V$ both have $H=\varnothing$ and remain \emph{unchanged} by this erasure. A known connected $\mathcal C_\star$ core with two exposed names in a paired support has only $28/32$ feasible boundary words, so the unguarded generalization is FALSIFIED/RETIRED.

\paragraph{M22-SINGLE-EXPOSURE-ERASURE108 (24 October 2026): exact one-exposed paired-core erasure with paid 2-SAT fallback.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT RESTRICTED; independent review pending.}
For valid original-name clausewise-monotone exact-$(2^+,2^-)$ $F\in\mathcal C_\star$, write $H=P\cap Q$ for all fully complementary supports, $P'=P\setminus H$, $Q'=Q\setminus H$, $D=\bigcup P'=\bigcup Q'$, and $Z=V\setminus D$. If every $S\in H$ contains \emph{exactly one} exposed original name $d_S\in D$, then fixing \emph{any} $a\in\{0,1\}^D$ reduces the two opposite-signed clauses of each $S$ to exactly one binary signed clause on $S\cap Z$: positive if $a(d_S)=0$ and negative if $a(d_S)=1$. Every internal original $z\in Z$ occurs in exactly two such paired supports; hence the paired-support-to-$Z$ incidence is $2$-regular on both sides. Hall supplies a distinct matched internal name for every paired clause; setting that name opposite to $a(d_S)$ satisfies the entire paired core. Thus
\[
\boxed{\operatorname{proj}_D\operatorname{SAT}(H_{\rm paired})=\{0,1\}^D,\qquad
\operatorname{SAT}(F)\iff\operatorname{SAT}(P'\wedge Q').}
\]
All recognition, incidence construction, failed augmentations, source reads, matching, witness lifting and replay are charged. This \emph{strengthens} M22-TWO-HALL-CUBE107: its additional guard $\deg_H(d)=1$ for every $d\in D$ is \emph{not necessary} for the paired projection or exact erasure, but is still necessary for its separate unconditional \emph{whole-source-SAT} matching. Under the weaker guard, $|H|=|Z|=2k/3-q$ and $|D|=k/3+q$. A pair-free in-target original $U$ can be UNSAT with $H=\varnothing$ and exact erasure equal to identity; therefore \emph{all-source SAT} and \emph{all-input ARC contraction} do not follow.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT RESTRICTED complementary case:} when every paired $S$ has at least one exposed name, a \emph{supplied} boundary assignment is testable by paid SCC on a source-generated 2-CNF, since $1\le p_S+\sum_{z\in S\cap Z}z\le2$ has at most two internal literals after fixing $D$. This does \emph{not} make the paired projection universally full. The connected six-name $\mathcal C_\star$ source $H=\{abz,cdz\}$, $P'=\{abe,cde\}$, $Q'=\{ace,bde\}$ has only $28/32$ feasible boundary words, exactly characterized by $(a\lor b\lor\neg c\lor\neg d)\land(\neg a\lor\neg b\lor c\lor d)$, a \emph{mixed-sign} predicate outside the canonical target.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL independent finite controls:} connected in-target nine-name $H=\{az_1z_2,bz_2z_3,cz_3z_1\}$, $P'=\{ade,bef,cdf\}$, $Q'=\{adf,bde,cef\}$ meets the one-exposed guard but violates the old each-$d$-once guard. Its paired core accepts all $64$ boundary words, unmatched subformula $26$, full source has $86$ satisfying assignments; source-indexed lengths $226\to118$ for original versus unmatched ordinary child, followed by \emph{two true ordinary restriction levels} $118\to79\to60$ on each of four pin branches. Ordinary children leave $\mathcal C_\star$ and fixed-factor shortening is not asymptotic power shrink. Exact $75^2=5625$ six-name valid M22 enumeration gives $75$ fully paired outside target, $2610$ one-exposed guard sources \emph{all vacuous $H=\varnothing$}, and $2940$ nonfull paired-core projections with each pair touching $D$; all finite checks passed. Original $k=198,m=264$ D\"ocker UNSAT $U$ and source-valid SAT mutant $V$ are both in target, both have $H=\varnothing$, and remain uncontracted; $V$ certificate replay was independently verified, $U$ UNSAT is from the published construction. Current bit traffic, temporal flips, peak live bits, cumulative bit-time and hardware resources were not physically measured. A declared exhaustive residual on $D$ would have $R_{\rm search}=k-|D|=|Z|$ and gives only a $2^{|D|}\operatorname{poly}(N)$ \emph{upper}, not mandatory lower, budget.

\textbf{OPEN receiving links unchanged:} ARC-EDGE-90 needs a jointly achieved, fully charged all-ordinary-input simultaneous machine-pair route from cofinal diagonals into strict FLMV $t<241/200,\ s<11642479/23232400$; the old cosine product region remains FALSIFIED/RETIRED as a universal endpoint. DIRECT-POST still needs an independently initialized high potential on complete post-acquisition states with decrease bounded under every paid adaptive operation; source-readable full cubes and compact mixed projection are countercontrols, not lower bounds. $P$ versus $NP$ remains \OPEN{}. Source work, all parameter selection, adaptive Horn/affine/SCC/factors, caches, failed candidates, both levels of child encodings, gluing, simultaneous writable/index bits and bit-time remain charged and distinct. \paragraph{M22-TWO-HALL-CUBE107 (24 October 2026): exact two-Hall full paired-boundary cube and connected high-defect polynomial witness.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT RESTRICTED; independent review pending.}
For canonical valid clausewise-monotone exact-$(2,2)$ $F\in\mathcal C_{\star}$, let $H=P\cap Q$, $P'=P\setminus Q$, $Q'=Q\setminus P$, $q=|P'|=|Q'|>0$, exposed original names $D=\bigcup P'=\bigcup Q'$, and internals $Z=V\setminus D$. Under the \emph{two simultaneous source-auditable guards}
\[
(\forall S\in H)\ |S\cap D|=1,\qquad
(\forall d\in D)\ \deg_H(d)=1,
\]
the original signed degree quota forces the incidence between $2q$ unmatched signed OR clauses and $D$ to be left-$3$/right-$2$ regular. Hall gives a distinct satisfying literal source name per unmatched clause. The separate paired-support-to-internal-$Z$ incidence is left-$2$/right-$2$ regular, hence Hall assigns each paired $\mathrm{NAE}_3$ triple a distinct internal $z_S$ opposite to its unique exposed bit $d_S$. This works for \emph{every} assignment on $D$: the exact paired-core projection is the full cube $\{0,1\}^{D}$, and the complete original formula is SAT by two uniform source-consistent polynomial matchings. Necessarily $|D|=|H|=|Z|=3q$, $k=6q$, $m=8q$. The second guard is indispensable: adjoining a pair-free published UNSAT M22 source $U$ to a guarded SAT source keeps the first guard but violates the second and remains UNSAT (UNSAT from the published source, not newly decided). This is not a general SAT result.

An explicit \emph{connected, in-target} family for every $q\ge2$ has $P'_j=\{d_{3j},d_{3j+1},d_{3j+2}\}$, $Q'_j=\{d_{3j},d_{3j+1},d_{3((j+1)\bmod q)+2}\}$, and $H_i=\{d_i,z_i,z_{(i+1)\bmod 3q}\}$, with indices $0\le i<3q$. Original-name witness $d_{3j}=1$, other $d=0$, $z_i=1-d_i$ satisfies all clauses. Its paired boundary has $2^{3q}$ feasible words despite a polynomial recognizer and certificate, falsifying a \emph{pointwise compulsory-work inference} from boundary cardinality, linear $q$ or projected rank alone, not a worst-case all-machine lower bound. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} independent finite guarded controls $q=2,3,4,6,10,32$, 72 relabelings, 160 randomized guarded sources, all 64 exposed words for $q=2$, and canonical $Y$'s 20 models passed. Two ordinary source-identity-preserving restriction levels have declared $q=2$ indexed bit lengths $298\to252\to216$ but both children leave $\mathcal C_\star$; this is not an ARC power-shrink edge. Actual FLMV-machine bit-work, traffic, flips, peak bits and bit-time were not measured.

The historic $t+s<2\cos(\pi/7)$ universal landing is retired. \paragraph{M22-Q3-INTERSECTION-HIT106 (24 October 2026): signed-OR four-color rescue and the exact $3\times3$ defect-grid obstruction.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted mathematical transfer; independent review pending.}
For any valid canonical M22 source $F$ with $q=|P\setminus Q|=|Q\setminus P|=3$, let $P,Q$ be original positive/negative three-name unsigned supports, $H=P\cap Q$, $P'=P\setminus Q$ and $Q'=Q\setminus P$. A proper four-coloring $c$ of the positive-primal graph exists and is constructible in polynomial work by the previously established Role2 Q2-FOURCOLOR140/Brooks lemma. Choosing \emph{any two false colors} satisfies all original positive and paired-negative clauses. It satisfies every unmatched negative clause $B\in Q'$ precisely when the two false colors hit the nonempty set $\{c(v):v\in B\}$. Three nonempty subsets of four colors lack a two-element transversal exactly when two are distinct singletons $\{a\},\{b\}$ and the third avoids both. Residual source occurrence equality $\deg_{P'}(v)=\deg_{Q'}(v)$ then forces a \emph{necessary} $3\times3$ Latin defect grid: the three $P'$ triples and the three $Q'$ triples are each pairwise disjoint on nine original names and $|P'_i\cap Q'_j|=1$ for every $i,j$. Thus every valid $q=3$ source with two intersecting triples in either unmatched family is SAT with a paid polynomial source-readable original-name witness; every hypothetical $q=3$ UNSAT must have the grid. This is a restricted YES class, not a worst-case lower bound.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL finite / previously failed palette repaired:} All $75^2=5625$ six-name valid signed sources were checked. The $1680$ $q=3$ cases are all in target and have overlapping unmatched triples. The previous three-pair NAE first-color method succeeds on $1080$ and fails on $600$; the new six oriented signed-OR false-color pairs succeed on all $1680$, rescuing the $600$. The three-set obstruction classification agrees with every finite check. An explicit \emph{connected} within-target nine-name $q=3$ source $P=\{012,345,678,048,156,237\}$, $Q=\{036,147,258,048,156,237\}$ (zero-based names) defeats all six pair choices for the proper coloring $c(v)=v\bmod3$ but is SAT with $60$ exact source models and $42$ NAE models. A grid or failed single coloring is \emph{not} an UNSAT/hardness certificate. Under two ordinary source-named pin-and-simplify levels, its declared Elias-gamma bit lengths are $226\to192\to148$, but both children leave the $\mathcal C_\star$ grammar; the finite shrink has no asymptotic ARC consequence. The large original D\"ocker UNSAT $k=198,q=132$ and its source-valid SAT mutant are distinct in-target opposite-label controls; the published source's UNSAT classification was not reproved by this six/nine-name audit.

\paragraph{M22-DELTA-INTERFACE105 (24 October 2026): source-named paired NAE delta boundary and exact full-join firewall.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted transfer of a published theorem; independent review pending.}
For every canonical valid M22 source $F$, write $P,Q$ for positive/negative unsigned three-name supports, $H=P\cap Q$, $P'=P\setminus Q$, $Q'=Q\setminus P$, and $D=\bigcup P'=\bigcup Q'$. All internal original names outside $D$ occur exactly twice in the paired $\operatorname{NAE}_3$ network, and all exposed $D$ names at most once. $\operatorname{NAE}_3$ is a \emph{non-even delta-matroid}. The existential named-boundary relation
\[
B_H=\left\{u\in\{0,1\}^D:\exists z\;\bigwedge_{S\in H}\operatorname{NAE}_S(u,z)\right\}
\]
is a \emph{nonempty delta-matroid} by Kazda--Kolmogorov--Rol\'inek's exact composition theorem (arXiv:1602.03124v6, Proposition~6), with separate nonemptiness from four-edge-colorability of the associated degree-three multigraph. The source's original $O(N)$-description NAE incidence network is cheap to parse and its named pinned membership is polynomial by the \emph{previously known} signed degree-two interval-factor service. No free boundary oracle or general even-delta polynomial solver is assumed. Delta exchange is distinct from NAE sign-cycle rank, linear/XOR rank, affine hull and nonaffine joint compatibility.

\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED full-join delta closure:} The connected in-target six-original-name source $P=\{abu,abv,cuw,cvw\}$, $Q=\{abu,abw,cuv,cvw\}$ has $H=\{abu,cvw\}$, $q=2$, paired-core delta boundary with $36$ words, but its fully joined relation has $24$ words and violates the delta exchange axiom. Complete named countercertificate: satisfying $X=\{a,c\}$, $Y=\{u,v\}$, $e=a$; no $f\in X\triangle Y=\{a,c,u,v\}$ restores feasibility after toggling $e,f$ (one toggle when $f=e$). Adding unmatched clauses reuses four signed occurrences per name, violating the composition theorem's two-occurrence premise. A connected nine-name $\mathcal C_\star$ source from NAE-PROJECTION99 has delta paired-core projection with $224$ words, full-affine-hull/nonaffine, and non-delta joined projection with $68$ feasible boundary words and $98$ full models. The earlier semantic two-level join is \emph{not} two power-short ordinary SAT children.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL finite:} For all $75^2=5625$ valid six-name signed M22 support pairs, $0$ paired-core delta violations and exactly $5550$ full-boundary non-delta instances (all in $\mathcal C_\star$); $75$ fully paired instances remain outside the target. Distribution $q2,|D|5:540$, $q2,|D|6:720$, $q3,|D|6:1680$, $q4,|D|6:2610$. This is \emph{not} a universal $k$ theorem, an unavoidable machine work lower bound, or a new $\mathcal C_\star$ hardness result. Independent source-grammar and SAT-mutant-witness replay on D\"ocker's original $k=198$ source $U$ and its SAT mutant $V$ confirms both are in target, $q=132,H=\varnothing$, so both have the same entire paired-core delta cube despite opposite root labels; $U$'s UNSAT label remains an imported published theorem.

\textbf{OPEN receiving gaps:} ARC-EDGE-90 needs a uniformly correct \emph{all-ordinary-input} same-machine simultaneous $(t,s)$ favorable charged successor or a finite cofinal diagonal route into the \emph{strict} FLMV box $t<241/200$, $s<11642479/23232400$. The historical $t+s<2\cos(\pi/7)$ universal endpoint remains retired. DIRECT-POST needs independent large noncircular complete-state $\Phi$ after source acquisition with bounded decrease under \emph{every} charged adaptive operation. An exponentially large projected relation, exchange defect, finite candidate count or semantic projection need not be materialized by an exact one-bit decider. Retain $T_A=C_A+T_{\rm finish,A}$, signed original-source identity, $R_{\rm search}$ only for independently specified exhaustive residuals, disjoint bit traffic, changed/unchanged writes, temporal flips, peak simultaneously writable bits, actual cumulative bit-time, all child bodies and gluing costs. P versus NP remains \OPEN{}. Previous symbolic six-name five-witness status (OPTIMAL-FIVE104) and earlier k6/k9/expander source controls remain unchanged.

\paragraph{M22-OPTIMAL-FIVE104 (24 October 2026): symbolic optimal five fixed balanced witnesses in the exact nonpaired target.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent review pending.}
For any six-active-name valid $F\in\mathcal C_{\rm M22}$, every five distinct weight-three assignments having any one fixed source bit $x_v=b$ contain a satisfying assignment. The exact reason is that positive and negative clause-failure families each contain \emph{only two} such balanced words. Conversely, any fixed four balanced candidate assignments can be falsified simultaneously by a source $F_W\in\mathcal C_\star$, with proper distinct names and both signed occurrence quotas intact. For two distinct triples intersecting in $2,1,0$ names, there are respectively $1,3,9$ degree-two four-triple completions; partition any four candidates into two pairs, complete each, and choose nonpaired positive/negative families. In the all-overlap-two case, unique completions are individually self-complementary but cannot coincide because a balanced four-family has pairwise-intersection sum $6$, not $12$. Thus five is \emph{exactly optimal} among source-oblivious, nonadaptive, weight-three test lists \emph{even against} $\mathcal C_\star$. This upgrades the \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL} five-test optimality of M22-ANTIPODAL-SIX103 to a short symbolic proof; it does not strengthen the prior $12+h$ witness theorem or the earlier all-SAT result for $k=6,9$.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL controls:} every one of $75^2=5625$ labelled six-name sources, all $4845$ four-word lists with source-valid target adversaries, all twelve fixed-variable/value five-word controls, $110596$ assertion-accounted checks and zero failures. The archived original D\"ocker 198-variable 264-clause UNSAT source and its SAT mutant both pass an independent exact signed-grammar replay; the archived 198-bit mutant witness satisfies all 264 clauses. Original UNSAT remains a published theorem dependency. Two recursive levels of disjoint $Y$ components ($4\to2\to1$) preserve $\mathcal C_\star$ and source identity but ordinary child bit length is $\Theta(N)$, not $N^{\lambda+o(1)}$ with $\lambda<1$.
\textbf{OPEN:} ARC-EDGE-90 still lacks any charged, all-input, single-machine simultaneous pair route into the verified strict FLMV box $t<241/200$, $s<11642479/23232400$; DIRECT-POST lacks an independently initialized complete-state $\Phi$ robust to source recognition, dynamic rules, caching, affine/SCC and alternative representations. Fixed-list optimality is no arbitrary adaptive lower bound. The historical cosine product endpoint is retired, P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.
