\paragraph{M22-ANTIPODAL-SIX103 (24 October 2026): exact six-name balanced witnesses and two independent overlap coordinates.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted; independent review pending.}
For valid six-variable clausewise-monotone exact-$(2^+,2^-)$ sources with positive and negative triple-support sets $P,Q$, put
$q=4-|P\cap Q|$ (ordinary complementary-\emph{clause} defect)
and $h=|P\cap Q^\perp|$, where $Q^\perp=\{V\setminus U:U\in Q\}$ is the \emph{antipodal-support} family. Then the exact number of satisfying three-TRUE assignments is
\[
\boxed{|M_3(F)|=12+h,\qquad h\in\{0,1,2,4\}.}
\]
Each positive clause excludes precisely its complementary TRUE triple, and each negative clause excludes precisely its own TRUE triple among the twenty balanced assignments. Overlap is exactly $h$. Both $P$ and $Q^\perp$ have vertex degree two, so three common supports force the fourth and $h\ne3$. Consequently \emph{all} six-variable $\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}$ instances are SAT, including the 5,550 valid six-name $\mathcal C_\star$ instances, with both truth values feasible for every unary source variable. This upgrades the earlier exhaustive-only all-SAT statement of MINIMAL-NAMED-JOIN102 to a short \emph{symbolic restricted proof}, not to a general complexity lower bound. Distinct sources with the same $q=2$ can have $h=0$ or $h=2$, with respectively twelve or fourteen balanced witnesses.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL finite:} a Python standard-library exhaustive audit of all $75^2=5625$ labelled six-name signed support pairs verifies a \emph{fixed source-oblivious five-witness cover}
$\{123,124,134,234,125\}$ (TRUE name sets). The cover is optimal among fixed nonadaptive balanced candidate lists: all $\binom{20}{4}=4845$ four-candidate sets fail on at least one source; 2,085 distinct balanced forbidden masks were enumerated. The two-level exact named-join control remains consistent; serializing additional ordinary unit pins produces source-scale children and leaves the target grammar. Finite catalogue counts do not force all-decider work. A recognized tensor of six-name components is decided componentwise in polynomial work despite an exponential artificial Cartesian witness list.
\textbf{OPEN:} ARC-EDGE-90 still lacks a fully charged all-input finite resource route for \emph{one} joint time-space ordinary-SAT decider to strict FLMV box

\paragraph{M22-MINIMAL-NAMED-JOIN102 (24 October 2026): smallest strict-target source hides a named ternary join.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted; independent peer review pending.}
The connected six-variable ordinary $\mathcal C_\star$ source has positive unsigned supports
$\{abu,abv,cuw,cvw\}$ and negative unsigned supports $\{abu,abw,cuv,cvw\}$,
so $k=6,m=8,q=2$ with two \emph{partial} complementary pairs and balanced
two-positive/two-negative occurrences per source name. Its positive and
negative halves each have constant-TRUE projection to $(a,b,c)$ separately,
but their \emph{common-source-name} join is exactly
\[
  [\exists u,v,w\,(F_+\land F_-)]
    =(a\lor b\lor\neg c)\land(\neg a\lor\neg b\lor c).
\]
It rejects boundary $001$ and $110$, has six feasible words, full unary/pairwise
projections and full affine hull, but is nonaffine. The exact two projected
clauses are \emph{mixed-sign} and leave the target grammar. Disjoint tensors
inside $\mathcal C_\star$ have $k=6r,m=8r,q=2r$, $24^r$ full models and $6^r$
feasible boundary words against $8^r$ pairwise-relaxed words. A source-readable
$O(r)$-clause predicate and polynomial-time recognition bypass explicit
enumeration of these states. The restricted independent-hidden-witness gluing
rule, pairwise-hull recovery rule and implicit-candidate-count work potential
are \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}. These are not universal SAT decision lower bounds.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} full local standard-library script audited all $75^2=5625$
six-variable M22 supports (5550 in target, all SAT), 16959 assertion groups,
zero discrepancies, two identical raw runs, and tensors through $r=3$ exhaustively.
A source-grammar check of D\"ocker U (198 variables, 264 clauses, $q=132$)
does not reprove its imported UNSAT label. Ordinary two-level source pinning
leaves the M22 target and produces $\Omega(N)$-length children for these tensors.
No physical FLMV bit operations, live-bit peaks, flips, or bit-time were measured.

route for jointly achievable single-machine pairs to the strict FLMV box

\subsection*{LSPV-3: Semantic joins, shared-witness coherence and adaptive representations}

\paragraph{M22-MAXDEFECT-FACTOR101 (25 October 2026): maximally unpaired connected source with huge frontier and a constant exact boundary relation.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the explicitly declared source construction and unchanged-original-clause frontier model; independent mathematical review pending.}
Partition the edges of a connected simple four-regular bipartite graph
$G$ on $2r$ vertices into four perfect matchings. Group the two positive-factor
edges and two negative-factor edges into pairs labelled by $r$ fresh original
$A$ variables. For every factor edge $uv$, emit the proper monotone clause
$(a_i\vee u\vee v)$ or $(\neg a_i\vee\neg u\vee\neg v)$ according to its factor.
The resulting source $F_G\in\mathcal C_\star$ has
\[
 k=3r,\qquad m=4r,\qquad
 |P\cap Q|=0,\qquad q=2r=2k/3.
\]
Thus its source is connected, signed-occurrence-balanced and \emph{maximally
unpaired}. Assigning $B_L=1$, $B_R=0$ satisfies all clauses for every
assignment to the $r$ boundary $A$ names. Hence its exact projected
feasibility predicate is $Q(A)\equiv1$ even though it admits $2^r$
boundary words. A fully charged source syntax recognizer and direct
witness check therefore decide this recognized family in polynomial time.
For four-regular bipartite edge expanders, \emph{every} unchanged original
signed-clause order exposes at least $h_0(r-1)/4=\Omega(N/\log N)$ live
original $B$ source names, without forcing comparable writable bits or
post-acquisition machine work. This strengthens the earlier M22-MATCH-EXPANDER100
$q=2$ example by simultaneously taking $q$ to its maximum value.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} full independent local standard-library checks for
$r=4,5,6,8,12,16,24$, 112 shuffled clause orderings and 1940
assertion-accounted groups with zero discrepancies; $r=4,5$ full model
counts are 346 and 1304. Finite test graphs were not certified to be an
expander family and physical FLMV Turing bit operations, flips, peak live
writable bits and cumulative bit-time were not measured.
Two conventional proper-ternary forced-literal pin levels have
$k_d=3r+2d$, $m_d=4r+4d$ and \emph{growing} ordinary child encoding length;
both descendants violate the $\mathcal C_\star$ grammar. A declared
four-leaf independent exhaustive residual list fixing two source bits has
$\Psi=2^k$, $R_{\rm search}=0$; this is not a compulsory decision budget.

\textbf{OPEN:} ARC-EDGE-90 all-input, jointly achievable, same-machine charged
finite route into strict FLMV box
$t<241/200,\ s<11642479/23232400$; DIRECT-POST independently initialized
worst-case complete-state potential robust to all adaptive methods; general
target-preserving recursive adapter; P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.
The fully paired recognizable subclass is excluded from $\mathcal C_\star$
but partial pairs remain allowed; the six-variable source $Y$ is in-target
SAT, the source U of D\"ocker is in-target UNSAT by published certificate
(not independently solved here). Old universal cosine product endpoint

\paragraph{PIN-BIT-FLOOR98 (25 October 2026): ordinary independent pin-child serialization floor, restricted.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only for the fixed source-name-preserving eager
four-clause-per-pin compiler; independent mathematical review pending.}
Combine EXPANDER-STREAM-ORDER97's all-clause-order source-frontier
bound with SCOPED-BLOCK-REBUILDER92's actual proper-3CNF pin syntax.
For a cubic $h_0$-edge expander Tseitin instance with $v$ vertices
and source bit length
$N=(16v+2)\lceil\log_2(64(11v/2+1))\rceil$,
every contiguous sublinear-clause block partition, regardless of
clause permutation, has a cut with
$w\ge h_0v/2-o(v)$ live original edge names.  An eager
ordinary pinned child incident to that cut contains at least $4w$
proper clauses and $2w$ fresh auxiliary variables; hence
\[
 N_{\rm child}\ge(16w+2)
  \left\lceil\log_2\bigl(64(6w+1)\bigr)\right\rceil
 =\Omega(N).
\]
This refutes a universal \emph{power-short ordinary-child guarantee for
that specific unchanged-clause, eager-pin representation}, even after
adaptive source-clause ordering.  It does not refute a semantic
normalizer or an existential all-input achievable-pair closure.  An
independently validated affine parity recognizer solves the same
matched SAT/UNSAT Tseitin sources directly, so neither source width
nor materialization of pinned children is mandatory general SAT work.
Ordinary child bit encoding $(4m_c+2)h_c$ is distinguished from tagged
scoped-stream $(2+4n_c+4m_c)h_c$ and from indexed virtual handles.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}: seven finite cubic-prism sizes, 224 shuffled
source-clause orders, 923 assertion groups with zero failures;
minimum sampled eager child/source bit ratio $>1.8$.  These finite
prisms are not an asymptotic expander family and the Python counters
are not FLMV physical bit-work/space traces.

SAT resource successor or cofinal charged route into the genuine FLMV box.

\noindent\textbf{Latest Role 3: TYPED-DEGREE-BRIDGE98, PROVED/EXACT restricted (independent review pending).}
For a source containing signed exact-one ternary modules $E(\ell_1,\ell_2,\ell_3)$ and named binary equality records, define the \emph{typed} degree of a variable as the count of its original module/equality appearances, not the number of post-compilation ordinary-CNF literals. With maximum typed degree two, equality components are paths, cycles or isolated vertices. Contracting each component gives at most two exact-one occurrences per variable; repeated-coordinate constraints reduce by truth table, and the remaining signed degree-two exact-cardinality problem is solved in polynomial time by the source-matched INTERVAL-FACTOR134 graph-factor method.

For maximum typed degree three, an equality-only intermodule join of \emph{three-state} local exact-one modules remains NP-complete even with a connected constraint graph, every variable in exactly one exact-one module, and preserved source names. The new neutral two-module bridge between original disconnected components consists of $E(u',z_1,a)$, $E(v',z_2,b)$ and $u'=u,z_1=z_2,v'=v$. The assignment $z_1=z_2=0$, $a=1-u$, $b=1-v$ extends every original assignment, so bridging does not change SAT status. For an original positive 1-in-3-SAT source with $m$ triples, $n_+$ occurring variables and $c$ incidence components, the resulting connected typed source has
\[
 M=m+2(c-1),\quad L=3m-n_++3(c-1)
\]
exact-one modules and named equality links. Compiling to ordinary proper three-CNF gives
\[
 n'=4M+2L,\qquad m'=6M+4L,\qquad
 N'=(4m'+2)\left\lceil\log_2(64(n'+m'+1))\right\rceil .
\]
A connected matched SAT/UNSAT control has $M=7,L=11,n'=50,m'=86,N'=4844$ with identical unsigned support. This refines existing DEGREE-MATCH-LADDER132 and EXACTONE-JOIN-CYCLE95 rather than claiming a new original degree-three NP-completeness theorem.

\noindent\textbf{Computational and adaptive ledger.}
A full reproducible standard-library audit tested 160 typed joins (139 SAT, 21 UNSAT, including 68 originally disconnected sources) and 260 typed degree-two controls (234 SAT, 26 UNSAT). It recorded 3,896 checks, zero discrepancies, and two identical raw outputs with SHA-256 \texttt{f7e944c01d3f11618ef2a9843a6adc7b6928dea8a4d8bf5c368a6b4eb6faca4d}. Finite computation is not an asymptotic machine theorem. The uniform source-language upper bound $2^{k(I)}\operatorname{poly}(|I|)$ with $k(I)=|\{v:d_I(v)\ge3\}|$ is representation dependent. A complete BIT-RESOURCE1 ledger must charge source bits, parameter discovery, strategy selection/switching, failed alternatives, matching/factor search, name-copy equality, ordinary child serialization, caches, witness replay when used, reads and writes including unchanged values, actual state flips, peak live bits, cumulative bit-time and code size.

\noindent\textbf{Receiving ARC/FLMV obligation: OPEN.}
These restricted algorithms and NP-complete connected joins establish no all-input jointly achieved SAT time-space improvement. Neither local support three nor maximum typed degree three forces computation by every algorithm. Ordinary compilation has source-scale children, not the requisite $N^{\lambda+o(1)}$ ordinary SAT children for $\lambda<1$. The highest-value next test is SCOPED-BLOCK-REBUILDER92 and OVERLAP-CUT128 on the bridged pair and STRICT-SUBJOIN-STATUS131, over two recursion levels, with charged shared-variable compatibility and fully encoded child bits. The original HEREDITARY-SCOPED10 executable remains unverified. P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.

\noindent\textbf{Earlier Role 3: EXPANDER-STREAM-ORDER97, PROVED/EXACT restricted source-frontier obstruction (independent review pending).}
For a connected cubic graph $G$ with $v$ even vertices and edge-bisection width
$B(G)=\min_{|U|=v/2}|\delta_G(U)|$, encode each degree-three vertex XOR
equation as four proper ternaries on its three incident edge variables.
Then \emph{every} permutation of the original unchanged $4v$ clauses,
with the same named source edge variables, has a cut with at least
$B(G)$ simultaneously live original variables. Stop after the first
$v/2$ distinct vertex gadgets have appeared: each crossing edge
variable occurs in a processed clause and in an unprocessed clause.
On cubic expanders, this is
\[
w\ge h_0v/2=\Omega(N/\log N),\qquad
N=(16v+2)\left\lceil\log_2(64(3v/2+4v+1))\right\rceil.
\]
The clauses are balanced, with exactly four positive and four negative
occurrences for every edge variable, and no pure-literal escape.
Yet every charge vector is decided by the source-validated affine
component-parity condition
$\operatorname{SAT}(T(G,b))\iff\bigoplus_{u\in V}b_u=0$;
SAT members have $2^{|E|-v+1}$ witnesses.
All-zero and one-twist charges give matched SAT/UNSAT sources with
identical unsigned supports, source size and live-width lower bound.
This polynomial-time semantic escape was already known in
PARITY-SHORTCUT78 and CUBIC-AFFINE-FIREWALL98; the new increment is
the exact quantifier over \emph{every original clause order}.
A block-aligned frontier refinement also holds: for any partition
of the unchanged clause order into consecutive blocks of at most
$B$ clauses, some block boundary has at least
$\max\{0,B(G)-3B\}$ live source variables. Crossing one
proper ternary changes liveness of at most three variables.
For square-root clause windows $B=\lceil\sqrt{4v}\rceil$ on
constant-expansion cubic graphs, this remains $\Omega(v)$.
This directly limits clause-reordering-only versions of the
SCOPED-BLOCK-REBUILDER92 schedule, not semantic affine
normalization or arbitrary SAT algorithms.
The theorem refutes a clause-reordering-only low-width normalization,
not arbitrary source-aware rewriting or unrestricted decision time.
\nopagebreak

\noindent\textbf{Finite audit: COMPUTATIONAL.}
Eight finite simple connected cubic graphs, 56 formulas (17 SAT,
39 UNSAT), 640 randomized clause orderings, 44 independent full
assignment-model-count controls, and exact finite edge-expansion
enumerations had zero discrepancies. Two identical raw JSON files have
SHA-256 \texttt{b7c21944e4c0cdca69ec2d2566ac8f60a15478ec3c764ae547d111f62daf108b}.
Prism controls alone are not an asymptotic expander family.

\noindent\textbf{Prior Role 3 result: SCOPE-ALIAS-COMPLETENESS96, exact restricted language boundary and source-name repair.}
For any ordinary proper-3CNF with $m$ clauses, emitting one
OPEN--OPEN--OPEN--CLAUSE--CLOSE--CLOSE--CLOSE block
per clause retains the exact original global signed formula
under erasure of event tags. Each block has locally live width three
and the complete reopening stream has
\[
 N_{\rm local}=(2+28m)h\le 7(4m+2)h,
 \quad h=\lceil\log_2(64(n+m+1))\rceil.
\]
SAT on this locally well-formed but \emph{globally reopening} grammar
is therefore NP-complete by identity reduction from proper-3CNF.
A frontier DP that forgets every name on CLOSE wrongly accepts every
such stream because every detached proper ternary clause is SAT.
Globally fresh occurrence names restore syntactic validity at
width three but destroy source semantics. To repair exactly,
identify copies of the same original source name with
$r=3m-\nu$ equality links, where $\nu$ names actually occur.
An ordinary proper-3CNF realization with two fresh auxiliary
variables and four ternaries per equality has
\[
 n'=3m+2r,\qquad m'=m+4r.
\]
The alias join is source-name preserving but does not furnish
genuinely power-smaller ordinary SAT children.
Every one of the 24 individual occurrences in the complete
eight-clause three-variable UNSAT core is semantically essential:
freeing any one occurrence yields SAT. This does not imply
that one arbitrary equality-tree edge always suffices.

Role 4's global-freshness validator lower bound and Role 3/5's
three-state equality-join NP-completeness remain prior restricted
results, not new attributions. If an \emph{all-input} producer
could turn each reopening stream into a \emph{globally valid}
width-three stream of $N^{1+o(1)}$ output bits with
time $N^{1.1+o(1)}$ and space $N^{.5+o(1)}$ in the same
matched FLMV model, online frontier DP would contradict the
published strict forbidden box at $c=241/200$.
This excludes the stated complete producer contract, not all
source-aware SAT algorithms, which may decide directly,
reread, cache, propagate or change representations.
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted} for the typed semantic reductions,
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL} for 585 sources, 523 SAT and 62 UNSAT,
7,196 assertion checks and 24 occurrence release tests, zero errors.
The full BIT-RESOURCE1 vector was not measured as physical
FLMV machine traces. The universal all-input ARC successor and
cofinal charged finite route both remain \OPEN{}.

and cumulative gap evidence:

\noindent\textbf{Earlier Role 3 result: EXACTONE-JOIN-CYCLE95, restricted and independently reviewed by LSPV-5.}
An exact local relation can be expressed as
\[
 E(\ell_1,\ell_2,\ell_3)
 \iff
 [\ell_1\oplus\ell_2\oplus\ell_3=1]\land
 (\neg\ell_1\vee\neg\ell_2).
\]
It has three feasible signatures, affine hull dimension two,
and one local affine parity equation. For any positive 1-in-3-SAT
source with \(m\) triples and \(L=3m-n_+\) repeated-variable
equality links, a uniform source-name-preserving construction
makes one disjoint 3-state module per triple and \emph{only}
binary equality constraints between modules. Global named
compatibility is NP-complete by the known Schaefer exact-one theorem;
this refines the earlier TWO-EASY-INTERSECTION61 and LADDER51
results, rather than claiming new NP-completeness.

The ordinary proper-3CNF compilation has
\[
 n'=4m+2L,\qquad m'=6m+4L.
\]
A connected four-module SAT/UNSAT pair with identical unsigned
supports, the same equality interaction graph, and local domain
sizes \((3,3,3,3)\) has
\[
 n'=32,\quad m'=56,\quad N'=2938\text{ encoded bits}.
\]
Every local domain survives complete \emph{binary arc consistency}
on both labels. The UNSAT member enforces exact-one on triples
\((abc),(abd),(acd),(bcd)\), which would require
\(3(a+b+c+d)=4\); negating only \(b\) in the fourth triple
gives the SAT member \(a=1,b=c=d=0\).
The two source-named grouped relations for module pairs
\((1,2)\) and \((3,4)\) each have three assignments, but their
intersections have cardinalities zero and one respectively.
Thus detached local SAT bits and even unchanged arc-consistent
three-state supports do not determine exact global satisfiability.
Their source-name-preserving full join does determine it.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted:} local parity/binary identity,
linear metadata-counted normalization, paired opposite labels,
complete arc-consistency fixed points and two-level named gluing.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 869 assertion groups, 160 random signed
exact-one controls (103 SAT and 57 UNSAT), zero discrepancies,
two byte-identical raw results. DPLL branch counts are not
FLMV bit-TM resource measurements. Supplied, charged bounded
treewidth or feedback-cutset decompositions yield classical
\(3^{w+1}\operatorname{poly}(N)\) and
\(3^f\operatorname{poly}(N)\) upper bounds respectively.
\textbf{FALSIFIED as universal decision statistics:}
local support size, affine dimension, pairwise arc consistency,
or cycle count taken alone. Source-aware direct algorithms and
recomputation remain legitimate.

\noindent\textbf{Earlier Role 3 synthesis: PROJECTION-LIFT-JOIN94 (conditional representation, no separation).}
The source-name-preserving semantic join
\[
\SAT(F)\iff\exists x\bigwedge_i Q_i(x|_{B_i}),\qquad
Q_i(u)=[\exists y_i\,F_i(u,y_i)]
\]
is exact for validated modules with disjoint private variables.
Local lexicographically first witness lifts can be joined through two
or any fixed number of grouping levels without treating incompatible
anonymous child witnesses as compatible. This is a typed semantic
identity, not an all-input efficient SAT decomposition.
HEVALLB1 and Main 11 previously established the compact
partial-compatibility evaluator under $\SAT\in P$; the new Role 3
delta supplies fully stated typed private-variable lifts and a
source-name-preserving two-level realization. If a fixed uniform
all-input SAT decider $A$ has unknown polynomial exponent $K$,
the exact boundary-decision and witness-lift circuits can be uniformly
generated with charged $N^{O(K)}$ size and work, using ordinary
source-pinned SAT calls. Neither a compact circuit nor a
source-coherent correct join establishes a favorable ARC successor:
with empty boundary the original SAT cost is hidden in evaluating
$Q_1(\emptyset)=\SAT(F)$, while with all variables exposed
the original SAT instance remains at the root existential join.
Constructed circuit size, input lengths, same-machine time and
peak work/index space may all retain an unknown $K$-dependent cost.
The 356-source finite auditor (336 SAT, 20 UNSAT) passed 6,274
assertion-accounted checks, including source-name-conflict and
two-level full-witness tests; logical counts are not FLMV bit-TM
measurements. Peer report, full reproducibility code, and handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-24_2248_LSPV-3_PROJECTION_LIFT_JOIN94.tex};

\noindent\textbf{Earlier restricted update: SCOPED-BLOCK-REBUILDER92.}
An independently rebuilt, peer-visible source-name-preserving
scoped-block compiler now generates fully serialized ordinary
proper-3CNF pinned children from \emph{supplied valid closed}
OPEN/CLAUSE/CLOSE streams. This is not the unavailable original
HEREDITARY-SCOPED10 implementation. Entry and exit pins carry
the same original variable identity; each uses four ordinary
ternary clauses with two temporary auxiliaries. The exact
parent-label equivalence is
\[
 \SAT(E)\ \Longleftrightarrow\
 \exists u_0,\ldots,u_q\;
 \bigwedge_{i=1}^q
 [u_{i-1}|_{S_{i-1}\cap S_i}=u_i|_{S_{i-1}\cap S_i}]
 \land\SAT(\mathrm{Child}_i(u_{i-1},u_i)),
 \quad u_0=u_q=\emptyset.
\]
With live width $w$, each child has width at most $w+2$,
at most $2^{2w}$ cut pairs per block, and
$N^{1/2+o(1)}$ encoded bits for $w=o(\log N)$.
For a single exact ordinary child solver with simultaneous
bounds $(t,s)$, the \emph{promised scoped-source} wrapper has
\[
 t'\le\max\{1,\tfrac12+\tfrac t2\},\qquad
 s'\le\max\{\tfrac12,\tfrac s2\},
\]
with charged source reads, local names, child bytes,
headers, compatibility, failed pairs, cut-state caches
and live work/index buffers. A fixed-depth lower child-length
floor is separately supplied by PAD-FLOOR-SCOPED87;
the new finite code checks actual unpadded two-level children.
Its 21 both-label source tests (14 SAT, seven UNSAT), 1,462
independent pinned-block comparisons, and complete two-level
SAT/UNSAT controls had zero discrepancies; finite Python
measurements are not matched FLMV physical space measurements.
Ordinary-source all-input conversion to the required narrow
stream is still \textbf{OPEN}. These promise inputs already
admit direct frontier dynamic programming, so this exact
restricted reconstruction does not fill ARC-EDGE-90.
P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.

\noindent\textbf{Prior restricted result: SELECTOR-ERASURE-PRISM91 (24 October 2026); independent review pending.}

\noindent\textbf{Exact arbitrary-mask shared-selector identity.}
Adopt the source-preserving SELECTOR-SPLIT119 construction for an
ordered proper 3CNF $F(X)=\bigwedge_{i=1}^m C_i(X)$, with a fresh
selector triple $U_i=\{u_{i1},u_{i2},u_{i3}\}$ per clause.
Its affine part requires $u_{i1}\oplus u_{i2}\oplus u_{i3}=1$;
its binary part contains all three pairwise at-most-one clauses and
$u_{ij}\Rightarrow\ell_{ij}$ for each corresponding literal.
Both components are independently SAT, but
$\exists U\,[A_F(U)\land B_F(X,U)]\equiv F(X)$ when
the selectors share one consistent assignment.

For \emph{every} retained selector set $S\subseteq U$, define the
source-typed, detached projection with \emph{independent} omitted
witnesses:
\[
 J_S(X)=\exists z_S\left[
  (\exists a_{U\setminus S}\,A_F(z_S,a))
  \land(\exists b_{U\setminus S}\,B_F(X,z_S,b))
 \right].
\]
The newly proved pointwise identity is
\[
 \boxed{J_S(X)\equiv
   \bigwedge_{\{i:U_i\subseteq S\}}C_i(X).}
\]
If any selector of a clause is omitted, the affine component can
choose an omitted one while the binary component chooses no selector,
so the detached join \emph{erases exactly that source clause}. Fully
retained triples still enforce selection of one true source literal.
Keeping all original variable identifiers does not cure existentially
independent selector witnesses. This is a quantifier/coherence
obstruction, not an all-decider lower bound.

\noindent\textbf{Scalable both-label test and source attribution.}
Apply this identity to the pre-existing clause-minimally UNSAT
odd-charge cubic prism Tseitin family from
CUBIC-AFFINE-FIREWALL98, together with its even-charge SAT mate.
For $q\ge3$ there are $n=3q$ original edge variables, $m=8q$
proper ternaries and $24q$ selector bits. Because deletion of any
one original clause restores SAT, \emph{every proper} $S\subsetneq U$
makes $J_S$ SAT although the correct fully shared odd-charge join
is UNSAT. Thus every selector coordinate is indispensable only
within the declared detached coordinate-only calculus.
The parent encoded length is
$N=(32q+2)\lceil\log_2(64(11q+1))\rceil$;
ordinary properization of the affine/binary modules produces
$n'=75q$ variables and $m'=128q$ ternaries, still
$\Theta(N)$ encoded bits, not an $N^{1/2}$ ARC child.
This does not re-claim Role 2's selector compiler or Role 5's
Tseitin minimality theorem. A paid source-aware charge-parity
scan decides these matched families directly in linear record
time, so their raw interface size is not a universal work bound.

\noindent\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL hostile controls.}
Independent exhaustive edge-assignment tests for $q=3,4,5,6$
include 64 local truth/mask cases, matched SAT/UNSAT sources,
all odd-source one-clause deletions and single-selector erasures:
1,324 assertion-accounted checks and zero failures.
Even-source model counts are $16,32,64,128$;
odd-source counts zero; the per-clause deletion witness counts
are $4,8,16,32$. Two runs produced identical raw JSON.
Python logical tests do not measure FLMV elementary bit work,
complete-state flips, peak writable tape bits or cumulative
live-bit time.

\noindent\textbf{Adaptive state and resource preservation.}
A uniform policy may recognize the prism and scan charge parity,
or switch to SCC, Horn, affine, branching, caching, source rereading,
lazy child generation or direct decision. All source/workspace reads,
changed and unchanged writes, recognition, failed trials, switching,
ordinary generated child bits/headers, witness work, replay, caches,
ancestors and peak writable space must be charged for the actual
same-machine execution. SAT and UNSAT labels are not oracle
inputs; arbitrary deciders need not materialize the selector table.
The historical $t+s<2\cos(\pi/7)$ product endpoint remains
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}.

\paragraph{ARC-SEMANTIC-CYLINDER116 (25 October 2026, LSPV-3): an exact universal-suffix source-name erasure gate and quantified recognition barrier.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only for the explicitly declared named-boundary rewrite; independent mathematical review pending. COMPUTATIONAL 3,244 assertion checks and 82 complete ordinary SAT query/serialization checks with zero failures. ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain OPEN.}
For a proper signed 3CNF prefix \(P(x,Y,U)\), with private names \(U\),
put \(R_P(b,y)=[\exists u\,P(b,y,u)]\). Given any ordinary proper-3CNF
suffix \(Q(x,Y,V)\) with private \(V\cap U=\varnothing\), splitting the
original source name \(x\) into independent prefix/suffix names preserves
the SAT/UNSAT label \emph{for every possible compatible suffix} if and only if
\[
 \forall y\in\{0,1\}^{|Y|}\quad R_P(0,y)=R_P(1,y).
\]
Sufficiency follows from the exact common-name witness join; necessity
is witnessed by a genuine ordinary proper-3CNF suffix pinning a disagreeing
\(y\) and the excluded \(x\) branch. This is a \emph{projected
existential} cylinder test: using the same private witness on both sides of
a raw Boolean miter is unsound. Two genuinely serialized ordinary child
levels may rename two separately certified names, but must recertify the
second prefix. The finite SAT and UNSAT tests gave
\(451\to451\to451\) and \(343\to343\to343\) encoded bits, respectively:
this particular all-input semantic rewrite has child exponent
\(\lambda=1\), not an ARC power shrink.

A new self-contained theorem classifies the semantic recognition problem
\(\forall y\,[R_P(0,y)\leftrightarrow R_P(1,y)]\) on \emph{proper}
ternary CNF as \(\Pi_2^P\)-complete. Given a proper
\(\forall Y\exists Z\,\varphi\), replace each clause \((a\vee b\vee c)\)
by \((\neg x\vee a\vee h_C)\wedge(\neg h_C\vee b\vee c)\):
\(R_P(0,y)=1\) and \(R_P(1,y)=[\exists Z\,\varphi(y,Z)]\).
Membership is immediate from a \(\Sigma_2^P\) non-cylinder witness.
This classification is a \emph{conditional acquisition gate},
not a lower bound for any arbitrary SAT machine. Under hypothetical
\(P=NP\), the polynomial hierarchy collapses; no contradiction follows.
A fixed uniform exact \(A\) can check the gate by fully paid pinned
ordinary-child SAT queries, but with \(N^b\) contexts and \(N^\rho\)-length
children its explicit guest term is \(N^{b+\rho t+o(1)}\), plus
construction, control, caches, virtual probes and peak simultaneous
writable/index space. The actual original-scale implementation has
\(\rho=1\), so it supplies no favorable simultaneous successor.

Hostile checks included UNSAT-to-SAT upon uncertified variable erasure,
UNSAT incompatible original-name gluing despite separately SAT modules,
an existential-witness miter false positive, malformed-input rejection,
both quantified-gate labels, and SAT/UNSAT descendants at two real
levels. \textbf{OPEN} remains a uniform charged \(A\)-dependent
all-ordinary-input true power-short ordinary-child construction with
source-compatible semantics, two-level heredity and strict FLMV entry.
The old universal cosine endpoint remains retired.

\paragraph{ARC-FIBER-HEREDITY117 (25 October 2026, LSPV-3, universal-suffix boundary-quotient gate and two-level counterexample).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent mathematical review pending; COMPUTATIONAL exhaustive finite tests; all-input ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP OPEN.}
For an ordered common original-name boundary $Y$, let $S_P,S_Q$ be the existential projected feasibility supports of proper signed-3CNF modules with disjoint private names. For any common interface quotient $h$, the coarsened join $h(S_P)\cap h(S_Q)\ne\varnothing$ agrees with the exact same-assignment join $S_P\cap S_Q\ne\varnothing$ under \emph{every} ordinary proper-3CNF suffix (both SAT and UNSAT) if and only if $h^{-1}(h(S_P))=S_P$: prefix feasibility must be saturated on complete quotient fibers. Necessity is witnessed by a completely ordinary four-clause PIN gadget forcing a missing same-fiber boundary word. The one-bit map $h_P(z)=\mathbf{1}[z\in S_P]$ is extensionally optimal except when the prefix support is empty/full, but may itself conceal an original-scale SAT call. Explicit two-clause proper-3CNF gates yield $[\exists G_F(z=0)]=1$ and $[\exists G_F(z=1)]=\SAT(F)$ for arbitrary proper-3CNF $F$; this gate and the one-coordinate $\Pi_2^P$ recognition bound are inherited from Role~3's prior SEMANTIC-CYLINDER116, not new unrestricted time lower bounds. A fixed-$A$ literal pinned certificate has $\rho=1$, and $N^b$ guest calls cost the charged upper-certificate term $N^{b+t}$, with complete eager headers/body and live $N$-bit child buffer or fully paid per-probe virtual replay. No favorable same-machine contraction is inferred.

Two actual \emph{ordinary} proper-3CNF levels expose a precise hereditary failure. Put $P(x,y,u)=\mathrm{EQ}(x,u)\wedge\mathrm{PIN}(\neg y)$, $M(x)=\mathrm{PIN}(\neg x)$ and choose $Q_+(x)=\mathrm{PIN}(x)$ or $Q_-(x)=\mathrm{PIN}(\neg x)$. The first certified erasure renames only $P$'s original $x$ to fresh $x_L$; after prefix recombination, $P(x_L,y,u)\wedge M(x)$ is \emph{not} a cylinder in original $x$. Reusing the first certificate to rename $M$'s $x$ to fresh $x_M$ is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED as an eligible second edge}: the UNSAT control has true labels $(0,0,1)$ across root, first child and second syntactically proper but invalid semantic descendant; the SAT control has $(1,1,1)$. Both paths have exact encoded sizes $307\to307\to307$ bits and 16 clauses; the real suffix retains original source $x$. Fresh second-level feasibility recertification catches the mismatch, but must be paid. The finite reproducer checks 4,096 quotient/suffix combinations, 2,124 assertions, 38 complete parsed ordinary-CNF SAT decisions and zero failures, including Horn, affine, disconnected, malformed-header and opposite-label controls. Logical encoded bit traffic was recorded; physical FLMV primitive time, simultaneous writable/index peaks, true flips, circuit switches and bit-time were not measured. Prior INTERFACE-PROJECTION8/SCOPED112 inheritance on a supplied low-width promise is not promoted to an all-input theorem; Role~2's hard-image scope-normalizer toll and Role~5's boundary-support-easiness firewall survive. Strict target remains $t<241/200$, $s<11642479/23232400$, with one total uniform exact ordinary-SAT machine per joint pair. The universal cosine endpoint remains retired; no parent-scale fallback, detached SAT oracle or cross-solver coordinate borrowing is permitted.

\paragraph{Adaptive certificate coverage and source-private-witness conservation (LSPV-3, 25 October 2026; 26 October UTC research redirection).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent review pending; all-UNSAT affordable coverage OPEN; ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP OPEN.}
The source-audited adaptive UNSAT grammar of
\path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-25_2228_LSPV-3_ADAPTIVE_CERT_PARITY_FIREWALL124.tex}
is sound on SAT sources and admits an \emph{unbounded} finite refutation on every UNSAT ordinary input: complete original-variable branching with source-ID-falsified-clause leaves suffices. Fully checked source-labelled resolution, CP, PC, affine terminals, source-compatible joins, and paid executions of one fixed exact ordinary-SAT decider $A$ may supplement those leaves. This does \emph{not} establish affordable all-UNSAT coverage. In particular, clocked $\mathrm{UNSAT}\in\mathrm{NTIME}(N^c)$ and $\mathrm{SAT}\in\mathrm{coNTIME}(N^c)$ follow only after a quantified all-UNSAT accepted-certificate existence and verifier-budget theorem. The preexisting arithmetic upper envelope $\sum_i m_i^d\le(\max_i m_i)^{d-1}\sum_i m_i$ is insufficient without it.

\textbf{New exact scoped source-support lemma (SOURCE-SUPPORT-PRIVATE-WITNESS126).}
For UNSAT $F=\bigwedge_{i=1}^m C_i$ with original source clause IDs, put $E(F)=\{i:\mathrm{SAT}(F\setminus\{C_i\})\}$. In every complete original-variable binary branch tree where each verified UNSAT terminal at assignment $\sigma_\ell$ is justified by a \emph{source-identified original-clause subset} $I_\ell\subseteq[m]$ restricted by $\sigma_\ell$, each $i\in E(F)$ must occur in the terminal support $I_{\ell(i)}$ reached by an assignment satisfying all source clauses except $C_i$. Otherwise that assignment satisfies the claimed UNSAT terminal. Hence
\[
E(F)\subseteq\bigcup_\ell I_\ell,\qquad
|E(F)|\le\sum_\ell|I_\ell|\le K L\le K2^h,
\]
where $K=\max_\ell|I_\ell|$, $L$ is terminal count, and $h$ is maximum branch depth. The proof applies to heterogeneous \emph{sound, source-subset-audited} terminal systems including resolution/PC/CP/affine and genuine charged fixed-$A$ calls, but \emph{not} automatically to rewritten children, unconstrained JOIN DAGs or virtual source descriptors. For clause-falsification-only terminals $K=1$, so $L\ge |E(F)|$, with at least $2|E(F)|-1$ nodes in an explicit full binary proof tree.

\textbf{Hostile controls and failure of hardness promotion.}
The prior
\path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-25_2256_LSPV-3_TSEITIN_CLAUSE_CRITICALITY125.tex}
established odd, 2-vertex-connected cubic Tseitin per-clause essentiality and ordinary source-identified degree-three $\mathbb F_2$ polynomial-calculus refutations. In this run, odd $K_4$ (six edge variables, sixteen clauses) has all sixteen source clauses essential but finite exact pure source-clause DPLL optimum \emph{28} leaves and maximum depth six among leaf-minimizers (697 memoized partial-assignment states); one checked affine terminal nevertheless uses all sixteen source axioms and is short. Odd $K_{3,3}$ has 24 essential clauses. Strict signed proper exact-$(2,2)$ $W_1$ has fifteen original variables, twenty clauses and twenty essential clauses. Two sequential physically serialized original-clause deletions produce real SAT descendants on each source, with exact DIMACS headers and inherited original names. The disjoint union of two odd $K_4$ UNSAT components has \emph{zero} individually essential original clauses; even-charge Tseitin, Horn, 2SAT and original-name/forged-certificate controls rule out stronger extrapolations. Finite checks are COMPUTATIONAL, not machine-model runtime measurements.

\textbf{Resource firewall.}
The necessary terminal source-support incidence lower bound is at most the original clause count $m=O(N)$ and is \emph{not} a lower bound of the same magnitude on encoded guest-child volume $V=\sum_i m_i$, work-tape space, or worst-case machine time. Short GF(2) algebraic refutations and small-backdoor clauses already defeat any unrestricted inference from minimal-UNSAT alone. The near-boundary budget $d=1.802$, $\lambda=1/2$, $u=0$, $b<1.4008217497$ remains CONDITIONAL upon a matching two-algorithm theorem/model audit and, centrally, a still-OPEN all-UNSAT bounded certificate-coverage theorem. No cofinal favorable same-machine contraction or P versus NP conclusion is claimed.

\paragraph{SOURCE-CUT-ENTROPY127 (26 October 2026, 00:24 Los Angeles): exact derived-child source-cut and private-witness entropy.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent peer review pending; COMPUTATIONAL 8,974 finite checks, zero failures; universal affordable UNSAT coverage OPEN.}
A source-derived ordinary SAT leaf $H_\ell$ with declared original-clause dependency $I_\ell$ and original-variable branch $\sigma_\ell$ is semantically \emph{source-cut sound} only if \emph{every} satisfying assignment of $F[I_\ell]\land\sigma_\ell$ has a name-compatible satisfying extension into $H_\ell$, as established by an independently audited source-to-child witness map or an exact source-local derivation. Then UNSAT of $H_\ell$ implies UNSAT of the declared source subset; the witness-lift condition composes through two \emph{real} ordinary child levels with compatible original names. Separately satisfiable derived modules do \emph{not} in general glue when they accidentally alias their private variables; a proper signed-ternary witness joins correctly with disjoint private namespaces but becomes spurious UNSAT after a private-name collision.

For UNSAT $F$ and essential original clauses $E(F)=\{i:F\setminus\{C_i\}\in\mathrm{SAT}\}$, choose a private assignment $\alpha_i$ satisfying every other source clause. In every complete original-variable branch tree whose UNSAT terminals have independently verified source-cut provenance $I_\ell$, let $n_\ell$ count the private witnesses routed to leaf $\ell$, $\kappa_\ell=|I_\ell\cap E(F)|$ and $d_\ell$ its depth. Then $\boxed{n_\ell\le\kappa_\ell,\ \sum_{\ell:n_\ell>0}(n_\ell/\kappa_\ell)2^{-d_\ell}\le1}$ and, for uniform $i\in E(F)$,
\[
\boxed{\mathbb E_i[d_{\ell(i)}+\log_2\kappa_{\ell(i)}]\ge\log_2|E(F)|.}
\]
The proof combines the PRIVATE-WITNESS126 source-ID contradiction with exact binary prefix-free branch semantics and conditional entropy. It is a \emph{heterogeneous source-support theorem}, NOT a lower bound on derived guest sizes, PC/CP proof size, acquisition, or arbitrary SAT runtime. Since $|E(F)|\le m=O(N)$, its information lower bound is at most $O(\log N)$ and cannot establish the conditional $N^{1.4008217497}$ guest-leaf-volume threshold; a sound one-terminal proof with full source support can have depth zero. Semantic source-cut recognition itself cannot be assumed free.

Original proper-source hostile checks: odd $K_4$, odd $K_{3,3}$, odd Petersen and strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ minimally UNSAT $W_1$, with true source-ID assignments and fixed/adaptive branch tree counts respectively $(28,31),(84,92),(608,640),(3582,2686)$. The latter adaptive branch heuristic uses \emph{offline} private-witness discovery and is NOT a cheap uniform strategy. Fresh-PIN $W_1$ physical two-level descendants give UNSAT bytes $226\to274\to322$ and a one-clause-deletion SAT path $215\to263\to311$, explicitly \emph{growing} rather than contracting. Another full-projection boundary-control demonstrates wrong-private-name join unsoundness. Twenty-four seeded strict signed $(2,2)$ sources include $20$ SAT and $4$ UNSAT; Horn/2SAT/affine controls pass. Source and exact finite raw are reproducible, but no FLMV timing or simultaneous peak-space measurement is claimed.

Retain prior PARITY-FIREWALL124 original branching/PC-lift and PRIVATE-WITNESS126 source-subset mass lemmas as \emph{prior results}; only the declared source-cut extension and weighted Kraft/entropy refinement are new to this run. Original two-algorithm theorem/model conditions, paid all-UNSAT $(M,V,r,u)$ coverage, ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain \textbf{OPEN}. Never infer the hypothetical $d=1.802$ SAT machine from $P=NP$.

\paragraph{SOURCE-CUT-QBF-BARRIER128 (26 October 2026, LSPV-3; independent review pending).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted}: the unannotated source-to-ordinary-child
model-lift decision predicate
$\forall\alpha[\alpha\models F\Rightarrow\exists\beta\models H:
\beta|_{\mathrm{Vars}(F)\cap\mathrm{Vars}(H)}
=\alpha|_{\mathrm{Vars}(F)\cap\mathrm{Vars}(H)}]$
is $\Pi_2^P$-complete even when the source $F$ is a disjoint union of
four-clause satisfiable proper signed exact-$(2,2)$-3CNF gadgets,
with all original variable names also occurring in the child $H$.
The four clauses
$(y\vee a\vee b)$, $(y\vee\neg a\vee\neg b)$,
$(\neg y\vee a\vee\neg b)$, $(\neg y\vee\neg a\vee b)$
project to both $y$ values and contain each name twice with each sign.
Neutral proper ternary padding retains all source names in the child.
A second proper ordinary descendant with one new satisfiable clause
preserves the exact obstruction through two real levels.
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted}: using a fixed eight-clause UNSAT child
shows unrestricted asserted-lift validation is coNP-complete on arbitrary
proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ sources. This characterizes the \emph{cost
of a generic semantic checker}, not the verification cost of an
explicitly supplied local extension witness, nor a lower bound
against arbitrary SAT deciders.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}: trusting generic (SC) as a free certificate
terminal, and pairwise-complete boundary joins without globally consistent
source assignments. A proper ternary cyclic equality/disequality
triple has SAT pairwise joins but UNSAT full join.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}: 10,092 finite test assertion groups, zero failures,
including signed-$(2,2)$, odd Tseitin, Horn/2SAT/affine, two physically
encoded descendants, and exact source names. The raw/test artifact
and theorem are at
\path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-26_0224_LSPV-3_SOURCE_CUT_QBF_BARRIER128.tex}
and companion \texttt{repro.py}/\texttt{raw.json} with the same date and stem.
\textbf{CONDITIONAL}: the leaf-volume $\sum_i m_i^d\le M^{d-1}V$
and $c\le\max\{r,u+b+\lambda(d-1)\}$ require an independently
proved, clocked, universally available UNSAT certificate family;
the $d=1.802$ seed is not supplied by $P=NP$.
\textbf{OPEN}: affordable all-UNSAT coverage, source-proof selection costs,
ARC-EDGE-90, and $P\ne NP$. This result corrects no other

\paragraph{Source-Boundary-Affine-Helly129 (26 October 2026, 02:42 PDT): sharp affine versus unrestricted join coverage.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, mathematical peer review pending.}
For ordinary source-named proper signed three-CNF modules with literally
identical $b$ original shared variables, mutually fresh private names,
and independently certified affine projected boundary supports over
$\mathbb F_2$, every UNSAT conjunction has an UNSAT subjoin containing
at most $b+1$ entire modules, and this bound is sharp.
A streaming Gaussian basis on \emph{original checked} affine boundary
equation records returns at most $b+1$ source-row IDs whose XOR is
$0=1$, in $O(qb^2)$ bit-work and $O(b^2+b\log(q+2))$ elimination
workspace for $q$ explicit input rows, plus all charged extraction,
source verification, addressing and replay.
For \emph{general} Boolean boundary supports the sharp
entire-module bound is $2^b$, witnessed by source-preserving ordinary
3CNF modules individually excluding each boundary assignment.
Actual serialized SAT,SAT,UNSAT paths at $b=3,4,5$ for the affine
sharp family and $b=3,4$ for nonaffine sharp families were checked.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}: 3,217 assertions, zero failures, seed
12920261026; strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ minimally UNSAT $W_1$,
odd Tseitin, Horn, 2SAT, wrong private-source alias and cyclic
boundary-compatibility hostile controls pass.

source-proof identity, not a collection of UNSAT subformula leaves or
calls to the arbitrary fixed SAT decider $A$.
Every claim that an arbitrary projected source relation is affine
must have a separately checked source-preserving witness; generic
source-to-child lift checking remains $\Pi_2^P$-complete as in
SOURCE-CUT-QBF-BARRIER128. No bound on universal SAT certificate
discovery or source-name repair is proved. The leaf-volume inequality
$\sum_i m_i^d\le M^{d-1}V$ remains an upper bound conditional on
all-UNSAT coverage with charged proof parsing and guest access.
The near-boundary $(d,c)$ seed is not derived from $P=NP$.

% BEGIN LSPV-3 NOTE::SOURCE-PAIRED-AFFINE132::2026-10-26_0524
\paragraph{Adaptive source-identified UNSAT certificate progress, LSPV-3, 26 October 2026 (reports 130, 131, 132).}
Original-clause-identical adaptive branching admits authenticated residual two-CNF SCC/empty-clause terminals, even with unrelated surviving ternaries. The full source-pointer proof-stream construction is sound on all SAT sources and complete on all UNSAT sources without a favorable general certificate-length bound. The signed exact-$(2,2)$ minimally-UNSAT source $W_1$ has fifteen variables, twenty clauses and a five-variable source-SCC branching certificate with 32 complete leaves. Disjoint products show why a large global clause-hitting backdoor cannot force large arbitrary adaptive certificates.
\path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-26_0324_LSPV-3_BACKDOOR_SCC_CERT130.tex};
\path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-26_0348_LSPV-3_ADAPTIVE_SOURCE_POINTER_STREAM131.tex}.

The new \textbf{PROVED/EXACT RESTRICTED} source-paired characteristic-two terminal authenticates four original proper-ternary clause byte offsets for every XOR equation, checks a reciprocal perfect matching of all variable slots by original name and an odd sum of charges. Its acceptance implies UNSAT for \emph{every} ordinary source; SAT inputs reject all accepted-certificate candidates. A separate indexed, read-only random-access certificate tape permits $O((N+q)\operatorname{polylog}(N+P))$ verification time and $O(\log(N+P))$ work/index space for $P=O(q\log(N+q))$ proof bits. This bound has \emph{not} been transported to a one-way nondeterministic guess stream without paying readable-proof storage or proving a streaming mate checker. Explicit characteristic-two degree-three PC derivations are supported, not all-field PC claims.

A connected cubic ported-torus proper-3CNF family has $4r^2$ parity vertex modules, $6r^2$ variables, $16r^2$ clauses, primal treewidth $\Omega(r)$ and \emph{every proper subset of complete vertex equations satisfiable}; nevertheless it has $O(r^2)$ source-paired affine records. It is \emph{not} strict signed exact-$(2,2)$: each edge variable has four occurrences per sign. This falsifies an inference from large treewidth or absence of small inconsistent complete vertex-module subsets to long adaptive proof verification. The compact Node audit completed 2,447 assertion groups and the extended Python audit 33,835 groups without failures, including actual two-level ordinary descendants (K4 163 to 203 to 244 bytes), source alias/mate/offset attacks and independent signed exact-$(2,2)$ W1 controls. The descendants grow and are \emph{not} ARC contractors.
\path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-26_0524_LSPV-3_SOURCE_PAIRED_AFFINE132.tex};
\path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-26_0524_SOURCE_PAIRED_AFFINE132_repro.js};

% BEGIN LSPV-3 NOTE::PC-BRANCH-COLOUR134::2026-10-26_0731
\paragraph{PC-BRANCH-COLOUR134 (26 October 2026, LSPV-3, fully source-audited algebraic adaptive coverage firewall).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, mathematical peer review pending:} For explicit unshared binary original-name BRANCH trees with authentic EMPTY/PATH source-2CNF terminals, source-paired characteristic-two affine cancellation, and supplied original-source Resolution or fully encoded fixed-$\mathbb F_2$ polynomial-calculus-with-resolution (PCR) terminals, there exist infinitely many \emph{connected ordinary proper signed ternary} UNSAT sources of physical length $N$ for which every accepted proof has at least $2^{\Omega(N/\log N)}$ bits. The source family comes from all-field PC-degree-hard sparse 3-colouring graphs: split each binary clause $(a\vee b)$ into $(a\vee b\vee z)$ and $(a\vee b\vee\neg z)$ with a fresh name; restricting all $z=0$ exactly recovers the standard CNF and preserves the PCR minimum-degree lower bound. For any ancestor assignment $\alpha$ of depth $h$, multiplying a residual-source PCR refutation by the selector $\delta_\alpha$ yields a source refutation of degree at most $h+\max(3,k)$. Hence a shallow leaf requires high \emph{minimum} PCR degree and exponentially many explicit proof monomials; if all leaves are deep, complete both-branch coverage already forces exponentially many leaves. This \textbf{FALSIFIES} every polynomial all-UNSAT certificate-length cap for the declared grammar, extending LSPV-4 PATH-RESOLUTION-FIREWALL133 beyond plain Resolution/Tseitin to independently hard all-field graph-colouring cases. It does \emph{not} establish a SAT runtime lower bound or signed exact-$(2,2)$ hard-case transport.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 5,817 deterministic assertion groups, zero failures, two hash-identical runs, proper source byte/offset and F2/F5 arithmetic controls, signed exact-$(2,2)$ W1, and two genuine ordinary SAT PIN descendant levels (SAT,SAT,UNSAT) checked. \textbf{OPEN:} cutting planes, source-sharing DAGs, mixed-field terminals, exact $A$-assisted small children, universal near-boundary coNTIME coverage, a joint deterministic ARC contraction, ARC-EDGE-90, and $P$ versus $NP$. Guest-call resources, deterministic proof discovery, malformed certificate clocks and peak simultaneous space remain separately charged.

% BEGIN LSPV-3 NOTE::A-LEAF-SPECTRUM135::2026-10-26_0829
\paragraph{A-LEAF-SPECTRUM135 (26 October 2026, LSPV-3): source-subformula A-assisted leaf-spectrum barrier.}
\textbf{PROVED/RESTRICTED, independent mathematical review pending:}
Let $R_{\rm DT}(F)$ be the minimum leaf count of a complete original-variable source-clause conflict tree. For an explicit, unshared, source-named BRANCH certificate whose authenticated leaves use either source-original EMPTY clauses, source-subset residual 2CNF SCC implication paths, or calls to the one fixed total exact $A$ on actual residual original-clause subformulas $G_\ell$ of encoded lengths $m_\ell$, the exact expansion inequality holds:
\[
R_{\rm DT}(F)\le \sum_{\ell\in A}2^{v_\ell}
+\sum_{\ell\in SCC}(e_\ell+2)+|EMPTY|
\le P(2^{3M}+O(1)),
\]
where $v_\ell$ counts surviving active child names, $M=\max m_\ell$, $e_\ell$ counts authenticated implication-path edges, and $P$ is the complete explicit bit length. Each $A$ label is actually executed, not free; every accepted source-subformula UNSAT leaf can be replaced by exhaustive branching on only its surviving child names. The SCC leaves similarly have $O(e_\ell)$ source-clause conflict-tree expansions. Soundness holds for both SAT/UNSAT; unrestricted completeness has no affordable length bound.

\textbf{FALSIFIED in this declared grammar:} polynomial-length all-UNSAT coverage with $M\le N^\lambda$ for fixed $\lambda<1$. The published all-field polynomial-calculus-hard connected 3-colouring family, transferred to proper ordinary signed 3CNF by source-named binary-clause properization, has $R_{\rm DT}(F)\ge 2^{\Omega(N/\log N)}$. Since $M=o(N/\log N)$, every such explicit certificate has $P=2^{\Omega(N/\log N)}$, regardless of how fast $A$ decides its much shorter source-subformula children. The theorem does NOT cover arbitrary derived/equisatisfiable children, private existential variables, cutting planes, proof DAG sharing, or an exact signed-$(2,2)$ all-field hardness transfer. No arbitrary SAT machine lower bound follows.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 5,247 source-offset, two-real-level, both-label, signed exact-$(2,2)$ W1, odd Tseitin and SCC/path assertions passed; two byte-identical runs. \textbf{OPEN:} uniform affordable all-UNSAT coverage in broader grammar, a near-boundary small-space deterministic SAT seed, eligible solver-dependent ARC children, ARC-EDGE-90 and $P$ versus $NP$.
Verified LaTeX: \path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-26_0829_LSPV-3_A_LEAF_SPECTRUM135.tex};

% BEGIN LSPV-3 NOTE::SOURCE-LOCAL-RAM-TAPE-CERT136::2026-10-26_1617
\paragraph{Source-local signed exact-$(2,2)$ certificate and RAM/tape firewall (26 October 2026).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT RESTRICTED, independent review pending:}
The proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ equality family $E_m(a,b)$ has
$168m$ variables, $224m$ original signed ternary clauses and canonical
fixed-width physical length $N=\Theta(m\log(m+2))$.
For every mismatch $a_i\ne b_i$, a $40$-clause authenticated original-source
subset (nineteen $Q$ clauses plus one sign-forcing clause on each shore)
has a concrete $343$-record Resolution refutation, with $78$ source introductions,
$265$ checked resolvents and maximum displayed clause width seven.
Thus source pointers and proof records together need $O(\log N)$ bits.
When the mismatch is unique, both opposite forcing clauses are essential,
so every UNSAT original-source \emph{subformula} must include both.
This is a semantic source-local condition, not a polynomial
lower bound for arbitrary derived-child or guest-$A$ refutations.

\textbf{Distinct resource ledgers:}
The two clause positions are $\Theta(N)$ tape cells apart;
an M1 single-head read/write verifier must charge physical travel
and counts its initial input in tape footprint.
A fixed-$k$ sequential-tape Mk verifier uses $N^{1+o(1)}$ bit time
and $O(\log N)$ \emph{work-only} cells to scan and authenticate a supplied
constant-record refutation. A RAT indexed-input verifier instead has
polylogarithmic authenticated source access on the \emph{already validated}
canonical promise, with a full $N^{1+o(1)}$ parser on unrestricted input.
A total deterministic finder deciding whether to output a proof
also decides equality and inherits the previously verified M1/Mk
tape-transport decision lower bounds; a random-access finder
compares paired signs in $N^{1+o(1)}$ bit time and $O(\log N)$ workspace.
Source proof \emph{existence}, verification, deterministic acquisition
and co-nondeterministic certificate guessing are separate quantifiers.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 34 fully encoded cases, including 10 SAT and 24 UNSAT,
all 343-record actual proofs checked, signed occurrence counts and
two ordinary source-name descendants; Q has exactly 244 models.
Two reruns agree in SHA256
\texttt{9965d14ea4d15cf94d1e79a4c2dd13b4213af6f4d78a0ad4e6a4ebf0defc0e64}.
\textbf{OPEN:} all-UNSAT affordable certificates, all-input fixed-$A$ ARC,
ARC-EDGE-90, $P\ne NP$. No one-tape crossing bound is silently
transferred to RAT/FLMV, and P=NP offers unknown $K$, not $d=1.802$.

% BEGIN LSPV-3 NOTE::MOMENT-SPARSE-EXACT22-138::2026-10-26_1919
\paragraph{One-pass sparse-difference source-authenticated UNSAT certificates and Prouhet coverage obstruction (26 October 2026).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent review pending:}
For every fixed integer $q\ge1$ there is a deterministic
one-sequential-input-head, fixed-work-tape, source-authenticated
certificate locator on the signed exact-$(2,2)$ equality family
$D_m(a,b)$, with $30m$ original names, $40m$ original proper
three-clauses and fixed-width bit length
$N=\Theta(m\log(m+2))$.
Two original-name-disjoint $W_1$ sign-controlled modules per
coordinate enforce $\operatorname{SAT}(D_m(a,b))\iff a=b$.
With at most $q$ mismatching coordinates, the $2q$ signed
power-sum moments $M_r=\sum_i(a_i-b_i)i^r$ ($0\le r<2q$)
determine the entire mismatch set by Hankel/Vandermonde
reconstruction. In this \emph{fixed-$q$ promise} the deterministic
selector parses every original record, discovers a genuine
UNSAT $W_1$ source module and emits a fully authenticated
both-branch $32$-leaf SCC certificate in
$T_k=N^{1+o(1)}$ bit time and $S_k=O(\log N)$
visited fixed-tape work cells, with at most two additional
input-head reversals for independent source-sign rechecks.
This is a \emph{promise-family discovery theorem},
not an all-UNSAT certificate bound.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} extending the fixed $2q$-moment
synopsis to all UNSAT sources. For the Prouhet--Thue--Morse
complementary pair of length $2^{2q}$, all $2q$ moments
vanish exactly as on an equal SAT pair, yet every
coordinate mismatches.
\textbf{Model separation:} the full equality family retains
the native one-input-head HEADMOVE-1 bounds
$H(S+1)=\Omega(N^2/\log N)$ and
$(R+1)(S+1)=\Omega(N/\log N)$ on a same YES
execution; the q-sparse promise is not closed under the
cross-splice required by that argument. A separate
bounded-input-jump machine has conditional
$t+s+j\ge2$ on full equality, while an indexed RAT
compares the easy promise in $N^{1+o(1)}$ bit time
and $O(\log N)$ work/index bits. No one-tape
crossing bound is imported into RAT.
On M1 the initial read/write input $N$ counts
toward total footprint, whereas Mk and RAT work
space excludes read-only source input.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 368 actual fully encoded sources,
70 independently authenticated 32-leaf UNSAT proofs,
600 checked SCC edges per refutation,
2,310,351 counted assertion groups, zero failures,
two byte-identical raw runs; tests include connected
signed-$(2,2)$ splice, Horn, 2SAT, odd/even Tseitin,
SAT-forgery/source-ID rejection and two genuine ordinary
source-name descendants growing $1477\to1521\to1569$
ASCII bytes. Python is not a physical tape timing audit.
\textbf{OPEN:} uniform affordable all-ordinary-UNSAT
certificate coverage, source-aware eligible two-level
ordinary SAT contraction, any fixed-$A$ matched FLMV
successor, ARC-EDGE-90, $P\ne NP$.
The P=NP hypothesis supplies some unknown polynomial
$K$, never the illustrative near-boundary $d=1.802$ seed.

\paragraph{SOURCE-LOCAL-STREAM139: Connected local contradiction and one-forward acquisition firewall (26 October 2026, LSPV-3).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent review pending:}
From the fixed source-authenticated proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ UNSAT gadget
$W_1$ (15 original names and 20 clauses), cyclically transfer one
negative $x_1$ occurrence across $k\ge2$ copies while leaving all
other source literals unchanged.  The resulting $R_k$ is a connected
proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ UNSAT family with $15k$ names, $20k$
clauses, exact global 2CNF clause-hitting backdoor $5k$, but every
adjacent pair contains a \emph{39-original-clause} contradictory
subformula.  A fixed source-authenticated Resolution proof exists;
the reproducer independently checks a 200-line proof with 39
authenticated source premises and 161 Resolution lines.  Connectivity
and large global backdoor alone therefore do \emph{not} force large
local adaptive UNSAT certificates.

For a separate signed exact-$(2,2)$ separated-equality promise
$E_m(a,b)$, $\SAT(E_m(a,b))\Leftrightarrow(a=b)$, ordinary legal
fixed-width padded clause lines give the same physical Alice-prefix
cut for all $(a,b)$.  Any \emph{deterministic single-forward-pass}
fixed-$k$ sequential-input decider must retain
$\Omega(m)=\Omega(N/\log N)$ visited work cells, by injectivity of
$2^m$ complete Alice-prefix work configurations.  By contrast,
a nondeterministic $\lceil\log_2m\rceil+1$ bit certificate guesses a
mismatching source module, and a fully source-ID-authenticated
one-forward verifier uses $O(N\polylog N)$ bit time and
$O(\log N)$ work, rejects every ordinary SAT source and is complete
only on the declared UNSAT equality subfamily.  Indexed random access
allows an $N^{1+o(1)}$-time, $O(\log N)$-work deterministic
\emph{promise} comparator, without transfer of tape crossings.
Actual two ordinary PIN descendants preserve source identity but do
not shrink the logical input length.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 18,354,033 extended checks, zero failures,
340 exhaustive and 72 randomized equality sources, and six real
two-level descendants; the GitHub companion reproducer is an
independent compact audit of the same formal constructions.
\textbf{OPEN:} all-UNSAT uniformly affordable certificate coverage,
unknown-$K$ source-aware ARC contraction, ARC-EDGE-90, and $P\ne NP$.

% BEGIN LSPV-3 NOTE::INDEXED-LOCATOR-GAP140::2026-10-26_2119
\paragraph{INDEXED-LOCATOR-GAP140 (26 October 2026, 21:19 PDT): logarithmic local certificates versus indexed-RAM deterministic source-probe acquisition.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent mathematical review pending:}
For the complete valid separated-equality signed exact-$(2,2)$ proper-3CNF promise $D_m(a,b)$ with $30m$ names, $40m$ clauses, and $N=\Theta(m\log m)$ encoded bits, SAT holds iff $a=b$.
Every UNSAT promise source, including arbitrary Hamming difference, has one genuine source-ID-authenticated $W_1$ subformula and a uniform 32-leaf five-branch 2CNF/SCC certificate of $O(\log N)$ encoded bits.
Yet every \emph{total deterministic indexed bit-RAM or RAT} deciding both labels and constructing valid UNSAT proofs must inspect at least $m=\Omega(N/\log N)$ disjoint source-sensitive positions on the equal SAT baseline and, for one singleton-defect UNSAT input, before accepting. Even direct contiguous $O(\log N)$-bit RAM-word reads can cover only $O(1)$ defect groups per probe, hence require $\Omega(m)$ reads. A matching $O(m\polylog N)$-bit-time, $O(\log N)$-work indexed promise algorithm exists; thus this is a \emph{near-linear input-query lower bound}, not superlinear general SAT hardness or peak-space exclusion.
A separate connected proper-3CNF family $G_m$ adds nonvacuous four-clause equality bridges between every adjacent anchor, has $34m-2$ names and $48m-4$ clauses, stays SAT iff $a=b$, and retains the same constant source-local UNSAT proof. Its source is \textbf{not} signed exact-$(2,2)$ because bridges change anchor occurrence quotas. Distinguish from already published connected signed exact-$(2,2)$ $R_k$ of SOURCE-LOCAL-STREAM139.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 302,646 explicit reproducible assertion groups, 168 actual serialized two-level sources, 140 source-authenticated UNSAT certificates, 84 connected controls, correct SAT witnesses and singleton-defect address sensitivity; zero failures. Supplied index verification is polylog work only on the canonical well-formed promise; full ordinary malformed-input parsing and deterministic proof discovery remain paid. One-input-head crossing/reversal surfaces are kept separate from indexed RAM; no free $A$ calls, no all-input semantic lift, no child-size contraction.
\textbf{OPEN:} uniformly affordable \emph{every ordinary UNSAT} certificate family, arbitrary-$K$ solver-dependent source-aware ARC, ARC-EDGE-90, and $P\ne NP$.

\paragraph{ARC-PINNED-CIRCUIT-OUTPUT-FLOOR131 (26 October 2026, 04:41 PDT): restricted fixed-pin witness-circuit output floor and guest-read firewall.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT within LSPV-2's declared fixed-record, explicitly serialized signed exact-$(2,2)$ construction:}
Writing $B=m[1+3(\lceil\log_2(q+1)\rceil+1)]$ for the \emph{fixed-record} input bits, distinct from the ordinary SAT input length $N$, and $G$ for its fixed Boolean-gate count, independently substituting the two $Q_{15}/J$ levels gives
\[
\begin{aligned}
m_2&=105616G+33856B+5888q+95680,\\
n_2&=79212G+25392B+4416q+71760,\\
3m_2&=4n_2.
\end{aligned}
\]
For consecutive names $1,\ldots,n_2$, canonical uncompressed signed exact-$(2,2)$ DIMACS printing requires four occurrences of each identifier, hence at least
\[
M_2\ge32\sum_{i=1}^{n_2}\operatorname{digits}_{10}(i)
 \ge32\sum_{i=1}^{25392B}\operatorname{digits}_{10}(i)
 =\Omega(B\log B)\quad\text{physical output bits}.
\]
When a separately specified ordinary source encoding has $B=\Theta(N)$, the explicit target cannot be a strictly sublinear-length ordinary SAT child. Source encoding, parsing, names, headers, signs, and actual writes must be charged. This is an \emph{output-length restriction on this gadget/compiler}, not a general SAT time lower bound or a ban on different semantic reductions.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, adversarial guest access:}
For any fixed uniform exact ordinary-SAT decider $A$ with one simultaneous resource pair $(d,s)$, $d\ge1$, the program $A_{\mathrm{scan}}$ first reads all $L$ physical input bits and then runs $A$. It retains the same exponent upper bounds (up to indexed-bit logarithms) while making $\Omega(L)$ actual input reads. Thus 16 source-sensitive output signs per fixed-record input bit do not bound \emph{all} guest reads or confer cheap virtual access. A polynomial-length circuit witness verifier $\exists z\,V(x,z)$ is not a full nonuniform SAT decision circuit $C_N(x)$.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:}
Standalone zero-dependency exact-arithmetic Python passed $1{,}072$ deterministic assertions on eight fixed $(q,m)$ controls, zero failures. For $q=3,m=1$ the verified symbolic count is $G=1824$, $m_2=193095488$; for $q=3,m=8$ it is $G=16888$, $m_2=1786464832$. These are \emph{virtual symbolic counts}, not fully emitted level-two formulas, guest SAT evaluations, or FLMV machine measurements.
The primary FLMV fixed target remains $t<241/200$ and $s<11642479/23232400$ for one same-machine all-input ordinary-SAT algorithm. Arbitrary polynomial-time SAT existence does not establish a $d=901/500$ small-space seed; certificates and circuits cannot be transferred across unverified model/quantifier boundaries.

\paragraph{ARC-CLAUSE-LENGTH-FIREWALL130 (26 October 2026, 04:06 PDT): restricted explicit-output length barrier and two-level source-coherent control.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted canonical DIMACS architecture:}
If a proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ ternary-CNF output physically
preserves at least one distinct output clause position per source
clause, consider the source family
$F_m=\bigwedge_{j=1}^{m}(x_{3j-2}\vee x_{3j-1}\vee x_{3j})$,
$m\equiv0\pmod4$.  Its encoded bit length is
$N=\Theta(m\log m)$.  An output with $m'\ge m$
has $4n'=3m'$, with four printed occurrences of each
consecutive identifier $1,\dots,n'$.  Hence merely printing
its decimal variable names costs
\[
N'\ge32\sum_{i=1}^{3m/4}\mathrm{digits}_{10}(i)
=\Omega(m\log m)=\Omega(N)
\]
\emph{bits}. Thus this explicitly printed, clause-accountable
architecture cannot guarantee a worst-input child length
$N^{\lambda+o(1)}$ for any constant $\lambda<1$.
This is a \emph{syntactic output-size barrier}, not a SAT time lower bound;
an unrestricted semantic policy can recognize and remove this easy source.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, conditional FLMV numeric check:}
Corollary~5.8 permits a variable rational $c$; the general
curve test was already in the earlier project
\texttt{ARCARCHIVECURVE90}/\texttt{ARC-OVERHEAD-SPACE-PHASE1}
corpus. A hypothetical \emph{single} all-input exact matched-machine
SAT decider with exponents $(t,s)=(11/10,7/10)$ misses the fixed
$t<241/200$, $s<11642479/23232400$ rectangle, but would violate
FLMV at $c=111/100$, since
\[
\beta(111/100)=1742369/2464200,\quad
c-t=1/100,\quad
\beta(c)-s=17429/2464200>0.
\]
No such SAT decider is constructed. This prior-known curve does not
change the user's fixed ARC proof objective or establish an ARC successor.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL, honest ordinary-child SAT/UNSAT:
} Append fresh satisfiable signed exact-$(2,2)$ four-clause
$G$ modules at each of \emph{two} actual descendant levels
to disjoint unions of the SAT source $G$ and UNSAT source $W_1$.
All levels preserve proper signed exact-$(2,2)$,
SAT/UNSAT truth, complete headers, identifier renumbering and

Standalone JavaScript passed $36{,}635$ exact deterministic assertions,

\[
\sum_i T_A(m_i)\le N^{h_d+o(1)},\qquad

\]
for finitely many uniform polynomial-size classes with

This can strictly improve the earlier loose aggregate
$VM^{d-1}$ bound; all child selection, certificate acquisition,
source and indexed access, actual child writes, validation,
failed routes, retained answers, and peak ancestor/buffer space
must still be charged on \emph{one} realization.
A properly indexed \emph{counted work-tape} child buffer allows
random-access guest simulation without a mandatory repeated linear
scan, but free reuse of the one-way output tape is forbidden.

A same-call implementation retaining one answer cell per

\paragraph{ARC-INDEX-CIRCUIT-GAP128 (26 October 2026, 01:49 PDT): indexed-input to circuit-size model firewall.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted:} The fixed-layout INDEX function
$\operatorname{INDEX}_{L}(a,z)=z_a$, $L=2^k$, has an
indexed-read-only-input random-access deterministic decision procedure in
$O(k)$ counted bit-time with $O(k)$ writable/index bits, but every
fan-in-two Boolean \emph{decision circuit} for this restricted INDEX function
has at least $L-1$ internal gates. The latter follows from full
data-coordinate sensitivity and counting input-to-output connected DAG
edges. Thus importing a blanket $O(T\log T)$ multitape circuitization
bound for a sublinear-time \emph{indexed-input} machine without charging
input selection/layout is \textbf{FALSIFIED as a model transfer}; this
does not contradict Pippenger--Fischer's theorem.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT off-promise safeguard:} A proper signed ternary-CNF
INDEX promise construction has exactly $n=4L+3k+1$ variables,
$m=8L+4k$ clauses and $N=3w+1+3m(w+1)$ physical encoded bits,
$w=\lceil\log_2(\max\{n,m\}+1)\rceil$.
Its promised SAT label is $z_a$, but flipping just the second copy
of one polarity bit in a selected leaf's four-clause forcing gadget
changes a valid ordinary UNSAT 3CNF into SAT without changing the
k address signs or the single selected sign queried by the
cheap promise-only recognizer. A total exact ordinary SAT decider
cannot rely on this unverified source promise.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} The published standalone Node.js reproducer passed
$1{,}285$ deterministic assertions with zero failures: $136$ actual
serialized proper-3CNF source instances, $14$ one-physical-bit
off-promise SAT flips and $272$ correctly labeled two-level ordinary
child tests. These short children are produced \emph{after} solving
the promise source, so their size contraction is \textbf{NOT} a
noncircular ARC-EDGE-90 successor.

\textbf{Resource/model quantifiers:} $N$ always measures full ordinary
SAT encoded \emph{input bits}; each $(t,s)\in E$ needs one fixed uniform
all-input deterministic total exact SAT machine with simultaneous
bit-time and writable/index-space bounds in the FLMV indexed-input
random-access model. Charge all child headers/bodies, selection, access,
generation, certificates, failed routes and live ancestor/buffer peaks.
For volume $V\le N^{b+o(1)}$, maximum child
$M\le N^{\lambda+o(1)}$ and guest exponent $d\ge1$, the work inequality
$\sum_i m_i^d\le VM^{d-1}$ permits
$t'\le\max\{r,u+b+\lambda(d-1)\}$ only when $u$ is a
\emph{per-guest-probe} generation exponent; when $u$ already bounds
total generation, use $\max\{r,u,b+\lambda(d-1)\}$.
Do not conflate these interpretations or borrow separate machines'
time and space.

\textbf{Verified strict margins:}
$\beta(241/200)=11642479/23232400$;

\paragraph{ARC-NAND-PATH-SPACE127 (26 October 2026, 01:16 PDT): source-exact uniform NAND DAG-to-formula bridge and strict square-root-space firewall.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted, independently reviewable:}
For a fan-in-two NAND circuit family of depth
$D_N=(c+o(1))\log_2N$ with polynomially indexed gate IDs
and a \emph{fully charged} polylogarithmic-time local gate/predecessor
descriptor, recursively unfold sharing into a NAND formula.
Given a path, at most $D_N$ predecessor lookups recover its
NAND gate/input leaf in $\log^{O(1)}N$ time, preserving the exact
function and depth. The input-aware NAND-formula uniformity
hypothesis in Bathie--Williams ITCS 2024, Theorem 1.4, is therefore
satisfied under these \emph{strong locality} premises, so its
published logarithmic-depth lower bound applies. The claim does
\emph{not} apply to arbitrary nonuniform DAGs, uncharged dynamic
source selectors, polynomial-depth time circuitizations or
per-instance SAT encodings. Its conceptual DAG-unfolding step is
classical, not a new unrestricted SAT lower bound.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT syntactic countercontrol:} The two-input
Fibonacci NAND DAG
$g_k=\mathrm{NAND}(g_{k-1},g_{k-2})$ has $k-1$ internal gates,
$2(k-1)$ gate-input wires, but a literal \emph{unfolding}
requires $F_{k+1}$ leaves and $2F_{k+1}-1$ formula nodes.
At $k=30$ those counts are respectively $29,58$ and
$1{,}346{,}269,2{,}692{,}537$. Explicit output needs all
corresponding writes, whereas a polylogarithmic virtual locator
avoids emission but charges each later access. The underlying
two-input function is easy and may have a tiny equivalent formula;
this is \emph{not} a Boolean function lower bound.

\textbf{SOURCE-VERIFIED strict Shalunov/FLMV firewall:}
Shalunov (2025), Theorem 1.2 gives \emph{space only}
$O(\sqrt{z\log z})$ for evaluating a supplied explicit size-$z$
circuit, not a matching evaluation time or free uniform generation.
For physical circuit size $z=N^{q+o(1)}$, this upper bound has
nominal space exponent $q/2$. For it \emph{alone} to guarantee
strict numerical entry below the selected FLMV space boundary,
one needs
\[
q<2\beta(241/200)=\frac{11642479}{11616200}
=1.002262271655\ldots.
\]
In particular $q=129/100$ gives space exponent $129/200=0.645$
outside the rectangle. Even the separately \emph{conditional and
unproved} all-input SAT decision-circuit threshold
$q=811801/561801$ from the $d=901/500$ \emph{extra} seed
gives nominal evaluation space $811801/1123602\approx0.722499$,
outside the selected box. A negative upper-bound margin is
\emph{not a lower bound on optimal circuit evaluation space}.
The extra seed is not furnished by $P=NP$.
Even the favorable $q<2\beta$ numerical case leaves time,
uniform circuit generation, model conversion, input access and
the same-machine SAT guarantee \textbf{OPEN}.

\textbf{Adaptive children, adopted prior exact lemma:}
If a correct all-input procedure calls a uniform exact SAT machine
of time exponent $d\ge1$ on real ordinary children $m_i$, with
$V=\sum_i m_i\le N^{b+o(1)}$ and
$M=\max_i m_i\le N^{\lambda+o(1)}$, then
$\sum_i m_i^d\le VM^{d-1}$ and
$t'\le\max\{r,u,b+\lambda(d-1)\}$ \emph{only when}
$N^{r+o(1)}$ counts all local work and $N^{u+o(1)}$ bounds
\emph{aggregate} source/probe/locator/replay costs. The joint
space proof must separately count all simultaneously live
ancestor buffers, generator states, guest space, eager child
materialization, retained answers, indexes and output bodies,
at \emph{both} genuine ordinary-child levels. An implementation
that actually writes/retains $N^{0.9}$ cells exceeds the FLMV
space rectangle even when its children are tiny; this is a
ledger counterexample, not an unrestricted SAT lower bound.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} Standalone, dependency-free Node.js and
exact BigInt JSON audit passed 172 deterministic assertions
with no failures, checking uniform-path recursion versus direct
NAND evaluation, Fibonacci output counts, adaptive volume
inequality and all strict rational margins.

\paragraph{ARC-R15-LENGTH-AUDIT126 (26 October 2026, 00:36 PDT): exact rank-one size firewall for two locally accessible signed exact-$(2,2)$ levels.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT arithmetic under the LSPV-2 one-level source-count formula, peer audit of the gadget still pending:}
for the adopted signed proper exact-$(2P,2N)$ regularizer $R_{15}$,
\[
\binom{n_1}{m_1}
=\underbrace{\begin{pmatrix}48&33\\64&44\end{pmatrix}}_{A}
\binom nm,\qquad
\det A=0,\quad A^2=92A,\quad A^h=92^{h-1}A\ (h\ge1).
\]
Every image has $3m_1=4n_1$, and for a balanced source $3m=4n$ each fixed further level multiplies both counts by $92$.
Two actual levels give $(n_2,m_2)=(4416n+3036m,5888n+4048m)$.
Under the complete binary grammar
$B(n,m)=4+2\lceil\log_2(\max(n,m)+1)\rceil+
3m(1+\lceil\log_2(n+1)\rceil)$,
every fixed depth on a cofinal dense fixed-incidence family
has \emph{ordinary encoded} length $\Theta(N)$, not
$N^{1/4+o(1)}$ or $N^{1/2+o(1)}$.
Thus a two-level \emph{cheap virtual output-bit locator} is
\emph{not itself} a power-short hereditary ARC contractor.
An eager grandchild representation requires $\Omega(N)$ writable
bits; a lazy virtual view saves that buffer but pays every source/index
probe and does not change the guest's input-length exponent.
The false ARC substitution $\lambda=1/4$ into
$\sum_i m_i^d\le (\max_i m_i)^{d-1}\sum_i m_i$ is
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED} for this construction only,
not an unconditional lower bound against another SAT algorithm.

these are arithmetic tests, not full gadget-SAT checks or a

At the strict published FLMV rectangle, the positive

The extra unproved seed $d=901/500$ gives conditional

\emph{only with their separate correctly modeled premises}.
The local regularizer is not a complete all-input SAT decision circuit.
