\paragraph{ARC-SELECTOR-ORACLE-AUDIT125 (25 October 2026, 23:36 PDT): exact restricted source-index policy toll and independently read circuit theorem.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, pending independent review:}
For a virtual bit $F_k(z,a)=a_{\oplus_i z_i}$ with two available data
bits $(a_0,a_1)=(0,1)$, each output is a one-coordinate copy after
the selector is known, yet every deterministic exact bit-probe computation
with no precomputed advice about $z$ must query \emph{all} $k$ selector
positions on \emph{every} source. Otherwise flipping an unqueried selector
bit preserves its transcript but flips the output. Thus fixed-literal
coordinate dependence alone does \emph{not} prove a low-cost inverse
occurrence/address selector. This is a deliberately easy affine/parity
\emph{restricted control}, not an ordinary SAT time lower bound and
not a refutation of LSPV-2's fixed-incidence Q15 gadget. A paid
one-time parity cache may amortize repeated virtual bits.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} A dependency-free physical ordinary-CNF regression
generated and parsed 8,188 SAT/UNSAT parent, child and grandchild
triples, each strictly decreasing in actual encoded byte length, with
90,112 selector single-bit perturbations and 1,171,433 deterministic
assertions, all passing. It accounted for 4,897,450 ASCII output
characters, \emph{not} machine-level bit-time or hardware flips.
The restricted parent is parity-recognizable, so decidability before
shortening remains noncontractive.

the conditional circuit threshold is exactly

 c<q_*=\frac{811801}{561801}=1.4449974279148667\ldots.

The original survey explicitly discusses substituting a \emph{stronger}

children for both labels \emph{without} contracting parent computation.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 264 assertion-accounted checks, 33 two-level
SAT/UNSAT cases; these are not physical FLMV TM traces.

\textbf{KNOWN THEOREMS with restricted models:}
FLMV's strict forbidden box remains
$t<241/200$ and
$s<11642479/23232400=\beta(241/200)$.
The conditional ideal $(6/5,1/2)$ cell has exact margins
$1/200$ and $26279/23232400$.
Williams's corrected polynomially bounded time-space \emph{product}
Theorem 4.11 implies a fixed same-machine $t+s<\varphi$
forbidden region, $\varphi=(1+\sqrt5)/2$, \emph{not}
$t+s<2\cos(\pi/7)$; the cosine SAT bound has an
$N^{o(1)}$-space hypothesis. Van Melkebeek Theorem 1.3
uses \emph{coNTIME(SAT) and deterministic small-space SAT}
on separate machines under an explicit two-algorithm disjunction.
For the \emph{extra} $d=901/500$ small-space seed,
the conditional coNTIME time threshold is
$c<651000/361301=1.801821749732\ldots$; $P=NP$ supplies
neither that seed nor the requisite companion speedup.
Theorem 6.3 involves \emph{nonuniform all-input SAT decision
circuits} and \emph{$\Sigma_2$SAT} small-space algorithms;
one-instance $q=1.29$ representation does not suffice.
The proposed $q=1.444997427915$ composition is \textbf{PENDING}
exact source verification. Bathie--Williams NAND
\emph{formula} uniform depth, Pippenger--Fischer multitape
circuitization, Allender--Kouck\'y TC$^0$ \emph{wires},
Borodin depth, and Shalunov 2025 explicit circuit-value
\emph{space} cannot be imported as unrestricted same-machine
SAT time/space bounds.
Guessing a SAT witness is not automatically an
UNSAT certificate for coNTIME, and printing any
deterministic adaptive certificate has charged cost.

all-ordinary-input adaptive certificate/child/circuit contractor with

\paragraph{ARC-PROBE-STREAM-FUSION123 (25 October 2026, 11:36 PDT): fully charged policy-selector guest probes, online stream DP and two-level parent-call firewall.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only under a declared globally correct A-dependent online scoped-stream producer interface; independent review pending:}
fix ONE uniform total exact ordinary-SAT guest $A$ with joint $(t,s)\in E$.
Suppose the fully paid producer emits $M\le N^{\mu+o(1)}$
serialized scoped-event bits of width at most $\delta\log_2N+o(\log N)$,
using background time $N^{r+o(1)}$, counted live
workspace $N^{\sigma+o(1)}$, and at most
$N^{b+o(1)}$ sequential guest calls on \emph{actual ordinary}
virtual children of length $N^{\rho+o(1)}$.
Each input-bit service costs $N^{g+o(1)}$ time and
$N^{\eta+o(1)}$ live workspace, and a separate \emph{proved}
guest probe bound is $L^{q+o(1)}$.
By intercepting the output bits at issuance rather than using a
write-only tape as free readable storage, a same-machine streaming
frontier DP realizes the conditional joint envelopes
\[
\begin{aligned}
t'_{\rm lazy}&\le\max\{r,\mu+\delta,b+\rho t,b+\rho q+g\},&
s'_{\rm lazy}&\le\max\{\sigma,\delta,\rho s,\eta\},\\
t'_{\rm eager}&\le\max\{r,\mu+\delta,b+\rho+g,b+\rho t\},&
s'_{\rm eager}&\le\max\{\sigma,\delta,\rho,\rho s,\eta\}.
\end{aligned}
\]
All failed selections, validation, input/output headers, named joins,
repeated source/index reads, changed \emph{and unchanged} writes,
ancestors, buffers and retained caches must be included in the
declared quantities, or added separately to the appropriate
simultaneous peak. No favorable guest probe $q<t$ follows from $E$;
the previous GUEST-PROBE-QUOTIENT117 and PIVOTAL-INPUT-FIREWALL121
remain applicable. With conservative $q=t$, a \emph{hypothetical fixed
reusable} lazy successor $f(t)=\max\{c,b+\rho t+g\}$,
$h(s)=\max\{z,\rho s\}$ has the exact two-level accounting identity
\[
f(f(t))=\max\{c,b+\rho c+g,b(1+\rho)+\rho^2t+(1+\rho)g\},
\qquad h(h(s))=\max\{z,\rho^2s\}.
\]
The $(1+\rho)g$ nested access tax is not free. The numerically
favorable illustrative cell
$(b,\rho,g,c,z)=(3/5,1/2,0,21/20,1/2)$
would reach the strict FLMV region from finite inflated diagonals
\emph{if} such an exact, all-input machine-producing compiler
existed. It has \emph{not} been constructed.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT counterexample to a false compression inference:}
for any total exact guest $A$, first pay a full parent-scale $A(F)$ call,

Even a width-one named stream can be produced from this answer,
but the original $A(F)$ has \emph{already} been executed;
a parent-scale call is noncontractive in the charged ledger.

number of opaque service calls is
\[

programs that compute summaries or avoid the prescribed transcript.

For the deliberately \emph{opaque} accessor with

uses $N^{3/5+o(1)}$ live bits, beyond $d_\star$.

not a new element of $E$ or a FLMV contradiction.}

\paragraph{ARC-EXT-SELECTOR-NOGO109: matched-model exclusion of extensional shorter SAT selectors (25 October 2026, 15:36 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT as a strictly scoped two-channel corollary of FLMV and earlier MODELBRIDGE-0011; COMPUTATIONAL finite controls; ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP OPEN.}
For one fixed total uniform deterministic ordinary-SAT oracle controller using only exact \emph{SAT truth bits}, suppose the fully charged controller makes $N^{a+o(1)}$ child queries of actual length $N^{\lambda+o(1)}$, $N^{b+o(1)}$ adaptive selector queries of actual length $N^{\rho+o(1)}$, with $\lambda,\rho<1$, nonoracle bit work $N^{r+o(1)}$ and simultaneously live writable/index bits $N^{h+o(1)}$ including source accesses, all output headers/payloads and buffers, unsuccessful trials, selector discovery, caches and ancestors. \[
(t',s')\le\left(\max\left\{1,r,\frac{a}{1-\lambda},\frac{b}{1-\rho}\right\},
                    \max\{h,\lambda,\rho\}\right)
\]
at exponent scale. At the ARC parameters $a=3/5$, $\lambda=1/2$, $r=11/10$ and $h=\max\{1/2,\rho\}$, this \emph{rules out} an all-input extensional controller whenever $b<(241/200)(1-\rho)$ and $\rho<11642479/23232400$: it would unconditionally construct a forbidden FLMV machine with $t'<241/200$ and $s'<\beta(241/200)$. In particular $b=3/5,\rho=1/2$ is impossible for this controller model, \emph{without assuming} $P=NP$; leaving that numeric test inconclusive does not prove feasibility. A current-scale SAT call fails strict downward elimination and retains the unknown starting solver exponent. Intensional program inspection, timing, hash/clock traces and equivalent-wrapper distinctions are outside this theorem and require an explicitly hypothesis-dependent, uniform, same-realization charged construction. The exact finite audit used 1581 rational selector pairs, 76 two-level exponent expansions, and nine actual two-level disjoint ordinary-CNF SAT/UNSAT controls ($p=256$: 1792-clause SAT / 1793-clause UNSAT; 16 then 256 child calls); all passed, but that easy promise class is not unrestricted SAT. The zero-failure checks do not prove the FLMV separation, which is the cited published theorem. The first missing implication remains an all-input \emph{intensional} cofinal ARC successor from each hypothetical fixed polynomial SAT program, with every ordinary child eligible and every access/peak-resource cost paid; no such proof exists. The retired $t+s<2\cos(\pi/7)$ universal endpoint stays retired.

For variable-disjoint UNSAT CNFs \(F_i\) with chosen clauses \(C_i=(\ell_i\vee D_i)\), replace each chosen literal occurrence by \(\ell_{\pi(i)}\) under an arbitrary permutation \(\pi\). Every assignment satisfying \(F_i\setminus\{C_i\}\) must falsify \emph{all} literals of its old \(C_i\); thus every permuted clause is false and the new formula is UNSAT. Moving one same-polarity literal of each chosen positive clause preserves all signed M22 degree quotas, provided unique proper supports are checked. Apply to \(p\ge2\) disjoint copies of D\"ocker's connected monotone 198-variable/264-clause UNSAT source \(U\) at clause \((x_{194}\vee x_{196}\vee x_{197})\), replacing its first literal by \(x_{194}\) of the next copy cyclically. The result \(W_p\in\mathcal C_\star\) has \(k=198p,m=264p,m_+=m_-=132p\), all \(q=132p\) positive supports unmatched, and a connected incidence graph, with each original source block's selected clause changed. Crucially, an actual checked 198-bit witness satisfies \(U\setminus\{C\}\) while making all \(194,196,197\) false, so there is \emph{no intact unsatisfiable original block}. The UNSAT proof is inherited from the published \(U\) and the exact port-permutation lemma, not independently inferred from finite enumeration.
For a selected block \(i\), exact source-coherent existential projection over its other 197 names yields the two boundary unit clauses \(\neg x_{194,i}\wedge x_{194,i+1}\). One real ordinary-CNF level replaces 264 original clause records with two units, leaving \(264(p-1)+2\) clauses. A second neighboring block yields \(264(p-2)+4\) clauses including \(x_{194,i+1}\wedge\neg x_{194,i+1}\), hence a charged unit-conflict check produces the constant empty-clause UNSAT CNF. The first descendant is still ordinary length \(\Theta(N)\), both descendants leave the strict monotone exact-$(2,2)$ target grammar, and this publicly recognisable family has a constant-size 527-clause two-block obstruction. Matched connected \(\mathcal C_\star\) SAT controls \(Z_{33p}\) from Role 1 share \(k,m,q\) and a public \(111000\) witness. This is a countercontrol against pointwise potential initialized only from these source statistics, \emph{not} a worst-case lower bound or a favorable all-input ARC successor. The fixed-template recognizer charges all indexed source reads, grammar/template checks, policy choices, fallback and program/addressing work, and can run \(O(N\operatorname{polylog}N)\) bit operations and \(O(\log N)\) work/index bits on the canonical special family; the independent executed checkers materialize formulas and do not measure FLMV physical steps. \(R_{\rm raw}=197\) names at each projection, while enumeration-specific \(R_{\rm search}\) is undefined. \paragraph{M22-DP-COVER106: a sharp one-third original-pivot threshold is necessary but insufficient (25 October 2026, 12:36 KST).}

The original U greedy clause cover after $k/3=66$ selected names still leaves 40 clauses, while $F_{66}$ has an exact 66-name cover; the greedy number is not a minimum or lower bound on arbitrary SAT work. \paragraph{M22-PC22-PORT-PROBE104: source-rank locality obstruction for one historical compiler (25 October 2026, 10:40 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the named source-bit query model; COMPUTATIONAL finite confirmation; independent mathematical review pending.}
The original \path{experiments/review/cp22_audit.py} compiler \texttt{compile22} maps a proper ordinary 3CNF with $m$ clauses to a \emph{signed}, not clausewise-monotone, proper exact-$(2^+,2^-)$ CNF with $72m$ variables and $96m$ clauses. It sorts source variable names, allocates $2d_F(x)$ consecutive clone IDs to each original source name $x$, and emits cyclic padding edges in that global order. This creates a specific source-degree prefix dependency.
For $q\ge1$, consider the ordinary positive 3CNF
\[
F_q=(1\vee3\vee4)\wedge(1\vee2\vee3)\wedge(2\vee3\vee4)
\wedge\bigwedge_{j=0}^{q-1}(1\vee3\vee(j+5)).
\]
At one fixed addressed output-padding field (zero-based clause $8(q+3)+2(q+2)-1$), its positive second literal magnitude is $1$. Changing any single trailing clause first literal from $1$ to $4$ changes the \emph{same output field} to $2q+5$, without changing source or target header/length. Since these $q$ variants differ in disjoint source variable-ID fields, deterministic stateless source-bit query evaluation of the high bit of that output field must read at least $q=\Omega(N/\log N)$ input bits on $F_q$, by exact adaptive transcript indistinguishability. Appending the eight contradictory signed clauses on three fresh variables gives an identical \emph{UNSAT} control. This proves only a \emph{named historical compiler output-bit locality floor}, not all-decider SAT time; an alternative locally ordered compiler or a paid precomputed rank directory may evade it. A source-structure recognizer solves the controls cheaply. Neither CP22LIFT1's integer Cutting Planes derivations nor this syntax-probe bound supplies an all-input ARC resource contraction or a post-acquisition universal potential.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} full independent local audit passed 3296 assertion-accounted checks with zero failures over 24 SAT/UNSAT parameter cases, all one-field changes and exact smaller compiled outputs; actual two-level controls have clause counts $4\to384\to36864$ (SAT) and $12\to1152\to110592$ (UNSAT). The standalone GitHub peer reproducer is separately published for independent execution. The historical $t+s<2\cos(\pi/7)$ universal endpoint remains \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}. ARC-EDGE-90, LOCAL-COOK-TAGGED-SIGNED22-1, DIRECT-POST and P versus NP remain \textbf{OPEN}. Canonical $L_\star$ excludes only \emph{fully} complementary-clause-paired strict monotone $(2,2)$ formulas, not individual pairs.

\paragraph{M22-DP-TWOLEVEL103: exact two-stage ordinary-CNF elimination and potential firewall (25 October 2026, 09:36 KST).}
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM} (classical Davis--Putnam existential resolution); \textbf{PROVED/EXACT} its declared M22-specific fixed-two-stage size/degree specialization, with independent mathematical review pending; \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL} finite SAT and source-dependent UNSAT controls. \textbf{ARC-EDGE-90, DIRECT-POST and P versus NP remain OPEN.} For each valid strict clausewise-monotone exact-$(2^+,2^-)$ source $F$, one pivot $x$ has precisely two positive tails $A_1,A_2$ and two negative tails $B_1,B_2$. Replace its four parent clauses by all non-tautological $A_i\lor B_j$, computing $\exists x F$ exactly on every remaining source assignment. With $h_x\le4$ surviving pairs, the new ordinary CNF has $m'=m-4+h_x\le m$ clauses and $L'=3m-12+4h_x$ literal occurrences; each new surviving clause is a proper \emph{mixed-sign} four-clause, so this transformation generally \emph{leaves} $\mathcal C_\star$. A greedy independent set in the original primal degree-eight graph eliminates $q_1\ge k/9$ names at once with disjoint parent clauses, $v_1\le8k/9$, $m_1\le m$, width at most four, and each remaining polarity-degree at most four. A second greedy set in the degree-at-most-24 intermediate primal graph eliminates $q_2\ge v_1/25$, giving $v_2\le64k/75$, $m_2\le m_1+8q_2$, width at most six. Both stages are exact full existential projections, \emph{not} merely SAT-bit-preserving neutral child repairs. A straightforward indexed, materialized two-stage machine pays $O(N\operatorname{polylog}N)$ elementary bit work and $O(N)$ writable/index bits, including source reads, pivot discovery, generated resolvents, headers, deduplication, live child buffers and ancestors. Its ordinary children have $O(N)$ encoded length, \emph{not} $N^{\lambda+o(1)}$ for $\lambda<1$, and its peak writable space is far outside the strict FLMV box $t<241/200$, $s<11642479/23232400$. One fixed machine must attain any simultaneous exponents; the retired cosine product endpoint does not apply.

For canonical eligible SAT $Y$, the two actual projections have $(v,m,L)=(6,8,24)\to(5,5,16)\to(4,4,14)$; paired SAT $H$ is intentionally outside $\mathcal C_\star$ and has $(6,8,24)\to(4,4,16)\to(0,0,0)$. On the original eligible published UNSAT D\"ocker source $U$ (its UNSAT label imported, not exhaustively re-proved), the independently computed stages are $(198,264,792)\to(156,264,960)\to(132,330,1588)$. The independent local Python auditor checked 72 complete small-source existential projection rows; the fetched original $U$ source (blob \texttt{f9dda41}) was transformed in two actual stages by a separately coded JavaScript check. The general two-level upper bound is a useful adaptive simplifier, but the $U$ literal stream \emph{grows} despite variable-count decrease. Disjoint replicated SAT/UNSAT controls retain $\Theta(N)$ encoded output for this fixed policy and are not hard-family lower bounds.

A complete-state candidate potential required to equal $\Phi=2^{v(G)}$ at exact-CNF committed checkpoints falls by at least $2^k-2^{8k/9}$ across the \emph{charged} polynomial-work first-stage projection. \paragraph{M22-QUAD-NEUTRAL102: sharp balanced four-clause neutral port repair and its semantic firewall (25 October 2026, 08:36 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT within the add-only independently assignable universal-port M22 clause-deletion model; COMPUTATIONAL two independently executed finite auditors and a cross-language raw-results check; mathematical peer review pending. ARC-EDGE-90, DIRECT-POST and P versus NP remain OPEN.}
For any valid clausewise-monotone unique proper signed $(2^+,2^-)$ formula $F$, delete two positive and two negative original triples with old ports $x_1,\ldots,x_6$ and $y_1,\ldots,y_6$. Introduce six fresh $a,b,c,d,e,f$ and add the positive triples $(a,d,x_1),(a,e,x_2),(b,e,x_3),(b,f,x_4),(c,f,x_5),(c,d,x_6)$ and negative triples $(-a,-d,-y_1),(-a,-f,-y_2),(-b,-d,-y_3),(-b,-e,-y_4),(-c,-e,-y_5),(-c,-f,-y_6)$. All old assignments extend to the added clauses using $a=b=c=1,d=e=f=0$, even with overlapping old names; each source signed incidence is repaired, all fresh degrees are $(2,2)$, every new triple is proper and unique, and the positive fresh pair $(a,e)$ has no negative counterpart. Thus
\[
 D_4(F)\in\mathcal C_\star,\quad
 \operatorname{SAT}(D_4(F))\iff\operatorname{SAT}(F\setminus D),\quad
 (k',m')=(k+6,m+8).
\]
The result improves NEUTRAL-PAIR101's two separate opposite-sign repairs (twelve auxiliaries and twenty inserted clauses) to one six-auxiliary twelve-clause repair for four deleted clauses. Signed quota conservation yields $p=d_++2h/3$, $q=d_-+2h/3$. For a universal independent-port interface, all old literals can be simultaneously falsified; with $t$ fresh true names each new positive clause needs a fresh true and each negative a fresh false, giving $2t\ge p$, $2(h-t)\ge q$, hence $h\ge3(d_++d_-)/2$. Balanced $2r+2r$ deletions therefore require $h\ge6r$, achieved by $r$ quad gadgets, with $12r$ inserted clauses. This sharp auxiliary floor does \emph{not} extend to old-name elimination, context-specialized ports, or every SAT algorithm. Type restoration also does not reconstruct the deleted original constraints: since every M22 source has even positive and negative clause counts, deleting \emph{all} source clauses in balanced quads always produces an eligible SAT formula, even when the parent is D\"ocker's published UNSAT $U$ (old $(198,264,7154)$, new $(594,792,26168)$ in $k,m,N$). Genuine source-compatible parent gluing remains necessary.
The dense indexed bit length increases by $3(m+1)(w'-w)+24(w'+1)>0$, with $w'=\lceil\log_2(k+7)\rceil$; two actual $Y$ repair levels have $(k,m,N,\#\mathrm{models})=(6,8,107,20)\to(12,16,254,314)\to(18,24,449,5936)$. The local full auditor passed 9,460 assertion-accounted checks and the published portable peer reproducer passed 9,303, with zero discrepancies; an independently generated raw table covers all 72 selected four-clause repairs on $Y$ and fully paired $H$. These are finite grammar/projection/model-count claims, not FLMV physical time or space measurements. Eager child bytes, source reads and output, address/index generation, policy acquisition/switching, replay, caches, ancestors and actual live writable/index bits remain charged; no $R_{\rm search}$ or direct superpolynomial post-acquisition $\Phi$ is proved. The FLMV region still concerns one fixed total uniform \emph{ordinary}-SAT machine simultaneously attaining both resources on all inputs, with corrected strict forbidden box $t<241/200$, $s<11642479/23232400$; the old cosine endpoint is retired. The missing all-input ARC successor and the direct post-acquisition lower-bound arrow remain OPEN. Exact report, peer script, machine-readable 72-case raw table and five-role ledger:

Therefore one fixed total uniform exact $\mathcal C_\star$ decider jointly achieving $(t,s)$ on all encodings yields one fixed total uniform $\mathcal C_{\rm M22}$ decider with joint pair $(t,s)$ up to $o(1)$, removing the former conservative $\max\{2,t\}$ time exponent. This \emph{does not} transfer the FLMV ordinary-SAT forbidden region to $\mathcal C_\star$ because the required ordinary-SAT-to-M22 near-linear bit-local same-machine reduction is still \textbf{OPEN}. \paragraph{M22-COMPONENT-CLOSURE97: exact original-clause deletion grammar and safe paired-component erasure (25 Oct 2026, 04:36 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT for the declared source-preserving component/deletion model, with independent peer checking pending. ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain OPEN.}
Let $F\in\mathcal C_{\rm M22}$ be an exact clausewise-monotone signed $(2^+,2^-)$ proper ternary CNF, with $c$ connected clause--variable incidence components, of which $d$ are not fully complementary paired. A subformula retaining an arbitrary subset of \emph{unchanged source clause records} remains in $\mathcal C_{\rm M22}$ if and only if its clauses are a union of whole incidence components. Indeed, keeping any occurrence of variable $x$ requires keeping all four original occurrences to retain both exact sign quotas, forcing every source clause in its component. Consequently exactly $2^c$ original-clause subsets remain valid $\mathcal C_{\rm M22}$ instances, and exactly $2^c-2^{c-d}$ belong to $\mathcal C_\star$, with the empty subset convention outside $\mathcal C_\star$. A connected nonpaired input has no strict in-target deletion child. This does not restrict semantic rewriting or paid type-restoring reductions.
A source-aware projection $\Pi(F)$ erases all fully complementary-paired incidence components and preserves the complete parent SAT label, by D\"ocker's paired-SAT theorem and disjoint-component factorization. It is idempotent and stays in $\mathcal C_\star$, but $\Pi(F)=F$ for every connected nonpaired $F$, preventing a universal power-shrinking successor from this route. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}: $66{,}048$ exhaustive clause-index masks of $Y,H,Y\dot\cup H$, $66{,}062$ assertion-accounted checks, no failures; valid $\mathcal C_{\rm M22}$ subset counts $2,2,4$, valid $\mathcal C_\star$ counts $1,0,2$, while $Y$ is SAT (20 models) and $H$ is fully paired SAT (18 models). The original connected 198-variable/264-clause D\"ocker target-UNSAT fixture had every single deletion exit the grammar, checked against blob \texttt{f9dda41a}; its UNSAT label is imported from the published theorem.
All source access, parser/recognizer work, component checks, clause headers/bodies, cache/replay, joint peak writable/index storage and fallback remain chargeable. This positive restricted component simplification supplies neither a noncircular post-acquisition potential nor a jointly favorable all-input ARC edge. In the FLMV-matched ordinary-SAT random-access model the valid forbidden box remains $t<241/200$ and $s<11642479/23232400$; the historical universal cosine-product landing remains \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}.

\paragraph{READ-LAST-SPACE-GAUGE95: logspace source cofactors preserve the joint machine bounds and defeat compulsory post-coverage work (25 Oct, 02:55 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the indexed read-only-input model; independent peer review pending. ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain OPEN.}
COVERCOF1 and PHASEBRIDGE1 previously showed that an arbitrary fixed
uniform total deterministic exact decider $A$ may compute both last-bit
cofactors before physically reading the last real input bit; their
particular compiler copied an $N$-bit prefix to writable memory.
LOGCOF1 further generalizes their copied-prefix form to
$2^{O(\log N)}$ precomputed cofactors while retaining an $N$-bit
writable source cache; neither earlier form attains the new
$O(\log N)$ incremental space overhead.

The new source-indexed compiler \emph{does not copy that prefix}:
scan $N-1$ source bits without retaining them, simulate $A$ on
the two complete virtual last-bit variants with charged indexed
reads of the same read-only source, reuse $A$'s writable tapes, then
read the last actual source bit and select the cached correct answer.
Both SAT and UNSAT, malformed bitstrings, controller work and all
source-address accesses are covered. For every all-input joint machine
pair $(t,s)$ with $t\ge1$, its read-last successor has
\[
 T_B(N)=O((N+T_A(N))\operatorname{polylog}N),\qquad
 S_B(N)=O(S_A(N)+\log(N+2)),
\]
and so merely \emph{preserves}, rather than improves, the same joint
exponents. On the exact 224-bit proper-3CNF opposite-label pair,
223 source bits and all metadata agree; the two charged virtual
answers are $(0,1)$ before reading the final sign bit.
The complete-state checkpoint after that last read has only $O(1)$
remaining charged work. Hence a terminal-zero potential whose every
legal bit transition decreases by at most $L(N)$ times its charged
work cannot take superpolynomial value at this checkpoint on
\emph{every} decider when $L$ is polynomial, by Role~5's exact
telescoping lemma. This is a \emph{phase-gauge counterexample},
\textbf{not} a whole-execution lower-bound theorem; a universal
all-input favorable ARC successor remains \textbf{OPEN}.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}: exact local full auditor 1,723 checks (172
inputs, 125 SAT/47 UNSAT) and independently written peer auditor
2,058 checks (514 finite source cases), with zero failures; these
are not physical FLMV switching/bit-time measurements.
GitHub-published report, executable full/peer checkers, machine-readable
summary, and source-coverage handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-25_0255_LSPV-4_READ_LAST_SPACE_GAUGE95.tex};

\paragraph{INDEXED-MERGE-FIREWALL94: a charged logspace random-access shortcut defeats a sequential-pass source-rank transfer (25 Oct, 02:30 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the source-recognized ordinary proper-3CNF family only. ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain OPEN.}
For the balanced sorted identifier-only source
$J_q(A,B)$ of NAME-ONLY-MULTIPASS93, with
$n=3q$, $m=4q$ and
$N=(16q+2)\lceil\log_2(64(7q+1))\rceil$, SAT holds
iff the two $q/2$-element source-name sets are disjoint.
A uniform indexed read-only-input machine pays for complete
canonical grammar recognition, then alternates random-access
reads between the two \emph{already sorted} name lists. It
compares their current names and advances the pointer
with the smaller value, halting UNSAT on equality or SAT
on exhaustion. At most $q-1$ comparisons and $2(q-1)$
name reads are required, with charged primitive bit time
$O(N\operatorname{polylog}N)$ and counted work/index
space $O(\log N)$ for the recognized family.
Even unsorted private-Pin gadget inputs permit a
$O(N^2\operatorname{polylog}N)$-time,
$O(\log N)$-space nested indexed comparison algorithm.
These algorithms receive no SAT label, do not compile
all child contexts, and charge source access, policy
selection, failed recognition, controller and all writes.
The earlier valid
$(2p-1)(B+c_A+2)\ge\log_2\binom q{q/2}$
fooling-set theorem remains \emph{correct for at most $p$
sequential forward passes}; alternating indexed seeks
lie outside that model. Thus an exponential name-join
rank cannot be promoted to the FLMV random-access
space lower bound without a new, charged model-preserving
simulation. Generic ordinary-SAT fallback remains
uncontracted, so no new jointly achievable all-input
SAT exponent pair has been established.

\paragraph{NAME-ONLY-MULTIPASS93: identifier-field global SAT gluing and a charged multi-pass source cut (25 Oct, 02:12 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only in the deterministic sequential-pass model; the general ARC successor and P versus NP remain OPEN.}
For even $q$, take $A,B\subseteq[q]$, $|A|=|B|=q/2$, and build a
proper-3CNF from four-clause positive $\operatorname{Pin}(x_i=1)$
gadgets for $i\in A$ followed by four-clause negative
$\operatorname{Pin}(x_i=0)$ gadgets for $i\in B$, with fresh helper pairs.
Every source has $n=3q$, $m=4q$ and
$N=(16q+2)\lceil\log_2(64(7q+1))\rceil$ encoded bits.
\emph{Every literal sign and helper identifier is fixed: only shared
primary variable-name records change.} Exactly,
$\SAT(J_q(A,B))$ iff $A\cap B=\varnothing$, equivalently
$B=[q]\setminus A$; positive inputs have $2^{2q}$ models.
The balanced-subset SAT compatibility matrix is a permutation matrix
of rank $\binom q{q/2}$ over every field. For any total deterministic
source-sequential controller with at most $p$ complete forward
passes and $B$ portable writable state bits, let
$c_A=\lceil\log_2(|Q_A|+1)\rceil$ charge the fixed finite control,
\[
(2p-1)(B+c_A+2)\ge \log_2\binom q{q/2}
\ge q-\log_2(q+1).
\]
The diagonal-fooling-set proof covers arbitrary adaptive state and rule
switching \emph{only within that restricted source channel}. A charged
$p$-pass name-interval bitmask algorithm uses
$\lceil q/p\rceil+O(\log N)$ live bits and
$O(pN\operatorname{polylog}N)$ primitive bit work, showing the bound is
nearly tight there. All-input indexed random access is a different model:
neither a mandatory post-acquisition lower bound nor an ARC successor
transfers. PASSMEM84 already proved a sign-field multi-pass analogue;
SCOPE-FRESHNESS91 proved one-pass identifier freshness. The exact
increment is name-only \emph{SAT decision} for all pass budgets, with an
explicit model/bit-resource ledger.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 79,082 deterministic full-auditor assertions
and 21,306 independent peer-reproducer assertions, zero failures;
original-CNF exhaustive model counting only through $q=4$, and
structured SAT/UNSAT controls through $q=8$.

\paragraph{PARITY-MARGINAL-COUNT92: every strict primary pin has an orientation-blind exact model count (25 Oct, 00:28 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only in the declared adaptive proper-pin model-count oracle; ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain OPEN.}
For $q\ge1$ and $q$ independent signed orientation groups $e$, a functional
XOR-prefix proper-3CNF with declared free pin helpers has
$n=4q+2$, $m=8q+2$ and
$N=(32q+10)\lceil\log_2(64(12q+5))\rceil$.
The full parent forces all $q$ primaries true and is SAT iff
$\bigoplus_i e_i=1$. For every \emph{strict} $S\subsetneq[q]$ and
arbitrary assigned signs $a$ to the pinned primaries, the ordinary
source-coherent partial-pin child has the exact complete model count
\[
\# C_{q,e}(S,a)=4^{q+1}2^{q-|S|-1},
\]
independent of $e$ and $a$. Therefore even unlimited adaptive exact
\emph{model-count} queries to these restricted children cannot distinguish
the parity cosets. A deterministic exact controller using paid source
field reads must inspect each of $q$ independently sensitive orientation
groups on the all-zero UNSAT member, giving only the addressed-source
acquisition lower bound $q=\Omega(N/\log N)$.
A charged canonical affine recognizer directly decides both labels in
$O(N\operatorname{polylog}N)$ elementary bit work and $O(\log N)$ live
writable bits; arbitrary unrecognized inputs require an exact fallback
without a favorable all-input bound. Full-pin \emph{counts} or a
$(q-1)$-pin \emph{witness} break oracle blindness, and every strict
ordinary child retains length $\Theta(N)$, not a fractional-power ARC
child. This extends the earlier support-only parity-coset firewall and
MINCORE89 status-only sensitivity but is no all-decider lower bound.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} complete local truth-table audit,

\paragraph{SCOPE-FRESHNESS-VALIDATION91: global name freshness and forbidden universal low-width normalizers (25 Oct, 00:13 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in a declared one-pass scope-grammar model; KNOWN FLMV theorem consequence; ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain OPEN.}
For even $q$, width-three closed event streams with $n=3q$ names, $m=q$ positive proper ternary clauses and $L=7q$ events encode a $q/2$-subset in their first half. Exact deterministic one-pass recognition of the \emph{global once-only OPEN} condition requires
\[
B\ge\left\lceil\log_2\binom{q}{q/2}\right\rceil
\ge q-\log_2(q+1)
=\Omega(N_{\rm scope}/\log N_{\rm scope})
\]
complete-state bits, despite live width three. This is an input-stream \emph{validation} bound, not unrestricted SAT time or a FLMV random-access lower bound.
An explicit same-length, eight-clause proper-3CNF opposite-pin pair has
ordinary model counts $(16,0)$ while a deliberately \emph{unvalidated}
local frontier-DP reports $(16,16)$ after reopening an old global source
name; both 20-event streams have 820 encoded bits and local width three.
A \emph{promised-valid} stream of $L$ events and width $w$ is directly
decidable in $O(L2^w\operatorname{polylog}N)$ charged bit time and
$O(2^w+w\log N)$ writable bits. Hence, by FLMV Corollary 5.8 and
Theorem 3.1, a uniform \emph{all-input} truth-preserving streaming
normalizer producing valid scope width $o(\log N)$, output bits
$N^{\lambda+o(1)}$, time $N^{\rho+o(1)}$ and space
$N^{\sigma+o(1)}$ cannot satisfy simultaneously
$\max\{\rho,\lambda\}<241/200$ and
$\sigma<11642479/23232400$ in the \emph{matched} random-access
machine model. This forbids that explicitly described normalizer,
not the existential ARC closure by an unrelated route.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} a full local standard-library audit completed

\paragraph{INTERVAL-PIN-COVER90: exact pinned-interval OR join and tight adaptive acquisition cover (23:38 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only in a restricted source-linked interval model; P versus NP and ARC-EDGE-90 OPEN.}
For the properized implication-chain promise
$P_q(b)$, which is SAT iff some of its $q$ orientation fields is zero,
an interval of $h$ consecutive orientations defines an
\emph{ordinary proper-3CNF child} $P_h(b|_I)$ with new endpoint pins,
$n=2h+7$, $m=2h+8$,
$L_h=(8h+34)\lceil\log_2(64(4h+16))\rceil$.
For every consecutive partition, the parent SAT bit equals the
\emph{OR} of these freshly pinned child SAT bits; the children
recursively remain in the same easy recognizable promise.
In the typed source-linked oracle model, every deterministic exact
adaptive controller on all-one UNSAT with $r$ directly read
orientations and $Q$ SAT-status queries supported on intervals
of length at most $K$ obeys the \emph{tight} bound
$r+KQ\ge q$.
This does not make source-derived child input bits free:
ordinary serialization and virtual dereferencing are separately charged.
With $K=\lceil\sqrt q\rceil$ and one fixed exact all-input child
decider $(t,s)$, the \emph{restricted} eager wrapper has
$t'\le\max\{1,(1+t)/2\}$ and
$s'\le\max\{1/2,s/2\}$, including paid child headers and
one readable buffer. Generic-source fallback retains the old bound,
so this is \emph{not} an all-input achievable-pair successor.
The matched controls
$A=(x\vee a)\wedge(x\vee\neg a)$,
$B_U=(\neg x\vee b)\wedge(\neg x\vee\neg b)$,
$B_S=(x\vee b)\wedge(x\vee\neg b)$
show that separately SAT blocks can join into either SAT or UNSAT
with identical metadata and SAT-status transcripts; source
boundary compatibility, not status aggregation, is indispensable.

\paragraph{MINCORE-ADAPTIVE-ORACLE89: unlimited proper-deletion SAT statuses have zero discriminating power (23:13 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted indexed source/deletion-SAT-oracle model; peer audit pending.}
Lift the scalable \(q\)-orientation 2-SAT chain \(F_b\) clausewise to proper
ternary \(P_q(b)\) by replacing each binary \(D_j\) with
\((D_j\vee t_j)\wedge(D_j\vee\neg t_j)\).
Then \(n=2q+7\), \(m=2q+8\), \(m-n=1\), and
\(N=(8q+34)\lceil\log_2(64(4q+16))\rceil=\Theta(q\log q)\).
The all-one member is \emph{minimally UNSAT}; every other orientation is SAT.
Every strict clause-deletion subformula is SAT on \emph{every} member.
Thus even arbitrarily many \emph{adaptively selected} exact SAT-status calls
on strict deletion children give an all-YES transcript for both labels.
When actual signed input fields are acquired only through charged bit/word
queries, every exact deterministic controller must still inspect one
coordinate in each of \(q\) disjoint orientation groups on the all-one UNSAT
source: \(R_{\mathrm{src}}\ge q=\Omega(N/\log N)\).
A paid direct scan/SCC matches this input order, so this is
\emph{not} a post-acquisition time lower bound and gives no positive charged
transport remainder \(L_{\mathrm{old}}-R-H\).

\paragraph{SCOPE-CLIQUE-FIREWALL88: connected opposite-label source width and paid direct decision (21:34 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in source-name/clause-preserving scoped event streams; independent review pending.}
For $p\ge2$ put $C_p=\bigwedge_{i<j}(x_i\vee x_j\vee a)$, and append
all eight signed clauses on $a,b,c$ for the UNSAT member $U_p$.
Replace $(a\vee b\vee c)$ by a duplicate of $(\neg a\vee b\vee c)$
for the SAT member $V_p$. Both have $n=p+3$,
$m=\binom p2+8$, and the same encoded length
$N=(4m+2)\lceil\log_2(64(n+m+1))\rceil=\Theta(p^2\log p)$,
with $\#\mathrm{SAT}(U_p)=0$ and $\#\mathrm{SAT}(V_p)=p+1$.
Every two variables in $\{x_1,\dots,x_p,a\}$ share a source clause,
hence their contiguous scoped live intervals intersect pairwise.
The interval Helly property forces one event with at least $p+1$
simultaneously live source variables. An explicit closed stream attains
$p+1$, so the exact minimum is $w=p+1$ under \emph{any} original-clause
reordering and independent satisfying pad blocks. Any fixed-polynomial
padding length $N'\le N^C$ retains
$w/\log N'=\Omega(p/\log p)\to\infty$: PAD-FLOOR-SCOPED87 does
\emph{not} supply a general all-input sublog-width normalizer.
\textbf{Restricted-model firewall:} source-preserving clause replay
is not arbitrary SAT decision. One uniform paid canonical recognizer
decides both labeled inputs in $O(N\operatorname{polylog}N)$ elementary
bit work and $O(\log N)$ writable bits, then uses an exact (potentially
exponential) fallback on other inputs. High syntactic live width does
not force a large machine state or compulsory boundary-table enumeration.

assertion-accounted checks, no failures, 22 matched instances;
independent GitHub mini reproducer, 180 checks, no failures.

\paragraph{REPLAY-ENTROPY86: compressed-state source replay versus one-bit SAT decision (20:06 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in a restricted indexed source-bit service, independent peer review pending.}
For the connected ordinary proper-3CNF parity-cycle family from BLOCK-CLOSED-ORACLE85,
with $q$ independent orientation bits, $n=3q$, $m=4q$ and
$N=(16q+2)\lceil\log_2(64(7q+1))\rceil$, even parity is SAT and
odd parity UNSAT, with matched source metadata.
An exact deterministic controller asked to \emph{replay all $q$ orientation bits
in order on each of $R$ rounds}, whose complete usable state at every
round boundary has at most $B$ bits (including working caches, control,
addresses and head position), must perform expected original-source
bit probes at least $R(q-B)_+$ for uniform orientations.
Even \emph{separately conditioned on either SAT or UNSAT}, it must perform
expected probes at least $R(q-1-B)_+$. Thus at least one input of
each label attains the latter total source-read floor.
The proof conditions on the current adaptive machine state and uses
a binary source-probe decision-tree/Kraft entropy bound on the remaining
source strings; the expected \emph{actual} reads are summed, not
hypothetical residual potentials.
One paid scan followed by caching $C$ orientation bits uses exactly
$q+(R-1)(q-C)$ addressed source reads,
with $C+O(\log(N+R))$ live control bits.

counted writable bits, including addressing and read-only parent dereferences,
without materializing either level.
For the explicit \(n=6\) matched pair \(U_d,V_d\), all eight signed
core clauses at positions \(rd\) make \(U_d\) UNSAT, while replacing

with \(K_1<d\), has the \emph{same} SAT label and exact model count under

and cannot reconstruct the parent label. For odd \(\gcd(K_1,M)=1\),
the \(MK_1\) nested index pairs yield exactly \(1+8K_2\) distinct
UNSAT-parent leaf bitstrings and \(1+7K_2\) SAT-parent leaf bitstrings.
Source-aware canonical recognition instead decides both parent labels
in \(O(N)\) bit work and \(O(\log N)\) live writable bits; all
recognition, failed attempts and source reads are charged.

\paragraph{PERIODIC-CHILD-COLLISION83: source-index versus literal-content orbits (17:36 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent review pending:}
For ordinary six-variable proper-3CNF parents with \(M=2^h\) repeated
records and a cyclic \(K\)-clause selector \(s(j,i)=(jK+i)\bmod M\),
choose odd \(K=\Theta(N^{1/2}/\log N)\),
\(p=2^{\lceil\log_2(8K)\rceil}\),
\(Q=\min\{M,\lfloor N^{3/5}\rfloor\}\).
An UNSAT source containing all eight signed clauses on a disjoint
core triple and its SAT sibling omitting one distinct core clause
have model counts \(0\) and \(7\), while \emph{every} indexed child
of either source has the same complete six-variable model count
\(49\) or \(56\), determined only by the source position.
The \(Q\) clause-index tuples are pairwise distinct but their ordinary
serialized child bitstrings occur in only \(1+8K\) or \(1+7K\)
distinct forms, respectively. Thus distinct index selections
do \emph{not} imply distinct child inputs when parent records repeat.
For these parents, two adjacent flat child arrays differ in at most
six literal-code fields, so \emph{changed-value} flat rewrites are
at most \(6(Q-1)\), rather than being forced to scale as \(QK\).
\textbf{Correction to CYCLIC-VIRTUAL-SELECTOR82:} its unqualified
claim of \(Q\) distinct children on every valid parent means
distinct \emph{index selections}; its flat-buffer
\(\Omega((Q-1)K)\) theorem remains valid with its stated premise
that \emph{all parent clause records are pairwise distinct}.

verification for every selected child at \(M=256,512,1024,4096\),

at \(M=4096\), \(N=311334\), \(K=29\), \(Q=1976\), there
are only 233 and 204 distinct child encodings.
A charged \(O(N\operatorname{polylog} N)\)-bit-work,
\(O(\log N)\)-space recognizer directly decides either canonical
label, falling back to a total exact solver on other sources.
This is not an all-input polynomial SAT algorithm.

\paragraph{CYCLIC-VIRTUAL-SELECTOR82: logarithmic-state cyclic child bit service (16:36 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent review pending:}
For canonical proper-3CNF input length

each ordinary child bit using charged $\polylog N$ bit work
and read-only parent accesses. When all parent clause records are pairwise distinct, a flat
materialized same-address buffer needs at least $(Q-1)K$
changed literal-code fields across the \emph{same} schedule;
without that premise the bound need not hold. Virtual access
does not incur this materialization requirement.
The $N^{1/2}$ all-selector index-array capacity theorem
remains valid under its stronger full-service quantifiers.
\textbf{CONDITIONAL ONLY:} with a separately proved all-input
both-label gluing algorithm using additional time
$N^{r+o(1)}$ and space $N^{\sigma+o(1)}$, the virtual
service would allow
$t'\le\max\{1,r,3/5+t/2\}$,
$s'\le\max\{0,\sigma,s/2\}$ for one simultaneous
fixed-machine pair. No such SAT-correct gluing exists here.
The archived minimally-UNSAT $F_m$ and SAT $G_m$ have
identical all-YES labels on all $K<M$ deletion children,
so this is a hostile \emph{semantic} control.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 53,005 assertion-accounted checks,
302,812 complete child-bit comparisons, 39,324 selected
checks, zero failures. At $M=4096$, $Q=1976$, $K=31$,
the flat representation changes 183,675 word fields;
its virtual index counter flips 3,941 bits, not a
measurement of all elementary work.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} mandatory $QK$ materialization
writes or $\Theta(K\log M)$ descriptor storage for every
selector schedule, and status-only strict-child gluing.
\textbf{OPEN:} the all-input favorable ARC successor;
hence P versus NP remains OPEN. Reports and peer handoff:

A fixed stateless full-child-bit decoder for \emph{every} $K$-subset

\paragraph{DUAL-PARITY-JOINT77: two parity checks create a rank-invisible, cheaply decidable compatibility hole (11:36 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT on the explicit canonical mixed-CNF family, independent
Role~5 review pending:} For $q\ge1$ and $e\in\{0,1\}^q$, let binary $B_e$
enforce $w_j=z_j\oplus e_j$, $(x\vee z_j)$ and $(y\vee\neg z_j)$
for every $j$, and encode both long affine checks
$x\oplus\bigoplus_j(z_j\oplus w_j)=0$ and
$y\oplus\bigoplus_j(z_j\oplus w_j)=0$ using separate functional
XOR3-prefix chains. The explicit full formula has
\[
n=6q,\quad m_2=4q+4,\quad k=16q-8,\quad M=20q-4,\quad
h_2^{\mathrm{raw}}=2q-1,\qquad
N=(80q-14)\lceil\log_2(64(26q-3))\rceil.
\]
The minimum raw strong syntactic 2-CNF backdoor is sharp: there are
$4q-2$ distinct ternary supports, each variable hits at most two,
and the $2q-1$ shared source operands $z_1,w_1,\ldots,z_q$ (excluding
$w_q$) hit every support. Every auxiliary is functional, the primal
graph is connected, and there are no unit clauses.
With $p=\bigoplus_je_j$, joint SAT holds iff $p=1$, with
$2^q$ witnesses if SAT and none otherwise; $B_e$ has $2^q+2$
models and each long check alone is compatible with $B_e$.
The joint syndrome image on $B_e$ is exactly
$\mathbb F_2^2\setminus\{(p,p)\}$: all individual parity marginals
and its affine hull are full, while one non-affine compatibility
point is absent.

A \emph{total uniform exact} policy pays for canonical grammar
recognition, complete template regeneration and verification, binary
SCC, reading all signed orientations, XOR cancellation and binary
compatibility, and then reconstructs one SAT witness or derives
an observed contradiction, with generic DPLL fallback off grammar.
The canonical trajectory changes
$(k,h_2)=(16q-8,2q-1)\to(0,0)$ in $O(q)$ word time
and $O(q)$ peak words. No true label is provided. The all-zero-$e$
UNSAT input requires $q=\Omega(N/\log N)$ independent addressed-word
reads for any exact deterministic decider because each sign flip
alone produces SAT; this is \emph{only} input acquisition.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 986 assertion-accounted check groups and
64 raw records, zero failures; matched SAT/UNSAT through $q=2048$,
small exhaustive counts, full target DPLL counts, generic fallback,
a three-syndrome pairwise projection firewall and complete restricted
fixed-backdoor comparisons. At $q=6$ the nonpruning enumerator
makes 2048 leaf checks; the canonical adaptive policy does linear
work. At $q=1$, recognition overhead makes adaptation more expensive.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} separate feasibility or full affine
syndrome hull proves joint parity feasibility, or a linear initial
backdoor forces exponential work against every adaptive SAT algorithm.
The single-global-parity polynomial theorem from SINGLE-PARITY-SCC76
remains valid. TWO-EASY-INTERSECTION61 already supplies the
unrestricted affine/2-CNF NP-complete endpoint; no NP-completeness
for \emph{exactly two} global checks is claimed.
\textbf{OPEN:} an independently measured post-acquisition
superpolynomial work obstruction covering every total uniform
deterministic exact decider; hence P versus NP.

and five-role handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-24_1136_LSPV-4_DUAL_PARITY_JOINT77.tex}.



\paragraph{SINGLE-PARITY-SCC76: one global parity plus arbitrary 2-CNF remains polynomial (10:46 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT within the declared mixed 2-CNF--one-XOR model; independent Role~5 review pending:}
For any explicitly encoded satisfiable 2-CNF $B$ and one global
linear check $\bigoplus_{i\in S}x_i=b$, group variables by complementary
signed implication-SCC pairs $V_g$ and define their parity weights
$\omega_g=|S\cap V_g|\bmod2$. Each SCC pair's truth orientation is either
forced or free, recognizable by paid unit-augmented SCC calls.
The image of the parity check on all $B$-witnesses is a singleton
if every free $V_g$ has $\omega_g=0$ and otherwise is $\{0,1\}$.
Indeed, any two $B$-witnesses are joined by a sequence of satisfying
assignments changing one SCC pair at a time, using a topological
order on the changing quotient blocks. This yields a uniform exact
SAT/UNSAT decider and SAT witness constructor in conservative
$O(n^2(n+m))$ logical-word time and $O(n+m)$ live words,
charging discovery, all failed tests, switching and source replay.
The affine hull of a bijunctive relation is therefore described by
its forced literals and signed within-SCC-block equality/disequality
relations. The theorem does \emph{not} extend by separately testing
multiple simultaneous global equations.

For even $q=2t\ge4$, a fully explicit source-encoded family
$P_{q,c,b}$ couples $t$ binary XOR pairs
$x_{2j+1}\oplus x_{2j+2}=c_j$ with a functional prefix-XOR
chain and final XOR output $b$. Its exact ledger is
\[
n=2q-2,\quad m_2=q+2,\quad k=4q-8,\quad M=5q-6,\quad
N=(20q-22)\lceil\log_2(64(7q-7))\rceil.
\]
There are $q$ original source variables, $q-2$ functional
auxiliaries, and no free auxiliaries.
The formula is SAT iff $b=\bigoplus_jc_j$; its SAT model count is
$2^{q/2}$, otherwise zero. Its minimum raw strong syntactic
2-CNF backdoor is $(q-2)/2$. Its \emph{single connected} affine
prefix component has nullity $q-1$ and excess $K=q-2$.
Nevertheless a charged adaptive solver recognizes the source,
eliminates the functional prefix variables, uses SCC to discover
even-weight free binary blocks, switches to parity cancellation
and verifies a witness or algebraic contradiction in $O(q)$
word operations and $O(q)$ live words. The designated all-zero-$c$,
$b=1$ UNSAT input has $q/2$ private orientation fields, each
single flip producing SAT, proving only
$\Omega(N/\log N)$ addressed-word acquisition on that input,
tight for the canonical scanner.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} six independent stdlib audit suites, seed
202610240136, 283,242 assertion-accounted checks and zero failures.
They include exhaustive three-/four-variable bijunctive relations
(166 and 4170), 5010 randomized parity masks, 2062 genuine
witness-switch cases, matched SAT/UNSAT controls through $q=4096$,
and restricted nonpruning-policy comparisons. These finite tests
do not prove asymptotic lower bounds.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED as an inference:}
large initial excess nullity, one connected affine component,
many ternaries, or a large raw backdoor forces exponential work
from \emph{every} adaptive exact SAT decider. The older
$2^K\operatorname{poly}(N)$ and forest-joining results remain
correct upper bounds; both may be pessimistic.
\textbf{OPEN:} universal superpolynomial post-acquisition lower bound
for any fixed NP-complete SAT language, hence $P$ versus $NP$.

\paragraph{Current reconciled ladder: AFFINE-BIJUNCTIVE-GATE75 (09:58 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT structural classification; independent Role~5
review pending.}  For any consistent projected affine relation
$R=\{c+At\}$ on a 2-CNF boundary, with $\operatorname{rank}(A)=d$ and
$s$ \emph{distinct nonzero coordinate covectors} (rows of $A$),
$R$ is equivalent to a 2-CNF iff $s=d$. When $s=d$ a charged
Gaussian-to-binary conversion emits at most $2|X|$ unit/equality
clauses; when $s>d$, the unary/pairwise closure has $2^s$ possible
tuples versus $2^d$ in $R$, precluding any exact 2-CNF on the same
boundary. This is a representation criterion, not a time lower bound.
The exact uniform algorithm first computes paid binary SCC equivalences,
substitutes them into its affine subsystem, projects via GF(2), and
switches to SCC when the updated excess vanishes; complete exact
fallback remains available.

For even $q\ge4$, use cyclic affine equations
$y_{i-1}\oplus y_i\oplus x_i=\beta_i$ and binary links
$x_i\leftrightarrow x_{i+1}$ for $i<q-1$. Encode each XOR3
with four proper ternaries. The resulting $H_{q,\beta}$ has
\[
n=2q,\quad m_2=2q-2,\quad k=4q,\quad M=6q-2,\quad
N=(24q-6)\lceil\log_2(64(8q-1))\rceil.
\]
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT:}
$H_{q,\beta}$ is SAT iff $\bigoplus_i\beta_i=0$; its model count
is exactly four or zero. Initially the raw strong syntactic 2-CNF
backdoor is $q/2$, and the existentially projected parity relation
has $d=q-1,s=q,\epsilon=s-d=1$ with minimum strong
\emph{semantic} 2-CNF variable-conditioning backdoor $q-2$.
A charged SCC discovers every $x_i=t$, after which the affine
relation becomes TRUE or FALSE by parity cancellation.
A uniform exact source-recognizing policy handles both labels in
$O(q)$ word time and $O(q)$ peak words, without initial units or
label advice. No mandatory enumeration follows.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted input acquisition:} flipping any one
$\beta_i$ changes four private signed target literal words and
reverses the SAT label on either a SAT or UNSAT member. Thus every
deterministic exact addressed-word decider reads $q=
\Omega(N/\log N)$ word groups on those designated sources,
matched by an $O(q)$ recognition scan. Deleting one cross-block
equality link at position $r$ produces
$(r\bmod 2)t_L\oplus((q-r)\bmod2)t_R=\bigoplus_i\beta_i$;
the new quotient can change UNSAT to SAT even though both
individual subsystems were already SAT.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 4,736 assertion-accounted tests,
zero failures; complete small model counts and $q=2560$
SAT/UNSAT scaling, one-link controls, and a restricted fixed-rule
enumerator. At $q=14$, that enumerator checks 4,096 leaves
(778,240 abstract events), versus adaptive 578 SAT and 510
UNSAT events. This is not a lower bound on arbitrary DPLL/CDCL.
\textbf{KNOWN NP-complete endpoint:} AFFINE-NULLITY-BOUNDARY73's
graph-3-colorability-to-mixed-XOR3/NAND reduction remains unchanged.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} inferring mandatory superlinear work
from initial $k$, a fixed backdoor, affine covector excess or
one chosen representation's branching depth.
\textbf{OPEN:} all-decider post-acquisition superpolynomial cost.
P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.

\paragraph{Current reconciled ladder: LOCAL-AFFINE-JOIN74 (09:08 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted; independent Role~5 review pending.}
For $q$ private ternary-XOR gates
$x_i\oplus y_i\oplus p_i=b_i$ coupled to binaries
$(x_i\vee y_i)$ and $(\neg x_i\vee p_{i+1})$,
the exact existential projection is
\[
\exists x_i,y_i\ 
[(x_i\vee y_i)\land(\neg x_i\vee p_{i+1})
 \land(x_i\oplus y_i\oplus p_i=b_i)]
\iff p_{i+1}\vee(p_i\oplus b_i).
\]
With binary anchors $p_0=1,p_q=0$, the canonical
family $G_{q,b}$ is UNSAT precisely for $b=1^q$.
Exact ledger:
$n=3q+3$, $m_2=2q+4$, $k=4q$,
$M=6q+4$,
$N=(24q+18)\lceil\log_2(64(9q+8))\rceil$.
Raw minimum strong syntactic 2CNF backdoor is $q$
yet paid local projection, SCC and optional witness
replay decide both labels in $O(q)$ word time with
$O(q)$ peak words; the uniform decider falls back to
exact DPLL outside its recognized grammar.
The $q$ independent orientation groups give a
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted}
$q=\Omega(N/\log N)$ deterministic addressed-word
input-probe lower bound on $G_{q,1^q}$, matched by a
linear source scanner, not a superpolynomial time theorem.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT one-link counterexample.}
Adding $x_i\leftrightarrow x_{i+1}$ for every adjacent
gate gives linked $H_{q,b}$ with
$n=3q+3$, $m_2=4q+2$, $k=4q$, $M=8q+2$,
and $N=(32q+10)\lceil\log_2(64(11q+6))\rceil$.
Its SAT label is exactly $b_0=0$.
For $q=2,b=(1,0)$, independent local projections
predict SAT but the linked formula is UNSAT,
because a shared hidden variable invalidates
factorization. SCC discovery identifies one
equivalent switch; branching on it and then
using SCC decides $H$ in $O(q)$ word time,
with charged recognition, failed branches and
verification. All but the first orientation-sign
sensitivity vanish when the $x_i$ are linked.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}: 3,103 named assertion
checks, zero failures; complete $q\le7$ baseline
orientation audit, $q\le8$ linked label audit,
and SAT/UNSAT scales to $q=2048$ and $512$.
A deliberately nonpruning raw-backdoor policy
visits $2^{12}=4096$ leaves at $q=12$, whereas
the adaptive projector uses 192 local truth
tests and one SCC solve. That is a
\emph{restricted-policy} comparison only.
The easy nullity-two construction refines
AFFINE-NULLITY-BOUNDARY73 without changing
its NP-complete endpoint.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}: independent
projection after hidden-variable coupling and
raw $k$, nullity or backdoor as a mandatory
all-algorithm continuation cost.
\textbf{OPEN}: a model-matched post-acquisition
superpolynomial lower bound for every uniform
exact state-adaptive SAT decider.
P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.

\paragraph{Previous reconciled ladder: AFFINE-NULLITY-BOUNDARY73 (08:52 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the explicit affine-component and uniform-reduction models; independent Role~5 peer review pending:}
If $B$ is arbitrary 2-CNF and $E$ a GF(2) system whose
variable-incidence components each have affine nullity at most one,
charged component discovery and Gaussian elimination express each
source variable as a constant or $t_i\oplus a_i$ for one component
bit $t_i$. Arbitrary $B$ then projects \emph{exactly} to 2-CNF,
solvable by SCC in polynomial time, even with total nullity $\Theta(n)$
and dense cross-component binary coupling.
The sharp next rung has \emph{local nullity two} and is
\textbf{KNOWN NP-complete} by an explicit parsimonious
GRAPH 3-COLORABILITY reduction: one odd XOR3 component on each
vertex, three negative NAND clauses within the vertex, and three
negative NAND clauses on every graph edge. All binary clauses are Horn
and all affine components are disjoint. For a graph with $v$ vertices
and $e$ edges the exact CNF ledger is
\[
 n=3v,\quad m_2=3v+3e,\quad k=4v,\quad M=7v+3e,
 \quad N=(28v+12e+2)\lceil\log_2(64(10v+3e+1))\rceil.
\]
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted input-acquisition and adaptive
hostile control:} for an odd $q$-cycle with one binary anchor pair per
vertex, $n=4q,m_2=8q,k=4q,M=12q$ and
$N=(48q+2)\lceil\log_2(64(16q+1))\rceil$. The all-zero anchor-sign
member is UNSAT; every single anchor-pair flip gives SAT with exactly
$2^{q+1}$ witnesses. Therefore each total exact addressed-word
decider needs $q=\Omega(N/\log N)$ sign-word probes on the
all-zero member. Yet a label-blind charged policy discovers all
$q$ implied anchor literals, shrinks the live ternary count from
$4q$ to zero and decides by SCC or direct certified conflict in
$O(q)$ source words and peak logical words. With $h$ anchors missing,
one selected variable per surviving ternary support yields a
$2^h\operatorname{poly}(N)$ upper bound, not a lower bound. The policy
permits different state-dependent branches and exact DPLL fallback
outside the recognized easy state.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 1,570 assertion-accounted checks, zero
failures; 64 four-vertex graph controls, 150 Gaussian quotient controls,
2-SAT chains, Horn/affine/padding controls, SAT/UNSAT cycle scales
through $q=1023$ and dynamic uncovered-support tests. At $q=1023$,
$N=982120$ bits and charged abstract-event counts were
$120718$ UNSAT and $149367$ SAT. A deliberately nonpruning
fixed-backdoor comparator visits $2^q$ leaves but supplies no
all-decider lower bound.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} initial local nullity two, $k=4q$ or a
raw strong 2-CNF backdoor $q$ as an unavoidable
$2^{\Omega(q)}$ running-time resource.
\textbf{OPEN:} universal post-acquisition superpolynomial work for all

\paragraph{Prior reconciled ladder: HETEROGENEOUS-GATE72 (07:45 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only for a specified source family and explicit

For even $q\ge4$, a canonical guarded CNF combines $q$ 3-XOR equations

without shortening any ternary or producing a unit. Branching on the
discovered representative produces \emph{different} tractable bases:

parity system, solved with charged GF(2) methods; at $x=1$ all XOR
gates vanish and the residual is exactly $F_b$, solved by 2SAT SCC.
A label-blind, source-recognizing, total exact policy pays all
recognition, rule selection, switching, failed attempts, optional
witness reconstruction and fallback; it runs in $O(q)$ word time and
$O(q)$ peak logical words on this family.
On the designated UNSAT input, $2q$ disjoint signed-coordinate groups
must be read by every total deterministic exact addressed-word
decider, giving only $\Omega(N/\log N)$ input acquisition, matched by
a direct source scan. No post-acquisition superlinear bound follows.
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM (Schaefer):} Unrestricted conjunctions of positive
binary OR and even ternary XOR are NP-complete, even though either
relation alone is polynomial-time tractable. This is a boundary
between a recognizable guarded disjunction and general mixed
constraints, not a separation of P and NP.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 25,195 assertion-accounted checks,
zero failures, and 21 additional large scaling cases through

checks $2^{q+1}$ leaves, but this is strictly a
\emph{restricted-policy} comparison.

It has both labels, $\mathrm{UNSAT}$ iff $b=1^q$, and every satisfying
source $z$-chain assignment has exactly one auxiliary extension.
The minimum raw strong 2-CNF backdoor is $h=q+3$. Initially there
are no units, no binary-only contradiction, and a full SCC computation
derives $a=\beta$ while reducing \emph{zero} genuine ternaries.
Importing that single certified equality into a verified sparse XOR
system raises affine rank from $3q+8$ to $3q+9$.
A charged state-dependent SCC $\to$ Gaussian $\to$ SCC policy
discovers $q+11$ forced variable values, reduces all ternaries to
width at most two, and decides either label in $O(q)$ source words
and $O(q)$ peak live words on this explicit family.
All work for source discovery, complete radix parsing, equations,
source restriction, policy switches, fallback tests, reconstruction,
and SAT witness replay is counted. The policy is total and exact on
arbitrary CNF via complete DPLL fallback, with \emph{no} generic
polynomial-time claim. At $q=1024$, $(n,M,k,h)=(4104,13346,12320,1027)$,
both labels reach $k'=0$ with 1035 certified units, and instrumented
SAT/UNSAT abstract-event counts are 358245/319237.
The 2048-leaf static enumeration comparator at $q=8$ is a
\emph{restricted deliberately non-pruning policy}, not a lower bound on
arbitrary branching/CDCL. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} all 62 small
orientation strings, 16 larger matched scaling runs, 120 independent
random-CNF labels, 60 signed-permutation controls, and relevant
Horn/XOR/2-SAT padding controls agreed with the exact solver, with
zero discrepancies. Role~2 QUIET-EQUIV67 already covered an

\textbf{RETIRED} as duplicative. This repaired test establishes a

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT within the declared reduction calculus, independent

A source-retaining cyclic-copy Horn priority selector encodes any
proper 3-CNF with $n_0$ variables, $m$ clauses, and $\nu$ active source
variables as $n=n_0+12m$ variables, $m_2=16m+\nu$ Horn binaries and

assignment has exactly one auxiliary extension; every physical variable
occurs at most three times. The output has mixed width two and three,
\emph{not} proper exactly-three width. Every proper three-CNF whose
variables occur at most three times is SAT by Hall matching, so such a
properization retaining the cap cannot cover UNSAT inputs. This is
a classical occurrence-cycle mechanism specialized to our functional
Horn selector, not a universal decision lower bound. Source:

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, adaptive switched-ladder hostile control:}
For each $q\ge1$ and orientation string $b\in\{0,1\}^q$, a mixed

\[
n=3q+5,\quad m_2=2q+5,\quad k=4q+4,\quad M=6q+9,
\quad N=(24q+38)\left\lceil\log_2(64(9q+15))\right\rceil.

\boxed{H_{q,b}\in\mathrm{UNSAT}\iff b=1^q.}

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, fixed-input model:} QUADRATIC-SOURCE-LADDER55

However $N=\Theta(d^2\log d)$ and an $O(d^2)$ word source scan
decides the entire family. This is a tight \emph{input-acquisition}
lower bound, not a superlinear decision-time bound.
Every one of the $k$ functional OR-gate ternary clauses is
essential on the designated UNSAT input despite polynomial
source-aware recognition.

\paragraph{PALETTE-SOURCE-CYCLE56: direct all-decider acquisition inside proper 3-CNF.}
For $q\ge3$, a palette triangle and $q$ pairs of opposite two-color
vertices with one oriented edge per pair yield a graph that is
3-colorable exactly when $\bigoplus_i\sigma_i=0$; consistent
instances have exactly $12$ colorings. Its mixed-width one-hot CNF has
$n=6q+9$, $k=2q+3$ genuine ternaries,
$m_2=18q+18$ binaries, and $m=20q+21$ total clauses,
with $N=(80q+86)w$ encoded bits in the declared fixed record model.
This source is \emph{not} proper exactly-three-clause CNF.
Padding each binary clause by its own fresh free variable and two
ternary copies yields the proper source with
$n'=24q+27$, $k'=m'=38q+39$, $m_2'=0$ and
$N'=(152q+158)w'$ encoded bits. The mixed formula changes three
source literal cells per orientation flip; the proper padded formula
changes exactly six distinct literal cells per flip. Both have opposite
SAT labels after a one-bit change. Thus on \emph{both} labels,
\emph{every} exact deterministic word-access decider for unrestricted
proper exactly-three-clause SAT reads at least $q$ addressed input
words on the padded family. This is only
$\Omega(N'/\log N')$ acquisition; a charged $O(q)$
paired-padding recognizer and parity scan matches it.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted structural upper bound:}
After fixing a palette triangle, if $t$ other vertices are not
adjacent to its base vertex, enumerating their $3^t$ colors leaves
an affine two-color constraint graph, decided by parity propagation
in $O(V+E)$ time per attempt. Thus total time is
$O(3^t(V+E))$ and peak space $O(V+E)$ words; dense chords
among palette-pinned nodes do not force exponential work.
A connected palette extension of arbitrary graph 3-colorability
gives the KNOWN NP-complete endpoint, not an amplification theorem.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, local transport failure:}
The baseline $q$-orientation 2-SAT UNSAT predicate is
$\bigwedge_i b_i$, whereas the palette cycle UNSAT predicate is
$\bigoplus_i\sigma_i$. A coordinatewise mapping
$\sigma_i=g_i(b_i)$ cannot preserve labels for $q\ge2$ because
XOR of unary Boolean functions is affine and AND is not.
A global deciding reduction pays $\Theta(q)$ original source
reads and consumes the known input-order lower bound.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} three independent Python auditors completed
35,753 counted checks with zero failures, including 504 original mixed
graph orientation strings (252 SAT and 252 UNSAT), 3,576
three-word mixed flips, 3,576 six-word proper padded flips,
504 proper padded SAT witness replays, 744 additional chord trials,
and 5,456 coordinatewise-map falsifiers. Corrected verified standalone
report: \path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-23_1845_LSPV-4_PALETTE_SOURCE_CYCLE56.tex}.
The earlier QUADRATIC-SOURCE-LADDER55 remains an independent
quadratic-field source control. Finite tests are not asymptotic proofs.

\paragraph{SHARED-SWITCH-MINIMAL-UNSAT57: clause-minimal UNSAT but one-variable backdoor.}
For every $q\ge3$, encode a signed XOR cycle on
$x_0,\ldots,x_{q-1}$ by lifting each of its $2q$ binary
clauses with a common positive literal $s$; add four
proper ternaries
$(\neg s\vee t\vee y_1)$,
$(\neg s\vee t\vee\neg y_1)$,
$(\neg s\vee\neg t\vee y_2)$,
$(\neg s\vee\neg t\vee\neg y_2)$.
Every variable has both polarities, and there are no initial
units, pure literals, or non-genuine clauses.
The source has $n=q+4$, $k=m=2q+4$ width-three clauses
and is SAT exactly when $\bigoplus_i\sigma_i=0$,
with 16 satisfying assignments on SAT and zero on UNSAT.
On every odd-parity UNSAT member, \emph{every single clause is
essential}: deleting one cycle clause gives exactly eight
models, and deleting any one of the four forcing clauses
gives exactly $2^{q+1}$ models.
Nevertheless $\{s\}$ is a minimum strong 2-CNF backdoor,
so a checked source recognizer and SCC/parity continuation
decide both labels in $O(q)$ word operations.
Every odd-parity member has an ordinary resolution
refutation constructed in exactly $4q+2$ inferences.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, encoding-scoped:}
With two header words and four $w$-bit words per clause,
$w=\lceil\log_2(64(3q+9))\rceil$ and
$N=(8q+18)w$.
Flipping one independent orientation changes the SAT label
and precisely two disjoint literal-sign word locations.
Therefore every exact deterministic proper-three-CNF decider
must read at least $q=\Omega(N/\log N)$ addressed input words
on every member in this specific model.
A linear source scan matches the bound.
This is input-acquisition order, not an unrestricted
superlinear or exponential SAT lower bound.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:}
The full independent auditor checked 254 original 2-SAT
chain inputs, 56 source formulas, 720 one-clause deletions,
248 one-bit source flips, 132 SCC restrictions, and
33 ordinary resolution certificates through $q=256$
(2,546 checked derived inferences), with zero failures.
Report, compact reproducer, raw summary and cross-role handoff
have been independently read back from
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-23_1941_LSPV-4_SHARED_SWITCH_MINIMAL_UNSAT57.tex}
and adjacent same-prefix artifacts.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:}
clause-essential ternary count or large syntactic residual as
an unrestricted SAT lower bound; a paid strong 2-CNF
backdoor can remain one even for minimally UNSAT
proper-three-CNF families with arbitrarily many clauses.

acquisition/compression/continuation lower bound for every total

\paragraph{CONNECTED-CRITICAL-BACKDOOR58: connected critical parity with linear minimum backdoor.}
Let $G_t$ be a $t$-gonal prism, with $2t$ vertices and $3t$
edges, and form proper 3-CNF from the four exclusion clauses for
each vertex equation $\bigoplus_{e\ni v}x_e=b_v$.
The resulting \emph{connected} family $P_{t,b}$ has
$n=3t$, $k=m=8t$, and
$N=(32t+2)\lceil\log_2(64(11t+1))\rceil$ bits.
It is SAT iff $\bigoplus_vb_v=0$, with exactly $2^{t+1}$
models if SAT. On the UNSAT label every single-clause deletion
makes SAT with exactly $2^{t-1}$ models.
The minimum strong \emph{syntactic} 2-CNF backdoor is the prism
edge-cover number $h=t=n/3=k/8$, despite an $O(t)$
source-aware parity recognizer/decider and reconstruction.
Flipping any one of the $2t$ independent vertex RHS fields reverses
SAT/UNSAT and changes four disjoint addressed source-literal cells.
Hence every exact deterministic decider for the full proper-3CNF
encoding reads at least $2t=\Omega(N/\log N)$ source words on
\emph{both} labels, matched by an $O(t)$ word source scan.
These are \textbf{PROVED/EXACT, encoding-scoped} statements and
counterexamples to any universal per-instance continuation lower
bound derived solely from connectivity, clause minimality, or
linear minimum syntactic strong backdoor. A separate affine-plus-$p$
non-affine-ternary clause model admits an exact
$O(3^p\operatorname{poly}(N))$ time algorithm by choosing one
true literal per clause and Gaussian-checking each choice. This is
an \emph{upper} bound only.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} standard-library audit, seed 2026102358:
10,828 assertion groups, 2,088 deletion witnesses,
1,044 sign flips, 60 source remappings, zero failures, with
$t\le128$. Verified role report:

peer handoff, code reproducer and raw summary share its prefix.

\paragraph{PROJECTION-BLOWUP59: sharp no-auxiliary 2-CNF boundary, no SAT lower bound.}
A source 2-CNF on disjoint $A=\{a_i\}_{i=1}^p$, $B=\{b_j\}_{j=1}^p$
and one mediator $u$,
\[
M_p=\bigwedge_i(\neg a_i\vee u)
\wedge\bigwedge_j(\neg u\vee\neg b_j),
\]
has $2p$ clauses; its exact existential projection $\exists u\,M_p$
onto $A\cup B$ is $\bigwedge_{i,j}(\neg a_i\vee\neg b_j)$.
Any logically equivalent CNF on these $2p$ variables without
auxiliaries, regardless of clause width, requires at least $p^2$
clauses: each forbidden assignment with only $a_i=b_j=1$
needs its own clause. This proves the quadratic output order
of MAIN88's 2-CNF projection upper bound is tight under an
explicit, auxiliary-free clause-record contract.
A paired genuine-ternary extension without initial unit or pure
literals has $n=10p+9$, $k=2p+2$, $m=12p+10$ and
$N=(48p+42)\lceil\log_2(64(n+m+1))\rceil$ bits.
The single-sign matched labels are SAT/UNSAT, with respectively
$64\cdot7^{p-1}3^p$ and zero assignments.
The full binary-backbone boundary projection retains the $p^2$
lower bound. Nevertheless keeping $u$ gives a linear-size
representation, and a checked source-aware parser decides either
label in $O(p)$ word time. Thus quadratic projected-CNF
\emph{output} is \textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted}, while any
translation into all-decider SAT running time is
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}. The separate stdlib audit checked
10,872 assertions across $p=1,\ldots,6$ with zero failures:
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}. Peer verification remains pending.
This does not supersede Role~3's stronger nonlinear projection
lower bound or assert a new $P\ne NP$ implication.
Verified report and cross-role code/results:
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-23_1234_LSPV-4_PROJECTION_BLOWUP59.tex}
and adjacent \path{_HANDOFF.md}, \path{_RAW.json},
\path{_MINI.py}. The all-decider continuation theorem remains
\textbf{OPEN}.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (HORN-SELECTOR-DEGREE60 and HALL-DEFICIENCY-TRANSPORT62, independent peer review pending):}
For proper exact-three source $\Phi$ on $n_0$ variables and $m$
clauses, the canonical disjoint-selector Horn lift
$H(\Phi)=\bigwedge_j[(s_{j1}\vee s_{j2}\vee s_{j3})
\wedge\bigwedge_{a=1}^{3}(\neg s_{ja}\vee\ell_{ja})]$
is SAT iff $\Phi$ is SAT. It has $n=n_0+3m$ variables,
$3m$ Horn binary clauses, $k=m$ disjoint positive ternaries, and
$N=(16m+2)\lceil\log_2(64(n_0+7m+1))\rceil$ encoded bits.
For source assignment $\alpha$, the exact selector-extension count is
$\prod_j(2^{t_j(\alpha)}-1)$. Strong raw syntactic 2-CNF and Horn
backdoor minima are $m$ and $2m$.

Define maximum Hall deficiency
$\delta^*(F)=\max_{Q\subseteq F}(|Q|-|\operatorname{var}(Q)|)$;
by Hall, $\delta^*(F)=|F|-\nu(F)$ for maximum clause-variable
matching size $\nu(F)$. The new gadget-specific identity
\[
\boxed{\delta^*(H(\Phi))=\delta^*(\Phi),\qquad
\nu(H(\Phi))=3m+\nu(\Phi)}
\]
is \textbf{PROVED/EXACT} by counting an arbitrary target clause
subset, with all source-variable sharing preserved. If each source
variable occurs at most four times and exactly $h$ attain four
occurrences, $3|Q|\le3|\operatorname{var}(Q)|+h$ for every source
clause subset $Q$, so
$\delta^*(\Phi)\le\lfloor h/3\rfloor$. Szeider's 2004
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM} gives a uniform
$2^{\lfloor h/3\rfloor}\operatorname{poly}(N)$ SAT decision
upper bound. In particular $h\le2$ guarantees SAT by matching,
and $h=O(\log N)$ is polynomial time.
The degree-four endpoint remains \textbf{KNOWN NP-complete} by
Tovey and D\"ocker. No lower bound is inferred from the FPT
upper bound or from the linear syntactic backdoors.

The connected signed exact-$(2,2)$ prism family $G_q$ from
MAIN101 has $n=6q,m=8q,h=6q$,
$\delta^*(G_q)=2q=h/3$, yet has an $O(q)$ source-aware
recognizer and SAT witness. Hence linear maximum deficiency
as a \emph{necessary} exponential-work measure is
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}. The stdlib audit recorded
11,924 assertions, zero failures, over 1,100 generated
bounded-occurrence sources, MU16 UNSAT, the tight signed
four-clause SAT example and connected prisms:
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}. Role~5 review remains pending.

adjacent \path{_HANDOFF.md}, \path{_MINI.py},
and \path{_RAW.json} preserve reproduction and transport accounting.
The source-input query lower bound remains input-order only.
The universal charged compression-versus-continuation
implication and $P\ne NP$ remain \textbf{OPEN}.


\paragraph{REGULAR-DEFICIENCY63: universal exact signed-$(2,2)$ Hall profile (24 October 2026).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, pending independent Role~5 audit:}
For \emph{every} properly encoded signed exact-$(2,2)$ 3-CNF with
$n=3t$ original variables and $m=4t$ genuine ternary clauses, not
just the connected prism control, the maximum clause-variable
incidence matching saturates all $n$ variables and
\[
  \boxed{\delta^*(F)=m-\nu(F)=m-n=t=n/3=m/4.}
\]
The \emph{unique} clause subset attaining maximum Hall deficiency
is the entire source $F$.  Every single-clause deletion lowers
maximum deficiency by exactly one,
$\delta^*(F\setminus\{C\})=t-1$ for all $C\in F$.
The exact identity
$3(|Q|-|V(Q)|)=h_Q-s_Q-\beta(Q)$ for every clause subset $Q$
of a max-degree-four proper 3-CNF separates degree-four
occurrence excess $h_Q$, degree slack $s_Q$ and outside incidence
boundary $\beta(Q)$.  For exact signed-$(2,2)$ sources,
$3\delta(Q)=|V(Q)|-\beta(Q)$.
All these quantities are \emph{unsigned}; within the full
NP-complete exact-$(2,2)$ class, maximum deficiency depends
only on the input counts and cannot classify SAT versus UNSAT
or force computation on each instance.  The earlier prism
saturation was structurally generic, not a special obstruction.
For the canonical Horn-selector lift $H(F)$,
$\delta^*(H(F))=t$ and the entire Horn target remains the unique
maximum-deficiency clause subset; the target has $5n$ variables,
$4m$ total clauses and $m$ genuine positive ternaries.
Full construction, grammar recognition, source reads, inverse
and witness lifting/replay are polynomial and explicitly charged.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} deterministic stdlib audit, seed
202610240063, 17 trials through $t=7$ with 13,650
assertion-accounted checks and zero failures, exhaustive clause
subsets through $t=3$.
The at-most-signed-$(2,2)$ smallest known 20-clause UNSAT
