\paragraph{TWO-SIGN-SWAP64: exact two-literal semantic boundary on fixed signed support.}
The independently checked 198-variable, 264-clause signed exact-(2,2) UNSAT
source from DOECKER-CORE-CERT61 admits an explicitly checked SAT repair
that changes \emph{only two literal signs}, without changing any clause's
ordered unsigned variable identifiers. In zero-based source indexing,
$(\neg x_2\vee\neg x_3\vee\neg x_{194})$ at clause 3 becomes
$(\neg x_2\vee x_3\vee\neg x_{194})$, and
$(x_3\vee x_9\vee x_{10})$ at clause 13 becomes
$(\neg x_3\vee x_9\vee x_{10})$. A supplied 198-bit source
assignment satisfies all 264 repaired clauses and falsifies only clause 3
of the original. The original UNSAT certificate is imported and was not
reproved in this run. Both formulas have $n=198$, $m=k=264$,
$N=15{,}870$ bits, exact $(2,2)$ balance, and identical full unsigned
incidence data; the SAT repair is \emph{not strictly monotone}.
The two-sign Hamming distance is the minimum nonzero legal distance.
In general, legal signings of a fixed support-simple degree-four support form
$J(4,2)^n$ under one-positive/one-negative swaps, with diameter $2n$.
Thus any support admitting both labels has some opposite-label pair
separated by one swap, although discovering it efficiently is OPEN.
Disjoint products of the concrete pair give a direct
$p=\Omega(N/\log N)$ addressed-input-word lower bound on the all-SAT
input for every exact deterministic decider of the fixed signed
exact-(2,2) language; a charged $O(p)$ template scan matches it.
This is no superlinear SAT time lower bound. Report, independent witness
reproducer, raw ledger and handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-24_0149_LSPV-4_TWO_SIGN_SWAP64.tex}.
\textbf{OPEN:} the universal superpolynomial acquisition/compression/
continuation implication for all exact deterministic deciders.
$P$ versus $NP$ remains \OPEN{}.


\paragraph{EULER-NAE-DOUBLE65: linear-time complementary witnesses and sharp two-witness monotone repair.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT within the signing-construction model; Role~5 independent review pending:}
For every proper \(r\)-uniform unsigned clause support with \(r\ge2\) and
even original variable degrees \(d_v\), and any \emph{prescribed} source
assignment \(a\), pair the \(d_v\) original occurrence sockets of each
variable to form edges in an \(r\)-regular clause multigraph. Pair odd-degree
clause vertices by temporary dummy edges, Euler-orient, and delete dummy
edges. Each original graph edge chooses its head socket as TRUE under \(a\).
The resulting signing has exactly \(d_v/2\) positive and \(d_v/2\) negative
occurrences per original variable; each clause has between
\(\lfloor r/2\rfloor\) and \(\lceil r/2\rceil\) TRUE literals under
\emph{both} \(a\) and its complement. All source reads, derived adjacency,
Euler traversals, output and witness replay take \(O(rm+n)\) word operations
and linear space. In the signed exact-$(2,2)$ proper-three-CNF endpoint,
\(n=3t,m=4t\), both prescribed complementary assignments satisfy the
new formula and every clause is mixed-sign for \(a=1^n\).
This strengthens MAIN102/CAPACITY70's one-witness construction,
not the original signing's SAT label.

For any \emph{monotone} proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ source with \(m\)
clauses, pair each variable's positive and negative occurrence sockets.
Their induced graph on positive and negative clauses is 3-regular bipartite,
so Hall's theorem supplies a perfect matching. Flip both occurrences
on each matched edge. Every clause now contains both signs, each source
variable stays balanced, and the output is satisfied by \emph{both}
all-TRUE and all-FALSE. Exactly \(m\) literal-sign fields change, optimal
because each original monochromatic clause needs one change. This
strictly strengthens MAIN103's distance-\(m\) \emph{all-TRUE only} repair
without raising the optimum cost. Conservative matching time
\(O(m\sqrt m)\) with linear live workspace.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} seed 202610240265, 496 assertion-accounted
checks and zero failures for both witnesses at source sizes up to
\(n=198,m=264\), additional arities, even degrees, monotone optimal repairs
through \(m=256\), and exact 2-SAT, padding, Horn/XOR controls.
Finite tests do not prove runtime hardness. Verified report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-24_0234_LSPV-4_EULER_NAE_DOUBLE65.tex};
adjacent \path{_HANDOFF.md}, \path{_MINI.py}, \path{_RAW.json}
contain peer challenges and reproduction. No existing task schedules changed.

\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} a high-degree or unsigned-support
two-witness normalization necessarily forces expensive SAT decision work.
The sign-constructor intentionally changes the problem's signed input,
and a source-aware canonical-image recognizer plus arbitrary exact fallback
decides this promised SAT family in linear word time.
\textbf{OPEN:} arbitrary-decider superpolynomial post-acquisition
compression/continuation and \(P\ne NP\).

\paragraph{FROZEN-ANTIPODAL-CUT66: exact two-witness frozen-sign circulation and nonlocal cut.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT within the original-socket sign-completion model (independent Role~5 review pending):}
Fix the complete witness assignment $a$ on a proper 3-uniform degree-four
unsigned source with each variable's final polarity quota exactly two
positive and two negative occurrences. Freeze an arbitrary set of
source-socket signs. Let $R_v=2-p_v$ be the number of remaining
true-under-$a$ sockets required at variable $v$; each clause $C$
with $t_C$ frozen-true and $f_C$ free sockets requires between
$L_C=\max(0,1-t_C)$ and $U_C=\min(f_C,2-t_C)$ selected free sockets.
Completion satisfying \emph{both} $a$ and its complement is an exact
integral circulation problem. After local interval checks, feasibility is
equivalent to the following pair of inequalities, for \emph{all}
variable subsets $X$ and clause subsets $Y$:
\[
 \boxed{\begin{aligned}
 R(X)&\le |E_f(X,\mathcal C\setminus Y)|+U(Y),\\
 L(Y)&\le R(V\setminus X)+|E_f(X,Y)|.
 \end{aligned}}
\]
These are Hoffman lower/upper circulation cuts, tested by polynomial
max-flow rather than exponential cut enumeration. Integer min-cost
circulation also yields a cheapest two-witness edit.
This extends MAIN103's \emph{one-witness} frozen-sign flow
and EULER65's \emph{unfrozen} complementary-witness constructor.

A fixed $n=6,m=8$ simple unsigned degree-four support admits seven
frozen occurrence signs for which every variable and clause passes its
individual capacity checks, but global completion fails. An explicit
cut $X=\{0,1\}$, $Y=\{C_0\}$ has $R(X)=2$,
$|E_f(X,\mathcal C\setminus Y)|=0$, $U(Y)=1$.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} standard-library independent residual flow
and exhaustive $6^6=46656$ balanced-signing oracle agree on 522 pin
tests, and all quantified cut pairs were checked for 24 cases, with zero
mismatches. On the fixed eight-clause support 5904 balanced signings
satisfy both constant complementary witnesses. Verified standalone
report, minimal reproducer, result ledger and cross-role handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-24_0336_LSPV-4_FROZEN_ANTIPODAL_CUT66.tex}
and adjacent same-prefix artifacts.
No SAT-variable auxiliaries, clause-support changes or silent
source rewrites are used; all input reads, network construction, failed
augmentations, cut certificates, complete output signs, witness replays
and peak storage are charged. The sign-completion operation may change
the SAT label and is not a SAT-preserving reduction.

\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} unavoidable exponential work in this
fixed-witness frozen-sign compatibility task. \textbf{OPEN:}
a machine-universal superpolynomial lower bound on SAT for
\emph{unchanged} original signed exact-$(2,2)$ inputs.
$P$ versus $NP$ remains \OPEN{}.


\paragraph{PRIORITY-HORN-PARSIMONY67: parsimonious Horn binary/positive-ternary boundary (04:36 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the local calculus:} For each source proper ternary
$(\ell_1\vee\ell_2\vee\ell_3)$, introduce fresh selectors
$(s_1,s_2,s_3)$ and add the positive selector ternary, three Horn
implications $(\neg s_i\vee\ell_i)$ and three Horn priority guards
$(\neg s_2\vee\neg\ell_1)$,
$(\neg s_3\vee\neg\ell_1)$,
$(\neg s_3\vee\neg\ell_2)$. Every satisfying source assignment has
exactly one extension, selecting its first true literal. The uniform
parsimonious output on $n_0$ source variables and $m$ proper ternaries
has $n=n_0+3m$, $m_2=6m$, $k=m$, $M=7m$ and $28m+2$ explicit
fixed-width output words. The binary backbone is Horn and the positive
ternaries are selector-disjoint. The old
three-implication and new unique-selector lifts have identical
minimum strong syntactic 2-CNF and Horn backdoors, $m$ and $2m$,
despite different numbers of selector extensions. \textbf{PROVED/EXACT local
no-go:} Any selector lift in which each original literal occurs only
in the implication $(\neg s_i\vee\ell_i)$ and all other constraints
are source-independent cannot be parsimonious for any clause width
$h\ge2$. The distinct extensions for singleton true-literal sets
$\{i\}$ and $\{j\}$ must both survive when the true-literal set
grows to $\{i,j\}$. The three source-comparison guards explicitly
break this monotone-extension obstruction. A uniquely forced
three-variable proper ternary anchor needs at least seven clauses
within the fixed three-variable anchor calculus. Under the \emph{restricted comparison-guard
calculus}, exactly three guards are necessary and the six correct sets
are the six transitive tournaments; this is not an unrestricted minimum
circuit/encoding theorem. Compared with HORN-SELECTOR-DEGREE60, the new
lift removes the $\prod_j(2^{t_j}-1)$ auxiliary multiplicity but adds
complemented source literal appearances. It does not preserve physical
target occurrence degree four, even though a canonically decoded source
degree-four language remains NP-complete.

The original binary chain is parsimoniously properized using three
\emph{functional} forced-zero variables and seven anchor ternaries;
each binary clause gains one false anchor literal. Applying the priority
lift gives $n=4q+39$, $m_2=6q+66$, $k=q+11$, $M=7q+77$ and
$N=(28q+310)\lceil\log_2(64(11q+117))\rceil$. A flip of one
orientation changes two private literal words. On the all-one UNSAT
input, every exact deterministic decider of the full canonical language
must read at least $q=\Omega(N/\log N)$ words; the canonical source
recognizer matches this in $O(q)$ word operations. Explicit
materialization spends $\Theta(q)$ charged writes, so paid lower-bound
transport has no positive amplification. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} seeded
stdlib audit, 1692 assertion-accounted checks, 64 comparison-guard
subsets, 36 small full-model-count instances and 254 chain orientation
controls, zero failures. The counting-preserving lift does not
prove any universal SAT-time lower bound; $P$ versus $NP$ remains
\OPEN{}. Verified role report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-24_0436_LSPV-4_PRIORITY_HORN_PARSIMONY67.tex};
mini-auditor, raw ledger and peer handoff share its prefix.


\paragraph{HIGH-ORDER-SYNDROME78: all proper syndrome projections can be full (12:36 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, growing explicit family:} For \(t\ge3\),
take the 2-CNF source \(B=(x_1\lor x_2)\) and the invertible
syndrome \(y_1=x_1\oplus P,\ y_2=x_2\oplus P,\ y_j=x_j\ (j\ge3)\),
where \(P=\bigoplus_{j=3}^{t}x_j\). The image of \(B\)'s satisfying
assignments in \(\mathbb F_2^t\) has size \(3\cdot2^{t-2}\), full affine
hull, and \emph{every strict coordinate projection full}, but it
excludes \(0^t\). All unit vectors \(e_j\) are admitted. This is an
arbitrary-order generalization of DUAL-PARITY-JOINT77's marginal hole.
Despite growing projection order, the exact membership predicate is
\((b_1\oplus P_b)\lor(b_2\oplus P_b)\), a degree-two Boolean polynomial
with an \(O(t)\) source-readable solver. High compatibility projection
order does not imply expensive one-bit SAT decision.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, proper binary/ternary realization:} The same
syndrome pins compile to \(G_{t,b}\), with
\[
n=3t-2,\qquad m_2=4t-3,\qquad k=4t-4,\qquad
M=8t-7,\qquad
N=(32t-26)\left\lceil\log_2(64(11t-8))\right\rceil.
\]
The compiler keeps \(t\) original variables, uses \(2t-3\)
functional parity auxiliaries and one free pin variable,
and has exactly two target models for \(b\) in the syndrome image
and zero otherwise. In the fixed-width addressed-word grammar,
the UNSAT input \(b=0^t\) is sensitive to each of \(t\) private pin
groups, yielding an all-decider \(t=\Omega(N/\log N)\) \emph{acquisition}
lower bound, matched by the linear canonical recognizer and
state-adaptive XOR/SCC/Horn/Gaussian/DPLL-fallback policy. The
minimum raw syntactic 2-CNF backdoor lies between
\(\lceil(t-1)/3\rceil\) and \(t-1\), but after paid pin resolution,
prefix propagation and source checking the effective ternary
residual is zero. The comparison with full-table enumeration is
\emph{restricted-policy only}. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:}
seed 202610240436, 375 assertion-accounted groups, zero failures,
both labels through \(t=4096\), exhaustive marginal controls through
\(t=11\), model-count and hostile adaptive-switching controls.
Verified report and same-prefix executable/raw/handoff artifacts:
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-24_1236_LSPV-4_HIGH_ORDER_SYNDROME78.tex}.

\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM (Role 5 FIXED-SYNDROME-FIREWALL113):}
2-CNF plus an arbitrary \emph{fixed} number of weighted mod-two
checks is polynomial by Brakensiek--Gopi--Guruswami; this report
neither reclaims novelty for the fixed-check case nor infers that
growing check rank is mandatory work. \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:}
all-proper-marginal completeness, affine-hull dimension or syntactic
backdoor size as standalone superpolynomial runtime amplifiers.
\textbf{OPEN:} a same-input, all-uniform-adaptive-decider
post-acquisition lower bound for one NP-complete language.
P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.


\paragraph{BALANCE-HEADER-ORACLE79: exact normalization source-acquisition firewall (13:52 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted:} For the original
\texttt{PC22\_ALLFIELD1} normalizer $U(F)$, let
$r_v=\max(p_v,n_v,2)$, $g(F)=\sum_v(2r_v-p_v-n_v)$, and
$V(U(F))=9(m+7g(F))$, $M(U(F))=12(m+7g(F))$.
The opposite-label source controls consist of $q\ge3$ proper
$(\ell_i\vee a_i\vee d_i)$ clauses with a common signed $x$,
with or without a disjoint all-eight-sign-pattern UNSAT core.
At the all-positive source, $g=7q$ in both controls, and
\[
(V,M)_{\mathrm{SAT}}=(450q,600q),\qquad
(V,M)_{\mathrm{UNSAT}}=(450q+72,600q+96).
\]
Flipping any one source orientation decreases $g$ by 2,
$V$ by 126 and $M$ by 168, thereby toggling the second
least-significant bit of the serialized variable-count header.
Every exact deterministic \emph{source-flat} evaluator of this one output
bit must query all $q$ private orientation fields on either source label,
an $\Omega(N/\log N)$ addressed-word acquisition result.
A fully charged $O(q)$ source scan and $O(\log q)$-bit cached metadata
make subsequent header access constant-word-time. Full target
serialization emits $\Theta(q)$ records, with no ARC child contraction
and no paid positive lower-bound amplification.

\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM / endpoint repair:}
The corrected FLMV forbidden box for one uniform all-input
same-machine exact SAT decider is
$t<241/200$, $s\le1/2$, since
$\beta(241/200)=11642479/23232400>1/2$ for
$\beta(c)=((c+2)/c^2-c)/2$.
The historical $t+s<2\cos(\pi/7)$ universal SAT endpoint is
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}: $(7/5,1/4)$ is a false positive and
$(101/100,9/10)$ is a false negative relative to the real FLMV curve.
CP22LIFT1 and PC22\_ALLFIELD1 establish transports in specific
Cutting Planes and Polynomial Calculus systems; none forces arbitrary
exact SAT deciders to emit their refutations.

\textbf{OPEN:} A correct all-input finite charged route through the
jointly achievable uniform SAT time--space region from cofinal
$(K,K)$ seeds into the corrected FLMV endpoint. Eager/lazy
bit-addressed child simulations and restricted exact joins do not

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 21,148 assertion-accounted checks, $q=3$ to $64$,
SAT/UNSAT and all single-sign flips, zero failures. Report, auditor,
raw data and additive coverage/handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-24_1352_LSPV-4_BALANCE_HEADER_ORACLE79.tex}.

\paragraph{SENSITIVITY-SATURATION80: exact source-read saturation for every explicit truth-preserving transport (14:36 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the separately addressed source-field model, with independent peer review pending:}
For the canonical proper-binary chain $F_b$ of $q$ independently oriented
fields, $U=F_{1^q}$ is UNSAT and each single-field flip is SAT with
the same input length and metadata.
Every deterministic exact source-to-target transducer, even one emitting
a constant-size ordinary SAT target, must read all $q$ orientation
fields on $U$: omission of field $i$ leaves an identical
execution transcript on the opposite-label one-flip neighbor.
The argument also holds for adaptive oracle Turing reductions whose
target queries are fully serialized, self-contained inputs and whose
source reads are charged outside the oracle.
Thus the earlier \texttt{INPUT-QUERY-CEILING86}
transport inequality, applied only to the original source bound
$L_{\rm old}=q$, has
\[
L_{\rm old}-R_q(U)-H_q\le q-q-H_q\le0.
\]
This \emph{retire}s positive lower-bound transport from that
$q$-query source argument through any such explicit compiler; it
does not restrict a future independently proved post-acquisition bound
or an existential all-input ARC successor.

A charged binary-to-proper-ternary padding encoder has
$n=2q+7$, $M=2q+8$ and $q+4$ free new variables, and must
read all $q$ source orientations. An explicit decide-first encoder
can emit one of two constant-size clause-distinct proper-3CNF
targets, both $n=4,m=8,N=340$ bits: an UNSAT eight-clause
partition of the Boolean four-cube and a SAT target formed by
replacing exactly one clause, with a unique witness.
Even this equal-metadata constant-output transformation still
reads every field on the all-one UNSAT source.
The associated adaptive scanner charges source recognition,
policy selection, early-zero detection, output writing,
and optional source witness reconstruction; no true label is supplied.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 25,690 assertion-accounted checks,
zero failures; independent SCC labels for all orientation strings
through $q=12$, exact small model counts, and 528 adversarial
single-field comparison pairs through $q=32$.
The source model uses
$N=(4q+18)\lceil\log_2(128(q+4))\rceil$ bits,
so the only all-decider bound is $q=\Omega(N/\log N)$
addressed-field acquisition, not unrestricted SAT decision time.
\textbf{PARTIAL/RESTRICTED:} virtual children that point back to
source input are not ordinary self-contained small SAT child strings
and must charge every dereference, replay and live ancestor.
\textbf{OPEN:} one jointly achieved all-input uniform SAT
time-space successor, or a cofinal finite charged route into the
verified FLMV forbidden region.
The historical cosine product is still retired.
Standalone verified report, mini-auditor, machine-readable results,

\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-24_1436_LSPV-4_SENSITIVITY_SATURATION80.tex}.

\paragraph{BLOCK-CLOSED-ORACLE85: all proper edge-block counts and strict-subset statuses.}
The verified standalone report
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-24_1910_LSPV-4_BLOCK_CLOSED_ORACLE85.tex}
and peer code/results/handoff use the same dated prefix.
For $q\ge3$, encode $x_i\oplus x_{i+1}=b_i$ on a cycle, two binary
clauses per edge, and properize each binary clause $C$ as
$(C\vee u)\wedge(C\vee\neg u)$ with a private $u$.
The connected duplicate-free parent has
\[
n=3q,\quad m=k=4q,\quad
N=(16q+2)\lceil\log_2(64(7q+1))\rceil.
\]
It has $2^{2q+1}$ full models for even parity and zero for odd.
Every proper selection $S\subsetneq[q]$ of \emph{whole}
four-clause edge blocks has exactly $2^{3q-|S|}$
models on the declared variable universe, independent of $b$.
An odd-parity parent is clause-minimally UNSAT, so every strict
individual-clause subset on either label is SAT.
Thus no source-blind adaptive controller using only those two
\emph{typed} oracles can recover the parent label.
Even with source field reads it must inspect $q$ independent
orientation groups, matched by a paid parity scan:
$\Omega(N/\log N)$ addressed-word acquisition, not superlinear
post-acquisition time.

\textbf{Sharp counterquery:}
a strict child retaining $q-1$ whole edge blocks plus
\emph{both} ternary twins of one closing binary half has
$2^{2q+1}$ models when parity is even and $2^{2q}$
when odd. Hence one free \emph{non-block-closed exact count}
would defeat the count-only indistinguishability, while
the child remains SAT on both labels.
The local independent finite audit reports 38,342 charged
assertions and zero failures; the compact peer script
tests a strict subset of those claims.
No uncharged exact-count service is admitted as an
ordinary FLMV SAT machine.
\textbf{OPEN:} a source-grounded, all-input,
same-machine favorable time-space ARC edge and the cofinal route.
P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.


\paragraph{PAD-FLOOR-SCOPED87: charged two-sided scoped-child length floor (20:47 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only for certified valid closed scoped proper-3CNF streams;
independent peer audit pending.}
Appending $g$ fresh independent one-clause, three-variable OPEN/CLAUSE/CLOSE
blocks multiplies the number of satisfying assignments by $7^g$ while
preserving the SAT label and every old boundary projection.
For the fixed-width event encoding
$L(n,m)=(2+4n+4m)\lceil\log_2(64(n+m+1))\rceil$,
the least $g$ meeting any floor $B\ge L(n,m)$ satisfies
\[
 B\le L(n+3g,m+g)\le 2B+O(\log(B+2)),\qquad
 w'\le\max\{w,3\}.
\]
A sequential paid generator takes $O((L+B)\operatorname{polylog}B)$
elementary bit work with $O(\log B)$ controller bits, excluding paid
validation, retained ancestors and any $\Theta(B)$ readable child buffer.
Under an \emph{independently proved}, correct scoped child factory with
bounded width increase, this repairs the missing \emph{lower} child-length
bound for any fixed recursion depth, as required by the earlier
CHILD-SCALE-PREFIX117 counterexample. It supplies \emph{no} all-input
normalizer, compatibility constructor or achieved SAT exponent pair.
The deterministic local audit records 151,274 checks and zero discrepancies,
including matched SAT/UNSAT proper-clause sources; large scales are
numerical tests and physical FLMV tape metrics remain unproved.

\subsection*{M22-UNPAIRED-ANCHOR96: canonical strict monotone target transport (25 October 2026)}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the declared proper monotone exact-(2,2) grammar; independent peer review pending.}
Canonical target \(\mathcal C_\star\) consists of unique proper three-distinct-variable,
clausewise uniform-sign CNFs with exactly \(2+\) and \(2-\) occurrences per active
variable, \emph{excluding only fully complementary-clause-paired formulas}.
Individual complementary pairs, disconnected components and easy islands are
allowed. For \(k\) active variables, \(m=4k/3\) and \(m_+=m_-=2k/3\).
Literalwise complement \(\bar C\) retains OR.

Using positive clause supports \(P=\{123,145,246,356\}\) and negative supports
\(Q=\{124,136,235,456\}\), let \(H=P^+\cup P^-\) and \(Y=P^+\cup Q^-\).
Both are valid 6-variable, 8-clause target-source cores; \(H\) is fully paired
with 18 satisfying assignments and \(Y\) has no complementary partners,
20 assignments and the explicit witness \(111000\). The no-test
anchor \(F\mapsto F\dot\cup Y\), with fresh variables, sends \emph{every valid}
\(F\in\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}\) into \(\mathcal C_\star\), preserves its SAT bit,
multiplies its model count by 20 and provides an exact witness map.
The full source grammar remains essential; the signed TWO-SIGN-SWAP64 SAT mutant
is not necessarily monotone and cannot be promoted to this target.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT matched hostile controls.}
Let \(U\) be D\"ocker's 198-variable, 264-clause UNSAT strict monotone core
(known theorem; source grammar rechecked), and put
\[
 A_p=H^{\dot\cup(p+33)}\dot\cup Y\in\mathrm{SAT},\qquad
 B_p=U\dot\cup H^{\dot\cup p}\dot\cup Y\in\mathrm{UNSAT}.
\]
For every \(p\ge0\), these are \emph{both} in \(\mathcal C_\star\) and
have identical \(k=6p+204\), \(m=8p+272\) and dense encoded length.
With \(D(F)=|\{C\in F:\bar C\notin F\}|\),
\[
D(A_p)=8,\quad D(B_p)=272,\quad
D(A_p)/m=1/(p+34)\to0,\quad D(B_p)/m=34/(p+34)\to0.
\]
Thus neither target membership nor opposite SAT labels require positive
unpaired-clause density. The source-aware canonical-block recognizer is an easy
restricted policy with paid \(O(N\operatorname{polylog}N)\) work and
\(O(\log N)\) live writable/index bits; it is not all-decider coverage.

For the explicitly declared unary-width header and fixed signed-ID records,
\(N=3w+2+3m(w+1)\), \(w=\lceil\log_2(k+1)\rceil\).
The anchor has the exact length increase
\[
 N'-N=3(m+1)(w'-w)+24(w'+1),\qquad
 w'=\lceil\log_2(k+7)\rceil,\quad w'-w\in\{0,1\},
\]
so \(N'=N(1+O(1/\log k))\). A valid-promise virtual accessor takes
polylogarithmic bit work per output bit and \(O(\log N)\) live space;
total malformed-input validation by an explicitly paid nested-scan routine
costs \(O(N^2\operatorname{polylog}N)\) bit work.
This only transports a hypothetical target solver to the restricted
\(\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}\) language with these losses. It does \emph{not}
derive the FLMV ordinary-SAT forbidden box from NP-completeness.

\subsection*{LSPV-4 ARC-REPLAY-TAPE-FIREWALL134 (26 October 2026): charged nondeterministic proof replay}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, scoped one-way interface:} When a $k$-bit commitment $u$ is supplied completely before an independently chosen $k$-bit replay $v$, an exact one-pass deterministic or nondeterministic checker for $u=v$ has at least $2^k$ distinguishable midpoint configurations. A standard finite-alphabet implementation needs $\Omega(k)$ live workspace bits. This \emph{does not} affect the original canonical source-authenticated depth-first TREE verifier or constitute a SAT lower bound; it prevents blindly assigning its $O((h+1)\log(N+P))$ space cost to a shared-DAG proof with separated replay.
For $k=\lceil N^{3/5}\rceil$ and constant branch depth $h$, the particular replay checker uses space exponent $3/5$ while
\[
 \frac35-\beta(241/200)=\frac{2296961}{23232400}>0,
 \qquad \beta(c)=\tfrac12((c+2)/c^2-c).
\]
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT conditional model bridge:} a uniformly capped $P_*$-bit all-UNSAT certificate may be nondeterministically guessed and \emph{physically stored} in $O(P_*\operatorname{polylog}(P_*+2))$ time and $O(P_*)$ space before an indexed proof verifier runs. With worst-case $P_*\le N^{a+o(1)}$ and near-linear verifier time in $N+P_*$, a correctly clocked co-nondeterministic SAT machine has time exponent at most $\max(1,a)$, but no corresponding deterministic small-space ARC machine or proof acquisition follows. Under the \emph{extra} $d=901/500$ seed the conditional van Melkebeek threshold is $651000/361301$ and the gap above $7/4$ is $74893/1445204$. $P=NP$ alone provides no such seed.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} Standalone standard-library audit passed 270,568 deterministic assertion groups over 87,380 both-label replay pairs and adversarial state collisions. This is abstract interface checking, not FLMV machine timing. \textbf{OPEN:} universally affordable affine/adaptive certificates, paid deterministic discovery, hereditary ordinary SAT children, same-machine FLMV pair, ARC-EDGE-90, and $P\ne NP$.

\subsection*{LSPV-4 ARC-PROVENANCE-RECONVERGENCE135 (26 October 2026): full-context DAG no-merge and source-offset replay}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, scoped:} a binary adaptive proof DAG retaining the \emph{complete, immutable} original-variable assignment has no nontrivial reconvergence after branch divergence. In an ordinary proper signed 3CNF of physical source length $N=\Theta(k\log(k+2))$, the pairs $(x_i\vee a_i\vee b_i)\wedge(\neg x_i\vee a_i\vee b_i)$ yield one identical normalized residual under all $2^k$ selector assignments but $2^k$ different source-byte origin vectors for the surviving clauses. A separated one-pass selector-prelude/offset-replay checker with exact authentication requires at least $2^k$ midpoint configurations and $\Omega(k)$ live bits in the stated bounded-time standard model, but this is \emph{not} an arbitrary SAT lower bound.
\textbf{CRITICAL COUNTERCONTROL:} interleaving each selector bit with its claimed offset in source clause order admits $O(\log N)$ writable/index workspace and $N\operatorname{polylog}N$ bit time for this \emph{special family}; the one-way memory result therefore does not generalize to different proof orderings, source regeneration, semantic certificates, or all-input SAT deciders.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 16,643 deterministic assertion groups, zero failures, both source SAT/UNSAT labels, exact byte offsets and source authentication, complete adaptive trees through depth ten. \textbf{KNOWN THEOREM SCOPE:} FLMV and van Melkebeek continue to require their original matched machine hypotheses. \textbf{OPEN:} globally correct source-authenticated history forgetting, affordable all-UNSAT certificates, full inverse Rank/Select, arbitrary-$K$ ARC contraction, ARC-EDGE-90, and $P\ne NP$.

\paragraph{LSPV-4: ARC-DEGREE-AUTH-FIREWALL136 (26 October 2026, 16:10 UTC): exact virtual-boundary degree probes and promise-authentication firewall.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, scoped (independent referee pending):} A deterministic addressed-value black-box identity check on Boolean functions of ANF degree at most $d$ over $k$ boundary bits needs exactly $D(k,d)=\sum_{j=0}^{d}\binom{k}{j}$ queries: low-Hamming-weight evaluations suffice by M\"obius inversion and fewer leave a nonzero coefficient-kernel adversary. For every \emph{fixed} $d$, deciding whether a \emph{general Boolean circuit} has ANF degree at most $d$ is coNP-complete by the uniform reduction $C_D(x,z)=D(x)\wedge z_1\wedge\cdots\wedge z_{d+1}$ from Circuit-UNSAT. The latter is a source-authentication obstacle, not an unconditional runtime lower bound.
\textbf{CONDITIONAL cost ledger:} If source-degree authentication costs $A_{\rm auth}$, there are $Q$ candidate summaries, and one fully charged virtual addressed-bit evaluation costs $U$, exact checks cost $A_{\rm auth}+QD(k,d)(U+\operatorname{poly}(k))$. For fixed $d$ and $k=O(\log N)$ the query multiplier is polylogarithmic, but a claimed low-degree representation is not valid without a source-equivalence proof. An $O(k)$-gate point-function circuit of degree $k$ defeats degree-$d$ canonical probes when its support address has weight $>d$.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 210,742 deterministic assertion groups, including 102,560 coefficient reconstructions, 255 nullspace witnesses and 1,612 proper ordinary signed-3CNF SAT/UNSAT circuit-degree controls, zero failures; no strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ reduction claimed. Exact source-verified strict margins for the separate FLMV and EXTRA-seed van Melkebeek scenarios remain $26279/23232400$ in space and $74893/1445204$ for illustrative $c=7/4$; the latter assumes an unproved $d=901/500$ small-space deterministic SAT seed.
\textbf{OPEN:} an all-UNSAT source-authenticated low-degree summary calculus, uniformly small verifier/certificate and actual construction/discovery costs, a two-level joint time-space ARC successor from unknown polynomial $K$, ARC-EDGE-90 and $P\ne NP$. Prior LSPV-3 AFFINE-HELLY129, LSPV-1 ARC184 and LSPV-5 PROOF-SYSTEM-FALLBACK163 are acknowledged and not claimed as new results.

\subsection*{LSPV-4 ARC-QUADRATIC-SOURCE137 (26 October 2026, 09:52 PDT): authenticated mixed algebraic certificates and a finite coverage firewall}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted:} A quadratic Boolean-ring identity $\sum_j m_jg_j=1$, where every $g_j$ is either the false indicator of an original clause restricted to width at most two or a linear parity equation authenticated by four genuine original source clauses, is a sound UNSAT leaf certificate. Four exhaustive two-variable branch leaves suffice for a source UNSAT proof. Original byte offsets, sign patterns, variable identities and multipliers are authenticated; no arbitrary circuit-degree promise is trusted.
A connected proper signed 12-clause 3CNF on six variables has no root or selected first-level identity in this grammar, but all four second-level leaves have the mixed five-term identity
\[
(1+a)(1+b)+ab+c(1+w)+cw+(a+b+c)=1
\quad\text{in }\mathbb F_2[a,b,c,w]/(x_i^2-x_i).
\]
Actual physical ASCII source/first-child/grandchild lengths are 1016/824/616 bits. Its serialized four-leaf proof is 7752 bits, so shorter children \emph{do not} imply a contraction.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} The signed exact-$(2,2)$ connected historical W20 UNSAT source ($n=15,m=20$), and two seeded connected seven-variable UNSAT controls, do not have a depth-two adaptive certificate in this declared restricted grammar (complete finite variable/branch span search). This does not lower-bound other proof systems or SAT algorithms. The full Python test passed 66 assertion groups, zero failures; a separate JavaScript mini reproducer and raw data were read back from GitHub.
\textbf{RESOURCE FIREWALL:} source-authenticated coefficient sorting of a physically stored guessed proof of $P$ bits gives conservative $T=O(N\operatorname{polylog}N+P\operatorname{polylog}(N+P))$ and $S=O(P\operatorname{polylog}(N+P))$ on multitape simulation. A $D=1+n+\binom n2$ coefficient accumulator cannot use free random work-tape writes in the FLMV model. \paragraph{DUAL-NATIVE-RAM-TAPE139 (26 October 2026, 22:42 UTC): native model-transfer and simulation firewall.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, stated simulator:} For a fixed finite number of sequential work
tapes and one sequential read-only input head, an initial-input-counted single-tape
simulator has
\[
T_1=O_k\!\bigl(T_k(N+S_k+\log(T_k+2))\bigr),\qquad
P_1=O_k\!\bigl(N+S_k+\log(T_k+2)\bigr).
\]
Hence the conservative ceiling-exponent translation is
\(t_1\le t_k+\max\{1,s_k\}\), \(p_1\le\max\{1,s_k\}\).
The known single-tape near-quadratic stripe, transported \emph{only} through
this upper-bound simulator, yields merely
\(t_k+\max\{1,s_k\}\ge2\), a vacuous inequality for the ordinary
\(t_k\ge1\) setting; it does not prove a multitape \(t_k+s_k\ge2\).
The latter needs an independent native sequential-input crossing argument.
A balanced \(h\)-node dictionary laid out in heap order has a
\(\Theta(h)\)-head-travel worst-case search although it uses
\(O(\log h)\) logical comparisons. Thus STREAM-DICT132's polylogarithmic
random-access accounting does \emph{not} automatically transfer to a
fixed-head sequential tape.
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM, model-qualified:} FLMV and Williams/Buss--Williams
alternation-trading lower bounds apply to their indexed random-access
SAT machines and separately verified hypotheses, not to arbitrary tape/RAM
conventions. Williams's corrected polynomial-resource product is
\(t+s\ge(1+\sqrt5)/2\), not the withdrawn universal cosine product.
Eight published Buss--Williams positive-space entries were
checked as rounded pointwise results, never an exact continuous frontier.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 8,274 deterministic assertion checks passed with
zero failures, including 1,364 exhaustive toy equality promise controls;
the toy is not an independent exact-$(2,2)$ gadget proof.
\textbf{OPEN:} all-input ARC contraction from arbitrary polynomial exponent
\(K\), same-machine SAT/UNSAT recursive certificate coverage,
ARC-EDGE-90, and \(P\ne NP\).
The original Santhanam 2001 full theorem PDF was not independently accessed;
its exact native hypotheses remain source-verification pending.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, PROMISE-SCOPED:}
For fixed left--gap--right bit records $I_m(a,b)$ of physical length $N=\Theta(m\log(m+2))$ with sequentially locatable paired fields, the SAT-labelled equality predicate $a=b$ has a co-nondeterministic verifier with ONE sequential read-only input head, $O(\log N)$ guessed mismatch-index bits, worst-branch $O(N\operatorname{polylog}N)$ time and $O(\log N)$ writable space. Every SAT/equality branch accepts, and some UNSAT/inequality branch rejects. Invalid guesses and timeouts accept under a length-only clock. The compiler transfer to the source-reported proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ $E_m(a,b)$ (168m variables, 224m clauses) is \textbf{CONDITIONAL} on independent full gadget and physical-layout review and applies only to that exact promised input family, not unrestricted SAT. This counters any promotion of a deterministic one-input-head equality crossing-sequence lower bound to all co-nondeterministic UNSAT certificate paths. Prior deterministic TWOHEAD86 and exact22 tape transport remain valid in their own declared models.

\textbf{MODEL LEDGERS:}
$M_1$ has one read/write tape and counts the initial $N$ input cells in footprint $P_1\ge N$; $M_k$ has ONE sequential read-only input head and fixed $k$ sequential work heads; RAT counts binary index-tape writes (at least logarithmic for polynomial address ranges). The conservative deterministic simulation $T_1=O(T_k(N+S_k+\log(T_k+2)))$, $P_1=O(N+S_k+\log(T_k+2))$ gives ceilings $t_1\le t_k+\max(1,s_k)$, $p_1\le\max(1,s_k)$, and a single-tape quadratic stripe only yields the trivial $t_k+\max(1,s_k)\ge2$ for $t_k\ge1$. The Buss--Williams positive-space RAT Figure~2 points are native sampled alternation-trading exclusions, not an optimal smooth curve; Williams's corrected polynomial product uses $\varphi=(1+\sqrt5)/2$, not a universal cosine product. Strict same-machine margins still required.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:}
5,460 exhaustive abstract record pairs $m=1,\dots,6$, 126 equality and 5,334 inequality controls; standalone GitHub JavaScript passed 114,697 assertions and an expanded independent local Python audit passed 111,061 assertions, both with zero failures. Neither is a full source-gadget compiler or physical TM benchmark.

promise family with $N=\Theta(m\log(m+2))$ and gap $G=\Theta(N)$,
the separately charged augmented one-input-head model $M_J$ permits
jumps of radius at most $J$ but reads only the target input symbol.
A complete work-configuration, head-endpoint, and direction crossing
record has $B=O_k(S+\log(N+T+2)+1)$ bits. Injection of accepting
gap-crossing transcripts followed by double averaging gives
ONE SAT YES source witnessing simultaneously
\[
 (R+1)B=\Omega(m),\qquad TJB=\Omega(Gm).
\]
Thus native polynomial exponent ceilings obey
$\boxed{r+s\ge1,\quad t+s+j\ge2}$.
Jump radius weakens the movement-time surface but does not remove
the independent reversal--space surface while there remains exactly
one deterministic input head. Two input heads, indexed RAT and
co-nondeterministic certificates do not inherit this constraint.

\paragraph{ARC-SENSITIVITY-LOCALITY144 (26 October 2026, 20:42 PDT): necessary two-level input-dependency surface for source-local SAT-preserving virtual children.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, peer review pending:}
On any well-formed source with $m$ separately SAT-producing sensitive sign flips, a deterministic fixed-layout two-level source-to-child bit generator with first-level shared source-preprocessing probes $p_1$, per-first-level-output source probes $q_1$, second-level shared first-level-child queries $p_2$, per-final-bit first-level queries $q_2$, and final encoded length $L_2$ must obey
\[
\boxed{m\le p_1+q_1p_2+q_1q_2L_2.}
\]
For $b$ \emph{disjoint} SAT-producing multi-sign changes that remain inside a signed occurrence quota class, the same bound holds with $b$ in place of $m$. The deterministic source-read transcript on the original UNSAT member must hit every legal SAT neighbor; otherwise the final child string is unchanged and both-label correctness fails. Descriptor creation, caches, output tags and source-address routing count toward the stated budgets. This is a necessity theorem for a \emph{bit-local reduction interface}, not a restriction on arbitrary nonlocal SAT algorithms.

\textbf{Connected witness and two-level exponent surface:}
LSPV-1's earlier ARC191 cubic-prism odd-Tseitin source has $m=24h=\Theta(N/\log N)$ ordinary-input sign-sensitive fields and $b=12h$ disjoint SAT-producing paired flips within the \emph{signed exact-$(4,4)$} class, \emph{not} exact-$(2,2)$. For unbounded $h$, the literal header must encode variable and clause counts with $O(\log h)$ self-delimiting bits; the peer's fixed 88-bit header is valid only for the finite audits before its 32-bit count fields overflow. With $p_1=N^{\pi_1+o(1)}$, $q_1=N^{\alpha_1+o(1)}$, $p_2=N^{\pi_2+o(1)}$, $q_2=N^{\alpha_2+o(1)}$ and $L_2=N^{\lambda+o(1)}$, one must have
\[
\max\{\pi_1,\alpha_1+\pi_2,\alpha_1+\alpha_2+\lambda\}\ge1.
\]
If $p_1+q_1p_2=o(N/\log N)$ and $q_1q_2=N^{o(1)}$, a fixed $\lambda<1$ is impossible \emph{in this locality model}. A source-global OR summary instead shrinks an easy sign-sensitive promise to one bit after reading all relevant bits, so no general SAT-decision lower bound follows. This is a distinct restricted dependency argument beyond the specific literal-output length floors of CLAUSE-LENGTH130 and PINNED131.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} independent connected prism $(N,m,b)=(448,72,36),(568,96,48),(688,120,60)$ exhaustive SAT/UNSAT source and quota-pair audits plus six exhaustive two-stage OR-block both-label tests, $36{,}423$ deterministic assertions, no failures. The OR maps are Boolean views, not two independently serialized ordinary-3CNF children. \section{GUARD-CONTINUATION-FIXEDPOINT126 update (LSPV-5)}
For any ordered proper 3CNF \(F\), define one guard layer by replacing
each \((\ell_1\vee\ell_2\vee\ell_3)\) with
\[
(\neg x\vee\ell_1\vee a)\wedge(\neg a\vee\ell_2\vee\ell_3),
\]
using one shared fresh guard and clause-local auxiliaries.  After \(d\)
fresh iterations, the exact feasible relation on the \(d\) guards is
\(\{0,1\}^d\) when \(F\) is SAT and
\(\{0,1\}^d\setminus\{1^d\}\) when \(F\) is UNSAT.  Thus the root is
always SAT, every boundary word except \(1^d\) is automatically feasible,
and the \(1^d\) corner is exactly the original SAT problem.

Pinning every guard true by proper four-clause unit gadgets gives an
ordinary proper 3CNF equivalent to \(F\) with
\[
M_d=2^dm+4d,\qquad n_d=n+(2^d-1)m+3d.
\]
For fixed \(d\), its encoded length is that of the guarded parent up to a
\(1+o(1)\) factor.  Hence the continuation exponent is \(\lambda=1\), not
\(1/2\), and the honest one-call recurrence is
\(t'\le\max\{r,t\}\): no strict ARC time contraction follows.  A full
table still includes this one full-size corner.  Conversely, a uniform
adaptive root decider can select the outer guard-zero witness and need not
acquire the exact boundary relation at all.  This is
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT for the declared transform}, not an unrestricted SAT
lower bound.

The finite audit covers 325 base formulas (all 255 nonempty signed-clause
subsets on three variables, explicit affine/Horn/padded-2SAT SAT and UNSAT
controls, and 64 seeded random formulas), with 11,198
assertion-accounted semantic checks and zero failures.  Report, code, raw
results and handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-24_1952_LSPV-5_GUARD_CONTINUATION_FIXEDPOINT126.tex}.
The exact remaining obligation is one all-input, jointly charged uniform
route into a verified FLMV forbidden box that removes the full-size hard
corner without deciding the source first or hiding compatibility in an
oracle.  P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.


\section{BOUNDARY-SUPPORT-UNIVERSALITY127 update (LSPV-5)}
For every ordered proper 3CNF \(F\), boundary dimension \(d\ge1\), and
\(1\le h\le2^d\), a uniform linear-record proper-3CNF construction has
exact pinned-boundary function
\[
Q_{F,d,h}(b)=[{\rm unsigned}(b)<h]\vee{\rm SAT}(F).
\]
The unpinned root is always SAT via \(b=0\).  If \(F\) is UNSAT, exactly
\(h\) boundary words are feasible and exactly \(2^d-h\) memberships carry
the source SAT bit.  Thus every hard-membership count from zero through
\(2^d-1\) coexists with a source-independent root witness.  Role~1's
DENSE-BOUNDARY-BYPASS128 is the \(h=1\) extreme, while
GUARD-CONTINUATION-FIXEDPOINT126 is the \(h=2^d-1\) extreme after boundary
renaming.

All hard memberships share the single bit \({\rm SAT}(F)\); an
\(O(d)\)-gate comparator plus that bit determines the whole boundary
function.  Explicit table output may still cost \(\Omega(2^d)\), but a
one-bit root decider need not compile it.  Pinning any hard boundary word
produces an ordinary child of the same asymptotic encoded size as the
parent, so \(\lambda=1\) and the transport recurrence yields only
\(t'\le\max\{r,t\}\).  Boundary-support density, cardinality and multiplicity
are therefore \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED as standalone ARC progress
measures}; this does not refute a different all-input semantic contractor.

The executable audit covered 326 source formulas, 4,138 constructed roots,
40,380 boundary restrictions, 4,138 exact support counts and 2,976 hard
corners: 52,284 assertion-accounted checks with zero failures, including
affine SAT/UNSAT, proper Horn and dual-Horn SAT, padded-2SAT SAT/UNSAT, and
independent-component controls.  Report, code, raw result and handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-24_2052_LSPV-5_BOUNDARY_SUPPORT_UNIVERSALITY127.tex}.

The original HEREDITARY-SCOPED10 proof body/executable remains unavailable;
only its continuity summary was recovered.  The exact all-input,
same-machine favorable ARC successor or cofinal finite route into a
verified FLMV forbidden box remains \OPEN{}.  P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.


\section{OUTPUT-USE-TRILEMMA170 update (LSPV-5)}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, model/interface theorem:} a generated ordinary child
used by a downstream random-access SAT solver must be (i) materialized on
readable storage, paying its literal length \(L\), (ii) served by a fully
charged virtual accessor from source and child address, or (iii) fused with a
separately proved compatible streaming consumer.  A never-reread write-only
port is not a fourth free random-access input.  The reverse-query hostile
control is exact: after the identity stream \(x\in\{0,1\}^L\) ends and source
rereads are unavailable, answering positions \(L,L-1,\ldots,1\) requires
\(L\) retained state bits; otherwise the source must be reread or recomputed
and that work belongs to the virtual-access ledger.

LSPV-1's ARC-OUTPUT-LEDGER190 materializing simulation survives independent
review.  With active simulated region
\(Q=\Theta(N+S_k+\log(T_k+N+2))\), its chronological prefix construction has
\[
 T_1=O(T_kQ),\qquad P_1=O(Q+L).
\]
The \(L\) term is necessary only when the output is physically retained on the
counted tape; for a one-bit decision output it reduces to the conservative
dual-TM governance bound.  These are construction upper bounds and are never
reversed into machine lower bounds.

The typed copy compiler
\[
 \Phi_r(F)=\bigwedge_{j<r}F(x_{jn+1},\ldots,x_{jn+n})
\]
preserves SAT and UNSAT but has child-length exponent \(\ell\ge1\).
Invoking an all-input solver with ceiling \(M^{t+o(1)}\) on that child costs
\(N^{\ell t+o(1)}\), so this compiler is \textbf{FALSIFIED as an ARC
contraction mechanism}.  Two levels collapse algebraically to
\(\Phi_{r_2}(\Phi_{r_1}(F))\cong\Phi_{r_1r_2}(F)\) and remain expansion;
write-only intermediate output cannot be reread for free.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} the dependency-free audit passed 14,815 assertions,
reproducing ARC190's exact \(392\mapsto337192\) and
\(176\mapsto52168\) length anchors, two physical typed levels
\(832\mapsto1832\), chronological materialization, all reverse transcripts
through \(L=12\), and 961 exponent controls.  This is an interface theorem and
does not lower-bound arbitrary SAT algorithms.

The one-head HEADMOVE-1 and radius-\(J\) inequalities remain restricted to
their declared models.  They are not RAM/RAT bounds.  The exact22 source is an
easy promise, and no promise result is promoted to full SAT.  Uniform
affordable all-ordinary-UNSAT coverage, a fully charged two-level child
accessor, an arbitrary-\(K\) same-machine ARC successor, ARC-EDGE-90, and
\(P\ne NP\) remain \OPEN{}.
